#include "wifi_command.h"

#include <stddef.h>

bool wifi_command_decode(const uint8_t *packet, uint32_t length,
			 wifi_command_t *command)
{
	wifi_command_t decoded;

	if (packet == NULL || command == NULL || length != WIFI_COMMAND_PACKET_SIZE)
		return false;
	decoded.type = packet[0] >> 5U;
	if (decoded.type < WIFI_COMMAND_START || decoded.type > WIFI_COMMAND_JUNCTION)
		return false;
	decoded.argument = packet[0] & 0x1fU;
	decoded.sequence = packet[1];
	*command = decoded;
	return true;
}
=== C:\Users\Yong Jun\school\INF2004-Embedded_Systems\project\INF2004_Project\app_program\subsystem1\wifi\wifi_command.h ===
#ifndef WIFI_COMMAND_H
#define WIFI_COMMAND_H

#include <stdbool.h>
#include <stdint.h>

#define WIFI_COMMAND_PACKET_SIZE 2U

typedef enum {
	WIFI_COMMAND_START = 1,
	WIFI_COMMAND_PAUSE,
	WIFI_COMMAND_RESUME,
	WIFI_COMMAND_STOP,
	WIFI_COMMAND_REQUEST_STATUS,
	WIFI_COMMAND_JUNCTION
} wifi_command_type_t;

typedef struct {
	uint8_t type;
	uint8_t argument;
	uint8_t sequence;
} wifi_command_t;

/* Decode the legacy two-byte dashboard packet. The sequence is eight bits and
 * wraps at 255. This format has no authentication or sender timestamp; a
 * successful decode does not establish freshness, authority, or permission to
 * move. Argument meaning must be validated by the vehicle controller.
 * The output is unchanged on failure. */
bool wifi_command_decode(const uint8_t *packet, uint32_t length,
			 wifi_command_t *command);

#endif
=== C:\Users\Yong Jun\school\INF2004-Embedded_Systems\project\INF2004_Project\app_program\subsystem1\wifi\wifi_mqtt_bridge.c ===
#include "../../app.h"
#include "../../vehicle_buffers.h"
#include "wifi_mqtt_bridge.h"
#include "wifi_mqtt_bridge_owner.h"
#include "wifi_telemetry.h"

#define BRIDGE_CAPACITY 4U
#define BRIDGE_WORDS (BRIDGE_CAPACITY * \
	(1U + ((sizeof(telemetry_message_t) + sizeof(INT) - 1U) / sizeof(INT))))

typedef struct {
	telemetry_message_t pending;
	uint32_t max_age_ms;
	uint32_t last_clock_ms;
	bool have_pending;
} bridge_state_t;

typedef struct {
	uint32_t sequence;
	uint32_t time;
	uint32_t flags;
} bridge_snapshot_t;

#if TM_MQTT
static INT s_storage[BRIDGE_WORDS];
#endif
static ID s_buffer;
static bridge_state_t s_bridge;
/* Published last by the single startup owner; only the network owner faults it. */
static int s_status;
static int s_stop_requested;
static uint32_t s_started_at;
/* One network-owner writer; the sequence prevents mixed cross-core snapshots. */
static bridge_snapshot_t s_snapshot;
static bool s_fault_sent;
static bool s_fault_delivery_reported;

static bool valid_packet(const telemetry_message_t *message)
{
	return message->header.type == VEHICLE_MESSAGE_TELEMETRY &&
	       message->header.version == VEHICLE_MESSAGE_VERSION &&
	       wifi_telemetry_packet_valid((const uint8_t *)message->payload,
					   message->payload_length);
}

static void snapshot_write(bridge_snapshot_t *snapshot, uint32_t now, uint32_t flags)
{
	uint32_t sequence = __atomic_load_n(&snapshot->sequence, __ATOMIC_SEQ_CST);
	sequence = sequence >= UINT32_MAX - 1U ? 2U : sequence + 2U;
	__atomic_store_n(&snapshot->sequence, sequence - 1U, __ATOMIC_SEQ_CST);
	__atomic_store_n(&snapshot->time, now, __ATOMIC_SEQ_CST);
	__atomic_store_n(&snapshot->flags, flags, __ATOMIC_SEQ_CST);
	__atomic_store_n(&snapshot->sequence, sequence, __ATOMIC_SEQ_CST);
}

static bool snapshot_read(const bridge_snapshot_t *snapshot, uint32_t started_at,
			  uint32_t *timestamp, uint32_t *flags)
{
	uint32_t before, after;
	unsigned i;

	for (i = 0; i < 3U; i++) {
		before = __atomic_load_n(&snapshot->sequence, __ATOMIC_SEQ_CST);
		if (before & 1U) continue;
		*timestamp = __atomic_load_n(&snapshot->time, __ATOMIC_SEQ_CST);
		*flags = __atomic_load_n(&snapshot->flags, __ATOMIC_SEQ_CST);
		after = __atomic_load_n(&snapshot->sequence, __ATOMIC_SEQ_CST);
		if (before != after) continue;
		if (before == 0U) {
			*timestamp = started_at;
			*flags = 0U;
		}
		return true;
	}
	return false;
}

static ER bridge_cycle(bridge_state_t *state, ID buffer, uint32_t now,
		       int connected, wifi_mqtt_bridge_publish_t publish)
{
	INT received;
	int result;

	if (!state->have_pending && connected) {
		received = tk_rcv_mbf(buffer, &state->pending, TMO_POL);
		if (received == E_TMOUT) return E_OK;
		if (received != (INT)sizeof(state->pending) || !valid_packet(&state->pending))
			return E_SYS;
		state->have_pending = true;
	}
	if (!state->have_pending) return E_OK;
	if (now - state->pending.header.timestamp_ms > state->max_age_ms)
		return E_SYS;
	if (!connected) return E_OK;
	result = publish((const uint8_t *)state->pending.payload);
	if (result == WIFI_MQTT_PUBLISH_BUSY) return E_OK;
	if (result != WIFI_MQTT_PUBLISH_OK) return E_SYS;
	state->have_pending = false;
	return E_OK;
}

ER wifi_mqtt_bridge_start(uint32_t max_age_ms)
{
#if TM_MQTT
	T_CMBF config = {0};
	SYSTIM now;
	ID buffer;
	ER result;

	if (__atomic_load_n(&s_status, __ATOMIC_ACQUIRE) != 0) return E_OBJ;
	if (max_age_ms == 0U || max_age_ms > INT32_MAX) return E_PAR;
	if (g_mbf_faults <= 0) return E_OBJ;
	result = tk_get_otm(&now);
	if (result != E_OK) return result < E_OK ? result : E_SYS;
	config.mbfatr = TA_TFIFO | TA_USERBUF;
	config.bufsz = (SZ)sizeof(s_storage);
	config.maxmsz = (INT)sizeof(telemetry_message_t);
	config.bufptr = s_storage;
	buffer = tk_cre_mbf(&config);
	if (buffer < E_OK) return buffer;
	s_buffer = buffer;
	s_bridge.max_age_ms = max_age_ms;
	s_bridge.last_clock_ms = now.lo;
	s_started_at = now.lo;
	__atomic_store_n(&s_status, 1, __ATOMIC_RELEASE);
	return E_OK;
#else
	(void)max_age_ms;
	return E_NOSPT;
#endif
}

ER wifi_mqtt_bridge_submit(const telemetry_message_t *message)
{
	if (__atomic_load_n(&s_status, __ATOMIC_ACQUIRE) != 1) return E_OBJ;
	if (__atomic_load_n(&s_stop_requested, __ATOMIC_ACQUIRE)) return E_OBJ;
	if (message == NULL || !valid_packet(message)) return E_PAR;
	return tk_snd_mbf(s_buffer, message, (INT)sizeof(*message), TMO_POL);
}

ER wifi_mqtt_bridge_link(uint32_t max_age_ms, bool *connected, bool *mqtt_ready)
{
	uint32_t timestamp, flags;
	SYSTIM now;
	ER result;

	if (max_age_ms == 0U || max_age_ms > INT32_MAX ||
	    connected == NULL || mqtt_ready == NULL) return E_PAR;
	if (__atomic_load_n(&s_status, __ATOMIC_ACQUIRE) != 1 ||
	    __atomic_load_n(&s_stop_requested, __ATOMIC_ACQUIRE)) return E_OBJ;
	if (!snapshot_read(&s_snapshot, s_started_at, &timestamp, &flags)) return E_TMOUT;
	result = tk_get_otm(&now);
	if (result != E_OK) return result < E_OK ? result : E_SYS;
	if (now.lo - timestamp > max_age_ms) return E_SYS;
	if (__atomic_load_n(&s_status, __ATOMIC_ACQUIRE) != 1 ||
	    __atomic_load_n(&s_stop_requested, __ATOMIC_ACQUIRE)) return E_OBJ;
	*connected = (flags & 1U) != 0U;
	*mqtt_ready = (flags & 2U) != 0U;
	return E_OK;
}

ER wifi_mqtt_bridge_stop(void)
{
	if (__atomic_load_n(&s_status, __ATOMIC_ACQUIRE) == 0) return E_OBJ;
	__atomic_store_n(&s_stop_requested, 1, __ATOMIC_RELEASE);
	return E_OK;
}

void wifi_mqtt_bridge_publish_failed(void)
{
	if (__atomic_load_n(&s_status, __ATOMIC_ACQUIRE) == 1) {
		__atomic_store_n(&s_status, -1, __ATOMIC_RELEASE);
		tm_printf((UB*)"MQTT telemetry bridge stopped: publish failure\n");
	}
}

void wifi_mqtt_bridge_poll(wifi_mqtt_bridge_publish_t publish, int link_up, int mqtt_ready)
{
	int status = __atomic_load_n(&s_status, __ATOMIC_ACQUIRE);
	SYSTIM now;
	ER result;
	fault_message_t fault = {0};

	if (status == 0) return;
	if (status == 1 && __atomic_load_n(&s_stop_requested, __ATOMIC_ACQUIRE)) return;
	if (status == 1) {
		result = tk_get_otm(&now);
		if (result != E_OK || publish == NULL ||
		    (mqtt_ready && !link_up) ||
		    now.lo - s_bridge.last_clock_ms > INT32_MAX ||
		    bridge_cycle(&s_bridge, s_buffer, now.lo, mqtt_ready, publish) != E_OK) {
			__atomic_store_n(&s_status, -1, __ATOMIC_RELEASE);
			tm_printf((UB*)"MQTT telemetry bridge stopped: clock, message or publish error\n");
			status = -1;
		} else {
			s_bridge.last_clock_ms = now.lo;
			snapshot_write(&s_snapshot, now.lo,
				       (link_up ? 1U : 0U) | (mqtt_ready ? 2U : 0U));
		}
	}
	if (status == -1 && !s_fault_sent) {
		fault.header.type = VEHICLE_MESSAGE_FAULT;
		fault.header.version = VEHICLE_MESSAGE_VERSION;
		fault.header.sequence = 1U;
		if (tk_get_otm(&now) != E_OK) return;
		fault.header.timestamp_ms = now.lo;
		fault.source = VEHICLE_FAULT_COMMUNICATION;
		fault.code = VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_WIFI_BACKEND;
		result = tk_snd_mbf(g_mbf_faults, &fault, (INT)sizeof(fault), TMO_POL);
		if (result == E_OK) s_fault_sent = true;
		else if (!s_fault_delivery_reported) {
			tm_printf((UB*)"MQTT bridge fault delivery failed: %d\n", (int)result);
			s_fault_delivery_reported = true;
		}
	}
}

#if APP_TEST
#include "../../test_report.h"

static int s_test_publish_result;
static unsigned s_test_calls;
static uint8_t s_test_payload[WIFI_TELEMETRY_PACKET_SIZE];

static int test_publish(const uint8_t *payload)
{
	unsigned i;
	s_test_calls++;
	if (s_test_publish_result == WIFI_MQTT_PUBLISH_OK) {
		for (i = 0; i < WIFI_TELEMETRY_PACKET_SIZE; i++) s_test_payload[i] = payload[i];
	}
	return s_test_publish_result;
}

BOOL wifi_mqtt_bridge_test(void)
{
	static INT storage[BRIDGE_WORDS];
	T_CMBF config = {0};
	bridge_state_t state = {0};
	telemetry_message_t message = {0};
	wifi_telemetry_data_t data = {0};
	ID buffer;
	BOOL ok;
	BOOL snapshot_ok;
	bridge_snapshot_t snapshot = {0};
	uint32_t timestamp, flags;
	unsigned i;
	TEST_INIT();

	config.mbfatr = TA_TFIFO | TA_USERBUF;
	config.bufsz = (SZ)sizeof(storage);
	config.maxmsz = (INT)sizeof(message);
	config.bufptr = storage;
	buffer = tk_cre_mbf(&config);
	if (buffer < E_OK) {
		TEST_CHECK("WIFI-U04", FALSE, "MQTT outbound bridge queue");
		TEST_SUMMARY("MQTT bridge");
		return FALSE;
	}
	state.max_age_ms = 20U;
	data.battery_pct = 100U;
	s_test_calls = 0U;
	s_test_publish_result = WIFI_MQTT_PUBLISH_BUSY;
	ok = wifi_telemetry_make(&data, 0U, UINT32_MAX - 5U, &message) &&
	     valid_packet(&message) &&
	     tk_snd_mbf(buffer, &message, (INT)sizeof(message), TMO_POL) == E_OK;
	message.payload[3] = 0;
	ok = bridge_cycle(&state, buffer, UINT32_MAX - 4U, 0, test_publish) == E_OK &&
	     s_test_calls == 0U && ok;
	ok = bridge_cycle(&state, buffer, UINT32_MAX - 4U, 1, test_publish) == E_OK &&
	     state.have_pending && s_test_calls == 1U && ok;
	s_test_publish_result = WIFI_MQTT_PUBLISH_OK;
	ok = bridge_cycle(&state, buffer, 3U, 1, test_publish) == E_OK &&
	     !state.have_pending && s_test_calls == 2U && s_test_payload[3] == 100U && ok;
	message.header.timestamp_ms = 10U;
	for (i = 0; i < BRIDGE_CAPACITY; i++)
		ok = tk_snd_mbf(buffer, &message, (INT)sizeof(message), TMO_POL) == E_OK && ok;
	ok = tk_snd_mbf(buffer, &message, (INT)sizeof(message), TMO_POL) == E_TMOUT && ok;
	for (i = 0; i < BRIDGE_CAPACITY; i++)
		ok = bridge_cycle(&state, buffer, 10U, 1, test_publish) == E_OK && ok;
	message.payload[4] = 1;
	ok = !valid_packet(&message) && ok;
	message.payload[4] = 0;
	message.payload[0] = (char)0x20;
	ok = !valid_packet(&message) && ok;
	message.payload[0] = 0;
	message.payload_length = 4U;
	ok = !valid_packet(&message) && ok;
	message.payload_length = WIFI_TELEMETRY_PACKET_SIZE;
	ok = tk_snd_mbf(buffer, &message, (INT)sizeof(message), TMO_POL) == E_OK &&
	     bridge_cycle(&state, buffer, 31U, 1, test_publish) == E_SYS && ok;
	state.have_pending = false;
	message.header.timestamp_ms = 50U;
	s_test_publish_result = WIFI_MQTT_PUBLISH_BUSY;
	ok = tk_snd_mbf(buffer, &message, (INT)sizeof(message), TMO_POL) == E_OK &&
	     bridge_cycle(&state, buffer, 50U, 1, test_publish) == E_OK &&
	     bridge_cycle(&state, buffer, 71U, 0, test_publish) == E_SYS && ok;
	state.have_pending = false;
	message.header.timestamp_ms = 80U;
	ok = tk_snd_mbf(buffer, &message, (INT)sizeof(message), TMO_POL) == E_OK &&
	     bridge_cycle(&state, buffer, 79U, 1, test_publish) == E_SYS && ok;
	state.have_pending = false;
	s_test_publish_result = WIFI_MQTT_PUBLISH_ERROR;
	message.header.timestamp_ms = 40U;
	ok = tk_snd_mbf(buffer, &message, (INT)sizeof(message), TMO_POL) == E_OK &&
	     bridge_cycle(&state, buffer, 40U, 1, test_publish) == E_SYS &&
	     state.have_pending && ok;
	ok = tk_del_mbf(buffer) == E_OK && ok;
	snapshot_ok = snapshot_read(&snapshot, 19U, &timestamp, &flags) &&
		      timestamp == 19U && flags == 0U;
	snapshot_write(&snapshot, 21U, 1U);
	snapshot_ok = snapshot_read(&snapshot, 19U, &timestamp, &flags) &&
		      timestamp == 21U && flags == 1U && snapshot_ok;
	snapshot.sequence = UINT32_MAX - 1U;
	snapshot_write(&snapshot, 22U, 3U);
	snapshot_ok = snapshot_read(&snapshot, 19U, &timestamp, &flags) &&
		      timestamp == 22U && flags == 3U && snapshot_ok;
	snapshot.sequence = 1U;
	snapshot_ok = !snapshot_read(&snapshot, 19U, &timestamp, &flags) && snapshot_ok;
	TEST_CHECK("WIFI-U04", ok, "MQTT bridge copied queue, retry and failures");
	TEST_CHECK("WIFI-U05", snapshot_ok, "network owner link snapshot");
	TEST_SUMMARY("MQTT bridge");
	return ok && snapshot_ok;
}

#ifndef WIFI_MQTT_BRIDGE_H
#define WIFI_MQTT_BRIDGE_H

#include <tk/tkernel.h>
#include "../../vehicle_messages.h"

/* Call once from one startup owner after vehicle_buffers_init() and startup
 * tests. NET=lwip MQTT=1 is required. The bridge remains enabled until reboot.
 * No task is created. max_age_ms uses the operating clock and must be <= INT32_MAX. */
ER wifi_mqtt_bridge_start(uint32_t max_age_ms);

/* Copies a telemetry message with a pre-packed legacy five-byte telemetry
 * payload to a separate outbound queue. event_type and priority are ignored.
 * E_OK means queued, not delivered; E_TMOUT means the queue is full.
 * Only the application WiFi service should submit. */
ER wifi_mqtt_bridge_submit(const telemetry_message_t *message);

/* Read an owner-published link snapshot, or return an error if the bridge has
 * faulted, stopped, or the owner has not polled within max_age_ms. */
ER wifi_mqtt_bridge_link(uint32_t max_age_ms, bool *connected, bool *mqtt_ready);

/* Request shutdown without waiting for the network owner. An already-running
 * publish may still complete; queued data is abandoned until reboot. */
ER wifi_mqtt_bridge_stop(void);

#if APP_TEST
BOOL wifi_mqtt_bridge_test(void);
#endif

#endif

	WIFI_MQTT_PUBLISH_OK,
	WIFI_MQTT_PUBLISH_BUSY,
	WIFI_MQTT_PUBLISH_ERROR

void wifi_mqtt_bridge_publish_failed(void);

#endif
=== C:\Users\Yong Jun\school\INF2004-Embedded_Systems\project\INF2004_Project\app_program\subsystem1\wifi\wifi_service.c ===
#include "../../app.h"
#include "../../vehicle_buffers.h"
#include "wifi_service.h"
#include "wifi_mqtt_bridge.h"

#define WIFI_SERVICE_PRIORITY 10
#define WIFI_FAULT_RETRY_MS 1000U

typedef struct {
	wifi_service_link_t link;
	telemetry_message_t pending;
	uint32_t last_clock_ms;
	uint32_t last_status_ms;
	uint32_t status_sequence;
	uint32_t telemetry_sequence;
	uint32_t telemetry_timestamp_ms;
	ER callback_error;
	bool have_status;
	bool have_pending;
	bool have_telemetry;
} wifi_service_state_t;

static wifi_service_config_t s_config;
static wifi_service_state_t s_state;
static bool s_started;

static bool valid_interval(uint32_t interval)
{
	return interval != 0U && interval <= INT32_MAX;
}

static uint16_t get_clock(wifi_service_state_t *state, uint32_t *now)
{
	SYSTIM time;
	ER er = tk_get_otm(&time);
	if (er != E_OK || time.lo - state->last_clock_ms > INT32_MAX) {
		state->callback_error = er;
		return VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_WIFI_CLOCK;
	}
	*now = time.lo;
	state->last_clock_ms = *now;
	return 0U;
}

static bool same_link(const wifi_service_link_t *a, const wifi_service_link_t *b)
{
	return a->connected == b->connected && a->mqtt_ready == b->mqtt_ready &&
	       a->retry_count == b->retry_count && a->error_code == b->error_code;
}

static uint16_t publish_status(wifi_service_state_t *state,
			       const wifi_service_config_t *config,
			       const wifi_service_link_t *link, uint32_t now)
{
	network_status_message_t status = {0};
	ER er;

	if (state->have_status && same_link(&state->link, link) &&
	    now - state->last_status_ms < config->status_period_ms)
		return 0U;
	status.header.type = VEHICLE_MESSAGE_NETWORK_STATUS;
	status.header.version = VEHICLE_MESSAGE_VERSION;
	status.header.sequence = state->status_sequence + 1U;
	status.header.timestamp_ms = now;
	status.connected = link->connected;
	status.mqtt_ready = link->mqtt_ready;
	status.retry_count = link->retry_count;
	status.error_code = link->error_code;
	er = tk_snd_mbf(g_mbf_network_status, &status, (INT)sizeof(status), TMO_POL);
	if (er != E_OK) {
		state->callback_error = er;
		return VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_WIFI_DELIVERY;
	}
	state->link = *link;
	state->status_sequence = status.header.sequence;
	state->last_status_ms = now;
	state->have_status = true;
	return 0U;
}

static uint16_t receive_telemetry(wifi_service_state_t *state,
				  const wifi_service_config_t *config, uint32_t now)
{
	telemetry_message_t message;
	INT received;

	if (state->have_pending) return 0U;
	received = tk_rcv_mbf(g_mbf_telemetry, &message, TMO_POL);
	if (received == E_TMOUT) return 0U;
	if (received != (INT)sizeof(message) ||
	    message.header.type != VEHICLE_MESSAGE_TELEMETRY ||
	    message.header.version != VEHICLE_MESSAGE_VERSION ||
	    message.payload_length > sizeof(message.payload) ||
	    now - message.header.timestamp_ms > config->telemetry_max_age_ms ||
	    (state->have_telemetry &&
	     (message.header.sequence - state->telemetry_sequence != 1U ||
	      message.header.timestamp_ms - state->telemetry_timestamp_ms > INT32_MAX)))
		return VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_WIFI_TELEMETRY;
	state->pending = message;
	state->have_pending = true;
	return 0U;
}

static uint16_t service_cycle(wifi_service_state_t *state,
			      const wifi_service_config_t *config)
{
	wifi_service_link_t link = {0};
	uint32_t now;
	uint16_t fault;
	ER er = config->poll(config->context, &link);

	if (er != E_OK) {
		state->callback_error = er;
		return VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_WIFI_BACKEND;
	}
	if (link.mqtt_ready && !link.connected)
		return VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_WIFI_BACKEND;
	fault = get_clock(state, &now);
	if (fault != 0U) return fault;
	fault = publish_status(state, config, &link, now);
	if (fault != 0U) return fault;
	if (state->have_pending &&
	    now - state->pending.header.timestamp_ms > config->telemetry_max_age_ms)
		return VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_WIFI_TELEMETRY;
	if (!link.mqtt_ready) return 0U;
	fault = receive_telemetry(state, config, now);
	if (fault != 0U || !state->have_pending) return fault;
	er = config->publish(config->context, &state->pending);
	if (er == E_TMOUT) return 0U;
	if (er != E_OK) {
		state->callback_error = er;
		return VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_WIFI_BACKEND;
	}
	state->telemetry_sequence = state->pending.header.sequence;
	state->telemetry_timestamp_ms = state->pending.header.timestamp_ms;
	state->have_telemetry = true;
	state->have_pending = false;
	return 0U;
}

static void report_fault(uint16_t code, uint32_t now)
{
	fault_message_t fault = {0};
	bool sent = false;
	ER stopped = s_config.stop(s_config.context);

	if (stopped != E_OK) tm_printf((UB*)"WiFi backend stop failed: %d\n", (int)stopped);
	fault.header.type = VEHICLE_MESSAGE_FAULT;
	fault.header.version = VEHICLE_MESSAGE_VERSION;
	fault.header.sequence = 1U;
	fault.header.timestamp_ms = now;
	fault.source = VEHICLE_FAULT_COMMUNICATION;
	fault.code = code;
	fault.recoverable = 0U;
	tm_printf((UB*)"WiFi service stopped; fault=%u error=%d\n",
		  (unsigned)code, (int)s_state.callback_error);
	for (;;) {
		ER er;
		if (!sent) {
			er = tk_snd_mbf(g_mbf_faults, &fault, (INT)sizeof(fault), TMO_POL);
			if (er == E_OK) sent = true;
			else tm_printf((UB*)"WiFi fault delivery failed: %d\n", (int)er);
		}
		er = tk_dly_tsk(WIFI_FAULT_RETRY_MS);
		if (er != E_OK) {
			tm_printf((UB*)"WiFi fault delay failed: %d\n", (int)er);
			er = tk_slp_tsk(TMO_FEVR);
			tm_printf((UB*)"WiFi fault sleep returned: %d\n", (int)er);
		}
	}
}

static void wifi_service_task(INT stacd, void *exinf)
{
	(void)stacd;
	(void)exinf;
	for (;;) {
		uint16_t fault = service_cycle(&s_state, &s_config);
		ER er;
		if (fault != 0U) report_fault(fault, s_state.last_clock_ms);
		er = tk_dly_tsk(s_config.period_ms);
		if (er != E_OK) {
			s_state.callback_error = er;
			report_fault(VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_WIFI_SCHEDULE, s_state.last_clock_ms);
		}
	}
}

ER wifi_service_start(const wifi_service_config_t *config)
{
	T_CTSK task = {0};
	SYSTIM now;
	ID id;
	ER er;

	if (s_started) return E_OBJ;
	if (config == NULL || !valid_interval(config->period_ms) ||
	    !valid_interval(config->status_period_ms) ||
	    !valid_interval(config->telemetry_max_age_ms) ||
	    config->period_ms > config->status_period_ms ||
	    config->period_ms > config->telemetry_max_age_ms ||
	    config->poll == NULL || config->publish == NULL || config->stop == NULL)
		return E_PAR;
	if (g_mbf_telemetry <= 0 || g_mbf_network_status <= 0 || g_mbf_faults <= 0)
		return E_OBJ;
	er = tk_get_otm(&now);
	if (er != E_OK) return er < E_OK ? er : E_SYS;
	s_config = *config;
	s_state = (wifi_service_state_t){0};
	s_state.last_clock_ms = now.lo;
	task.task = (FP)wifi_service_task;
	task.tskatr = TA_HLNG | TA_RNG3;
	task.itskpri = WIFI_SERVICE_PRIORITY;
	task.stksz = TASK_STACK_SIZE;
	id = tk_cre_tsk(&task);
	if (id < E_OK) return id;
	s_started = true;
	er = tk_sta_tsk(id, 0);
	if (er != E_OK) {
		ER cleanup = tk_del_tsk(id);
		if (cleanup != E_OK) tm_printf((UB*)"WiFi task cleanup failed: %d\n", (int)cleanup);
		else s_started = false;
		return er < E_OK ? er : E_SYS;
	}
	return E_OK;
}

static ER mqtt_backend_poll(void *context, wifi_service_link_t *link)
{
	const uint32_t *max_age_ms = context;
	return wifi_mqtt_bridge_link(*max_age_ms, &link->connected, &link->mqtt_ready);
}

static ER mqtt_backend_publish(void *context, const telemetry_message_t *message)
{
	(void)context;
	return wifi_mqtt_bridge_submit(message);
}

static ER mqtt_backend_stop(void *context)
{
	(void)context;
	return wifi_mqtt_bridge_stop();
}

ER wifi_service_start_mqtt(RELTIM period_ms, uint32_t status_period_ms,
			   uint32_t telemetry_max_age_ms, uint32_t owner_max_age_ms)
{
	static uint32_t s_owner_max_age_ms;
	wifi_service_config_t config;
	ER result;

	if (s_started) return E_OBJ;
	if (!valid_interval(period_ms) || !valid_interval(status_period_ms) ||
	    !valid_interval(telemetry_max_age_ms) || !valid_interval(owner_max_age_ms) ||
	    period_ms > status_period_ms || period_ms > telemetry_max_age_ms ||
	    period_ms > owner_max_age_ms) return E_PAR;
	if (g_mbf_telemetry <= 0 || g_mbf_network_status <= 0 || g_mbf_faults <= 0)
		return E_OBJ;
	result = wifi_mqtt_bridge_start(telemetry_max_age_ms);
	if (result != E_OK) return result;
	s_owner_max_age_ms = owner_max_age_ms;
	config = (wifi_service_config_t){period_ms, status_period_ms, telemetry_max_age_ms,
					 mqtt_backend_poll, mqtt_backend_publish,
					 mqtt_backend_stop, &s_owner_max_age_ms};
	result = wifi_service_start(&config);
	if (result != E_OK) {
		ER stopped = wifi_mqtt_bridge_stop();
		if (stopped != E_OK)
			tm_printf((UB*)"MQTT bridge stop request failed: %d\n", (int)stopped);
	}
	return result;
}

#if APP_TEST
#include "../../test_report.h"
#include "wifi_command.h"
#include "wifi_telemetry.h"
#include "wifi_telemetry_producer.h"

typedef struct {
	wifi_service_link_t link;
	telemetry_message_t published;
	ER poll_result;
	ER publish_result;
	unsigned publish_calls;
} wifi_test_backend_t;

static ER test_poll(void *context, wifi_service_link_t *link)
{
	wifi_test_backend_t *backend = context;
	*link = backend->link;
	return backend->poll_result;
}

static ER test_publish(void *context, const telemetry_message_t *message)
{
	wifi_test_backend_t *backend = context;
	backend->publish_calls++;
	if (backend->publish_result == E_OK) backend->published = *message;
	return backend->publish_result;
}

static ER test_stop(void *context)
{
	(void)context;
	return E_OK;
}

static BOOL drain_test_buffers(void)
{
	union {
		telemetry_message_t telemetry;
		network_status_message_t status;
	} message;
	INT received;
	BOOL ok;
	do {
		received = tk_rcv_mbf(g_mbf_telemetry, &message, TMO_POL);
	} while (received > 0);
	ok = received == E_TMOUT;
	do {
		received = tk_rcv_mbf(g_mbf_network_status, &message, TMO_POL);
	} while (received > 0);
	return received == E_TMOUT && ok;
}

static BOOL wifi_flow_test(void)
{
	wifi_test_backend_t backend = {0};
	wifi_service_config_t config = {10U, 1000U, 10000U, test_poll, test_publish, test_stop,
					 &backend};
	wifi_service_state_t state = {0};
	telemetry_message_t message = {0};
	network_status_message_t status;
	SYSTIM now;
	BOOL ok;

	if (tk_get_otm(&now) != E_OK) return FALSE;
	state.last_clock_ms = now.lo;
	message.header.type = VEHICLE_MESSAGE_TELEMETRY;
	message.header.version = VEHICLE_MESSAGE_VERSION;
	message.header.sequence = UINT32_MAX;
	message.header.timestamp_ms = now.lo;
	message.payload_length = 2U;
	message.payload[0] = 'O';
	message.payload[1] = 'K';
	ok = tk_snd_mbf(g_mbf_telemetry, &message, (INT)sizeof(message), TMO_POL) == E_OK;
	ok = service_cycle(&state, &config) == 0U && backend.publish_calls == 0U &&
	     tk_rcv_mbf(g_mbf_network_status, &status, TMO_POL) == (INT)sizeof(status) &&
	     !status.connected && !status.mqtt_ready && status.header.sequence == 1U && ok;
	backend.link.connected = true;
	backend.link.mqtt_ready = true;
	backend.publish_result = E_TMOUT;
	ok = service_cycle(&state, &config) == 0U && state.have_pending &&
	     backend.publish_calls == 1U &&
	     tk_rcv_mbf(g_mbf_network_status, &status, TMO_POL) == (INT)sizeof(status) &&
	     status.connected && status.mqtt_ready && status.header.sequence == 2U && ok;
	backend.publish_result = E_OK;
	ok = service_cycle(&state, &config) == 0U && !state.have_pending &&
	     backend.publish_calls == 2U && backend.published.header.sequence == UINT32_MAX &&
	     backend.published.payload[0] == 'O' && backend.published.payload[1] == 'K' && ok;
	message.header.sequence = 0U;
	ok = tk_snd_mbf(g_mbf_telemetry, &message, (INT)sizeof(message), TMO_POL) == E_OK &&
	     service_cycle(&state, &config) == 0U && backend.publish_calls == 3U &&
	     backend.published.header.sequence == 0U && ok;
	state.last_status_ms = UINT32_MAX - 100U;
	ok = publish_status(&state, &config, &backend.link, 898U) == 0U &&
	     tk_rcv_mbf(g_mbf_network_status, &status, TMO_POL) == E_TMOUT && ok;
	ok = publish_status(&state, &config, &backend.link, 899U) == 0U &&
	     tk_rcv_mbf(g_mbf_network_status, &status, TMO_POL) == (INT)sizeof(status) &&
	     status.header.sequence == 3U && status.header.timestamp_ms == 899U && ok;
	return drain_test_buffers() && ok;
}

static BOOL wifi_fault_test(void)
{
	wifi_test_backend_t backend = {0};
	wifi_service_config_t config = {10U, 1000U, 10000U, test_poll, test_publish, test_stop,
					 &backend};
	wifi_service_state_t state = {0};
	telemetry_message_t message = {0};
	network_status_message_t status = {0};
	SYSTIM now;
	BOOL ok;
	unsigned i;

	if (tk_get_otm(&now) != E_OK) return FALSE;
	state.last_clock_ms = now.lo;
	backend.link.mqtt_ready = true;
	ok = service_cycle(&state, &config) == VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_WIFI_BACKEND;
	backend.link.connected = true;
	message.header.type = VEHICLE_MESSAGE_TELEMETRY;
	message.header.version = VEHICLE_MESSAGE_VERSION;
	message.header.sequence = 1U;
	message.header.timestamp_ms = now.lo;
	message.payload_length = VEHICLE_TELEMETRY_PAYLOAD_SIZE + 1U;
	ok = tk_snd_mbf(g_mbf_telemetry, &message, (INT)sizeof(message), TMO_POL) == E_OK &&
	     service_cycle(&state, &config) == VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_WIFI_TELEMETRY && ok;
	ok = drain_test_buffers() && ok;
	state = (wifi_service_state_t){0};
	state.last_clock_ms = now.lo;
	message.payload_length = 0U;
	message.header.timestamp_ms = now.lo - config.telemetry_max_age_ms - 1U;
	ok = tk_snd_mbf(g_mbf_telemetry, &message, (INT)sizeof(message), TMO_POL) == E_OK &&
	     service_cycle(&state, &config) == VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_WIFI_TELEMETRY && ok;
	ok = drain_test_buffers() && ok;
	state = (wifi_service_state_t){0};
	state.last_clock_ms = now.lo;
	for (i = 0U; i < VEHICLE_MB_NETWORK_CAPACITY; i++)
		ok = tk_snd_mbf(g_mbf_network_status, &status, (INT)sizeof(status), TMO_POL) ==
		     E_OK && ok;
	ok = service_cycle(&state, &config) == VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_WIFI_DELIVERY &&
	     state.callback_error == E_TMOUT && ok;
	ok = drain_test_buffers() && ok;
	state = (wifi_service_state_t){0};
	state.last_clock_ms = now.lo;
	message.header.timestamp_ms = now.lo;
	backend.publish_result = E_TMOUT;
	ok = tk_snd_mbf(g_mbf_telemetry, &message, (INT)sizeof(message), TMO_POL) == E_OK &&
	     service_cycle(&state, &config) == 0U && state.have_pending && ok;
	state.pending.header.timestamp_ms = now.lo - config.telemetry_max_age_ms - 1U;
	ok = service_cycle(&state, &config) == VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_WIFI_TELEMETRY &&
	     state.have_pending && ok;
	ok = drain_test_buffers() && ok;
	state = (wifi_service_state_t){0};
	state.last_clock_ms = now.lo;
	backend.publish_result = E_SYS;
	ok = tk_snd_mbf(g_mbf_telemetry, &message, (INT)sizeof(message), TMO_POL) == E_OK &&
	     service_cycle(&state, &config) == VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_WIFI_BACKEND &&
	     state.have_pending && state.callback_error == E_SYS && ok;
	ok = drain_test_buffers() && ok;
	state = (wifi_service_state_t){0};
	state.last_clock_ms = now.lo;
	state.have_telemetry = true;
	state.telemetry_sequence = 5U;
	state.telemetry_timestamp_ms = now.lo;
	message.header.sequence = 7U;
	ok = tk_snd_mbf(g_mbf_telemetry, &message, (INT)sizeof(message), TMO_POL) == E_OK &&
	     service_cycle(&state, &config) == VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_WIFI_TELEMETRY && ok;
	ok = drain_test_buffers() && ok;
	state = (wifi_service_state_t){0};
	state.last_clock_ms = now.lo;
	backend.poll_result = E_SYS;
	ok = service_cycle(&state, &config) == VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_WIFI_BACKEND &&
	     state.callback_error == E_SYS && ok;
	return drain_test_buffers() && ok;
}

static BOOL wifi_telemetry_test(void)
{
	wifi_telemetry_data_t data = {WIFI_TELEMETRY_VEHICLE_RUNNING,
		WIFI_TELEMETRY_LINE_FOUND, WIFI_TELEMETRY_OBSTACLE_DETECTED,
		WIFI_TELEMETRY_TERRAIN_FLAT,
		WIFI_TELEMETRY_FAULT_MOTOR_STALL | WIFI_TELEMETRY_FAULT_WIFI_LOSS, 100U};
	telemetry_message_t message;
	const uint8_t *packet = (const uint8_t *)message.payload;
	BOOL ok;
	unsigned i, j;

	ok = wifi_telemetry_make(&data, 128U, UINT32_MAX, &message) &&
	     message.header.type == VEHICLE_MESSAGE_TELEMETRY &&
	     message.header.version == VEHICLE_MESSAGE_VERSION &&
	     message.header.sequence == 128U && message.header.timestamp_ms == UINT32_MAX &&
	     message.payload_length == WIFI_TELEMETRY_PACKET_SIZE &&
	     packet[0] == 0x05U && packet[1] == 0x91U && packet[2] == 0x01U &&
	     packet[3] == 100U && packet[4] == 0U &&
	     wifi_telemetry_packet_valid(packet, message.payload_length);
	data.vehicle_state = WIFI_TELEMETRY_VEHICLE_COMPLETE;
	data.line_state = WIFI_TELEMETRY_LINE_LOST;
	data.obstacle_state = WIFI_TELEMETRY_OBSTACLE_DETECTED;
	data.terrain_state = WIFI_TELEMETRY_TERRAIN_HUMP;
	data.fault_flags = WIFI_TELEMETRY_FAULT_ALL;
	ok = wifi_telemetry_make(&data, 127U, 0U, &message) &&
	     packet[0] == 0x12U && packet[1] == 0xafU && packet[2] == 0xffU && ok;
	for (i = 0U; i < sizeof(message); i++) ((uint8_t *)&message)[i] = 0xa5U;
	for (i = 0U; i < 6U; i++) {
		wifi_telemetry_data_t invalid = data;
		switch (i) {
		case 0: invalid.vehicle_state = 5U; break;
		case 1: invalid.line_state = 3U; break;
		case 2: invalid.obstacle_state = 3U; break;
		case 3: invalid.terrain_state = 3U; break;
		case 4: invalid.fault_flags = 32U; break;
		default: invalid.battery_pct = 101U; break;
		}
		ok = !wifi_telemetry_make(&invalid, 0U, 0U, &message) && ok;
	}
	ok = !wifi_telemetry_make(NULL, 0U, 0U, &message) &&
	     !wifi_telemetry_make(&data, 0U, 0U, NULL) && ok;
	for (j = 0U; j < sizeof(message); j++)
		ok = ((const uint8_t *)&message)[j] == 0xa5U && ok;
	{
		uint8_t bytes[WIFI_TELEMETRY_PACKET_SIZE] = {0};
		ok = wifi_telemetry_packet_valid(bytes, sizeof(bytes)) &&
		     !wifi_telemetry_packet_valid(NULL, sizeof(bytes)) &&
		     !wifi_telemetry_packet_valid(bytes, sizeof(bytes) - 1U) && ok;
		for (i = 0U; i < 7U; i++) {
			bytes[0] = bytes[1] = bytes[3] = bytes[4] = 0U;
			switch (i) {
			case 0: bytes[0] = 0x20U; break;
			case 1: bytes[0] = 0x14U; break;
			case 2: bytes[0] = 0x03U; break;
			case 3: bytes[1] = 0xc0U; break;
			case 4: bytes[1] = 0x30U; break;
			case 5: bytes[3] = 101U; break;
			default: bytes[4] = 1U; break;
			}
			ok = !wifi_telemetry_packet_valid(bytes, sizeof(bytes)) && ok;
		}
	}
	return ok;
}

static BOOL wifi_producer_test(void)
{
	wifi_telemetry_producer_t producer = {0};
	wifi_telemetry_data_t data = {0};
	wifi_test_backend_t backend = {0};
	wifi_service_config_t config = {10U, 1000U, 10000U, test_poll, test_publish,
					 test_stop, &backend};
	wifi_service_state_t state = {0};
	telemetry_message_t message = {0};
	network_status_message_t status;
	SYSTIM now;
	BOOL ok;
	unsigned i;

	if (tk_get_otm(&now) != E_OK) return FALSE;
	ok = wifi_telemetry_producer_init(&producer, UINT32_MAX, 0U) == E_PAR &&
	     wifi_telemetry_producer_send(&producer, &data, now.lo) == E_OBJ &&
	     wifi_telemetry_producer_init(&producer, UINT32_MAX, 10000U) == E_OK &&
	     wifi_telemetry_producer_init(&producer, 1U, 10000U) == E_OBJ;
	data.battery_pct = 101U;
	ok = wifi_telemetry_producer_send(&producer, &data, now.lo) == E_PAR &&
	     producer.next_sequence == UINT32_MAX && ok;
	data.battery_pct = 80U;
	ok = wifi_telemetry_producer_send(&producer, &data, now.lo - 11000U) == E_PAR &&
	     wifi_telemetry_producer_send(&producer, &data, now.lo + 1000000U) == E_PAR && ok;
	for (i = 0U; i < VEHICLE_MB_TELEMETRY_CAPACITY; i++)
		ok = tk_snd_mbf(g_mbf_telemetry, &message, (INT)sizeof(message), TMO_POL) ==
		     E_OK && ok;
	ok = wifi_telemetry_producer_send(&producer, &data, now.lo) == E_TMOUT &&
	     producer.next_sequence == UINT32_MAX && !producer.have_sent && ok;
	ok = drain_test_buffers() && ok;
	backend.link.connected = true;
	backend.link.mqtt_ready = true;
	state.last_clock_ms = now.lo;
	ok = wifi_telemetry_producer_send(&producer, &data, now.lo) == E_OK &&
	     service_cycle(&state, &config) == 0U && backend.publish_calls == 1U &&
	     backend.published.header.sequence == UINT32_MAX &&
	     (uint8_t)backend.published.payload[2] == 0xfeU &&
	     (uint8_t)backend.published.payload[3] == 80U &&
	     tk_rcv_mbf(g_mbf_network_status, &status, TMO_POL) == (INT)sizeof(status) && ok;
	ok = wifi_telemetry_producer_send(&producer, &data, now.lo - 1U) == E_PAR &&
	     producer.next_sequence == 0U &&
	     wifi_telemetry_producer_send(&producer, &data, now.lo) == E_OK &&
	     service_cycle(&state, &config) == 0U && backend.publish_calls == 2U &&
	     backend.published.header.sequence == 0U &&
	     (uint8_t)backend.published.payload[2] == 0U && ok;
	return drain_test_buffers() && ok;
}

static BOOL wifi_command_test(void)
{
	wifi_command_t command = {99U, 88U, 77U};
	uint8_t packet[WIFI_COMMAND_PACKET_SIZE] = {0x80U, 44U};
	BOOL ok;
	unsigned type;

	ok = wifi_command_decode(packet, sizeof(packet), &command) &&
	     command.type == WIFI_COMMAND_STOP && command.argument == 0U &&
	     command.sequence == 44U;
	packet[0] = 0xc4U;
	packet[1] = 255U;
	ok = wifi_command_decode(packet, sizeof(packet), &command) &&
	     command.type == WIFI_COMMAND_JUNCTION && command.argument == 4U &&
	     command.sequence == 255U && ok;
	for (type = WIFI_COMMAND_START; type <= WIFI_COMMAND_JUNCTION; type++) {
		packet[0] = (uint8_t)((type << 5U) | 31U);
		packet[1] = 0U;
		ok = wifi_command_decode(packet, sizeof(packet), &command) &&
		     command.type == type && command.argument == 31U && command.sequence == 0U && ok;
	}
	packet[0] = 0xc4U;
	packet[1] = 255U;
	ok = wifi_command_decode(packet, sizeof(packet), &command) && ok;
	packet[0] = 0U;
	ok = !wifi_command_decode(packet, sizeof(packet), &command) &&
	     command.type == WIFI_COMMAND_JUNCTION && command.argument == 4U &&
	     command.sequence == 255U && ok;
	packet[0] = 0xe0U;
	ok = !wifi_command_decode(packet, sizeof(packet), &command) &&
	     !wifi_command_decode(packet, 1U, &command) &&
	     !wifi_command_decode(packet, 3U, &command) &&
	     !wifi_command_decode(NULL, sizeof(packet), &command) &&
	     !wifi_command_decode(packet, sizeof(packet), NULL) &&
	     command.type == WIFI_COMMAND_JUNCTION && command.argument == 4U &&
	     command.sequence == 255U && ok;
	return ok;
}

BOOL wifi_service_test(void)
{
	BOOL flow_ok;
	BOOL fault_ok;
	BOOL command_ok;
	BOOL telemetry_ok;
	BOOL producer_ok;
	TEST_INIT();

	flow_ok = wifi_flow_test();
	fault_ok = wifi_fault_test();
	command_ok = wifi_command_test();
	telemetry_ok = wifi_telemetry_test();
	producer_ok = wifi_producer_test();
	TEST_CHECK("WIFI-U01", flow_ok, "link status and telemetry backpressure");
	TEST_CHECK("WIFI-U02", fault_ok, "invalid telemetry and link or delivery failures");
	TEST_CHECK("WIFI-U03", command_ok, "legacy command decode and malformed packets");
	TEST_CHECK("WIFI-U06", telemetry_ok, "legacy telemetry encoding and validation");
	TEST_CHECK("WIFI-U07", producer_ok, "telemetry producer and service queue");
	TEST_SUMMARY("WiFi service");
	return flow_ok && fault_ok && command_ok && telemetry_ok && producer_ok;
}

#ifndef WIFI_SERVICE_H
#define WIFI_SERVICE_H

#include <tk/tkernel.h>
#include "../../vehicle_messages.h"

typedef struct {
	bool connected;
	bool mqtt_ready;
	uint8_t retry_count;
	uint8_t error_code;
} wifi_service_link_t;

/* These callbacks run in the application task and must return
 * promptly. They must not call MQTT, lwIP, CYW43 or other network APIs: the
 * template's built-in cyw43_utk_task owns the network stack. poll reports link
 * state and any non-E_OK result stops the service. publish must copy any data
 * needed beyond the call;
 * E_OK means it accepted the whole message, E_TMOUT means nothing was accepted
 * and the service should retry. Other results are failures. stop requests that
 * the backend stop promptly. Context and callbacks must remain valid for the
 * task's entire lifetime. */
typedef ER (*wifi_service_poll_t)(void *context, wifi_service_link_t *link);
typedef ER (*wifi_service_publish_t)(void *context, const telemetry_message_t *message);
typedef ER (*wifi_service_stop_t)(void *context);

typedef struct {
	RELTIM period_ms;
	uint32_t status_period_ms;
	uint32_t telemetry_max_age_ms;
	wifi_service_poll_t poll;
	wifi_service_publish_t publish;
	wifi_service_stop_t stop;
	void *context;
} wifi_service_config_t;

/* Opt-in service. The controller must consume status and faults and handle
 * queue-full errors. Remote commands are not implemented. Callback stalls
 * require independent supervision. */
ER wifi_service_start(const wifi_service_config_t *config);

/* Starts the outbound MQTT bridge and service after buffers and tests. The
 * owner snapshot must be no older than owner_max_age_ms. Startup must be
 * serialized by one owner; a failure after bridge creation requires reboot.
 * The bridge accepts only pre-packed legacy five-byte telemetry messages. */
ER wifi_service_start_mqtt(RELTIM period_ms, uint32_t status_period_ms,
			   uint32_t telemetry_max_age_ms, uint32_t owner_max_age_ms);

#if APP_TEST
BOOL wifi_service_test(void);
#endif

#endif
=== C:\Users\Yong Jun\school\INF2004-Embedded_Systems\project\INF2004_Project\app_program\subsystem1\wifi\wifi_telemetry.c ===
#include "wifi_telemetry.h"

#include <stddef.h>

bool wifi_telemetry_make(const wifi_telemetry_data_t *data, uint32_t sequence,
			 uint32_t timestamp_ms, telemetry_message_t *message)
{
	telemetry_message_t result = {0};
	uint8_t *packet = (uint8_t *)result.payload;

	if (data == NULL || message == NULL ||
	    data->vehicle_state > WIFI_TELEMETRY_VEHICLE_COMPLETE ||
	    data->line_state > WIFI_TELEMETRY_LINE_LOST ||
	    data->obstacle_state > WIFI_TELEMETRY_OBSTACLE_DETECTED ||
	    data->terrain_state > WIFI_TELEMETRY_TERRAIN_HUMP ||
	    data->fault_flags > WIFI_TELEMETRY_FAULT_ALL || data->battery_pct > 100U)
		return false;

	result.header.type = VEHICLE_MESSAGE_TELEMETRY;
	result.header.version = VEHICLE_MESSAGE_VERSION;
	result.header.sequence = sequence;
	result.header.timestamp_ms = timestamp_ms;
	result.payload_length = WIFI_TELEMETRY_PACKET_SIZE;
	packet[0] = (uint8_t)((data->vehicle_state << 2U) | data->line_state);
	packet[1] = (uint8_t)((data->obstacle_state << 6U) |
			      (data->terrain_state << 4U) | (data->fault_flags & 0x0fU));
	packet[2] = (uint8_t)(((sequence & 0x7fU) << 1U) |
			      ((data->fault_flags >> 4U) & 1U));
	packet[3] = data->battery_pct;
	packet[4] = 0U;
	*message = result;
	return true;
}

bool wifi_telemetry_packet_valid(const uint8_t *packet, uint32_t length)
{
	return packet != NULL && length == WIFI_TELEMETRY_PACKET_SIZE &&
	       (packet[0] >> 5U) == 0U &&
	       ((packet[0] >> 2U) & 7U) <= WIFI_TELEMETRY_VEHICLE_COMPLETE &&
	       (packet[0] & 3U) <= WIFI_TELEMETRY_LINE_LOST &&
	       (packet[1] >> 6U) <= WIFI_TELEMETRY_OBSTACLE_DETECTED &&
	       ((packet[1] >> 4U) & 3U) <= WIFI_TELEMETRY_TERRAIN_HUMP &&
	       packet[3] <= 100U && packet[4] == 0U;
}

#ifndef WIFI_TELEMETRY_H
#define WIFI_TELEMETRY_H

#include "../../vehicle_messages.h"

#define WIFI_TELEMETRY_PACKET_SIZE 5U

typedef enum {
	WIFI_TELEMETRY_VEHICLE_IDLE = 0,
	WIFI_TELEMETRY_VEHICLE_RUNNING,
	WIFI_TELEMETRY_VEHICLE_PAUSED,
	WIFI_TELEMETRY_VEHICLE_FAULT,
	WIFI_TELEMETRY_VEHICLE_COMPLETE
} wifi_telemetry_vehicle_state_t;

typedef enum {
	WIFI_TELEMETRY_LINE_UNKNOWN = 0,
	WIFI_TELEMETRY_LINE_FOUND,
	WIFI_TELEMETRY_LINE_LOST
} wifi_telemetry_line_state_t;

typedef enum {
	WIFI_TELEMETRY_OBSTACLE_UNKNOWN = 0,
	WIFI_TELEMETRY_OBSTACLE_CLEAR,
	WIFI_TELEMETRY_OBSTACLE_DETECTED
} wifi_telemetry_obstacle_state_t;

typedef enum {
	WIFI_TELEMETRY_TERRAIN_UNKNOWN = 0,
	WIFI_TELEMETRY_TERRAIN_FLAT,
	WIFI_TELEMETRY_TERRAIN_HUMP
} wifi_telemetry_terrain_state_t;

#define WIFI_TELEMETRY_FAULT_MOTOR_STALL 0x01U
#define WIFI_TELEMETRY_FAULT_ENCODER     0x02U
#define WIFI_TELEMETRY_FAULT_IMU         0x04U
#define WIFI_TELEMETRY_FAULT_NO_ECHO     0x08U
#define WIFI_TELEMETRY_FAULT_WIFI_LOSS   0x10U
#define WIFI_TELEMETRY_FAULT_ALL         0x1fU

typedef struct {
	uint8_t vehicle_state;
	uint8_t line_state;
	uint8_t obstacle_state;
	uint8_t terrain_state;
	uint8_t fault_flags;
	uint8_t battery_pct;
} wifi_telemetry_data_t;

/* The seven-bit wire sequence is derived from the low bits of sequence.
 * timestamp_ms must be the operating-clock timestamp of the input data.
 * No unknown-battery value exists in this legacy format. The caller must
 * supply verified data and check the result; output is unchanged on failure. */
bool wifi_telemetry_make(const wifi_telemetry_data_t *data, uint32_t sequence,
			 uint32_t timestamp_ms, telemetry_message_t *message);

bool wifi_telemetry_packet_valid(const uint8_t *packet, uint32_t length);

=== C:\Users\Yong Jun\school\INF2004-Embedded_Systems\project\INF2004_Project\app_program\subsystem1\wifi\wifi_telemetry_producer.c ===
#include "../../app.h"
#include "../../vehicle_buffers.h"
#include "wifi_telemetry_producer.h"

ER wifi_telemetry_producer_init(wifi_telemetry_producer_t *producer,
				uint32_t first_sequence, uint32_t max_age_ms)
{
	SYSTIM now;
	ER result;

	if (producer == NULL || max_age_ms == 0U || max_age_ms > INT32_MAX) return E_PAR;
	if (producer->initialized || g_mbf_telemetry <= 0) return E_OBJ;
	result = tk_get_otm(&now);
	if (result != E_OK) return result < E_OK ? result : E_SYS;
	*producer = (wifi_telemetry_producer_t){0};
	producer->next_sequence = first_sequence;
	producer->max_age_ms = max_age_ms;
	producer->last_clock_ms = now.lo;
	producer->initialized = true;
	return E_OK;
}

ER wifi_telemetry_producer_send(wifi_telemetry_producer_t *producer,
				const wifi_telemetry_data_t *data, uint32_t timestamp_ms)
{
	telemetry_message_t message;
	SYSTIM now;
	ER result;

	if (producer == NULL || data == NULL) return E_PAR;
	if (!producer->initialized || producer->clock_fault) return E_OBJ;
	result = tk_get_otm(&now);
	if (result != E_OK || now.lo - producer->last_clock_ms > INT32_MAX) {
		producer->clock_fault = true;
		return result < E_OK ? result : E_SYS;
	}
	producer->last_clock_ms = now.lo;
	if (now.lo - timestamp_ms > producer->max_age_ms ||
	    (producer->have_sent &&
	     timestamp_ms - producer->last_timestamp_ms > INT32_MAX) ||
	    !wifi_telemetry_make(data, producer->next_sequence, timestamp_ms, &message))
		return E_PAR;
	result = tk_snd_mbf(g_mbf_telemetry, &message, (INT)sizeof(message), TMO_POL);
	if (result == E_OK) {
		producer->last_timestamp_ms = timestamp_ms;
		producer->have_sent = true;
		producer->next_sequence++;
	}
	return result;
}
=== C:\Users\Yong Jun\school\INF2004-Embedded_Systems\project\INF2004_Project\app_program\subsystem1\wifi\wifi_telemetry_producer.h ===
#ifndef WIFI_TELEMETRY_PRODUCER_H
#define WIFI_TELEMETRY_PRODUCER_H

#include <tk/tkernel.h>
#include "wifi_telemetry.h"

typedef struct {
	uint32_t next_sequence;
	uint32_t max_age_ms;
	uint32_t last_clock_ms;
	uint32_t last_timestamp_ms;
	bool initialized;
	bool have_sent;
	bool clock_fault;
} wifi_telemetry_producer_t;

/* Initialize a zeroed state after vehicle_buffers_init(). Exactly one task
 * owns this state and is the sole producer of g_mbf_telemetry. max_age_ms
 * must not exceed the service's telemetry age limit. Start the service first. */
ER wifi_telemetry_producer_init(wifi_telemetry_producer_t *producer,
				uint32_t first_sequence, uint32_t max_age_ms);

/* timestamp_ms is the operating-clock timestamp of the oldest input used.
 * The caller supplies verified values, including battery percentage. E_OK
 * means queued, not delivered; E_TMOUT means the caller may retry without
 * advancing sequence. Handle all other errors. A clock fault latches until
 * restart. This function does not start a task or call network APIs. */
ER wifi_telemetry_producer_send(wifi_telemetry_producer_t *producer,
				const wifi_telemetry_data_t *data, uint32_t timestamp_ms);

#endif
=== C:\Users\Yong Jun\school\INF2004-Embedded_Systems\project\INF2004_Project\app_program\subsystem2\controller\car_gate.c ===
#include "car_gate.h"

bool car_gate_motion(bool go, bool emergency_stopped, uint32_t faults)
{
    return go && !emergency_stopped && faults == 0U;
}

bool car_gate_follow(bool go, bool emergency_stopped, uint32_t faults,
                     bool obstacle)
{
    return car_gate_motion(go, emergency_stopped, faults) && !obstacle;
}

bool car_gate_rearm(bool go_rising, bool emergency_stopped, uint32_t faults,
                    bool obstacle)
{
    return go_rising && car_gate_follow(true, emergency_stopped, faults, obstacle);
}

bool car_gate_follow(bool go, bool emergency_stopped, uint32_t faults,
                     bool obstacle);
/* Rearms line following only after the motor E-stop is already clear. */

#include "../../app.h"
#include "car_gate_test.h"

#if APP_TEST
#include "../../test_report.h"
#include "car_gate.h"
#include "car_safety.h"
#include "controller_state.h"
#include "../../subsystem5/obstacle/obs_profile_config.h"

static const car_safety_config_t safety_config = {100U, 100U, 100U, 200U, 500U, 300U};

static line_observation_message_t safety_line(uint32_t sequence, uint32_t timestamp,
					      uint8_t detected)
{
	line_observation_message_t message = {0};
	message.header.type = VEHICLE_MESSAGE_LINE_OBSERVATION;
	message.header.version = VEHICLE_MESSAGE_VERSION;
	message.header.sequence = sequence;
	message.header.timestamp_ms = timestamp;
	message.line_detected = detected;
	message.confidence = detected ? 1000U : 0U;
	return message;
}

static imu_estimate_message_t safety_imu(uint32_t sequence, uint32_t timestamp)
{
	imu_estimate_message_t message = {0};
	message.header.type = VEHICLE_MESSAGE_IMU_ESTIMATE;
	message.header.version = VEHICLE_MESSAGE_VERSION;
	message.header.sequence = sequence;
	message.header.timestamp_ms = timestamp;
	message.validity = VEHICLE_IMU_VALID_ROLL_PITCH;
	return message;
}

static obstacle_event_message_t safety_obstacle(uint32_t sequence, uint32_t timestamp,
						 uint8_t type, uint16_t distance)
{
	obstacle_event_message_t message = {0};
	message.header.type = VEHICLE_MESSAGE_OBSTACLE_EVENT;
	message.header.version = VEHICLE_MESSAGE_VERSION;
	message.header.sequence = sequence;
	message.header.timestamp_ms = timestamp;
	message.obstacle_class = type;
	message.distance_mm = distance;
	return message;
}

static BOOL safety_inputs(car_safety_t *safety, uint32_t sequence, uint32_t now)
{
	line_observation_message_t line = safety_line(sequence, now, 1U);
	imu_estimate_message_t imu = safety_imu(sequence, now);
	obstacle_event_message_t obstacle =
		safety_obstacle(sequence, now, VEHICLE_OBSTACLE_AHEAD_READING, OBS_DIST_NONE);
	return car_safety_line(safety, &line, now) && car_safety_imu(safety, &imu, now) &&
	       car_safety_obstacle(safety, &obstacle, now);
}

static BOOL safety_rearm_test(void)
{
	car_safety_t safety = {0};
	line_observation_message_t line;
	imu_estimate_message_t imu;
	obstacle_event_message_t obstacle;
	BOOL ok;

	ok = car_safety_init(&safety, &safety_config, 100U) &&
	     safety_inputs(&safety, 1U, 100U) &&
	     !car_safety_follow(&safety, 100U, true, false, false) &&
	     car_safety_follow(&safety, 100U, true, true, false);
	line = safety_line(2U, 101U, 0U);
	ok = car_safety_line(&safety, &line, 101U) &&
	     !car_safety_follow(&safety, 101U, true, false, false) && ok;
	line = safety_line(3U, 102U, 1U);
	ok = car_safety_line(&safety, &line, 102U) &&
	     !car_safety_follow(&safety, 102U, true, false, false) &&
	     car_safety_follow(&safety, 102U, true, true, false) && ok;
	obstacle = safety_obstacle(2U, 103U, VEHICLE_OBSTACLE_AHEAD_READING, 300U);
	ok = car_safety_obstacle(&safety, &obstacle, 103U) &&
	     !car_safety_follow(&safety, 103U, true, false, false) && ok;
	obstacle = safety_obstacle(3U, 104U, VEHICLE_OBSTACLE_AHEAD_READING, OBS_DIST_NONE);
	ok = car_safety_obstacle(&safety, &obstacle, 104U) &&
	     !car_safety_follow(&safety, 104U, true, false, false) &&
	     car_safety_follow(&safety, 104U, true, true, false) && ok;
	imu = safety_imu(2U, 105U);
	imu.tilt_detected = 1U;
	ok = car_safety_imu(&safety, &imu, 105U) &&
	     !car_safety_follow(&safety, 105U, true, true, false) && ok;
	imu = safety_imu(3U, 106U);
	ok = car_safety_imu(&safety, &imu, 106U) &&
	     !car_safety_follow(&safety, 106U, true, false, false) &&
	     car_safety_follow(&safety, 106U, true, true, false) && ok;
	return ok;
}

	     safety_inputs(&safety, 1U, 100U) &&

	line = safety_line(3U, 230U, 1U);
	imu = safety_imu(3U, 230U);

	     !car_safety_follow(&safety, 230U, true, true, false) && safety.faults == 0U && ok;
	obstacle = safety_obstacle(2U, 240U, VEHICLE_OBSTACLE_SCAN_CLEAR, OBS_DIST_NONE);
	ok = car_safety_obstacle(&safety, &obstacle, 240U) &&
	     !car_safety_follow(&safety, 240U, true, true, false) && ok;
	obstacle = safety_obstacle(3U, 245U, VEHICLE_OBSTACLE_AHEAD_READING, OBS_DIST_NONE);
	ok = car_safety_obstacle(&safety, &obstacle, 245U) &&
	     !car_safety_follow(&safety, 245U, true, false, false) &&
	     car_safety_follow(&safety, 245U, true, true, false) && ok;
	return ok;
}

static BOOL safety_fault_test(void)
{
	car_safety_t safety = {0};
	car_safety_config_t config = safety_config;
	line_observation_message_t line;
	obstacle_event_message_t obstacle;
	BOOL ok;

	ok = car_safety_init(&safety, &config, 100U) &&
	     safety_inputs(&safety, 1U, 100U);
	line = safety_line(3U, 101U, 1U);
	ok = !car_safety_line(&safety, &line, 101U) &&
	     (safety.faults & CAR_SAFETY_FAULT_LINE) != 0U &&
	     !car_safety_follow(&safety, 101U, true, true, false) && ok;
	ok = car_safety_init(&safety, &config, 100U) &&
	     safety_inputs(&safety, 1U, 100U) && ok;
	line = safety_line(2U, 201U, 1U);
	ok = !car_safety_line(&safety, &line, 201U) &&
	     (safety.faults & CAR_SAFETY_FAULT_LINE) != 0U && ok;
	ok = car_safety_init(&safety, &config, 100U) &&
	     safety_inputs(&safety, 1U, 100U) && ok;
	obstacle = safety_obstacle(2U, 101U, VEHICLE_OBSTACLE_AHEAD_READING, 100U);
	obstacle.emergency = 1U;
	ok = !car_safety_obstacle(&safety, &obstacle, 101U) &&
	     (safety.faults & CAR_SAFETY_FAULT_EMERGENCY) != 0U &&
	     !car_safety_follow(&safety, 101U, true, true, false) && ok;
	config.line_max_age_ms = 1000U;
	config.imu_max_age_ms = 1000U;
	ok = car_safety_init(&safety, &config, 100U) &&
	     safety_inputs(&safety, 1U, 100U) &&
	     car_safety_begin_scan(&safety, 100U) && ok;
	obstacle = safety_obstacle(2U, 301U, VEHICLE_OBSTACLE_SCAN_CLEAR, OBS_DIST_NONE);
	ok = !car_safety_obstacle(&safety, &obstacle, 301U) &&
	     (safety.faults & CAR_SAFETY_FAULT_OBSTACLE) != 0U && ok;
	ok = car_safety_init(&safety, &config, 100U) &&
	     safety_inputs(&safety, 1U, 100U) && car_safety_begin_scan(&safety, 110U) && ok;
	obstacle = safety_obstacle(2U, 109U, VEHICLE_OBSTACLE_SCAN_CLEAR, OBS_DIST_NONE);
	ok = !car_safety_obstacle(&safety, &obstacle, 110U) &&
	     (safety.faults & CAR_SAFETY_FAULT_OBSTACLE) != 0U && ok;
	ok = car_safety_init(&safety, &config, 100U) &&
	     safety_inputs(&safety, 1U, 100U) && car_safety_begin_scan(&safety, 110U) && ok;
	obstacle = safety_obstacle(2U, 111U, VEHICLE_OBSTACLE_SCAN_CLEAR, OBS_DIST_NONE);
	ok = car_safety_obstacle(&safety, &obstacle, 120U) && ok;
	obstacle = safety_obstacle(3U, 119U, VEHICLE_OBSTACLE_AHEAD_READING, OBS_DIST_NONE);
	ok = !car_safety_obstacle(&safety, &obstacle, 120U) &&
	     (safety.faults & CAR_SAFETY_FAULT_OBSTACLE) != 0U && ok;
	config.ahead_max_age_ms = 0U;
	ok = !car_safety_init(&safety, &config, 400U) &&
	     !car_safety_follow(&safety, 400U, true, true, false) && ok;
	return ok;
}

static BOOL controller_state_flow_test(void)
{
	controller_state_t controller = {0};
	controller_state_t startup = {0};
	line_observation_message_t line;
	obstacle_event_message_t obstacle;
	BOOL ok;

	ok = controller_state_init(&controller, &safety_config, 100U) &&
	     !controller_state_init(&controller, &safety_config, 100U) &&
	     safety_inputs(&controller.safety, 1U, 100U) &&
	     controller_state_step(&controller, 100U, true, false) == CONTROLLER_BLOCKED &&
	     controller_state_step(&controller, 100U, false, false) == CONTROLLER_WAITING &&
	     controller_state_step(&controller, 100U, true, false) == CONTROLLER_FOLLOWING;
	line = safety_line(2U, 101U, 0U);
	ok = car_safety_line(&controller.safety, &line, 101U) &&
	     controller_state_step(&controller, 101U, true, false) == CONTROLLER_BLOCKED && ok;
	line = safety_line(3U, 102U, 1U);
	ok = car_safety_line(&controller.safety, &line, 102U) &&
	     controller_state_step(&controller, 102U, true, false) == CONTROLLER_BLOCKED &&
	     controller_state_step(&controller, 102U, false, false) == CONTROLLER_WAITING &&
	     controller_state_step(&controller, 102U, true, false) == CONTROLLER_FOLLOWING && ok;
	obstacle = safety_obstacle(2U, 103U, VEHICLE_OBSTACLE_AHEAD_READING, 300U);
	ok = car_safety_obstacle(&controller.safety, &obstacle, 103U) &&
	     controller_state_step(&controller, 103U, true, false) == CONTROLLER_BLOCKED && ok;
	obstacle = safety_obstacle(3U, 104U, VEHICLE_OBSTACLE_AHEAD_READING, OBS_DIST_NONE);
	ok = car_safety_obstacle(&controller.safety, &obstacle, 104U) &&
	     controller_state_step(&controller, 104U, true, false) == CONTROLLER_BLOCKED &&
	     controller_state_step(&controller, 104U, false, false) == CONTROLLER_WAITING &&
	     controller_state_step(&controller, 104U, true, false) == CONTROLLER_FOLLOWING && ok;
	ok = controller_state_init(&startup, &safety_config, 200U) &&
	     controller_state_step(&startup, 200U, false, false) == CONTROLLER_WAITING &&
	     controller_state_step(&startup, 200U, true, false) == CONTROLLER_BLOCKED &&
	     safety_inputs(&startup.safety, 1U, 201U) &&
	     controller_state_step(&startup, 201U, true, false) == CONTROLLER_BLOCKED &&
	     controller_state_step(&startup, 201U, false, false) == CONTROLLER_WAITING &&
	     controller_state_step(&startup, 201U, true, false) == CONTROLLER_FOLLOWING && ok;
	return ok;
}

static BOOL controller_state_fault_test(void)
{
	controller_state_t controller = {0};
	controller_state_t stale = {0};
	controller_state_t wrap = {0};
	controller_state_t bad = {0};
	car_safety_config_t invalid = safety_config;
	line_observation_message_t line;
	BOOL ok;

	invalid.line_max_age_ms = 0U;
	ok = controller_state_step(NULL, 0U, false, false) == CONTROLLER_FAULTED &&
	     controller_state_step(&controller, 0U, false, false) == CONTROLLER_FAULTED &&
	     !controller_state_init(&bad, &invalid, 100U) &&
	     controller_state_step(&bad, 100U, false, false) == CONTROLLER_FAULTED &&
	     controller_state_init(&controller, &safety_config, 100U) &&
	     safety_inputs(&controller.safety, 1U, 100U) &&
	     controller_state_step(&controller, 100U, false, false) == CONTROLLER_WAITING &&
	     controller_state_step(&controller, 100U, true, false) == CONTROLLER_FOLLOWING &&
	     controller_state_step(&controller, 101U, true, true) == CONTROLLER_FAULTED &&
	     controller_state_step(&controller, 102U, false, false) == CONTROLLER_FAULTED &&
	     !controller_state_init(&controller, &safety_config, 102U);
	ok = controller_state_init(&stale, &safety_config, 100U) &&
	     safety_inputs(&stale.safety, 1U, 100U) &&
	     controller_state_step(&stale, 201U, false, false) == CONTROLLER_FAULTED && ok;
	line = safety_line(3U, 101U, 1U);
	ok = controller_state_init(&bad, &safety_config, 100U) &&
	     safety_inputs(&bad.safety, 1U, 100U) &&
	     !car_safety_line(&bad.safety, &line, 101U) &&
	     controller_state_step(&bad, 101U, true, false) == CONTROLLER_FAULTED && ok;
	ok = controller_state_init(&wrap, &safety_config, UINT32_MAX - 5U) &&
	     safety_inputs(&wrap.safety, UINT32_MAX, UINT32_MAX - 5U) &&
	     controller_state_step(&wrap, UINT32_MAX - 5U, false, false) == CONTROLLER_WAITING &&
	     controller_state_step(&wrap, 3U, true, false) == CONTROLLER_FOLLOWING &&
	     controller_state_step(&wrap, 2U, true, false) == CONTROLLER_FAULTED && ok;
	return ok;
}

static BOOL controller_state_scan_test(void)
{
	controller_state_t controller = {0};
	obstacle_event_message_t obstacle;
	BOOL ok;

	ok = controller_state_init(&controller, &safety_config, 100U) &&
	     safety_inputs(&controller.safety, 1U, 100U) &&
	     controller_state_step(&controller, 100U, false, false) == CONTROLLER_WAITING &&
	     car_safety_begin_scan(&controller.safety, 101U) &&
	     controller_state_step(&controller, 101U, true, false) == CONTROLLER_SCANNING;
	obstacle = safety_obstacle(2U, 102U, VEHICLE_OBSTACLE_SCAN_CLEAR, OBS_DIST_NONE);
	ok = car_safety_obstacle(&controller.safety, &obstacle, 102U) &&
	     controller_state_step(&controller, 102U, true, false) == CONTROLLER_BLOCKED && ok;
	obstacle = safety_obstacle(3U, 103U, VEHICLE_OBSTACLE_AHEAD_READING, OBS_DIST_NONE);
	ok = car_safety_obstacle(&controller.safety, &obstacle, 103U) &&
	     controller_state_step(&controller, 103U, true, false) == CONTROLLER_BLOCKED &&
	     controller_state_step(&controller, 103U, false, false) == CONTROLLER_WAITING &&
	     controller_state_step(&controller, 103U, true, false) == CONTROLLER_FOLLOWING && ok;
	return ok;
}

BOOL car_gate_test(void)
{
	BOOL motion_ok;
	BOOL follow_ok;
	BOOL rearm_ok;
	BOOL safety_ok;
	BOOL scan_ok;
	BOOL fault_ok;
	BOOL wrap_ok;
	BOOL state_flow_ok;
	BOOL state_fault_ok;
	BOOL state_scan_ok;
	TEST_INIT();

	motion_ok = car_gate_motion(true, false, 0U) &&
		    !car_gate_motion(false, false, 0U) &&
		    !car_gate_motion(true, true, 0U) &&
		    !car_gate_motion(true, false, 1U) &&
		    !car_gate_motion(true, false, 1UL << 31);

	follow_ok = car_gate_follow(true, false, 0U, false) &&
		    !car_gate_follow(true, false, 0U, true) &&
		    !car_gate_follow(false, false, 0U, false) &&
		    !car_gate_follow(true, true, 0U, false) &&
		    !car_gate_follow(true, false, 1U, false) &&
		    car_gate_motion(true, false, 0U);

	rearm_ok = car_gate_rearm(true, false, 0U, false) &&
		   !car_gate_rearm(false, false, 0U, false) &&
		   !car_gate_rearm(true, true, 0U, false) &&
		   !car_gate_rearm(true, false, 1U, false) &&
		   !car_gate_rearm(true, false, 0U, true);
	safety_ok = safety_rearm_test();
	scan_ok = safety_scan_test();
	fault_ok = safety_fault_test();
	wrap_ok = safety_wrap_test();
	state_flow_ok = controller_state_flow_test();
	state_fault_ok = controller_state_fault_test();
	state_scan_ok = controller_state_scan_test();

	TEST_CHECK("VC-U01", motion_ok, "stop, emergency and fault veto motion");
	TEST_CHECK("VC-U02", follow_ok, "obstacle vetoes line following");
	TEST_CHECK("VC-U03", rearm_ok, "line following requires a fresh go edge");
	TEST_CHECK("VC-U04", safety_ok, "sensor vetoes require a fresh go edge");
	TEST_CHECK("VC-U05", scan_ok, "scan requires fresh ahead data before rearming");
	TEST_CHECK("VC-U06", fault_ok, "invalid, late and emergency inputs latch faults");
	TEST_CHECK("VC-U07", wrap_ok, "sensor and clock wrap with backward clock rejection");
	TEST_CHECK("VC-U10", state_flow_ok, "state transitions and fresh go edges");
	TEST_CHECK("VC-U11", state_fault_ok, "state faults, emergency latch and clock wrap");
	TEST_CHECK("VC-U12", state_scan_ok, "scan state and ahead reacquisition");
	TEST_SUMMARY("controller gate");
	return motion_ok && follow_ok && rearm_ok && safety_ok && scan_ok && fault_ok &&
	       wrap_ok && state_flow_ok && state_fault_ok && state_scan_ok;
}

=== C:\Users\Yong Jun\school\INF2004-Embedded_Systems\project\INF2004_Project\app_program\subsystem2\controller\car_safety.c ===
#include "car_safety.h"
#include "car_gate.h"
#include "../../subsystem5/obstacle/obs_profile_config.h"

#include <stddef.h>

static bool interval_valid(uint32_t value)
{
	return value != 0U && value <= INT32_MAX;
}

static bool fail(car_safety_t *safety, uint32_t fault)
{
	safety->faults |= fault;
	safety->armed = false;
	return false;
}

static bool clock_valid(car_safety_t *safety, uint32_t now)
{
	if (now - safety->last_clock_ms > INT32_MAX)
		return fail(safety, CAR_SAFETY_FAULT_CLOCK);
	safety->last_clock_ms = now;
	return true;
}

static bool check_deadlines(car_safety_t *safety, uint32_t now)
{
	if (now - (safety->line.have_message ? safety->line.timestamp_ms :
		   safety->line_wait_ms) > safety->config.line_max_age_ms)
		(void)fail(safety, CAR_SAFETY_FAULT_LINE);
	if (now - (safety->imu.have_message ? safety->imu.timestamp_ms :
		   safety->imu_wait_ms) > safety->config.imu_max_age_ms)
		(void)fail(safety, CAR_SAFETY_FAULT_IMU);
	if (safety->scanning) {
		if (now - safety->scan_start_ms > safety->config.scan_timeout_ms)
			(void)fail(safety, CAR_SAFETY_FAULT_OBSTACLE);
	} else if (now - (safety->have_ahead ? safety->obstacle.timestamp_ms :
			  safety->ahead_wait_ms) > safety->config.ahead_max_age_ms) {
		(void)fail(safety, CAR_SAFETY_FAULT_OBSTACLE);
	}
	return safety->faults == 0U;
}

static bool accept(car_safety_t *safety, car_safety_stream_t *stream,
		   const vehicle_message_header_t *header, uint16_t type,
		   uint32_t now, uint32_t max_age, uint32_t not_before, uint32_t fault)
{
	if (header == NULL || header->type != type ||
	    header->version != VEHICLE_MESSAGE_VERSION ||
	    now - header->timestamp_ms > max_age ||
	    (!stream->have_message && header->timestamp_ms - not_before > INT32_MAX) ||
	    (stream->have_message &&
	     (header->sequence - stream->sequence != 1U ||
	      header->timestamp_ms - stream->timestamp_ms > INT32_MAX)))
		return fail(safety, fault);
	stream->sequence = header->sequence;
	stream->timestamp_ms = header->timestamp_ms;
	stream->have_message = true;
	return true;
}

bool car_safety_init(car_safety_t *safety, const car_safety_config_t *config,
		     uint32_t now_ms)
{
	car_safety_t initial = {0};

	if (safety == NULL) return false;
	if (config == NULL ||
	    !interval_valid(config->line_max_age_ms) ||
	    !interval_valid(config->imu_max_age_ms) ||
	    !interval_valid(config->ahead_max_age_ms) ||
	    !interval_valid(config->scan_timeout_ms) ||
	    config->min_line_confidence > 1000U ||
	    config->obstacle_stop_mm < OBS_MIN_RANGE_MM ||
	    config->obstacle_stop_mm > OBS_MAX_RANGE_MM) {
		*safety = (car_safety_t){.faults = CAR_SAFETY_FAULT_EXTERNAL};
		return false;
	}
	initial.config = *config;
	initial.last_clock_ms = now_ms;
	initial.line_wait_ms = now_ms;
	initial.imu_wait_ms = now_ms;
	initial.ahead_wait_ms = now_ms;
	initial.initialized = true;
	*safety = initial;
	return true;
}

bool car_safety_line(car_safety_t *safety, const line_observation_message_t *message,
		     uint32_t now_ms)
{
	if (safety == NULL || !safety->initialized) return false;
	if (!clock_valid(safety, now_ms) || !check_deadlines(safety, now_ms)) return false;
	if (message == NULL || message->line_detected > 1U ||
	    message->intersection_detected > 1U || message->confidence > 1000U)
		return fail(safety, CAR_SAFETY_FAULT_LINE);
	if (!accept(safety, &safety->line, &message->header, VEHICLE_MESSAGE_LINE_OBSERVATION,
		    now_ms, safety->config.line_max_age_ms, safety->line_wait_ms,
		    CAR_SAFETY_FAULT_LINE))
		return false;
	safety->line_clear = message->line_detected == 1U &&
			     message->confidence >= safety->config.min_line_confidence;
	if (!safety->line_clear) safety->armed = false;
	return true;
}

bool car_safety_imu(car_safety_t *safety, const imu_estimate_message_t *message,
		    uint32_t now_ms)
{
	if (safety == NULL || !safety->initialized) return false;
	if (!clock_valid(safety, now_ms) || !check_deadlines(safety, now_ms)) return false;
	if (message == NULL || !(message->validity & VEHICLE_IMU_VALID_ROLL_PITCH) ||
	    message->shock_detected > 1U || message->tilt_detected > 1U)
		return fail(safety, CAR_SAFETY_FAULT_IMU);
	if (!accept(safety, &safety->imu, &message->header, VEHICLE_MESSAGE_IMU_ESTIMATE,
		    now_ms, safety->config.imu_max_age_ms, safety->imu_wait_ms,
		    CAR_SAFETY_FAULT_IMU))
		return false;
	safety->imu_clear = !message->shock_detected && !message->tilt_detected;
	if (!safety->imu_clear) safety->armed = false;
	return true;
}

bool car_safety_obstacle(car_safety_t *safety, const obstacle_event_message_t *message,
			 uint32_t now_ms)
{
	uint32_t max_age;

	if (safety == NULL || !safety->initialized) return false;
	if (!clock_valid(safety, now_ms) || !check_deadlines(safety, now_ms)) return false;
	if (message == NULL || message->emergency > 1U ||
	    (message->distance_mm != OBS_DIST_NONE &&
	     (message->distance_mm < OBS_MIN_RANGE_MM || message->distance_mm > OBS_MAX_RANGE_MM)))
		return fail(safety, CAR_SAFETY_FAULT_OBSTACLE);
	max_age = safety->scanning ? safety->config.scan_timeout_ms :
				    safety->config.ahead_max_age_ms;
	if (!accept(safety, &safety->obstacle, &message->header, VEHICLE_MESSAGE_OBSTACLE_EVENT,
		    now_ms, max_age, safety->ahead_wait_ms, CAR_SAFETY_FAULT_OBSTACLE))
		return false;
	if (message->emergency) return fail(safety, CAR_SAFETY_FAULT_EMERGENCY);
	if (message->obstacle_class == VEHICLE_OBSTACLE_AHEAD_READING && !safety->scanning) {
		if (!safety->have_ahead &&
		    message->header.timestamp_ms - safety->ahead_wait_ms > INT32_MAX)
			return fail(safety, CAR_SAFETY_FAULT_OBSTACLE);
		safety->have_ahead = true;
		safety->ahead_clear = message->distance_mm == OBS_DIST_NONE ||
				      message->distance_mm > safety->config.obstacle_stop_mm;
		if (!safety->ahead_clear) safety->armed = false;
		return true;
	}
	if (safety->scanning &&
	    (message->obstacle_class == VEHICLE_OBSTACLE_SCAN_CLEAR ||
	     message->obstacle_class == VEHICLE_OBSTACLE_SCAN_FOUND)) {
		if (message->header.timestamp_ms - safety->scan_start_ms > INT32_MAX)
			return fail(safety, CAR_SAFETY_FAULT_OBSTACLE);
		safety->scanning = false;
		safety->have_ahead = false;
		safety->ahead_clear = false;
		safety->ahead_wait_ms = now_ms;
		safety->armed = false;
		return true;
	}
	return fail(safety, CAR_SAFETY_FAULT_OBSTACLE);
}

bool car_safety_begin_scan(car_safety_t *safety, uint32_t now_ms)
{
	if (safety == NULL || !safety->initialized) return false;
	if (!clock_valid(safety, now_ms) || !check_deadlines(safety, now_ms)) return false;
	safety->armed = false;
	if (safety->faults != 0U || safety->scanning || !safety->have_ahead ||
	    now_ms - safety->obstacle.timestamp_ms > safety->config.ahead_max_age_ms)
		return false;
	safety->scanning = true;
	safety->scan_start_ms = now_ms;
	safety->have_ahead = false;
	safety->ahead_clear = false;
	return true;
}

void car_safety_latch_fault(car_safety_t *safety)
{
	if (safety == NULL || !safety->initialized) return;
	(void)fail(safety, CAR_SAFETY_FAULT_EXTERNAL);
}

bool car_safety_follow(car_safety_t *safety, uint32_t now_ms,
		       bool go, bool go_rising, bool emergency_stopped)
{
	bool blocked;

	if (safety == NULL || !safety->initialized) return false;
	if (!clock_valid(safety, now_ms)) return false;
	(void)check_deadlines(safety, now_ms);
	if (emergency_stopped) (void)fail(safety, CAR_SAFETY_FAULT_EMERGENCY);
	blocked = safety->scanning || !safety->line.have_message || !safety->line_clear ||
		  !safety->imu.have_message || !safety->imu_clear ||
		  !safety->have_ahead || !safety->ahead_clear;
	if (!car_gate_follow(go, emergency_stopped, safety->faults, blocked)) {
		safety->armed = false;
	} else if (!safety->armed &&
		   car_gate_rearm(go_rising, emergency_stopped, safety->faults, blocked)) {
		safety->armed = true;
	}
	return safety->armed;
}
