#ifndef CAR_SAFETY_H
#define CAR_SAFETY_H

#include "../../vehicle_messages.h"

#define CAR_SAFETY_FAULT_LINE      0x01U
#define CAR_SAFETY_FAULT_IMU       0x02U
#define CAR_SAFETY_FAULT_OBSTACLE  0x04U
#define CAR_SAFETY_FAULT_CLOCK     0x08U
#define CAR_SAFETY_FAULT_EXTERNAL  0x10U
#define CAR_SAFETY_FAULT_EMERGENCY 0x20U

typedef struct {
	uint32_t line_max_age_ms;
	uint32_t imu_max_age_ms;
	uint32_t ahead_max_age_ms;
	uint32_t scan_timeout_ms;
	uint16_t min_line_confidence;
	uint16_t obstacle_stop_mm;
} car_safety_config_t;

typedef struct {
	uint32_t sequence;
	uint32_t timestamp_ms;
	bool have_message;
} car_safety_stream_t;

typedef struct {
	car_safety_config_t config;
	car_safety_stream_t line;
	car_safety_stream_t imu;
	car_safety_stream_t obstacle;
	uint32_t last_clock_ms;
	uint32_t line_wait_ms;
	uint32_t imu_wait_ms;
	uint32_t ahead_wait_ms;
	uint32_t scan_start_ms;
	uint32_t faults;
	bool initialized;
	bool line_clear;
	bool imu_clear;
	bool ahead_clear;
	bool have_ahead;
	bool scanning;
	bool armed;
} car_safety_t;

/* Single controller owner only. Configuration and thresholds must be verified
 * for the robot. Faults latch until reinitialization after system restart. */
bool car_safety_init(car_safety_t *safety, const car_safety_config_t *config,
		     uint32_t now_ms);
bool car_safety_line(car_safety_t *safety, const line_observation_message_t *message,
		     uint32_t now_ms);
bool car_safety_imu(car_safety_t *safety, const imu_estimate_message_t *message,
		    uint32_t now_ms);
bool car_safety_obstacle(car_safety_t *safety, const obstacle_event_message_t *message,
			 uint32_t now_ms);

/* Call only after independently confirming the physical robot stopped, before
 * requesting a scan. Motion must stay stopped until a fresh ahead reading.
 * The helper cannot itself stop or verify the motors. */
bool car_safety_begin_scan(car_safety_t *safety, uint32_t now_ms);

/* Latch a received subsystem fault or an independently detected stop fault. */
void car_safety_latch_fault(car_safety_t *safety);

/* Call periodically even when no messages arrive. A fresh go edge is needed
 * after any stop or unsafe observation. This is only an advisory predicate:
 * it does not send commands, verify a motor stop, or replace a watchdog. */
bool car_safety_follow(car_safety_t *safety, uint32_t now_ms,
		       bool go, bool go_rising, bool emergency_stopped);

#endif
=== C:\Users\Yong Jun\school\INF2004-Embedded_Systems\project\INF2004_Project\app_program\subsystem2\controller\controller_monitor.c ===
#include "../../app.h"
#include "../../vehicle_buffers.h"
#include "controller_monitor.h"

#define CONTROLLER_MONITOR_PRIORITY 10
#define CONTROLLER_MONITOR_FAULT_DELAY_MS 1000U

static controller_monitor_config_t s_config;
static controller_state_t s_controller;
static bool s_started;

static bool read_clock(car_safety_t *safety, uint32_t *now)
{
	SYSTIM time;
	ER er = tk_get_otm(&time);
	if (er != E_OK) {
		tm_printf((UB*)"controller monitor clock failed: %d\n", (int)er);
		safety->faults |= CAR_SAFETY_FAULT_CLOCK;
		safety->armed = false;
		return false;
	}
	*now = time.lo;
	return true;
}

static bool receive_faults(car_safety_t *safety)
{
	fault_message_t fault;
	INT received = tk_rcv_mbf(g_mbf_faults, &fault, TMO_POL);
	if (received == E_TMOUT) return true;
	if (received != (INT)sizeof(fault) ||
	    fault.header.type != VEHICLE_MESSAGE_FAULT ||
	    fault.header.version != VEHICLE_MESSAGE_VERSION ||
	    fault.source < VEHICLE_FAULT_SENSOR || fault.source > VEHICLE_FAULT_SYSTEM ||
	    fault.recoverable > 1U || fault.code == 0U) {
		tm_printf((UB*)"controller monitor invalid fault message: %d\n", (int)received);
	} else {
		tm_printf((UB*)"controller monitor subsystem fault: source=%u code=%u\n",
			  (unsigned)fault.source, (unsigned)fault.code);
	}
	car_safety_latch_fault(safety);
	return false;
}

static bool receive_line(car_safety_t *safety)
{
	unsigned i;
	for (i = 0U; i < VEHICLE_MB_LINE_CAPACITY; i++) {
		line_observation_message_t message;
		uint32_t now;
		INT received = tk_rcv_mbf(g_mbf_line_observations, &message, TMO_POL);
		if (received == E_TMOUT) return true;
		if (received != (INT)sizeof(message)) {
			tm_printf((UB*)"controller monitor line receive failed: %d\n", (int)received);
			car_safety_latch_fault(safety);
			return false;
		}
		if (!read_clock(safety, &now) || !car_safety_line(safety, &message, now)) return false;
	}
	return true;
}

static bool receive_imu(car_safety_t *safety)
{
	unsigned i;
	for (i = 0U; i < VEHICLE_MB_IMU_CAPACITY; i++) {
		imu_estimate_message_t message;
		uint32_t now;
		INT received = tk_rcv_mbf(g_mbf_imu_estimates, &message, TMO_POL);
		if (received == E_TMOUT) return true;
		if (received != (INT)sizeof(message)) {
			tm_printf((UB*)"controller monitor IMU receive failed: %d\n", (int)received);
			car_safety_latch_fault(safety);
			return false;
		}
		if (!read_clock(safety, &now) || !car_safety_imu(safety, &message, now)) return false;
	}
	return true;
}

static bool receive_obstacle(car_safety_t *safety)
{
	unsigned i;
	for (i = 0U; i < VEHICLE_MB_OBSTACLE_CAPACITY; i++) {
		obstacle_event_message_t message;
		uint32_t now;
		INT received = tk_rcv_mbf(g_mbf_obstacle_events, &message, TMO_POL);
		if (received == E_TMOUT) return true;
		if (received != (INT)sizeof(message)) {
			tm_printf((UB*)"controller monitor obstacle receive failed: %d\n", (int)received);
			car_safety_latch_fault(safety);
			return false;
		}
		if (!read_clock(safety, &now) || !car_safety_obstacle(safety, &message, now))
			return false;
	}
	return true;
}

static bool monitor_cycle(controller_state_t *controller)
{
	car_safety_t *safety = &controller->safety;
	uint32_t now = safety->last_clock_ms;

	if (!receive_faults(safety) || !receive_line(safety) || !receive_imu(safety) ||
	    !receive_obstacle(safety) || !read_clock(safety, &now)) {
		(void)controller_state_step(controller, safety->last_clock_ms, false, false);
		return false;
	}
	return controller_state_step(controller, now, false, false) != CONTROLLER_FAULTED;
}

static void monitor_stopped(void)
{
	tm_printf((UB*)"controller monitor stopped; safety faults=0x%x\n",
		  (unsigned)s_controller.safety.faults);
	for (;;) {
		ER er = tk_dly_tsk(CONTROLLER_MONITOR_FAULT_DELAY_MS);
		if (er != E_OK) {
			tm_printf((UB*)"controller monitor delay failed: %d\n", (int)er);
			er = tk_slp_tsk(TMO_FEVR);
			tm_printf((UB*)"controller monitor sleep returned: %d\n", (int)er);
		}
	}
}

static void controller_monitor_task(INT stacd, void *exinf)
{
	ER er;

	(void)stacd;
	(void)exinf;
	for (;;) {
		if (!monitor_cycle(&s_controller)) monitor_stopped();
		er = tk_dly_tsk(s_config.period_ms);
		if (er != E_OK) {
			tm_printf((UB*)"controller monitor schedule failed: %d\n", (int)er);
			car_safety_latch_fault(&s_controller.safety);
			(void)controller_state_step(&s_controller, s_controller.safety.last_clock_ms,
						    false, false);
			monitor_stopped();
		}
	}
}

ER controller_monitor_start(const controller_monitor_config_t *config)
{
	controller_state_t probe = {0};
	controller_state_t validation = {0};
	T_CTSK task = {0};
	SYSTIM now;
	ID id;
	ER er;

	if (s_started) return E_OBJ;
	if (config == NULL || config->period_ms == 0U ||
	    config->period_ms > config->safety.line_max_age_ms ||
	    config->period_ms > config->safety.imu_max_age_ms ||
	    config->period_ms > config->safety.ahead_max_age_ms ||
	    !controller_state_init(&probe, &config->safety, 0U))
		return E_PAR;
	if (g_mbf_line_observations <= 0 || g_mbf_imu_estimates <= 0 ||
	    g_mbf_obstacle_events <= 0 || g_mbf_faults <= 0)
		return E_OBJ;
	er = tk_get_otm(&now);
	if (er != E_OK) return er < E_OK ? er : E_SYS;
	if (!controller_state_init(&validation, &config->safety, now.lo)) return E_PAR;
	s_config = *config;
	s_controller = validation;
	task.task = (FP)controller_monitor_task;
	task.tskatr = TA_HLNG | TA_RNG3;
	task.itskpri = CONTROLLER_MONITOR_PRIORITY;
	task.stksz = TASK_STACK_SIZE;
	id = tk_cre_tsk(&task);
	if (id < E_OK) return id;
	s_started = true;
	er = tk_sta_tsk(id, 0);
	if (er != E_OK) {
		ER cleanup = tk_del_tsk(id);
		if (cleanup != E_OK)
			tm_printf((UB*)"controller monitor task cleanup failed: %d\n", (int)cleanup);
		else s_started = false;
		return er < E_OK ? er : E_SYS;
	}
	return E_OK;
}

#if APP_TEST
#include "../../test_report.h"
#include "../../subsystem5/obstacle/obs_profile_config.h"

static BOOL drain_test_buffer(ID id)
{
	union {
		line_observation_message_t line;
		imu_estimate_message_t imu;
		obstacle_event_message_t obstacle;
		fault_message_t fault;
	} message;
	INT received;
	do {
		received = tk_rcv_mbf(id, &message, TMO_POL);
	} while (received > 0);
	return received == E_TMOUT;
}

static BOOL drain_test_buffers(void)
{
	BOOL ok = drain_test_buffer(g_mbf_line_observations);
	ok = drain_test_buffer(g_mbf_imu_estimates) && ok;
	ok = drain_test_buffer(g_mbf_obstacle_events) && ok;
	ok = drain_test_buffer(g_mbf_faults) && ok;
	return ok;
}

static const car_safety_config_t test_config = {10000U, 10000U, 10000U, 10000U, 500U, 300U};

static BOOL monitor_flow_test(void)
{
	controller_state_t controller = {0};
	car_safety_t *safety = &controller.safety;
	line_observation_message_t line = {0};
	imu_estimate_message_t imu = {0};
	obstacle_event_message_t obstacle = {0};
	uint32_t now;
	BOOL ok;

	if (!read_clock(safety, &now) || !controller_state_init(&controller, &test_config, now))
		return FALSE;
	line.header = (vehicle_message_header_t){VEHICLE_MESSAGE_LINE_OBSERVATION,
						  VEHICLE_MESSAGE_VERSION, 1U, now};
	line.line_detected = 1U;
	line.confidence = 1000U;
	imu.header = (vehicle_message_header_t){VEHICLE_MESSAGE_IMU_ESTIMATE,
						 VEHICLE_MESSAGE_VERSION, 1U, now};
	imu.validity = VEHICLE_IMU_VALID_ROLL_PITCH;
	obstacle.header = (vehicle_message_header_t){VEHICLE_MESSAGE_OBSTACLE_EVENT,
						      VEHICLE_MESSAGE_VERSION, 1U, now};
	obstacle.obstacle_class = VEHICLE_OBSTACLE_AHEAD_READING;
	obstacle.distance_mm = OBS_DIST_NONE;
	ok = tk_snd_mbf(g_mbf_line_observations, &line, (INT)sizeof(line), TMO_POL) == E_OK;
	ok = tk_snd_mbf(g_mbf_imu_estimates, &imu, (INT)sizeof(imu), TMO_POL) == E_OK && ok;
	ok = tk_snd_mbf(g_mbf_obstacle_events, &obstacle, (INT)sizeof(obstacle), TMO_POL) ==
	     E_OK && ok;
	ok = monitor_cycle(&controller) && safety->line.have_message && safety->imu.have_message &&
	     safety->have_ahead && safety->faults == 0U && !safety->armed &&
	     controller.state == CONTROLLER_WAITING && ok;
	ok = monitor_cycle(&controller) && !safety->armed &&
	     controller.state == CONTROLLER_WAITING && ok;
	return drain_test_buffers() && ok;
}

static BOOL monitor_fault_test(void)
{
	controller_state_t controller = {0};
	car_safety_t *safety = &controller.safety;
	car_safety_config_t config = test_config;
	line_observation_message_t line = {0};
	fault_message_t fault = {0};
	uint32_t now;
	BOOL ok;

	if (!read_clock(safety, &now) || !controller_state_init(&controller, &config, now))
		return FALSE;
	line.header = (vehicle_message_header_t){VEHICLE_MESSAGE_LINE_OBSERVATION,
						  VEHICLE_MESSAGE_VERSION, 1U, now};
	line.line_detected = 1U;
	line.confidence = 1000U;
	ok = tk_snd_mbf(g_mbf_line_observations, &line, (INT)sizeof(line) - 1, TMO_POL) == E_OK;
	ok = !monitor_cycle(&controller) && (safety->faults & CAR_SAFETY_FAULT_EXTERNAL) != 0U &&
	     controller.state == CONTROLLER_FAULTED && ok;
	controller = (controller_state_t){0};
	ok = controller_state_init(&controller, &config, now) && ok;
	ok = tk_snd_mbf(g_mbf_line_observations, &line, (INT)sizeof(line), TMO_POL) == E_OK && ok;
	line.header.sequence = 3U;
	ok = tk_snd_mbf(g_mbf_line_observations, &line, (INT)sizeof(line), TMO_POL) == E_OK && ok;
	ok = !monitor_cycle(&controller) && (safety->faults & CAR_SAFETY_FAULT_LINE) != 0U &&
	     controller.state == CONTROLLER_FAULTED && ok;
	controller = (controller_state_t){0};
	ok = controller_state_init(&controller, &config, now) && ok;
	fault.header.type = VEHICLE_MESSAGE_FAULT;
	fault.header.version = VEHICLE_MESSAGE_VERSION;
	fault.source = VEHICLE_FAULT_SENSOR;
	fault.code = VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_IMU_SENSOR;
	ok = tk_snd_mbf(g_mbf_faults, &fault, (INT)sizeof(fault), TMO_POL) == E_OK && ok;
	ok = !monitor_cycle(&controller) && (safety->faults & CAR_SAFETY_FAULT_EXTERNAL) != 0U &&
	     controller.state == CONTROLLER_FAULTED && ok;
	config.line_max_age_ms = 10U;
	config.imu_max_age_ms = 10U;
	config.ahead_max_age_ms = 10U;
	controller = (controller_state_t){0};
	ok = controller_state_init(&controller, &config, now - 11U) && ok;
	ok = !monitor_cycle(&controller) && (safety->faults & CAR_SAFETY_FAULT_LINE) != 0U &&
	     controller.state == CONTROLLER_FAULTED && ok;
	return drain_test_buffers() && ok;
}

BOOL controller_monitor_test(void)
{
	BOOL flow_ok;
	BOOL fault_ok;
	TEST_INIT();

	flow_ok = monitor_flow_test();
	fault_ok = monitor_fault_test();
	TEST_CHECK("VC-U08", flow_ok, "controller consumes copied sensor messages while waiting");
	TEST_CHECK("VC-U09", fault_ok, "invalid, missing and fault messages enter faulted state");
	TEST_SUMMARY("controller monitor");
	return flow_ok && fault_ok;
}

#ifndef CONTROLLER_MONITOR_H
#define CONTROLLER_MONITOR_H

#include <tk/tkernel.h>
#include "controller_state.h"

typedef struct {
	car_safety_config_t safety;
	RELTIM period_ms;
} controller_monitor_config_t;

/* Opt-in diagnostic only; exclusively consumes the controller's line, IMU,
 * obstacle and fault buffers. Start before their producers, not alongside
 * another consumer. It never issues motor, scan, or network commands and
 * cannot stop or verify the physical robot. It steps the advisory controller
 * state with go held low, so it cannot arm. Keep motor power safely disabled.
 * Only forward obstacle observations are supported; do not request scans.
 * Faults latch until restart and are reported on the console. */
ER controller_monitor_start(const controller_monitor_config_t *config);

=== C:\Users\Yong Jun\school\INF2004-Embedded_Systems\project\INF2004_Project\app_program\subsystem2\controller\controller_state.c ===
#include "controller_state.h"

#include <stddef.h>

bool controller_state_init(controller_state_t *controller,
			   const car_safety_config_t *config, uint32_t now_ms)
{
	controller_state_t initial = {0};

	if (controller == NULL || controller->initialized ||
	    !car_safety_init(&initial.safety, config, now_ms))
		return false;
	initial.previous_go = true;
	initial.initialized = true;
	*controller = initial;
	return true;
}

controller_state_value_t controller_state_step(controller_state_t *controller,
						uint32_t now_ms, bool go,
						bool emergency_stopped)
{
	bool rising, following;

	if (controller == NULL || !controller->initialized) return CONTROLLER_FAULTED;
	rising = go && !controller->previous_go;
	controller->previous_go = go;
	following = car_safety_follow(&controller->safety, now_ms, go, rising,
				      emergency_stopped);
	if (controller->safety.faults != 0U)
		controller->state = CONTROLLER_FAULTED;
	else if (controller->safety.scanning)
		controller->state = CONTROLLER_SCANNING;
	else if (following)
		controller->state = CONTROLLER_FOLLOWING;
	else if (go)
		controller->state = CONTROLLER_BLOCKED;
	else
		controller->state = CONTROLLER_WAITING;
	return controller->state;
}

#ifndef CONTROLLER_STATE_H
#define CONTROLLER_STATE_H

#include "car_safety.h"

typedef enum {
	CONTROLLER_WAITING = 0,
	CONTROLLER_FOLLOWING,
	CONTROLLER_BLOCKED,
	CONTROLLER_SCANNING,
	CONTROLLER_FAULTED
} controller_state_value_t;

typedef struct {
	car_safety_t safety;
	controller_state_value_t state;
	bool previous_go;
	bool initialized;
} controller_state_t;

/* Single controller owner only. Initialize a zeroed object once; do not reset
 * it during operation. The owner feeds observations into safety and calls
 * controller_state_step every cycle, including when no messages arrive. */
bool controller_state_init(controller_state_t *controller,
			   const car_safety_config_t *config, uint32_t now_ms);

/* Advisory simulation only: go must be a current trusted local input, not a
 * decoded network command. A go level already high at startup cannot arm.
 * The returned state does not send commands, stop motors, request scans or
 * confirm a physical stop; do not use it as an independent safety system. */
controller_state_value_t controller_state_step(controller_state_t *controller,
						uint32_t now_ms, bool go,
						bool emergency_stopped);

#include "line_steering.h"
#include "../../subsystem3/line_barcode/line_observation.h"

#include <stddef.h>

#define FORWARD_PWM 25
#define CORRECTION_PWM 10
#define LOST_CONTINUE_MS 300U

void line_steering_reset(line_steering_t *steering)
{
	if (steering == NULL) return;
	*steering = (line_steering_t){0};
}

line_steering_command_t line_steering_update(
	line_steering_t *steering, uint8_t position, uint32_t now_ms)
{
	line_steering_command_t stop = {0, 0, true};
	line_steering_command_t command = {0, 0, false};

	if (steering == NULL || steering->stop_latched) return stop;

	if (position == POS_LOST) {
		if (!steering->line_lost) {
			steering->line_lost = true;
			steering->lost_since_ms = now_ms;
		}
		if ((uint32_t)(now_ms - steering->lost_since_ms) >= LOST_CONTINUE_MS) {
			steering->stop_latched = true;
			return stop;
		}
		command.motor_1_pwm = steering->last_motor_1_pwm;
		command.motor_2_pwm = steering->last_motor_2_pwm;
		return command;
	}

	steering->line_lost = false;
	switch (position) {
	case POS_JUNCTION:
		command.motor_1_pwm = FORWARD_PWM;
		command.motor_2_pwm = -FORWARD_PWM;
		break;
	case POS_LEFT:
		command.motor_1_pwm = FORWARD_PWM - CORRECTION_PWM;
		command.motor_2_pwm = -FORWARD_PWM;
		break;
	case POS_RIGHT:
		command.motor_1_pwm = FORWARD_PWM;
		command.motor_2_pwm = -(FORWARD_PWM - CORRECTION_PWM);
		break;
	default:
		steering->stop_latched = true;
		return stop;
	}

	steering->last_motor_1_pwm = command.motor_1_pwm;
	steering->last_motor_2_pwm = command.motor_2_pwm;
	return command;
}

#include "../../app.h"
#include "line_steering_test.h"

#if APP_TEST
#include "../../test_report.h"
#include "../../subsystem3/line_barcode/line_observation.h"
#include "line_steering.h"

BOOL line_steering_test(void)
{
	line_steering_t steering = {0};
	line_steering_command_t command;
	BOOL positions_ok;
	BOOL lost_line_ok;
	BOOL latch_ok;
	BOOL invalid_position_ok;
	TEST_INIT();

	line_steering_reset(&steering);
	command = line_steering_update(&steering, POS_JUNCTION, 1000U);
	positions_ok = command.motor_1_pwm == 25 &&
		       command.motor_2_pwm == -25 && !command.stop_latched;
	command = line_steering_update(&steering, POS_LEFT, 1010U);
	positions_ok = command.motor_1_pwm == 15 &&
		       command.motor_2_pwm == -25 && !command.stop_latched &&
		       positions_ok;
	command = line_steering_update(&steering, POS_RIGHT, 1020U);
	positions_ok = command.motor_1_pwm == 25 &&
		       command.motor_2_pwm == -15 && !command.stop_latched &&
		       positions_ok;
	TEST_CHECK("VC-U18", positions_ok,
		   "junction, left and right positions preserve source PWM decisions");

	command = line_steering_update(&steering, POS_LOST, 1030U);
	lost_line_ok = command.motor_1_pwm == 25 &&
		       command.motor_2_pwm == -15 && !command.stop_latched;
	command = line_steering_update(&steering, POS_LOST, 1329U);
	lost_line_ok = !command.stop_latched && lost_line_ok;
	command = line_steering_update(&steering, POS_LOST, 1330U);
	lost_line_ok = command.stop_latched && command.motor_1_pwm == 0 &&
		       command.motor_2_pwm == 0 && lost_line_ok;
	TEST_CHECK("VC-U19", lost_line_ok,
		   "lost-line hold expires at 300 ms with a zero-output latch");

	command = line_steering_update(&steering, POS_JUNCTION, 1400U);
	latch_ok = command.stop_latched && command.motor_1_pwm == 0 &&
		   command.motor_2_pwm == 0;
	line_steering_reset(&steering);
	command = line_steering_update(&steering, POS_JUNCTION, 1410U);
	latch_ok = !command.stop_latched && command.motor_1_pwm == 25 &&
		   command.motor_2_pwm == -25 && latch_ok;
	TEST_CHECK("VC-U20", latch_ok,
		   "stop latch persists until explicit reset");

	line_steering_reset(&steering);
	command = line_steering_update(&steering, UINT8_MAX, 1420U);
	invalid_position_ok = command.stop_latched &&
			      command.motor_1_pwm == 0 &&
			      command.motor_2_pwm == 0;
	command = line_steering_update(&steering, POS_JUNCTION, 1430U);
	invalid_position_ok = command.stop_latched &&
			      command.motor_1_pwm == 0 &&
			      command.motor_2_pwm == 0 && invalid_position_ok;
	TEST_CHECK("VC-U21", invalid_position_ok,
		   "invalid line positions fail closed until reset");

	TEST_SUMMARY("line steering");
	return positions_ok && lost_line_ok && latch_ok && invalid_position_ok;
}

#include "../../app.h"
#include "../../vehicle_buffers.h"
#include "vehicle_controller_task.h"

#define VEHICLE_CONTROLLER_PRIORITY 10

typedef struct {
	controller_state_t controller;
	uint32_t motion_sequence;
	uint32_t status_wait_ms;
	uint32_t status_sequence;
	uint32_t status_timestamp_ms;
	motion_status_message_t motion_status;
	bool arm_requested;
	bool emergency_sent;
	bool have_motion_status;
} vehicle_controller_context_t;

static vehicle_controller_task_config_t s_config;
static vehicle_controller_context_t s_context;
static bool s_started;

static bool publish_motion_command(vehicle_controller_context_t *context,
				   uint8_t mode, uint8_t stop_reason,
				   uint32_t now)
{
	motion_command_message_t message = {0};
	INT result;

	message.header.type = VEHICLE_MESSAGE_MOTION_COMMAND;
	message.header.version = VEHICLE_MESSAGE_VERSION;
	message.header.sequence = context->motion_sequence + 1U;
	message.header.timestamp_ms = now;
	message.mode = mode;
	message.stop_reason = stop_reason;
	result = tk_snd_mbf(g_mbf_motion_commands, &message,
			    (INT)sizeof(message), TMO_POL);
	if (result != E_OK) {
		tm_printf((UB*)"vehicle controller command delivery failed: %d\n",
			  (int)result);
		return false;
	}
	context->motion_sequence = message.header.sequence;
	return true;
}

static bool receive_line(vehicle_controller_context_t *context, uint32_t now)
{
	unsigned i;

	for (i = 0U; i < VEHICLE_MB_LINE_CAPACITY; i++) {
		line_observation_message_t message;
		INT received = tk_rcv_mbf(g_mbf_line_observations, &message, TMO_POL);

		if (received == E_TMOUT) return true;
		if (received != (INT)sizeof(message)) {
			tm_printf((UB*)"vehicle controller line receive failed: %d\n",
				  (int)received);
			car_safety_latch_fault(&context->controller.safety);
			return false;
		}
		if (!car_safety_line(&context->controller.safety, &message, now))
			return false;
	}
	return true;
}

static bool receive_imu(vehicle_controller_context_t *context, uint32_t now)
{
	unsigned i;

	for (i = 0U; i < VEHICLE_MB_IMU_CAPACITY; i++) {
		imu_estimate_message_t message;
		INT received = tk_rcv_mbf(g_mbf_imu_estimates, &message, TMO_POL);

		if (received == E_TMOUT) return true;
		if (received != (INT)sizeof(message)) {
			tm_printf((UB*)"vehicle controller IMU receive failed: %d\n",
				  (int)received);
			car_safety_latch_fault(&context->controller.safety);
			return false;
		}
		if (!car_safety_imu(&context->controller.safety, &message, now))
			return false;
	}
	return true;
}

static bool receive_obstacle(vehicle_controller_context_t *context, uint32_t now)
{
	unsigned i;

	for (i = 0U; i < VEHICLE_MB_OBSTACLE_CAPACITY; i++) {
		obstacle_event_message_t message;
		INT received = tk_rcv_mbf(g_mbf_obstacle_events, &message, TMO_POL);

		if (received == E_TMOUT) return true;
		if (received != (INT)sizeof(message)) {
			tm_printf((UB*)"vehicle controller obstacle receive failed: %d\n",
				  (int)received);
			car_safety_latch_fault(&context->controller.safety);
			return false;
		}
		if (!car_safety_obstacle(&context->controller.safety, &message, now))
			return false;
	}
	return true;
}

static bool receive_faults(vehicle_controller_context_t *context)
{
	for (;;) {
		fault_message_t message;
		INT received = tk_rcv_mbf(g_mbf_faults, &message, TMO_POL);

		if (received == E_TMOUT) return true;
		if (received != (INT)sizeof(message) ||
		    message.header.type != VEHICLE_MESSAGE_FAULT ||
		    message.header.version != VEHICLE_MESSAGE_VERSION ||
		    message.source < VEHICLE_FAULT_SENSOR ||
		    message.source > VEHICLE_FAULT_SYSTEM ||
		    message.recoverable > 1U || message.code == 0U) {
			tm_printf((UB*)"vehicle controller invalid fault message: %d\n",
				  (int)received);
		} else {
			tm_printf((UB*)"vehicle controller subsystem fault: source=%u code=%u\n",
				  (unsigned)message.source, (unsigned)message.code);
		}
		car_safety_latch_fault(&context->controller.safety);
		return false;
	}
}

static bool receive_motion_status(vehicle_controller_context_t *context,
				  uint32_t now)
{
	unsigned i;

	for (i = 0U; i < VEHICLE_MB_STATUS_CAPACITY; i++) {
		motion_status_message_t message;
		uint32_t age;
		uint32_t future;
		INT received = tk_rcv_mbf(g_mbf_motion_status, &message, TMO_POL);

		if (received == E_TMOUT) return true;
		if (received != (INT)sizeof(message) ||
		    message.header.type != VEHICLE_MESSAGE_MOTION_STATUS ||
		    message.header.version != VEHICLE_MESSAGE_VERSION ||
		    message.active > 1U || message.faulted > 1U ||
		    (message.faulted != 0U && message.fault_code == 0U) ||
		    (message.faulted == 0U && message.fault_code != 0U)) {
			tm_printf((UB*)"vehicle controller invalid motion status: %d\n",
				  (int)received);
			car_safety_latch_fault(&context->controller.safety);
			return false;
		}
		age = now - message.header.timestamp_ms;
		future = message.header.timestamp_ms - now;
		if (age > s_config.motion_status_max_age_ms ||
		    (future != 0U && future <= INT32_MAX) ||
		    (context->have_motion_status &&
		     (message.header.sequence - context->status_sequence != 1U ||
		      message.header.timestamp_ms - context->status_timestamp_ms >
			      INT32_MAX))) {
			tm_printf((UB*)"vehicle controller stale or out-of-order motion status\n");
			car_safety_latch_fault(&context->controller.safety);
			return false;
		}
		context->motion_status = message;
		context->status_sequence = message.header.sequence;
		context->status_timestamp_ms = message.header.timestamp_ms;
		context->have_motion_status = true;
		if (message.faulted != 0U || message.active != 0U ||
		    message.motor_1_pwm != 0 || message.motor_2_pwm != 0) {
			tm_printf((UB*)"vehicle controller motion status is not safely idle\n");
			car_safety_latch_fault(&context->controller.safety);
			return false;
		}
	}
	return true;
}

static void latch_controller_fault(vehicle_controller_context_t *context,
				   uint32_t now)
{
	car_safety_latch_fault(&context->controller.safety);
	(void)controller_state_step(&context->controller, now, false, false);
}

static bool controller_cycle(vehicle_controller_context_t *context,
			     uint32_t now,
			     const vehicle_controller_inputs_t *inputs)
{
	controller_state_value_t state;
	bool messages_valid = true;

	if (!receive_faults(context)) messages_valid = false;
	if (!receive_motion_status(context, now)) messages_valid = false;
	if (!receive_line(context, now)) messages_valid = false;
	if (!receive_imu(context, now)) messages_valid = false;
	if (!receive_obstacle(context, now)) messages_valid = false;
	if ((!context->have_motion_status &&
	     now - context->status_wait_ms > s_config.motion_status_max_age_ms) ||
	    (context->have_motion_status &&
	     now - context->status_timestamp_ms > s_config.motion_status_max_age_ms)) {
		tm_printf((UB*)"vehicle controller motion status deadline expired\n");
		messages_valid = false;
	}
	if (!messages_valid) car_safety_latch_fault(&context->controller.safety);

	state = controller_state_step(&context->controller, now,
				      inputs->go, inputs->emergency_stopped);
	if (state == CONTROLLER_FAULTED) {
		if (!context->emergency_sent &&
		    publish_motion_command(context, VEHICLE_MOTION_EMERGENCY_STOP,
					   (uint8_t)context->controller.safety.faults,
					   now))
			context->emergency_sent = true;
		return false;
	}

	if (state == CONTROLLER_FOLLOWING) {
		if (!context->have_motion_status) return true;
		if (!context->arm_requested) {
			if (publish_motion_command(context, VEHICLE_MOTION_ARM, 0U, now)) {
				context->arm_requested = true;
			} else {
				latch_controller_fault(context, now);
				if (publish_motion_command(context,
							   VEHICLE_MOTION_EMERGENCY_STOP,
							   (uint8_t)context->controller.safety.faults,
							   now))
					context->emergency_sent = true;
				return false;
			}
		}
		return true;
	}

	if (context->arm_requested) {
		if (publish_motion_command(context, VEHICLE_MOTION_STOP, 0U, now)) {
			context->arm_requested = false;
		} else {
			latch_controller_fault(context, now);
			if (publish_motion_command(context, VEHICLE_MOTION_EMERGENCY_STOP,
						   (uint8_t)context->controller.safety.faults,
						   now))
				context->emergency_sent = true;
			return false;
		}
	}
	return true;
}

static void vehicle_controller_task(INT stacd, void *exinf)
{
	(void)stacd;
	(void)exinf;
	for (;;) {
		vehicle_controller_inputs_t inputs = {0};
		SYSTIM now;
		ER er = tk_get_otm(&now);

		if (er != E_OK) {
			tm_printf((UB*)"vehicle controller clock failed: %d\n", (int)er);
			latch_controller_fault(&s_context,
					       s_context.controller.safety.last_clock_ms);
			if (!s_context.emergency_sent &&
			    publish_motion_command(&s_context,
						   VEHICLE_MOTION_EMERGENCY_STOP,
						   (uint8_t)s_context.controller.safety.faults,
						   s_context.controller.safety.last_clock_ms))
				s_context.emergency_sent = true;
		} else {
			bool inputs_valid = s_config.read_inputs(s_config.input_context, &inputs);
			if (!inputs_valid) {
				tm_printf((UB*)"vehicle controller local input read failed\n");
				latch_controller_fault(&s_context, now.lo);
			}
			(void)controller_cycle(&s_context, now.lo, &inputs);
		}

		er = tk_dly_tsk(s_config.period_ms);
		if (er != E_OK) {
			tm_printf((UB*)"vehicle controller schedule failed: %d\n", (int)er);
			latch_controller_fault(&s_context,
					       s_context.controller.safety.last_clock_ms);
			if (!s_context.emergency_sent &&
			    publish_motion_command(&s_context,
						   VEHICLE_MOTION_EMERGENCY_STOP,
						   (uint8_t)s_context.controller.safety.faults,
						   s_context.controller.safety.last_clock_ms))
				s_context.emergency_sent = true;
			for (;;) {
				er = tk_slp_tsk(TMO_FEVR);
				if (er != E_OK)
					tm_printf((UB*)"vehicle controller sleep failed: %d\n",
						  (int)er);
			}
		}
	}
}

ER vehicle_controller_task_start(const vehicle_controller_task_config_t *config)
{
	controller_state_t validation = {0};
	controller_state_t initial = {0};
	T_CTSK task = {0};
	SYSTIM now;
	ID id;
	ER er;

	if (s_started) return E_OBJ;
	if (config == NULL || config->read_inputs == NULL ||
	    config->period_ms == 0U ||
	    config->period_ms > VEHICLE_MOTION_STATUS_PERIOD_MS / 2U ||
	    config->motion_status_max_age_ms < 100U ||
	    config->motion_status_max_age_ms > INT32_MAX ||
	    config->period_ms > config->motion_status_max_age_ms ||
	    config->period_ms > config->safety.line_max_age_ms ||
	    config->period_ms > config->safety.imu_max_age_ms ||
	    config->period_ms > config->safety.ahead_max_age_ms ||
	    !controller_state_init(&validation, &config->safety, 0U))
		return E_PAR;
	if (g_mbf_line_observations <= 0 || g_mbf_imu_estimates <= 0 ||
	    g_mbf_obstacle_events <= 0 || g_mbf_faults <= 0 ||
	    g_mbf_motion_commands <= 0 || g_mbf_motion_status <= 0)
		return E_OBJ;
	er = tk_get_otm(&now);
	if (er != E_OK) return er < E_OK ? er : E_SYS;
	if (!controller_state_init(&initial, &config->safety, now.lo))
		return E_PAR;

	s_config = *config;
	s_context = (vehicle_controller_context_t){0};
	s_context.controller = initial;
	s_context.status_wait_ms = now.lo;
	task.task = (FP)vehicle_controller_task;
	task.tskatr = TA_HLNG | TA_RNG3;
	task.itskpri = VEHICLE_CONTROLLER_PRIORITY;
	task.stksz = TASK_STACK_SIZE;
	id = tk_cre_tsk(&task);
	if (id < E_OK) return id;
	s_started = true;
	er = tk_sta_tsk(id, 0);
	if (er != E_OK) {
		ER cleanup = tk_del_tsk(id);
		if (cleanup != E_OK)
			tm_printf((UB*)"vehicle controller cleanup failed: %d\n", (int)cleanup);
		else
			s_started = false;
		return er < E_OK ? er : E_SYS;
	}
	return E_OK;
}

#if APP_TEST
#include "../../test_report.h"
#include "../../subsystem5/obstacle/obs_profile_config.h"

static BOOL drain_controller_test_buffer(ID id)
{
	union {
		line_observation_message_t line;
		imu_estimate_message_t imu;
		obstacle_event_message_t obstacle;
		fault_message_t fault;
		motion_status_message_t status;
		motion_command_message_t command;
	} message;
	INT received;

	do {
		received = tk_rcv_mbf(id, &message, TMO_POL);
	} while (received > 0);
	return received == E_TMOUT;
}

static BOOL drain_controller_test_buffers(void)
{
	BOOL ok = drain_controller_test_buffer(g_mbf_line_observations);
	ok = drain_controller_test_buffer(g_mbf_imu_estimates) && ok;
	ok = drain_controller_test_buffer(g_mbf_obstacle_events) && ok;
	ok = drain_controller_test_buffer(g_mbf_faults) && ok;
	ok = drain_controller_test_buffer(g_mbf_motion_status) && ok;
	ok = drain_controller_test_buffer(g_mbf_motion_commands) && ok;
	return ok;
}

static BOOL queue_motion_status(uint32_t now, uint32_t sequence,
				uint8_t active, int16_t motor_1, int16_t motor_2)
{
	motion_status_message_t status = {0};

	status.header.type = VEHICLE_MESSAGE_MOTION_STATUS;
	status.header.version = VEHICLE_MESSAGE_VERSION;
	status.header.sequence = sequence;
	status.header.timestamp_ms = now;
	status.active = active;
	status.motor_1_pwm = motor_1;
	status.motor_2_pwm = motor_2;
	return tk_snd_mbf(g_mbf_motion_status, &status, (INT)sizeof(status),
			  TMO_POL) == E_OK;
}

static BOOL queue_safe_sensor_messages(uint32_t now, uint32_t sequence)
{
	line_observation_message_t line = {0};
	imu_estimate_message_t imu = {0};
	obstacle_event_message_t obstacle = {0};
	BOOL ok;

	line.header = (vehicle_message_header_t){VEHICLE_MESSAGE_LINE_OBSERVATION,
						  VEHICLE_MESSAGE_VERSION, sequence, now};
	line.line_detected = 1U;
	line.confidence = 1000U;
	imu.header = (vehicle_message_header_t){VEHICLE_MESSAGE_IMU_ESTIMATE,
						 VEHICLE_MESSAGE_VERSION, sequence, now};
	imu.validity = VEHICLE_IMU_VALID_ROLL_PITCH;
	obstacle.header = (vehicle_message_header_t){VEHICLE_MESSAGE_OBSTACLE_EVENT,
						      VEHICLE_MESSAGE_VERSION, sequence, now};
	obstacle.obstacle_class = VEHICLE_OBSTACLE_AHEAD_READING;
	obstacle.distance_mm = OBS_DIST_NONE;
	ok = tk_snd_mbf(g_mbf_line_observations, &line, (INT)sizeof(line), TMO_POL) == E_OK;
	ok = tk_snd_mbf(g_mbf_imu_estimates, &imu, (INT)sizeof(imu), TMO_POL) == E_OK && ok;
	ok = tk_snd_mbf(g_mbf_obstacle_events, &obstacle, (INT)sizeof(obstacle),
			TMO_POL) == E_OK && ok;
	return ok;
}

static BOOL receive_test_command(uint8_t expected_mode, uint32_t expected_sequence)
{
	motion_command_message_t command;
	INT received = tk_rcv_mbf(g_mbf_motion_commands, &command, TMO_POL);

	return received == (INT)sizeof(command) &&
	       command.header.type == VEHICLE_MESSAGE_MOTION_COMMAND &&
	       command.header.version == VEHICLE_MESSAGE_VERSION &&
	       command.header.sequence == expected_sequence &&
	       command.mode == expected_mode &&
	       command.linear_velocity == 0 && command.angular_velocity == 0 &&
	       command.duration_ms == 0U;
}

static const vehicle_controller_task_config_t test_config = {
	{200U, 200U, 200U, 200U, 500U, 300U},
	10U, 100U, NULL, NULL
};

static BOOL controller_arm_test(void)
{
	vehicle_controller_context_t context = {0};
	vehicle_controller_inputs_t inputs = {false, false};
	motion_command_message_t unexpected;
	BOOL ok = controller_state_init(&context.controller, &test_config.safety, 100U);

	context.status_wait_ms = 100U;
	ok = queue_safe_sensor_messages(100U, 1U) && ok;
	ok = controller_cycle(&context, 100U, &inputs) &&
	     context.controller.state == CONTROLLER_WAITING &&
	     tk_rcv_mbf(g_mbf_motion_commands, &unexpected, TMO_POL) == E_TMOUT && ok;
	inputs.go = true;
	ok = controller_cycle(&context, 101U, &inputs) &&
	     context.controller.state == CONTROLLER_FOLLOWING &&
	     !context.arm_requested &&
	     tk_rcv_mbf(g_mbf_motion_commands, &unexpected, TMO_POL) == E_TMOUT && ok;
	ok = queue_motion_status(101U, 1U, 0U, 0, 0) && ok;
	ok = controller_cycle(&context, 102U, &inputs) &&
	     context.controller.state == CONTROLLER_FOLLOWING &&
	     context.arm_requested &&
	     receive_test_command(VEHICLE_MOTION_ARM, 1U) && ok;
	inputs.go = false;
	ok = controller_cycle(&context, 103U, &inputs) &&
	     context.controller.state == CONTROLLER_WAITING &&
	     !context.arm_requested &&
	     receive_test_command(VEHICLE_MOTION_STOP, 2U) && ok;
	return drain_controller_test_buffers() && ok;
}

static BOOL controller_fault_test(void)
{
	vehicle_controller_context_t context = {0};
	vehicle_controller_inputs_t inputs = {false, false};
	BOOL ok = controller_state_init(&context.controller, &test_config.safety, 100U);

	context.status_wait_ms = 100U;
	ok = queue_motion_status(301U, 1U, 0U, 0, 0) && ok;
	ok = !controller_cycle(&context, 301U, &inputs) &&
	     context.controller.state == CONTROLLER_FAULTED &&
	     context.emergency_sent &&
	     receive_test_command(VEHICLE_MOTION_EMERGENCY_STOP, 1U) && ok;
	return drain_controller_test_buffers() && ok;
}

static BOOL controller_local_emergency_test(void)
{
	vehicle_controller_context_t context = {0};
	vehicle_controller_inputs_t inputs = {true, true};
	BOOL ok = controller_state_init(&context.controller, &test_config.safety, 100U);

	context.status_wait_ms = 100U;
	ok = queue_safe_sensor_messages(100U, 1U) && ok;
	ok = queue_motion_status(100U, 1U, 0U, 0, 0) && ok;
	ok = !controller_cycle(&context, 100U, &inputs) &&
	     context.controller.state == CONTROLLER_FAULTED &&
	     context.emergency_sent &&
	     receive_test_command(VEHICLE_MOTION_EMERGENCY_STOP, 1U) && ok;
	return drain_controller_test_buffers() && ok;
}

static BOOL controller_nonidle_status_test(void)
{
	vehicle_controller_context_t context = {0};
	vehicle_controller_inputs_t inputs = {false, false};
	BOOL ok = controller_state_init(&context.controller, &test_config.safety, 100U);

	context.status_wait_ms = 100U;
	ok = queue_safe_sensor_messages(100U, 1U) && ok;
	ok = queue_motion_status(100U, 1U, 0U, 20, -20) && ok;
	ok = !controller_cycle(&context, 100U, &inputs) &&
	     context.controller.state == CONTROLLER_FAULTED &&
	     context.emergency_sent &&
	     receive_test_command(VEHICLE_MOTION_EMERGENCY_STOP, 1U) && ok;
	return drain_controller_test_buffers() && ok;
}

static BOOL controller_stale_status_test(void)
{
	vehicle_controller_context_t context = {0};
	vehicle_controller_inputs_t inputs = {false, false};
	BOOL ok = controller_state_init(&context.controller, &test_config.safety, 100U);

	context.status_wait_ms = 100U;
	ok = queue_safe_sensor_messages(100U, 1U) && ok;
	ok = queue_motion_status(100U, 1U, 0U, 0, 0) && ok;
	ok = controller_cycle(&context, 100U, &inputs) && ok;
	ok = queue_safe_sensor_messages(201U, 2U) && ok;
	ok = !controller_cycle(&context, 201U, &inputs) &&
	     context.controller.state == CONTROLLER_FAULTED &&
	     context.emergency_sent &&
	     receive_test_command(VEHICLE_MOTION_EMERGENCY_STOP, 1U) && ok;
	return drain_controller_test_buffers() && ok;
}

BOOL vehicle_controller_task_test(void)
{
	BOOL arm_ok;
	BOOL fault_ok;
	BOOL emergency_ok;
	BOOL status_ok;
	BOOL stale_status_ok;
	TEST_INIT();

	/* controller_cycle() reads the module config (s_config) for the motion
	   status deadline.  The task is not started during the boot-time test
	   run, so s_config would otherwise still be all zeros (max age 0 ms)
	   and every advancing-clock cycle would fault.  Install the test
	   config; vehicle_controller_task_start() overwrites it later. */
	s_config = test_config;

	arm_ok = controller_arm_test();
	fault_ok = controller_fault_test();
	emergency_ok = controller_local_emergency_test();
	status_ok = controller_nonidle_status_test();
	stale_status_ok = controller_stale_status_test();
	TEST_CHECK("VC-U13", arm_ok, "fresh safety inputs and local GO gate ARM/STOP commands");
	TEST_CHECK("VC-U14", emergency_ok, "local emergency input sends a latched emergency stop");
	TEST_CHECK("VC-U15", fault_ok, "missing sensor deadlines latch fault and request emergency stop");
	TEST_CHECK("VC-U16", status_ok, "nonzero commanded output status blocks arming and requests emergency stop");
	TEST_CHECK("VC-U17", stale_status_ok, "stale motion status faults despite fresh sensor observations");
	TEST_SUMMARY("vehicle controller task");
	return arm_ok && fault_ok && emergency_ok && status_ok && stale_status_ok;
}

#ifndef VEHICLE_CONTROLLER_TASK_H
#define VEHICLE_CONTROLLER_TASK_H

#include <tk/tkernel.h>
#include "controller_state.h"

typedef struct {
	bool go;
	bool emergency_stopped;
} vehicle_controller_inputs_t;

typedef bool (*vehicle_controller_read_inputs_t)(
	void *context, vehicle_controller_inputs_t *inputs);

typedef struct {
	car_safety_config_t safety;
	RELTIM period_ms;
	uint32_t motion_status_max_age_ms;
	vehicle_controller_read_inputs_t read_inputs;
	void *input_context;
} vehicle_controller_task_config_t;

/* Opt-in safety/arming task. The callback supplies trusted local GO and
 * emergency-stop inputs and must return promptly without network access.
 * The callback context must remain valid while the task runs. The task
 * exclusively consumes line, IMU, obstacle, fault and motion-status buffers.
 * It sends ARM only after the safety gate passes and a fresh zero-output
 * motion status is received; it does not yet issue motion commands. Motion
 * status is commanded state, not evidence that the vehicle physically stopped.
 * The task period must be no more than half the motion-status publish interval.
 * Do not start it alongside controller_monitor_start(). */
ER vehicle_controller_task_start(const vehicle_controller_task_config_t *config);

#if APP_TEST
BOOL vehicle_controller_task_test(void);
#endif

#include "motion_control.h"

#include <stddef.h>

#define MOTION_MAX_CONTROL_INTERVAL_MS 250U

bool motion_control_start(motion_control_t *control,
			  const motion_control_config_t *config,
			  int8_t direction, uint32_t now_ms)
{
	motion_control_t initial = {0};

	if (control == NULL) return false;
	if (config == NULL || (direction != 1 && direction != -1) ||
	    config->feedback.max_interval_ms > MOTION_MAX_CONTROL_INTERVAL_MS ||
	    config->feedback.max_age_ms > MOTION_MAX_CONTROL_INTERVAL_MS ||
	    !motion_feedback_init(&initial.feedback, &config->feedback) ||
	    !wheel_speed_control_init(&initial.speed, &config->speed)) {
		*control = (motion_control_t){.faulted = true};
		return false;
	}
	initial.start_ms = now_ms;
	initial.last_check_ms = now_ms;
	initial.direction = direction;
	initial.running = true;
	*control = initial;
	return true;
}

void motion_control_stop(motion_control_t *control)
{
	if (control == NULL) return;
	control->output = (wheel_speed_output_t){0};
	if (control->speed.initialized)
		(void)wheel_speed_control_reset(&control->speed);
	control->have_output = false;
	control->running = false;
}

static motion_control_result_t fault(motion_control_t *control,
				     wheel_speed_output_t *output)
{
	if (output != NULL) *output = (wheel_speed_output_t){0};
	if (control != NULL) {
		motion_control_stop(control);
		control->faulted = true;
	}
	return MOTION_CONTROL_FAULT;
}

motion_control_result_t motion_control_step(motion_control_t *control,
			 const wheel_odometry_message_t *message,
			 uint32_t now_ms, wheel_speed_output_t *output)
{
	motion_feedback_result_t result;
	motion_wheel_speeds_t speeds;
	uint32_t last_sample;
	float left, right;

	if (control == NULL || output == NULL || !control->running || control->faulted)
		return fault(control, output);
	last_sample = control->feedback.have_baseline ?
		      control->feedback.last_timestamp_ms : control->start_ms;
	if (now_ms - control->last_check_ms > INT32_MAX ||
	    now_ms - last_sample > control->feedback.config.max_age_ms)
		return fault(control, output);
	control->last_check_ms = now_ms;
	if (message != NULL) {
		if (message->header.timestamp_ms - control->start_ms > INT32_MAX)
			return fault(control, output);
		result = motion_feedback_update(&control->feedback, message, now_ms, &speeds);
		if (result == MOTION_FEEDBACK_INVALID) return fault(control, output);
		if (result == MOTION_FEEDBACK_READY) {
			left = speeds.left_cm_per_second * control->direction;
			right = speeds.right_cm_per_second * control->direction;
			if (left < 0.0f || right < 0.0f ||
			    !wheel_speed_control_update(&control->speed, left, right,
							speeds.elapsed_seconds, &control->output))
				return fault(control, output);
			control->have_output = true;
		}
	}
	*output = control->output;
	return control->have_output ? MOTION_CONTROL_READY : MOTION_CONTROL_WAITING;
}
=== C:\Users\Yong Jun\school\INF2004-Embedded_Systems\project\INF2004_Project\app_program\subsystem2\motion\motion_control.h ===
#ifndef MOTION_CONTROL_H
#define MOTION_CONTROL_H

#include "motion_feedback.h"
#include "wheel_speed_control.h"

typedef enum {
	MOTION_CONTROL_WAITING = 0,
	MOTION_CONTROL_READY,
	MOTION_CONTROL_FAULT
} motion_control_result_t;

typedef struct {
	motion_feedback_config_t feedback;
	wheel_speed_config_t speed;
} motion_control_config_t;

typedef struct {
	motion_feedback_t feedback;
	wheel_speed_control_t speed;
	wheel_speed_output_t output;
	uint32_t start_ms;
	uint32_t last_check_ms;
	int8_t direction;
	bool have_output;
	bool running;
	bool faulted;
} motion_control_t;

/* Straight motion only: direction is +1 forward or -1 reverse for both
 * polarity-normalized wheels. Start only after the hardware is stopped.
 * The owner must use verified calibration and reset on every restart.
 * Failure clears the control state and latches a fault. */
bool motion_control_start(motion_control_t *control,
			  const motion_control_config_t *config,
			  int8_t direction, uint32_t now_ms);

/* Call periodically even without a message. The first message after start
 * establishes a baseline; output remains zero until a second valid message.
 * On any fault output is zero and the fault is latched. READY output may be
 * retained between samples only while the caller continues checking time.
 * A NULL output or invalid control is a fault; the caller must stop hardware.
 * This calculation cannot stop hardware or replace an independent watchdog. */
motion_control_result_t motion_control_step(motion_control_t *control,
			 const wheel_odometry_message_t *message,
			 uint32_t now_ms, wheel_speed_output_t *output);

/* Clear the output and PID state; caller must also stop the hardware. */
void motion_control_stop(motion_control_t *control);

#endif
=== C:\Users\Yong Jun\school\INF2004-Embedded_Systems\project\INF2004_Project\app_program\subsystem2\motion\motion_feedback.c ===
#include "motion_feedback.h"

#include <stddef.h>

bool motion_feedback_init(motion_feedback_t *feedback,
			  const motion_feedback_config_t *config)
{
	motion_feedback_t initial = {0};
	double distance;

	if (feedback == NULL || config == NULL || config->max_age_ms == 0U ||
	    config->max_age_ms > INT32_MAX || config->max_interval_ms == 0U ||
	    config->max_interval_ms > INT32_MAX ||
	    !wheel_odometry_distance_precise(&config->left, 0, &distance) ||
	    !wheel_odometry_distance_precise(&config->right, 0, &distance))
		return false;
	initial.config = *config;
	initial.initialized = true;
	*feedback = initial;
	return true;
}

motion_feedback_result_t motion_feedback_update(motion_feedback_t *feedback,
				 const wheel_odometry_message_t *message,
				 uint32_t now_ms, motion_wheel_speeds_t *speeds)
{
	motion_wheel_speeds_t next;
	wheel_odometry_result_t left, right;
	uint32_t left_count, right_count, elapsed;
	int32_t left_delta, right_delta;

	if (feedback == NULL || message == NULL || speeds == NULL ||
	    !feedback->initialized ||
	    message->header.type != VEHICLE_MESSAGE_WHEEL_ODOMETRY ||
	    message->header.version != VEHICLE_MESSAGE_VERSION || message->valid != 1U ||
	    now_ms - message->header.timestamp_ms > feedback->config.max_age_ms)
		return MOTION_FEEDBACK_INVALID;
	left_count = (uint32_t)message->left_count;
	right_count = (uint32_t)message->right_count;
	if (feedback->have_baseline) {
		elapsed = message->header.timestamp_ms - feedback->last_timestamp_ms;
		if (message->header.sequence - feedback->last_sequence != 1U ||
		    elapsed == 0U || elapsed > feedback->config.max_interval_ms ||
		    !wheel_odometry_count_delta(left_count, feedback->last_left, &left_delta) ||
		    !wheel_odometry_count_delta(right_count, feedback->last_right, &right_delta))
			return MOTION_FEEDBACK_INVALID;
		next.elapsed_seconds = (float)elapsed / 1000.0f;
		if (!wheel_odometry_calculate(&feedback->config.left, left_delta,
					       next.elapsed_seconds, &left) ||
		    !wheel_odometry_calculate(&feedback->config.right, right_delta,
					       next.elapsed_seconds, &right))
			return MOTION_FEEDBACK_INVALID;
		next.timestamp_ms = message->header.timestamp_ms;
		next.left_cm_per_second = left.speed_cm_per_second;
		next.right_cm_per_second = right.speed_cm_per_second;
	}
	feedback->last_left = left_count;
	feedback->last_right = right_count;
	feedback->last_sequence = message->header.sequence;
	feedback->last_timestamp_ms = message->header.timestamp_ms;
	if (!feedback->have_baseline) {
		feedback->have_baseline = true;
		return MOTION_FEEDBACK_BASELINE;
	}
	*speeds = next;
	return MOTION_FEEDBACK_READY;
}

#ifndef MOTION_FEEDBACK_H
#define MOTION_FEEDBACK_H

#include "../../subsystem6/odometry/wheel_odometry.h"
#include "../../vehicle_messages.h"

typedef struct {
	wheel_odometry_config_t left;
	wheel_odometry_config_t right;
	uint32_t max_age_ms;
	uint32_t max_interval_ms;
} motion_feedback_config_t;

typedef struct {
	motion_feedback_config_t config;
	uint32_t last_left;
	uint32_t last_right;
	uint32_t last_sequence;
	uint32_t last_timestamp_ms;
	bool initialized;
	bool have_baseline;
} motion_feedback_t;

typedef struct {
	uint32_t timestamp_ms;
	float elapsed_seconds;
	/* Signed speeds after odometry's per-wheel polarity normalization. */
	float left_cm_per_second;
	float right_cm_per_second;
} motion_wheel_speeds_t;

typedef enum {
	MOTION_FEEDBACK_INVALID = 0,
	MOTION_FEEDBACK_BASELINE,
	MOTION_FEEDBACK_READY
} motion_feedback_result_t;

/* Calibration must be independently verified; the owner is the motion task. */
bool motion_feedback_init(motion_feedback_t *feedback,
			  const motion_feedback_config_t *config);

/* Messages must be fresh, valid and consecutive; timestamps and wheel counts
 * may wrap, but differences of half a counter range are ambiguous. The first
 * valid message establishes a baseline without changing speeds. On failure,
 * state and speeds are unchanged. The caller must stop on failure, check
 * direction and freshness, and reset the baseline when restarting control. */
motion_feedback_result_t motion_feedback_update(motion_feedback_t *feedback,
				 const wheel_odometry_message_t *message,
				 uint32_t now_ms, motion_wheel_speeds_t *speeds);

#endif
=== C:\Users\Yong Jun\school\INF2004-Embedded_Systems\project\INF2004_Project\app_program\subsystem2\motion\motion_task.c ===
#include "../../app.h"
#include "../../vehicle_buffers.h"
#include "motor_driver.h"
#include "motion_task.h"

#define MOTION_TASK_PRIORITY 10
#define MOTION_FAULT_RETRY_MS 1000U

typedef struct {
	motion_control_t control;
	wheel_odometry_message_t pending;
	uint32_t last_clock_ms;
	uint32_t last_command_ms;
	uint32_t last_sequence;
	uint32_t issued_ms;
	uint32_t barrier_ms;
	uint32_t status_sequence;
	uint32_t last_status_ms;
	uint16_t duration_ms;
	uint16_t fault_code;
	int16_t velocity;
	int16_t motor_1_pwm;
	int16_t motor_2_pwm;
	bool have_command;
	bool have_pending;
	bool have_barrier;
	bool have_status;
	bool status_dirty;
	bool active;
	bool hardware_armed;
	bool faulted;
} motion_task_state_t;

static motion_task_config_t s_config;
static motion_task_state_t s_state;
static bool s_started;
static bool s_hardware_enabled;

#define MOTION_LEFT_MOTOR MOTOR_1
#define MOTION_RIGHT_MOTOR MOTOR_2
#define MOTION_LEFT_POLARITY 1
#define MOTION_RIGHT_POLARITY (-1)

static int16_t motor_command(uint8_t pwm, int8_t direction, int8_t polarity)
{
	return (int16_t)((int16_t)pwm * direction * polarity);
}

static void set_commanded_pwm(motion_task_state_t *state, int16_t motor_1,
			      int16_t motor_2)
{
	if (state->motor_1_pwm != motor_1 || state->motor_2_pwm != motor_2) {
		state->motor_1_pwm = motor_1;
		state->motor_2_pwm = motor_2;
		state->status_dirty = true;
	}
}

static void make_motion_status_message(const motion_task_state_t *state,
				       uint32_t sequence, uint32_t now,
				       motion_status_message_t *message)
{
	*message = (motion_status_message_t){0};
	message->header.type = VEHICLE_MESSAGE_MOTION_STATUS;
	message->header.version = VEHICLE_MESSAGE_VERSION;
	message->header.sequence = sequence;
	message->header.timestamp_ms = now;
	message->motor_1_pwm = state->motor_1_pwm;
	message->motor_2_pwm = state->motor_2_pwm;
	message->active = state->active ? 1U : 0U;
	message->faulted = state->faulted ? 1U : 0U;
	message->fault_code = state->faulted ? state->fault_code : 0U;
}

static uint16_t publish_motion_status(motion_task_state_t *state, uint32_t now)
{
	motion_status_message_t message;
	INT result;

	if (!s_hardware_enabled) return 0U;
	if (state->have_status && !state->status_dirty &&
	    now - state->last_status_ms < VEHICLE_MOTION_STATUS_PERIOD_MS)
		return 0U;
	make_motion_status_message(state, state->status_sequence + 1U, now, &message);
	result = tk_snd_mbf(g_mbf_motion_status, &message,
			    (INT)sizeof(message), TMO_POL);
	if (result != E_OK) {
		tm_printf((UB*)"motion status delivery failed: %d\n", (int)result);
		return VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_MOTION_STATUS;
	}
	state->status_sequence = message.header.sequence;
	state->last_status_ms = now;
	state->have_status = true;
	state->status_dirty = false;
	return 0U;
}

static void stop_motion(motion_task_state_t *state)
{
	motion_control_stop(&state->control);
	if (state->active) state->status_dirty = true;
	state->active = false;
	if (s_hardware_enabled) {
		ER er = motor_brake_all();
		if (er != E_OK)
			tm_printf((UB*)"motor brake failed: %d\n", (int)er);
		else
			set_commanded_pwm(state, 0, 0);
	} else {
		set_commanded_pwm(state, 0, 0);
	}
}

static ER apply_motor_output(motion_task_state_t *state,
			     const wheel_speed_output_t *output)
{
	ER left_er;
	ER right_er;
	int16_t motor_1;
	int16_t motor_2;

	if (!s_hardware_enabled) return E_OK;
	if (!state->hardware_armed || !state->active ||
	    !state->control.have_output)
	{
		ER er = motor_brake_all();
		if (er == E_OK) set_commanded_pwm(state, 0, 0);
		return er;
	}
	motor_1 = motor_command(output->left_pwm_percent,
				state->control.direction, MOTION_LEFT_POLARITY);
	motor_2 = motor_command(output->right_pwm_percent,
				state->control.direction, MOTION_RIGHT_POLARITY);
	left_er = motor_set_speed(MOTION_LEFT_MOTOR,
				  motor_1);
	right_er = motor_set_speed(MOTION_RIGHT_MOTOR,
				   motor_2);
	if (left_er != E_OK && left_er != E_BUSY) return left_er;
	if (right_er != E_OK && right_er != E_BUSY) return right_er;
	if (left_er == E_BUSY || right_er == E_BUSY) {
		ER er = motor_brake_all();
		if (er == E_OK) set_commanded_pwm(state, 0, 0);
		return er;
	}
	set_commanded_pwm(state, motor_1, motor_2);
	return E_OK;
}

static uint16_t discard_odometry(void)
{
	unsigned i;

	for (i = 0U; i < VEHICLE_MB_ODOMETRY_CAPACITY; i++) {
		wheel_odometry_message_t message;
		INT received = tk_rcv_mbf(g_mbf_motion_odometry, &message, TMO_POL);
		if (received == E_TMOUT) break;
		if (received != (INT)sizeof(message))
			return VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_MOTION_ODOMETRY;
	}
	return 0U;
}

static uint16_t receive_commands(motion_task_state_t *state,
				 const motion_task_config_t *config,
				 uint32_t now, bool suppress_motion)
{
	unsigned i;

	for (i = 0U; i < VEHICLE_MB_MOTION_CAPACITY; i++) {
		motion_command_message_t command;
		uint32_t age;
		int32_t speed;
		INT received = tk_rcv_mbf(g_mbf_motion_commands, &command, TMO_POL);
		if (received == E_TMOUT) break;
		if (received != (INT)sizeof(command) ||
		    command.header.type != VEHICLE_MESSAGE_MOTION_COMMAND ||
		    command.header.version != VEHICLE_MESSAGE_VERSION)
			return VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_MOTION_COMMAND;
		if (command.mode == VEHICLE_MOTION_EMERGENCY_STOP) {
			stop_motion(state);
			return VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_MOTION_EMERGENCY;
		}
		if (command.mode == VEHICLE_MOTION_STOP) {
			stop_motion(state);
			state->barrier_ms = now;
			state->have_barrier = true;
			suppress_motion = true;
		}
		age = now - command.header.timestamp_ms;
		if ((state->have_command &&
		     (command.header.sequence - state->last_sequence != 1U ||
		      command.header.timestamp_ms - state->last_command_ms > INT32_MAX)) ||
		    (command.mode != VEHICLE_MOTION_STOP && age > config->command_max_age_ms))
			return VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_MOTION_COMMAND;
		switch (command.mode) {
		case VEHICLE_MOTION_STOP:
			break;
		case VEHICLE_MOTION_ARM:
			if (!s_hardware_enabled || state->active ||
			    command.linear_velocity != 0 ||
			    command.angular_velocity != 0 ||
			    command.duration_ms != 0U)
				return VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_MOTION_COMMAND;
			if (motor_clear_emergency_stop() != E_OK)
				return VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_MOTION_ACTUATOR;
			state->hardware_armed = true;
			state->barrier_ms = command.header.timestamp_ms;
			state->have_barrier = true;
			break;
		case VEHICLE_MOTION_VELOCITY:
			speed = command.linear_velocity;
			if (speed < 0) speed = -speed;
			if (speed == 0 || speed > config->max_linear_speed_cm_s ||
			    command.angular_velocity != 0 || command.duration_ms == 0U ||
			    command.duration_ms > config->max_duration_ms ||
			    age >= command.duration_ms ||
			    (s_hardware_enabled && !state->hardware_armed))
				return VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_MOTION_COMMAND;
			if (!suppress_motion && (!state->have_barrier ||
			    (command.header.timestamp_ms - state->barrier_ms != 0U &&
			     command.header.timestamp_ms - state->barrier_ms <= INT32_MAX))) {
				if (state->active && state->velocity != command.linear_velocity)
					return VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_MOTION_COMMAND;
				if (!state->active) {
					motion_control_config_t control = config->control;
					control.speed.target_speed_cm_s = (float)speed;
					if (!motion_control_start(&state->control, &control,
					    command.linear_velocity > 0 ? 1 : -1, now))
						return VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_MOTION_CONTROL;
					state->velocity = command.linear_velocity;
					state->active = true;
					state->status_dirty = true;
				}
				state->issued_ms = command.header.timestamp_ms;
				state->duration_ms = command.duration_ms;
			}
			break;
		default:
			return VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_MOTION_COMMAND;
		}
		state->last_command_ms = command.header.timestamp_ms;
		state->last_sequence = command.header.sequence;
		state->have_command = true;
	}
	return 0U;
}

static uint16_t run_cycle(motion_task_state_t *state,
			  const motion_task_config_t *config, uint32_t now)
{
	wheel_speed_output_t output;
	motion_control_result_t control_result;
	uint16_t fault;
	bool expired = false;
	unsigned i;

	if (now - state->last_clock_ms > INT32_MAX)
		return VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_MOTION_CLOCK;
	state->last_clock_ms = now;
	if (state->active && now - state->issued_ms >= state->duration_ms) {
		stop_motion(state);
		state->barrier_ms = now;
		state->have_barrier = true;
		expired = true;
	}
	if (!state->active) {
		state->have_pending = false;
		fault = discard_odometry();
		if (fault != 0U) return fault;
	}
	fault = receive_commands(state, config, now, expired);
	if (fault != 0U) return fault;
	if (!state->active) {
		fault = discard_odometry();
		if (fault != 0U) return fault;
		return publish_motion_status(state, now);
	}
	for (i = 0U; i < VEHICLE_MB_ODOMETRY_CAPACITY + 1U; i++) {
		wheel_odometry_message_t message;
		uint32_t ahead;
		if (state->have_pending) {
			message = state->pending;
		} else {
			INT received = tk_rcv_mbf(g_mbf_motion_odometry, &message, TMO_POL);
			if (received == E_TMOUT) break;
			if (received != (INT)sizeof(message))
				return VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_MOTION_ODOMETRY;
		}
		ahead = message.header.timestamp_ms - now;
		if (ahead != 0U && ahead <= INT32_MAX) {
			state->pending = message;
			state->have_pending = true;
			break;
		}
		state->have_pending = false;
		if (motion_control_step(&state->control, &message, now, &output) ==
		    MOTION_CONTROL_FAULT)
			return VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_MOTION_ODOMETRY;
	}
	control_result = motion_control_step(&state->control, NULL, now, &output);
	if (control_result == MOTION_CONTROL_FAULT)
		return VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_MOTION_ODOMETRY;
	if (apply_motor_output(state, &output) != E_OK)
		return VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_MOTION_ACTUATOR;
	return publish_motion_status(state, now);
}

static void report_fault(uint16_t code, uint32_t now)
{
	fault_message_t fault = {0};
	bool sent = false;

	stop_motion(&s_state);
	if (s_hardware_enabled) {
		ER er = motor_emergency_stop();
		if (er != E_OK) {
			tm_printf((UB*)"motor emergency stop failed: %d\n", (int)er);
		} else {
			set_commanded_pwm(&s_state, 0, 0);
		}
	}
	s_state.faulted = true;
	s_state.fault_code = code;
	s_state.status_dirty = true;
	if (publish_motion_status(&s_state, now) != 0U)
		tm_printf((UB*)"motion fault status could not be queued\n");
	fault.header.type = VEHICLE_MESSAGE_FAULT;
	fault.header.version = VEHICLE_MESSAGE_VERSION;
	fault.header.sequence = 1U;
	fault.header.timestamp_ms = now;
	fault.source = VEHICLE_FAULT_MOTION;
	fault.code = code;
	fault.recoverable = 0U;
	tm_printf((UB*)"motion task fault; code %u\n", (unsigned)code);
	for (;;) {
		ER er;
		if (!sent) {
			er = tk_snd_mbf(g_mbf_faults, &fault, (INT)sizeof(fault), TMO_POL);
			if (er == E_OK) sent = true;
			else tm_printf((UB*)"motion fault delivery failed: %d\n", (int)er);
		}
		er = tk_dly_tsk(MOTION_FAULT_RETRY_MS);
		if (er != E_OK) {
			tm_printf((UB*)"motion fault retry delay failed: %d\n", (int)er);
			er = tk_slp_tsk(TMO_FEVR);
			tm_printf((UB*)"motion fault sleep returned: %d\n", (int)er);
		}
	}
}

static void motion_task(INT stacd, void *exinf)
{
	SYSTIM now;
	uint32_t timestamp = 0U;
	ER er;

	(void)stacd;
	(void)exinf;
	if (s_hardware_enabled) {
		er = motor_driver_init();
		if (er != E_OK) report_fault(VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_MOTION_ACTUATOR, timestamp);
	}
	er = tk_get_otm(&now);
	if (er != E_OK) report_fault(VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_MOTION_CLOCK, timestamp);
	timestamp = now.lo;
	s_state.last_clock_ms = timestamp;
	for (;;) {
		uint16_t fault;
		er = tk_get_otm(&now);
		if (er != E_OK) report_fault(VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_MOTION_CLOCK, timestamp);
		timestamp = now.lo;
		fault = run_cycle(&s_state, &s_config, timestamp);
		if (fault != 0U) report_fault(fault, timestamp);
		er = tk_dly_tsk(s_config.period_ms);
		if (er != E_OK) report_fault(VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_MOTION_SCHEDULE, timestamp);
	}
}

static ER motion_task_start_internal(const motion_task_config_t *config,
				     bool hardware_enabled)
{
	motion_control_t validation = {0};
	T_CTSK task = {0};
	ID id;
	ER er;

	if (s_started) return E_OBJ;
	if (config == NULL || config->period_ms == 0U ||
	    config->period_ms > config->control.feedback.max_age_ms ||
	    config->period_ms > config->control.feedback.max_interval_ms ||
	    config->command_max_age_ms == 0U || config->command_max_age_ms > INT32_MAX ||
	    config->max_duration_ms == 0U || config->max_linear_speed_cm_s == 0U ||
	    !motion_control_start(&validation, &config->control, 1, 0U))
		return E_PAR;
	if (g_mbf_motion_commands <= 0 || g_mbf_motion_odometry <= 0 ||
	    g_mbf_faults <= 0 ||
	    (hardware_enabled && g_mbf_motion_status <= 0))
		return E_OBJ;
	s_config = *config;
	s_state = (motion_task_state_t){0};
	s_hardware_enabled = hardware_enabled;
	s_state.status_dirty = hardware_enabled;
	task.task = (FP)motion_task;
	task.tskatr = TA_HLNG | TA_RNG3;
	task.itskpri = MOTION_TASK_PRIORITY;
	task.stksz = TASK_STACK_SIZE;
	id = tk_cre_tsk(&task);
	if (id < E_OK) {
		s_hardware_enabled = false;
		return id;
	}
	s_started = true;
	er = tk_sta_tsk(id, 0);
	if (er != E_OK) {
		ER cleanup = tk_del_tsk(id);
		if (cleanup != E_OK) tm_printf((UB*)"motion task cleanup failed: %d\n", (int)cleanup);
		else {
			s_started = false;
			s_hardware_enabled = false;
		}
		return er < E_OK ? er : E_SYS;
	}
	return E_OK;
}

ER motion_task_start(const motion_task_config_t *config)
{
	return motion_task_start_internal(config, false);
}

ER motion_task_start_hardware(const motion_task_config_t *config)
{
	return motion_task_start_internal(config, true);
}

#if APP_TEST
#include "../../test_report.h"

static motion_command_message_t test_command(uint32_t sequence, uint32_t timestamp,
					      uint8_t mode, int16_t velocity,
					      uint16_t duration)
{
	motion_command_message_t command = {0};
	command.header.type = VEHICLE_MESSAGE_MOTION_COMMAND;
	command.header.version = VEHICLE_MESSAGE_VERSION;
	command.header.sequence = sequence;
	command.header.timestamp_ms = timestamp;
	command.mode = mode;
	command.linear_velocity = velocity;
	command.duration_ms = duration;
	return command;
}

static wheel_odometry_message_t test_odometry(uint32_t sequence, uint32_t timestamp,
					      int32_t count)
{
	wheel_odometry_message_t message = {0};
	message.header.type = VEHICLE_MESSAGE_WHEEL_ODOMETRY;
	message.header.version = VEHICLE_MESSAGE_VERSION;
	message.header.sequence = sequence;
	message.header.timestamp_ms = timestamp;
	message.left_count = count;
	message.right_count = count;
	message.valid = 1U;
	return message;
}

static BOOL send_command(const motion_command_message_t *command)
{
	return tk_snd_mbf(g_mbf_motion_commands, command, (INT)sizeof(*command), TMO_POL) == E_OK;
}

static BOOL send_odometry(const wheel_odometry_message_t *message)
{
	return tk_snd_mbf(g_mbf_motion_odometry, message, (INT)sizeof(*message), TMO_POL) == E_OK;
}

static BOOL drain_test_buffers(void)
{
	union {
		motion_command_message_t command;
		wheel_odometry_message_t odometry;
	} message;
	INT result;

	do {
		result = tk_rcv_mbf(g_mbf_motion_commands, &message, TMO_POL);
	} while (result > 0);
	if (result != E_TMOUT) return FALSE;
	return discard_odometry() == 0U;
}

static const motion_task_config_t test_config = {
	{{{2500U, 20.0f}, {2500U, 20.0f}, 150U, 250U},
	 {2.0f, 0.0f, 0.0f, 10.0f, 20U, 60U}},
	10U, 100U, 500U, 20U
};

static BOOL simulation_flow_test(void)
{
	motion_task_state_t state = {0};
	motion_command_message_t command;
	wheel_odometry_message_t odometry;
	BOOL ok;

	state.last_clock_ms = 100U;
	odometry = test_odometry(20U, 99U, 0);
	command = test_command(1U, 100U, VEHICLE_MOTION_VELOCITY, 10, 300U);
	ok = send_odometry(&odometry) && send_command(&command);
	ok = run_cycle(&state, &test_config, 100U) == 0U && state.active &&
	     !state.control.have_output && ok;
	odometry = test_odometry(21U, 101U, 0);
	ok = send_odometry(&odometry) && run_cycle(&state, &test_config, 100U) == 0U &&
	     state.have_pending && ok;
	ok = run_cycle(&state, &test_config, 101U) == 0U && !state.have_pending &&
	     state.control.feedback.have_baseline && ok;
	odometry = test_odometry(22U, 201U, 125);
	ok = send_odometry(&odometry) && run_cycle(&state, &test_config, 201U) == 0U &&
	     state.control.have_output && state.control.output.left_pwm_percent == 20U && ok;
	command = test_command(2U, 202U, VEHICLE_MOTION_VELOCITY, 10, 300U);
	ok = send_command(&command) && run_cycle(&state, &test_config, 202U) == 0U &&
	     state.issued_ms == 202U && state.control.have_output && ok;
	command = test_command(3U, 502U, VEHICLE_MOTION_VELOCITY, 10, 300U);
	ok = send_command(&command) && run_cycle(&state, &test_config, 502U) == 0U &&
	     !state.active && ok;
	command = test_command(4U, 503U, VEHICLE_MOTION_VELOCITY, -10, 300U);
	ok = send_command(&command) && run_cycle(&state, &test_config, 503U) == 0U &&
	     state.active && state.control.direction == -1 && ok;
	command = test_command(5U, 504U, VEHICLE_MOTION_STOP, 0, 0U);
	ok = send_command(&command) && ok;
	command = test_command(6U, 504U, VEHICLE_MOTION_VELOCITY, 10, 300U);
	ok = send_command(&command) && run_cycle(&state, &test_config, 504U) == 0U &&
	     !state.active && ok;
	command = test_command(7U, 504U, VEHICLE_MOTION_VELOCITY, 10, 300U);
	ok = send_command(&command) && run_cycle(&state, &test_config, 505U) == 0U &&
	     !state.active && ok;
	command = test_command(8U, 505U, VEHICLE_MOTION_VELOCITY, 10, 300U);
	ok = send_command(&command) && run_cycle(&state, &test_config, 505U) == 0U &&
	     state.active && ok;
	stop_motion(&state);
	return drain_test_buffers() && ok;
}

static BOOL simulation_fault_test(void)
{
	motion_task_state_t state = {0};
	motion_command_message_t command;
	wheel_odometry_message_t odometry;
	BOOL ok;

	state.last_clock_ms = 100U;
	command = test_command(1U, 100U, VEHICLE_MOTION_ARM, 0, 0U);
	ok = send_command(&command) &&
	     run_cycle(&state, &test_config, 100U) ==
	     VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_MOTION_COMMAND;
	command = test_command(1U, 100U, VEHICLE_MOTION_VELOCITY, 10, 100U);
	command.angular_velocity = 1;
	ok = send_command(&command) &&
	     run_cycle(&state, &test_config, 100U) == VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_MOTION_COMMAND && ok;
	command = test_command(1U, 99U, VEHICLE_MOTION_VELOCITY, 10, 1U);
	ok = send_command(&command) &&
	     run_cycle(&state, &test_config, 100U) == VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_MOTION_COMMAND && ok;
	command = test_command(1U, 101U, VEHICLE_MOTION_VELOCITY, 10, 100U);
	ok = send_command(&command) &&
	     run_cycle(&state, &test_config, 100U) == VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_MOTION_COMMAND && ok;
	command = test_command(1U, 100U, VEHICLE_MOTION_VELOCITY, 21, 100U);
	ok = send_command(&command) &&
	     run_cycle(&state, &test_config, 100U) == VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_MOTION_COMMAND && ok;
	command = test_command(1U, 100U, VEHICLE_MOTION_VELOCITY, 10, 300U);
	ok = send_command(&command) && run_cycle(&state, &test_config, 100U) == 0U && ok;
	command = test_command(3U, 101U, VEHICLE_MOTION_VELOCITY, 10, 300U);
	ok = send_command(&command) &&
	     run_cycle(&state, &test_config, 101U) == VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_MOTION_COMMAND && ok;
	command = test_command(9U, 0U, VEHICLE_MOTION_EMERGENCY_STOP, 0, 0U);
	ok = send_command(&command) &&
	     run_cycle(&state, &test_config, 101U) == VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_MOTION_EMERGENCY &&
	     !state.active && ok;
	state = (motion_task_state_t){0};
	state.last_clock_ms = 100U;
	command = test_command(1U, 100U, VEHICLE_MOTION_VELOCITY, 10, 300U);
	ok = send_command(&command) && run_cycle(&state, &test_config, 100U) == 0U && ok;
	odometry = test_odometry(1U, 100U, 0);
	ok = send_odometry(&odometry) && run_cycle(&state, &test_config, 100U) == 0U && ok;
	odometry = test_odometry(3U, 101U, 0);
	ok = send_odometry(&odometry) &&
	     run_cycle(&state, &test_config, 101U) == VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_MOTION_ODOMETRY && ok;
	stop_motion(&state);
	return drain_test_buffers() && ok;
}

static BOOL simulation_deadline_test(void)
{
	motion_task_state_t state = {0};
	motion_command_message_t command;
	wheel_odometry_message_t odometry;
	BOOL ok;

	state.last_clock_ms = UINT32_MAX - 10U;
	command = test_command(UINT32_MAX, UINT32_MAX - 10U,
			       VEHICLE_MOTION_VELOCITY, 10, 100U);
	ok = send_command(&command) &&
	     run_cycle(&state, &test_config, UINT32_MAX - 10U) == 0U && state.active;
	odometry = test_odometry(UINT32_MAX, UINT32_MAX - 10U, 0);
	ok = send_odometry(&odometry) &&
	     run_cycle(&state, &test_config, UINT32_MAX - 10U) == 0U && ok;
	odometry = test_odometry(0U, 39U, 62);
	ok = send_odometry(&odometry) && run_cycle(&state, &test_config, 39U) == 0U &&
	     state.control.have_output && ok;
	ok = run_cycle(&state, &test_config, 88U) == 0U && state.active && ok;
	ok = run_cycle(&state, &test_config, 89U) == 0U && !state.active &&
	     state.control.output.left_pwm_percent == 0U && ok;
	command = test_command(0U, 0U, VEHICLE_MOTION_VELOCITY, 10, 500U);
	ok = send_command(&command) &&
	     run_cycle(&state, &test_config, 101U) == VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_MOTION_COMMAND && ok;
	return drain_test_buffers() && ok;
}

static BOOL simulation_no_status_test(void)
{
	motion_task_state_t state = {0};
	motion_command_message_t command;
	motion_status_message_t status;
	BOOL ok;

	state.last_clock_ms = 100U;
	ok = run_cycle(&state, &test_config, 100U) == 0U &&
	     tk_rcv_mbf(g_mbf_motion_status, &status, TMO_POL) == E_TMOUT;
	command = test_command(1U, 100U, VEHICLE_MOTION_VELOCITY, 10, 300U);
	ok = send_command(&command) && run_cycle(&state, &test_config, 100U) == 0U &&
	     state.active && tk_rcv_mbf(g_mbf_motion_status, &status, TMO_POL) == E_TMOUT && ok;
	command = test_command(2U, 101U, VEHICLE_MOTION_STOP, 0, 0U);
	ok = send_command(&command) && run_cycle(&state, &test_config, 101U) == 0U &&
	     !state.active && tk_rcv_mbf(g_mbf_motion_status, &status, TMO_POL) == E_TMOUT && ok;
	stop_motion(&state);
	return drain_test_buffers() && ok;
}

static BOOL actuator_mapping_test(void)
{
	return motor_command(40U, 1, MOTION_LEFT_POLARITY) == 40 &&
	       motor_command(40U, 1, MOTION_RIGHT_POLARITY) == -40 &&
	       motor_command(40U, -1, MOTION_LEFT_POLARITY) == -40 &&
	       motor_command(40U, -1, MOTION_RIGHT_POLARITY) == 40;
}

static BOOL status_format_test(void)
{
	motion_task_state_t state = {0};
	motion_status_message_t status;

	state.active = true;
	state.motor_1_pwm = 40;
	state.motor_2_pwm = -40;
	make_motion_status_message(&state, 3U, 125U, &status);
	if (status.header.type != VEHICLE_MESSAGE_MOTION_STATUS ||
	    status.header.version != VEHICLE_MESSAGE_VERSION ||
	    status.header.sequence != 3U || status.header.timestamp_ms != 125U ||
	    status.active != 1U || status.faulted != 0U || status.fault_code != 0U ||
	    status.motor_1_pwm != 40 || status.motor_2_pwm != -40)
		return false;
	state.faulted = true;
	state.fault_code = VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_MOTION_ACTUATOR;
	make_motion_status_message(&state, 4U, 225U, &status);
	return status.faulted == 1U &&
	       status.fault_code == VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_MOTION_ACTUATOR &&
	       status.motor_1_pwm == 40 && status.motor_2_pwm == -40;
}

BOOL motion_task_test(void)
{
	BOOL flow_ok;
	BOOL fault_ok;
	BOOL deadline_ok;
	BOOL status_ok;
	BOOL mapping_ok;
	BOOL status_format_ok;
	TEST_INIT();

	flow_ok = simulation_flow_test();
	fault_ok = simulation_fault_test();
	deadline_ok = simulation_deadline_test();
	status_ok = simulation_no_status_test();
	mapping_ok = actuator_mapping_test();
	status_format_ok = status_format_test();
	TEST_CHECK("MOT-U07", flow_ok, "simulation command lifetime, feedback and stop precedence");
	TEST_CHECK("MOT-U08", fault_ok, "invalid commands, emergency and odometry faults");
	TEST_CHECK("MOT-U09", deadline_ok, "timestamp expiry and clock wrap");
	TEST_CHECK("MOT-U10", status_ok, "simulation does not publish motion status");
	TEST_CHECK("MOT-U11", mapping_ok, "forward and reverse commands preserve source motor polarity");
	TEST_CHECK("MOT-U12", status_format_ok, "hardware status reports commanded PWM and latched faults");
	TEST_SUMMARY("motion task simulation");
	return flow_ok && fault_ok && deadline_ok && status_ok && mapping_ok &&
	       status_format_ok;
}
