#ifndef MOTION_TASK_H
#define MOTION_TASK_H

#include <tk/tkernel.h>
#include "motion_control.h"

typedef struct {
	motion_control_config_t control;
	RELTIM period_ms;
	uint32_t command_max_age_ms;
	uint16_t max_duration_ms;
	uint16_t max_linear_speed_cm_s;
} motion_task_config_t;

/* Simulation entry point: never accesses motors or publishes motion status. */
ER motion_task_start(const motion_task_config_t *config);

/* Hardware entry point: explicitly initializes the motor driver in its
 * braked/emergency-latched state. The controller must send a fresh ARM command
 * after its safety checks, followed by a fresh VELOCITY command. This is not
 * called by application startup. */
ER motion_task_start_hardware(const motion_task_config_t *config);

/* Both modes consume the dedicated motion and odometry buffers and neither
 * publishes motion status. The hardware mode uses the original motor polarity
 * mapping and requires independent freshness supervision; no physical stop is
 * guaranteed if this task stalls.
 *
 * VELOCITY accepts straight motion in signed cm/s; angular velocity must be
 * zero. Its nonzero duration is measured from the message timestamp. Hardware
 * mode requires ARM while stopped before VELOCITY; STOP does not re-arm.
 * STOP takes precedence over movement in the same queue batch; EMERGENCY_STOP
 * is latched until restart. A changed speed or direction requires STOP first.
 * The controller must issue consecutive sequence numbers, supervise stale
 * data independently, and handle faults. */

#if APP_TEST
BOOL motion_task_test(void);
#endif

#endif
=== C:\Users\Yong Jun\school\INF2004-Embedded_Systems\project\INF2004_Project\app_program\subsystem2\motion\motor_driver.c ===
#include "../../app.h"
#include "motor_driver.h"

#include <tk/syslib.h>
#include <sys/sysdef.h>

#define MOTOR_1_A_PIN 8U
#define MOTOR_1_B_PIN 9U
#define MOTOR_2_A_PIN 10U
#define MOTOR_2_B_PIN 11U
#define MOTOR_PWM_FREQUENCY_HZ 20000U
#define MOTOR_REVERSE_BRAKE_US 50000U
#define MOTOR_PWM_DIVIDER 1U
#define MOTOR_PWM_DIV_REG(slice) \
	(PWM_BASE + PWM_CHx_DIV + ((slice) * 0x14U))
#define MOTOR_PWM_CC_REG(slice) \
	(PWM_BASE + PWM_CHx_CC + ((slice) * 0x14U))
#define MOTOR_PWM_SLICE(pin) (((pin) >> 1) & 0x07U)
#define MOTOR_CLOCK_HZ (TMCLK_MHz * 1000000U)

typedef struct {
	int8_t last_direction;
	bool braking;
	uint32_t brake_started_us;
} motor_state_t;

static const UINT s_motor_pins[4] = {
	MOTOR_1_A_PIN, MOTOR_1_B_PIN, MOTOR_2_A_PIN, MOTOR_2_B_PIN
};
static motor_state_t s_motor_states[2];
static ID s_owner_tid;
static uint16_t s_pwm_period_ticks;
static bool s_initialized;
static bool s_emergency_stopped = true;

static uint32_t timer_us(void)
{
	return in_w(TIMER_TIMELR);
}

static ER check_owner(void)
{
	if (s_owner_tid <= 0) return E_OBJ;
	return tk_get_tid() == s_owner_tid ? E_OK : E_CTX;
}

static bool valid_motor(motor_id_t motor)
{
	return motor == MOTOR_1 || motor == MOTOR_2;
}

static void write_pair(motor_id_t motor, uint16_t duty_a, uint16_t duty_b)
{
	UINT pin_a = motor == MOTOR_1 ? MOTOR_1_A_PIN : MOTOR_2_A_PIN;
	UINT slice = MOTOR_PWM_SLICE(pin_a);
	out_w(MOTOR_PWM_CC_REG(slice),
	      (UW)duty_a | ((UW)duty_b << 16));
}

static void brake(motor_id_t motor)
{
	motor_state_t *state = &s_motor_states[(UINT)motor];

	write_pair(motor, 0U, 0U);
	if (!state->braking) {
		state->braking = true;
		state->brake_started_us = timer_us();
	}
}

static ER safe_gpio_low(void)
{
	UINT i;
	ER result = E_OK;

	for (i = 0U; i < 4U; i++) {
		ER er = gpio_set_pin(s_motor_pins[i], GPIO_MODE_OUT);
		if (er < E_OK && result == E_OK) result = er;
		er = gpio_set_val(s_motor_pins[i], 0U);
		if (er < E_OK && result == E_OK) result = er;
	}
	return result;
}

static ER stop_pwm_slices(void)
{
	ER first = E_OK;
	ER er;

	er = pwm_set_enabled(MOTOR_1_A_PIN, FALSE);
	if (er < E_OK) first = er;
	er = pwm_set_enabled(MOTOR_2_A_PIN, FALSE);
	if (er < E_OK && first == E_OK) first = er;
	return first;
}

static ER initialization_failure(ER cause)
{
	ER cleanup;

	write_pair(MOTOR_1, 0U, 0U);
	write_pair(MOTOR_2, 0U, 0U);
	cleanup = stop_pwm_slices();
	if (cleanup < E_OK)
		tm_printf((UB*)"motor PWM cleanup failed: %d\n", (int)cleanup);
	cleanup = safe_gpio_low();
	if (cleanup < E_OK)
		tm_printf((UB*)"motor GPIO cleanup failed: %d\n", (int)cleanup);
	s_initialized = false;
	s_emergency_stopped = true;
	return cause;
}

ER motor_driver_init(void)
{
	ID tid = tk_get_tid();
	uint32_t period_ticks;
	UINT i;
	ER er;

	if (tid <= 0) return E_CTX;
	if (s_owner_tid > 0 && s_owner_tid != tid) return E_OBJ;
	if (s_initialized) return E_OBJ;
	s_owner_tid = tid;
	s_emergency_stopped = true;
	s_pwm_period_ticks = 0U;

	er = safe_gpio_low();
	if (er < E_OK) return initialization_failure(er);
	er = stop_pwm_slices();
	if (er < E_OK) return initialization_failure(er);

	period_ticks = (MOTOR_CLOCK_HZ + MOTOR_PWM_FREQUENCY_HZ - 1U) /
		       MOTOR_PWM_FREQUENCY_HZ;
	if (period_ticks < 2U || period_ticks > UINT16_MAX)
		return initialization_failure(E_SYS);
	s_pwm_period_ticks = (uint16_t)period_ticks;

	out_w(MOTOR_PWM_DIV_REG(MOTOR_PWM_SLICE(MOTOR_1_A_PIN)),
	      MOTOR_PWM_DIVIDER << 4);
	out_w(MOTOR_PWM_DIV_REG(MOTOR_PWM_SLICE(MOTOR_2_A_PIN)),
	      MOTOR_PWM_DIVIDER << 4);
	er = pwm_set_wrap(MOTOR_1_A_PIN, (UW)(s_pwm_period_ticks - 1U));
	if (er < E_OK) return initialization_failure(er);
	er = pwm_set_wrap(MOTOR_2_A_PIN, (UW)(s_pwm_period_ticks - 1U));
	if (er < E_OK) return initialization_failure(er);
	write_pair(MOTOR_1, 0U, 0U);
	write_pair(MOTOR_2, 0U, 0U);

	for (i = 0U; i < 4U; i++) {
		er = pwm_set_pin(s_motor_pins[i]);
		if (er < E_OK) return initialization_failure(er);
	}
	er = pwm_set_enabled(MOTOR_1_A_PIN, TRUE);
	if (er < E_OK) return initialization_failure(er);
	er = pwm_set_enabled(MOTOR_2_A_PIN, TRUE);
	if (er < E_OK) return initialization_failure(er);

	for (i = 0U; i < 2U; i++) {
		s_motor_states[i].last_direction = 0;
		s_motor_states[i].braking = true;
		s_motor_states[i].brake_started_us = timer_us();
	}
	s_initialized = true;
	return E_OK;
}

ER motor_set_speed(motor_id_t motor, int16_t speed_percent)
{
	motor_state_t *state;
	int8_t direction;
	uint16_t magnitude;
	uint16_t duty;
	ER er = check_owner();

	if (er < E_OK) return er;
	if (!s_initialized || s_emergency_stopped) return E_OBJ;
	if (!valid_motor(motor)) return E_PAR;
	if (speed_percent > 100) speed_percent = 100;
	else if (speed_percent < -100) speed_percent = -100;
	if (speed_percent == 0) {
		brake(motor);
		return E_OK;
	}

	state = &s_motor_states[(UINT)motor];
	direction = speed_percent > 0 ? 1 : -1;
	if (state->last_direction != 0 &&
	    direction != state->last_direction) {
		if (!state->braking) {
			brake(motor);
			return E_BUSY;
		}
		if (timer_us() - state->brake_started_us <
		    MOTOR_REVERSE_BRAKE_US)
			return E_BUSY;
	}

	magnitude = (uint16_t)(speed_percent < 0 ?
			      -speed_percent : speed_percent);
	duty = (uint16_t)(((uint32_t)magnitude * s_pwm_period_ticks) / 100U);
	if (direction > 0) write_pair(motor, duty, 0U);
	else write_pair(motor, 0U, duty);
	state->last_direction = direction;
	state->braking = false;
	return E_OK;
}

ER motor_brake_all(void)
{
	ER er = check_owner();

	if (er < E_OK) return er;
	if (!s_initialized) return E_OBJ;
	brake(MOTOR_1);
	brake(MOTOR_2);
	return E_OK;
}

ER motor_emergency_stop(void)
{
	ER er = check_owner();

	if (er < E_OK) return er;
	s_emergency_stopped = true;
	if (!s_initialized) return E_OBJ;
	brake(MOTOR_1);
	brake(MOTOR_2);
	return E_OK;
}

bool motor_is_emergency_stopped(void)
{
	return s_emergency_stopped;
}

ER motor_clear_emergency_stop(void)
{
	ER er = check_owner();

	if (er < E_OK) return er;
	if (!s_initialized) return E_OBJ;
	brake(MOTOR_1);
	brake(MOTOR_2);
	s_emergency_stopped = false;
	return E_OK;
}

#ifndef MOTOR_DRIVER_H
#define MOTOR_DRIVER_H

#include <stdbool.h>
#include <stdint.h>
#include <tk/tkernel.h>

typedef enum {
	MOTOR_1 = 0,
	MOTOR_2 = 1
} motor_id_t;

/* Call only from the motion task. Initialization leaves both motors braked
 * and emergency-stop latched; all driver calls require the initializing task. */
ER motor_driver_init(void);

/* Signed command from -100 to 100. Opposite direction is delayed by a brake
 * interval; repeat the command after E_BUSY. */
ER motor_set_speed(motor_id_t motor, int16_t speed_percent);

ER motor_brake_all(void);
ER motor_emergency_stop(void);
bool motor_is_emergency_stopped(void);

/* Clearing does not restart the motors; the motion task must issue a new
 * command after the controller authorizes re-arming. */
ER motor_clear_emergency_stop(void);

#endif
=== C:\Users\Yong Jun\school\INF2004-Embedded_Systems\project\INF2004_Project\app_program\subsystem2\motion\pid_controller.c ===

#include "pid_controller.h"

#include <math.h>
#include <stddef.h>

bool pid_init(pid_controller_t *pid,
              float kp,
              float ki,
              float kd,
              float output_min,
              float output_max)
{
    if (pid == NULL || !isfinite(kp) || !isfinite(ki) || !isfinite(kd) ||
        !isfinite(output_min) || !isfinite(output_max))
    {
        return false;
    }

    pid->kp = kp;
    pid->ki = ki;
    pid->kd = kd;

    if (output_min <= output_max)
    {
        pid->output_min = output_min;
        pid->output_max = output_max;
    }
    else
    {
        pid->output_min = output_max;
        pid->output_max = output_min;
    }

    return pid_reset(pid);
}

bool pid_reset(pid_controller_t *pid)
{
    if (pid == NULL)
    {
        return false;
    }

    pid->integral = 0.0f;
    pid->previous_error = 0.0f;
    pid->first_update = true;
    return true;
}

bool pid_update(pid_controller_t *pid,
                 float setpoint,
                 float measurement,
                 float dt_seconds,
                 float *result)
{
    if ((pid == NULL) ||
        (result == NULL) ||
        (!isfinite(pid->kp)) || (!isfinite(pid->ki)) || (!isfinite(pid->kd)) ||
        (!isfinite(pid->integral)) || (!isfinite(pid->previous_error)) ||
        (!isfinite(pid->output_min)) || (!isfinite(pid->output_max)) ||
        (pid->output_min > pid->output_max) ||
        (!isfinite(setpoint)) ||
        (!isfinite(measurement)) ||
        (!isfinite(dt_seconds)) ||
        (dt_seconds <= 0.0f))
    {
        return false;
    }

    float error = setpoint - measurement;

    float proportional = pid->kp * error;

    float derivative = 0.0f;

    if (!pid->first_update)
    {
        derivative =
            pid->kd *
            (error - pid->previous_error) /
            dt_seconds;
    }

    float candidate_integral =
        pid->integral + (error * dt_seconds);

    float candidate_output =
        proportional +
        (pid->ki * candidate_integral) +
        derivative;

    if (!isfinite(error) || !isfinite(proportional) ||
        !isfinite(derivative) || !isfinite(candidate_integral) ||
        !isfinite(candidate_output))
    {
        return false;
    }

    /*
     * Conditional integration:
     * Do not accumulate more integral error when
     * the output is saturated in the same direction.
     */
    bool allow_integration =
        ((candidate_output <= pid->output_max) &&
         (candidate_output >= pid->output_min)) ||
        ((candidate_output > pid->output_max) &&
         (error < 0.0f)) ||
        ((candidate_output < pid->output_min) &&
         (error > 0.0f));

    float output =
        proportional +
        (pid->ki * (allow_integration ? candidate_integral : pid->integral)) +
        derivative;

    if (!isfinite(output))
    {
        return false;
    }

    if (output > pid->output_max)
    {
        output = pid->output_max;
    }
    else if (output < pid->output_min)
    {
        output = pid->output_min;
    }

    if (allow_integration)
    {
        pid->integral = candidate_integral;
    }
    pid->previous_error = error;
    pid->first_update = false;

    *result = output;
    return true;
}
=== C:\Users\Yong Jun\school\INF2004-Embedded_Systems\project\INF2004_Project\app_program\subsystem2\motion\pid_controller.h ===

#ifndef PID_CONTROLLER_H
#define PID_CONTROLLER_H

#include <stdbool.h>

typedef struct
{
    float kp;
    float ki;
    float kd;

    float integral;
    float previous_error;

    float output_min;
    float output_max;

    bool first_update;
} pid_controller_t;

/* Initialise a PID controller with finite gains and output limits. */
bool pid_init(pid_controller_t *pid,
              float kp,
              float ki,
              float kd,
              float output_min,
              float output_max);

/* False means invalid input or arithmetic; state and output are unchanged. */
bool pid_update(pid_controller_t *pid,
                 float setpoint,
                 float measurement,
                 float dt_seconds,
                 float *output);

/* Reset accumulated state when stopping or restarting. */
bool pid_reset(pid_controller_t *pid);

#endif

#include "wheel_speed_control.h"

#include <math.h>
#include <stddef.h>

/*
 * Buddy 2 - Dual Wheel PID Speed Controller
 *
 * Produces unsigned PWM magnitudes.
 * Motor directions and emergency stopping remain
 * the responsibility of the motion-control task.
 */

#define MAX_CONTROL_INTERVAL_SECONDS 0.25f

static bool valid_config(
    const wheel_speed_config_t *config)
{
    if (config == NULL)
    {
        return false;
    }

    if (!isfinite(config->kp) ||
        !isfinite(config->ki) ||
        !isfinite(config->kd) ||
        !isfinite(config->target_speed_cm_s))
    {
        return false;
    }

    if ((config->kp < 0.0f) ||
        (config->ki < 0.0f) ||
        (config->kd < 0.0f) ||
        (config->target_speed_cm_s <= 0.0f))
    {
        return false;
    }

    if ((config->base_pwm_percent == 0U) ||
        (config->max_pwm_percent == 0U) ||
        (config->max_pwm_percent > 100U) ||
        (config->base_pwm_percent >
         config->max_pwm_percent))
    {
        return false;
    }

    return true;
}

static uint8_t calculate_pwm(
    float base_pwm,
    float correction,
    uint8_t maximum)
{
    float command = base_pwm + correction;

    if (command < 0.0f)
    {
        command = 0.0f;
    }

    if (command > (float)maximum)
    {
        command = (float)maximum;
    }

    return (uint8_t)(command + 0.5f);
}

bool wheel_speed_control_init(
    wheel_speed_control_t *controller,
    const wheel_speed_config_t *config)
{
    if (controller == NULL)
    {
        return false;
    }

    controller->initialized = false;

    if (!valid_config(config))
    {
        return false;
    }

    controller->config = *config;

    /*
     * Limit PID correction so base + correction
     * always remains within the configured
     * motor PWM range.
     */
    float minimum_correction =
        -(float)config->base_pwm_percent;

    float maximum_correction =
        (float)config->max_pwm_percent -
        (float)config->base_pwm_percent;

    if (!pid_init(
        &controller->left_pid,
        config->kp,
        config->ki,
        config->kd,
        minimum_correction,
        maximum_correction))
    {
        return false;
    }

    if (!pid_init(
        &controller->right_pid,
        config->kp,
        config->ki,
        config->kd,
        minimum_correction,
        maximum_correction))
    {
        return false;
    }

    controller->initialized = true;

    return true;
}

bool wheel_speed_control_update(
    wheel_speed_control_t *controller,
    float left_speed_cm_s,
    float right_speed_cm_s,
    float dt_seconds,
    wheel_speed_output_t *output)
{
    if (output == NULL)
    {
        if (controller != NULL && controller->initialized)
        {
            (void)wheel_speed_control_reset(controller);
        }
        return false;
    }

    /* Safe result whenever an update fails. */
    output->left_pwm_percent = 0U;
    output->right_pwm_percent = 0U;

    if ((controller == NULL) ||
        (!controller->initialized))
    {
        return false;
    }

    if (!isfinite(left_speed_cm_s) ||
        !isfinite(right_speed_cm_s) ||
        !isfinite(dt_seconds))
    {
        (void)wheel_speed_control_reset(controller);
        return false;
    }

    if ((left_speed_cm_s < 0.0f) ||
        (right_speed_cm_s < 0.0f) ||
        (dt_seconds <= 0.0f) ||
        (dt_seconds > MAX_CONTROL_INTERVAL_SECONDS))
    {
        (void)wheel_speed_control_reset(controller);
        return false;
    }

    float target =
        controller->config.target_speed_cm_s;

    float left_correction;
    float right_correction;

    if (!pid_update(
        &controller->left_pid,
        target,
        left_speed_cm_s,
        dt_seconds,
        &left_correction) ||
        !pid_update(
        &controller->right_pid,
        target,
        right_speed_cm_s,
        dt_seconds,
        &right_correction))
    {
        (void)wheel_speed_control_reset(controller);
        return false;
    }

    float base =
        (float)controller->config.base_pwm_percent;

    uint8_t maximum =
        controller->config.max_pwm_percent;

    output->left_pwm_percent = calculate_pwm(
        base, left_correction, maximum);

    output->right_pwm_percent = calculate_pwm(
        base, right_correction, maximum);

    return true;
}

bool wheel_speed_control_reset(
    wheel_speed_control_t *controller)
{
    if ((controller == NULL) ||
        (!controller->initialized))
    {
        return false;
    }

    return pid_reset(&controller->left_pid) &&
           pid_reset(&controller->right_pid);
}

#ifndef WHEEL_SPEED_CONTROL_H
#define WHEEL_SPEED_CONTROL_H

#include <stdbool.h>
#include <stdint.h>

#include "pid_controller.h"

/*
 * Buddy 2 - Closed-Loop Wheel Speed Control
 *
 * Calculates PWM commands using two PID
 * controllers and measured wheel speeds.
 *
 * No direct motor hardware access.
 *
 * Target and measured speeds are expressed
 * as positive forward-equivalent magnitudes
 * in cm/s.
 *
 * Physical motor direction is handled by
 * the motion controller separately.
 */

typedef struct
{
    float kp;
    float ki;
    float kd;

    float target_speed_cm_s;

    uint8_t base_pwm_percent;
    uint8_t max_pwm_percent;
} wheel_speed_config_t;

typedef struct
{
    pid_controller_t left_pid;
    pid_controller_t right_pid;

    wheel_speed_config_t config;

    bool initialized;
} wheel_speed_control_t;

typedef struct
{
    uint8_t left_pwm_percent;
    uint8_t right_pwm_percent;
} wheel_speed_output_t;

/*
 * Initialise both wheel PID controllers.
 *
 * Rejects invalid or non-finite settings.
 */
bool wheel_speed_control_init(
    wheel_speed_control_t *controller,
    const wheel_speed_config_t *config);

/*
 * Compute corrected PWM values.
 *
 * Measured speeds must be valid
 * non-negative wheel-speed magnitudes.
 *
 * dt_seconds must be greater than zero.
 *
 * Returns false without issuing motor
 * commands if input validation fails. A failed
 * update zeros the output and resets both PIDs;
 * the caller must stop the motors.
 */
bool wheel_speed_control_update(
    wheel_speed_control_t *controller,
    float left_speed_cm_s,
    float right_speed_cm_s,
    float dt_seconds,
    wheel_speed_output_t *output);

/*
 * Reset both PID controllers.
 *
 * Call whenever movement stops,
 * changes direction or is restarted.
 */
bool wheel_speed_control_reset(
    wheel_speed_control_t *controller);

#endif

static BOOL correction_test(void)
{
	wheel_speed_control_t control;
	wheel_speed_config_t config = {2.0f, 0.0f, 0.0f, 10.0f, 20U, 60U};
	wheel_speed_output_t out;

	if (!wheel_speed_control_init(&control, &config) ||
	    !wheel_speed_control_update(&control, 10.0f, 8.0f, 0.1f, &out) ||
	    out.left_pwm_percent != 20U || out.right_pwm_percent != 24U ||
	    !wheel_speed_control_update(&control, 7.0f, 15.0f, 0.1f, &out) ||
	    out.left_pwm_percent != 26U || out.right_pwm_percent != 10U)
		return false;
	config.kp = 100.0f;
	return wheel_speed_control_init(&control, &config) &&
	       wheel_speed_control_update(&control, 0.0f, 100.0f, 0.1f, &out) &&
	       out.left_pwm_percent == 60U && out.right_pwm_percent == 0U;
}

static BOOL pid_state_test(void)
{
	wheel_speed_control_t control;
	wheel_speed_config_t config = {0.0f, 1.0f, 0.0f, 20.0f, 20U, 25U};
	wheel_speed_output_t out;

	if (!wheel_speed_control_init(&control, &config) ||
	    !wheel_speed_control_update(&control, 0.0f, 0.0f, 0.25f, &out) ||
	    out.left_pwm_percent != 25U ||
	    !wheel_speed_control_update(&control, 0.0f, 0.0f, 0.25f, &out) ||
	    out.left_pwm_percent != 25U ||
	    !wheel_speed_control_update(&control, 25.0f, 25.0f, 0.25f, &out) ||
	    out.left_pwm_percent != 24U) return false;

	config = (wheel_speed_config_t){0.0f, 0.0f, 1.0f, 10.0f, 20U, 60U};
	return wheel_speed_control_init(&control, &config) &&
	       wheel_speed_control_update(&control, 10.0f, 10.0f, 0.1f, &out) &&
	       out.left_pwm_percent == 20U &&
	       wheel_speed_control_update(&control, 9.0f, 10.0f, 0.1f, &out) &&
	       out.left_pwm_percent == 30U &&
	       wheel_speed_control_reset(&control) &&
	       wheel_speed_control_update(&control, 9.0f, 10.0f, 0.1f, &out) &&
	       out.left_pwm_percent == 20U;
}

static BOOL feedback_test(void)
{
	motion_feedback_t feedback;
	motion_feedback_config_t config = {{2500U, 20.0f}, {2500U, 20.0f}, 50U, 250U};
	motion_wheel_speeds_t speeds = {0U, 0.0f, 99.0f, 99.0f};
	wheel_odometry_message_t message = {0};
	BOOL ok;

	ok = motion_feedback_init(&feedback, &config);
	message.header.type = VEHICLE_MESSAGE_WHEEL_ODOMETRY;
	message.header.version = VEHICLE_MESSAGE_VERSION;
	message.header.sequence = 10U;
	message.header.timestamp_ms = 100U;
	message.valid = 1U;
	ok = motion_feedback_update(&feedback, &message, 100U, &speeds) ==
	     MOTION_FEEDBACK_BASELINE && speeds.left_cm_per_second == 99.0f && ok;
	message.header.sequence = 11U;
	message.header.timestamp_ms = 200U;
	message.left_count = 1250;
	message.right_count = -625;
	ok = motion_feedback_update(&feedback, &message, 210U, &speeds) ==
	     MOTION_FEEDBACK_READY &&
	     speeds.left_cm_per_second > 99.99f && speeds.left_cm_per_second < 100.01f &&
	     speeds.right_cm_per_second < -49.99f && speeds.right_cm_per_second > -50.01f &&
	     speeds.timestamp_ms == 200U && ok;
	message.header.sequence = 13U;
	message.header.timestamp_ms = 210U;
	ok = motion_feedback_update(&feedback, &message, 210U, &speeds) ==
	     MOTION_FEEDBACK_INVALID && feedback.last_sequence == 11U &&
	     speeds.timestamp_ms == 200U && ok;
	message.header.sequence = 12U;
	ok = motion_feedback_update(&feedback, &message, 209U, &speeds) ==
	     MOTION_FEEDBACK_INVALID &&
	     motion_feedback_update(&feedback, &message, 261U, &speeds) ==
	     MOTION_FEEDBACK_INVALID && ok;
	message.header.timestamp_ms = 451U;
	ok = motion_feedback_update(&feedback, &message, 451U, &speeds) ==
	     MOTION_FEEDBACK_INVALID && feedback.last_sequence == 11U && ok;
	message.valid = 0U;
	ok = motion_feedback_update(&feedback, &message, 451U, &speeds) ==
	     MOTION_FEEDBACK_INVALID && ok;

	ok = motion_feedback_init(&feedback, &config) && ok;
	message.valid = 1U;
	message.header.sequence = UINT32_MAX;
	message.header.timestamp_ms = UINT32_MAX - 50U;
	message.left_count = INT32_MAX - 1;
	message.right_count = INT32_MIN + 2;
	ok = motion_feedback_update(&feedback, &message, UINT32_MAX - 50U, &speeds) ==
	     MOTION_FEEDBACK_BASELINE && ok;
	message.header.sequence = 0U;
	message.header.timestamp_ms = 49U;
	message.left_count = INT32_MIN + 1;
	message.right_count = INT32_MAX;
	ok = motion_feedback_update(&feedback, &message, 49U, &speeds) ==
	     MOTION_FEEDBACK_READY && speeds.left_cm_per_second > 0.239f &&
	     speeds.left_cm_per_second < 0.241f && speeds.right_cm_per_second < -0.239f &&
	     speeds.right_cm_per_second > -0.241f && ok;
	message.header.sequence = 1U;
	message.header.timestamp_ms = 50U;
	message.left_count = 1;
	ok = motion_feedback_update(&feedback, &message, 50U, &speeds) ==
	     MOTION_FEEDBACK_INVALID && feedback.last_sequence == 0U && ok;
	config.right.counts_per_revolution = 0U;
	return !motion_feedback_init(&feedback, &config) && feedback.initialized && ok;
}

static BOOL invalid_test(void)
{
	wheel_speed_control_t control;
	wheel_speed_config_t config = {2.0f, 0.0f, 0.0f, 10.0f, 20U, 60U};
	wheel_speed_output_t out = {1U, 1U};

	config.max_pwm_percent = 101U;
	if (wheel_speed_control_init(&control, &config)) return false;
	config.max_pwm_percent = 60U;
	if (!wheel_speed_control_init(&control, &config) ||
	    !wheel_speed_control_update(&control, 10.0f, 10.0f, 0.1f, &out) ||
	    wheel_speed_control_update(&control, -1.0f, 10.0f, 0.1f, &out) ||
	    out.left_pwm_percent != 0U || out.right_pwm_percent != 0U ||
	    !control.left_pid.first_update || !control.right_pid.first_update ||
	    wheel_speed_control_update(&control, 10.0f, 10.0f, 0.251f, &out) ||
	    wheel_speed_control_update(&control, __builtin_huge_valf(), 10.0f, 0.1f, &out))
		return false;

	config.kp = __FLT_MAX__;
	return wheel_speed_control_init(&control, &config) &&
	       !wheel_speed_control_update(&control, 0.0f, 0.0f, 0.1f, &out) &&
	       out.left_pwm_percent == 0U && out.right_pwm_percent == 0U &&
	       control.left_pid.first_update && control.right_pid.first_update;
}

static wheel_odometry_message_t control_odometry(uint32_t sequence, uint32_t timestamp,
						  int32_t left, int32_t right)
{
	wheel_odometry_message_t message = {0};
	message.header.type = VEHICLE_MESSAGE_WHEEL_ODOMETRY;
	message.header.version = VEHICLE_MESSAGE_VERSION;
	message.header.sequence = sequence;
	message.header.timestamp_ms = timestamp;
	message.left_count = left;
	message.right_count = right;
	message.valid = 1U;
	return message;
}

static BOOL control_flow_test(void)
{
	motion_control_t control = {0};
	motion_control_config_t config = {
		{{2500U, 20.0f}, {2500U, 20.0f}, 150U, 250U},
		{2.0f, 0.0f, 0.0f, 10.0f, 20U, 60U}
	};
	wheel_odometry_message_t message = control_odometry(UINT32_MAX, UINT32_MAX - 50U, 0, 0);
	wheel_speed_output_t output = {99U, 99U};
	BOOL ok;

	ok = motion_control_start(&control, &config, 1, UINT32_MAX - 50U);
	ok = motion_control_step(&control, &message, UINT32_MAX - 50U, &output) ==
	     MOTION_CONTROL_WAITING && output.left_pwm_percent == 0U &&
	     output.right_pwm_percent == 0U && ok;
	message = control_odometry(0U, 49U, 125, 100);
	ok = motion_control_step(&control, &message, 49U, &output) == MOTION_CONTROL_READY &&
	     output.left_pwm_percent == 20U && output.right_pwm_percent == 24U && ok;
	ok = motion_control_step(&control, NULL, 50U, &output) == MOTION_CONTROL_READY &&
	     output.left_pwm_percent == 20U && output.right_pwm_percent == 24U && ok;
	motion_control_stop(&control);
	ok = !control.running && !control.have_output &&
	     control.output.left_pwm_percent == 0U && control.speed.left_pid.first_update && ok;

	ok = motion_control_start(&control, &config, -1, 200U) && ok;
	message = control_odometry(1U, 200U, 0, 0);
	ok = motion_control_step(&control, &message, 200U, &output) ==
	     MOTION_CONTROL_WAITING && ok;
	message = control_odometry(2U, 300U, -125, -125);
	ok = motion_control_step(&control, &message, 300U, &output) == MOTION_CONTROL_READY &&
	     output.left_pwm_percent == 20U && output.right_pwm_percent == 20U && ok;
	ok = motion_control_step(&control, NULL, 450U, &output) == MOTION_CONTROL_READY && ok;
	ok = motion_control_step(&control, NULL, 451U, &output) == MOTION_CONTROL_FAULT &&
	     output.left_pwm_percent == 0U && output.right_pwm_percent == 0U && ok;
	return ok;
}

static BOOL control_fault_test(void)
{
	motion_control_t control = {0};
	motion_control_config_t config = {
		{{2500U, 20.0f}, {2500U, 20.0f}, 150U, 250U},
		{2.0f, 0.0f, 0.0f, 10.0f, 20U, 60U}
	};
	wheel_odometry_message_t message;
	wheel_speed_output_t output;
	BOOL ok;

	ok = motion_control_start(&control, &config, 1, 100U);
	message = control_odometry(1U, 100U, 0, 0);
	ok = motion_control_step(&control, &message, 100U, &output) ==
	     MOTION_CONTROL_WAITING && ok;
	message = control_odometry(2U, 200U, -1, 1);
	ok = motion_control_step(&control, &message, 200U, &output) == MOTION_CONTROL_FAULT &&
	     control.faulted && !control.running && output.left_pwm_percent == 0U &&
	     output.right_pwm_percent == 0U && control.speed.left_pid.first_update && ok;
	message = control_odometry(3U, 201U, 0, 0);
	ok = motion_control_step(&control, &message, 201U, &output) == MOTION_CONTROL_FAULT && ok;

	ok = motion_control_start(&control, &config, 1, 300U) && ok;
	message = control_odometry(1U, 299U, 0, 0);
	ok = motion_control_step(&control, &message, 300U, &output) == MOTION_CONTROL_FAULT && ok;
	ok = motion_control_start(&control, &config, 1, 400U) && ok;
	message = control_odometry(1U, 400U, 0, 0);
	ok = motion_control_step(&control, &message, 400U, &output) == MOTION_CONTROL_WAITING && ok;
	message = control_odometry(3U, 450U, 0, 0);
	ok = motion_control_step(&control, &message, 450U, &output) == MOTION_CONTROL_FAULT && ok;
	ok = motion_control_start(&control, &config, 1, 500U) && ok;
	message = control_odometry(1U, 500U, 0, 0);
	ok = motion_control_step(&control, &message, 500U, &output) == MOTION_CONTROL_WAITING && ok;
	message = control_odometry(2U, 651U, 0, 0);
	ok = motion_control_step(&control, &message, 651U, &output) == MOTION_CONTROL_FAULT && ok;
	ok = motion_control_start(&control, &config, 1, 700U) && ok;
	ok = motion_control_step(&control, NULL, 699U, &output) == MOTION_CONTROL_FAULT && ok;
	config.feedback.max_interval_ms = 251U;
	ok = !motion_control_start(&control, &config, 1, 800U) &&
	     control.faulted && !control.running && control.output.left_pwm_percent == 0U && ok;
	return ok;
}

BOOL wheel_speed_test(void)
{
	BOOL correction_ok;
	BOOL pid_ok;
	BOOL invalid_ok;
	BOOL feedback_ok;
	BOOL control_ok;
	BOOL control_faults_ok;
	TEST_INIT();

	correction_ok = correction_test();
	pid_ok = pid_state_test();
	invalid_ok = invalid_test();
	feedback_ok = feedback_test();
	control_ok = control_flow_test();
	control_faults_ok = control_fault_test();
	TEST_CHECK("MOT-U01", correction_ok, "independent wheel correction and limits");
	TEST_CHECK("MOT-U02", pid_ok, "anti-windup, derivative and reset");
	TEST_CHECK("MOT-U03", invalid_ok, "invalid and overflow inputs fail safely");
	TEST_CHECK("MOT-U04", feedback_ok, "signed wheel feedback and stale inputs");
	TEST_CHECK("MOT-U05", control_ok, "straight control waits for fresh wheel feedback");
	TEST_CHECK("MOT-U06", control_faults_ok, "control faults latch and clear output");
	TEST_SUMMARY("wheel speed");
	return correction_ok && pid_ok && invalid_ok && feedback_ok &&
	       control_ok && control_faults_ok;
}

#ifndef WHEEL_SPEED_TEST_H
#define WHEEL_SPEED_TEST_H

=== C:\Users\Yong Jun\school\INF2004-Embedded_Systems\project\INF2004_Project\app_program\subsystem3\line_barcode\barcode_scanner.c ===
#include "barcode_scanner.h"

#include <string.h>

#define CODE39_PATTERN_COUNT        44U
#define CODE39_STAR_INDEX           43U   /* '*' start/stop is the 44th pattern */
#define CODE39_WIDE_ELEMENTS        3U    /* every Code 39 character has 3 wide */
#define DEFAULT_WIDE_RATIO_PERCENT  130U  /* narrowest wide vs widest narrow    */
#define DEFAULT_MAX_GAP_MODULES     4U    /* max inter-character gap            */

static const char code39_characters[] =
    "0123456789ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZ-. $/+%";

static const char *const code39_patterns[CODE39_PATTERN_COUNT] =
{
    "nnnwwnwnn", "wnnwnnnnw", "nnwwnnnnw", "wnwwnnnnn",
    "nnnwwnnnw", "wnnwwnnnn", "nnwwwnnnn", "nnnwnnwnw",
    "wnnwnnwnn", "nnwwnnwnn", "wnnnnwnnw", "nnwnnwnnw",
    "wnwnnwnnn", "nnnnwwnnw", "wnnnwwnnn", "nnwnwwnnn",
    "nnnnnwwnw", "wnnnnwwnn", "nnwnnwwnn", "nnnnwwwnn",
    "wnnnnnnww", "nnwnnnnww", "wnwnnnnwn", "nnnnwnnww",
    "wnnnwnnwn", "nnwnwnnwn", "nnnnnnwwn", "wnnnnnwwn",
    "nnwnnnwwn", "nnnnwnwwn", "wwnnnnnnw", "nwwnnnnnw",
    "wwwnnnnnn", "nwnnwnnnw", "wwnnwnnnn", "nwwnwnnnn",
    "nwnnnnwnw", "wwnnnnwnn", "nwwnnnwnn", "nwnwnwnnn",
    "nwnwnnnwn", "nwnnnwnwn", "nnnwnwnwn",
    "nwnnwnwnn"   /* '*' */
};

/* Must match the order of barcode_reject_t. */
static const char *const reject_names[BARCODE_REJECT_COUNT] =
{
    "OK", "GLITCH", "GAP", "DIRECTION", "SEPARATION",
    "NO_MATCH", "WRONG_ORIENTATION", "OVERFLOW"
};

const char *barcode_reject_name(barcode_reject_t reject)
{
    return ((unsigned)reject < (unsigned)BARCODE_REJECT_COUNT) ?
           reject_names[reject] : "?";
}

/* ---------------------------------------------------------------- config */

static bool config_valid(const barcode_scanner_config_t *config)
{
    return (config != NULL) &&
           (config->counts_per_module != 0U) &&
           (config->module_tolerance_percent <= 50U) &&
           ((config->wide_ratio_percent == 0U) ||
            (config->wide_ratio_percent >= 100U));
}

/*
 * Runs shorter than this are treated as noise. By default the limit is half
 * a module, scaled down by the tolerance (tolerance 0 -> 0.5 module,
 * tolerance 50 -> 0.25 module).
 */
uint32_t barcode_scanner_min_run_counts(const barcode_scanner_t *scanner)
{
    uint64_t limit;

    if (scanner->config.min_run_counts != 0U)
    {
        return scanner->config.min_run_counts;
    }
    limit = ((uint64_t)scanner->config.counts_per_module *
             (100U - scanner->config.module_tolerance_percent)) / 200U;
    return (limit < 1U) ? 1U : (uint32_t)limit;
}

uint32_t barcode_scanner_max_gap_counts(const barcode_scanner_t *scanner)
{
    uint64_t limit;

    if (scanner->config.max_gap_counts != 0U)
    {
        return scanner->config.max_gap_counts;
    }
    limit = (uint64_t)scanner->config.counts_per_module *
            DEFAULT_MAX_GAP_MODULES;
    return (limit > UINT32_MAX) ? UINT32_MAX : (uint32_t)limit;
}

uint8_t barcode_scanner_wide_ratio_percent(const barcode_scanner_t *scanner)
{
    return (scanner->config.wide_ratio_percent != 0U) ?
           scanner->config.wide_ratio_percent :
           (uint8_t)DEFAULT_WIDE_RATIO_PERCENT;
}

bool barcode_scanner_set_config(
    barcode_scanner_t *scanner,
    const barcode_scanner_config_t *config)
{
    if ((scanner == NULL) || !scanner->initialized || !config_valid(config))
    {
        return false;
    }
    scanner->config = *config;
    return true;
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------ arithmetic */

/* Wrap-safe difference of two free-running 32-bit encoder counters. */
static int32_t signed_count_delta(int32_t from, int32_t to)
{
    return (int32_t)((uint32_t)to - (uint32_t)from);
}

static uint32_t absolute_value(int32_t value)
{
    return (value < 0) ? (uint32_t)(0U - (uint32_t)value) : (uint32_t)value;
}

static void add_sample(barcode_sample_stats_t *stats, uint32_t value)
{
    if (stats->count == 0U)
    {
        stats->min = value;
        stats->max = value;
    }
    else
    {
        if (value < stats->min)
        {
            stats->min = value;
        }
        if (value > stats->max)
        {
            stats->max = value;
        }
    }
    stats->sum += value;
    stats->count++;
}

/* ---------------------------------------------------------------- events */

static void init_info(const barcode_scanner_t *scanner, barcode_char_info_t *info)
{
    memset(info, 0, sizeof(*info));
    info->run_count = scanner->run_count;
    memcpy(info->run_counts, scanner->run_counts, sizeof(info->run_counts));
    info->gap_counts = scanner->gap_counts;
}

static void publish_event(barcode_scanner_t *scanner, barcode_char_info_t *info)
{
    if (info->direction == 0)
    {
        info->direction = scanner->scan_direction;
    }
    info->in_frame = scanner->in_frame;

    scanner->stats.rejects[info->reject]++;
    if (scanner->event_pending)
    {
        scanner->stats.events_dropped++;
    }
    scanner->event = *info;
    scanner->event_pending = true;
}

/* -------------------------------------------------------------- decoding */

static void reset_character(barcode_scanner_t *scanner)
{
    memset(scanner->run_counts, 0, sizeof(scanner->run_counts));
    scanner->run_count = 0U;
}

/*
 * Drop the frame in progress, returning whether there was one. A finished
 * result that has not been collected yet keeps its payload and length.
 */
static bool abort_frame(barcode_scanner_t *scanner)
{
    bool was_in_frame = scanner->in_frame;

    scanner->in_frame = false;
    if (!scanner->result_ready)
    {
        scanner->payload_length = 0U;
    }
    return was_in_frame;
}

static int find_pattern(const char *pattern)
{
    uint8_t j;

    for (j = 0U; j < CODE39_PATTERN_COUNT; j++)
    {
        if (memcmp(pattern, code39_patterns[j], BARCODE_ELEMENTS) == 0)
        {
            return (int)j;
        }
    }
    return -1;
}

static char symbol_for_index(int index)
{
    return (index == (int)CODE39_STAR_INDEX) ? '*' : code39_characters[index];
}

/*
 * Every Code 39 character has exactly 3 wide elements out of 9, so the 3
 * widest runs are marked wide. This needs no speed or module calibration.
 * The character is rejected if the wide/narrow split is not clearly
 * separated. Fills the pattern, symbol and width fields of info.
 */
static barcode_reject_t classify_character(
    const barcode_scanner_t *scanner,
    bool reverse,
    barcode_char_info_t *info)
{
    bool is_wide[BARCODE_ELEMENTS] = { false };
    char reversed[BARCODE_PATTERN_LENGTH];
    uint32_t narrow_max = 0U;
    uint32_t wide_min = UINT32_MAX;
    int index;
    int alt_index;
    uint8_t i;
    uint8_t k;

    for (k = 0U; k < CODE39_WIDE_ELEMENTS; k++)
    {
        uint8_t best = BARCODE_ELEMENTS;
        for (i = 0U; i < BARCODE_ELEMENTS; i++)
        {
            if (!is_wide[i] &&
                ((best == BARCODE_ELEMENTS) ||
                 (scanner->run_counts[i] > scanner->run_counts[best])))
            {
                best = i;
            }
        }
        is_wide[best] = true;
    }

    for (i = 0U; i < BARCODE_ELEMENTS; i++)
    {
        uint32_t count = scanner->run_counts[i];

        if (is_wide[i])
        {
            if (count < wide_min)
            {
                wide_min = count;
            }
        }
        else if (count > narrow_max)
        {
            narrow_max = count;
        }
        info->raw_pattern[i] = is_wide[i] ? 'w' : 'n';
        reversed[i] = is_wide[BARCODE_ELEMENTS - 1U - i] ? 'w' : 'n';
    }
    info->raw_pattern[BARCODE_ELEMENTS] = '\0';
    reversed[BARCODE_ELEMENTS] = '\0';
    memcpy(info->pattern, reverse ? reversed : info->raw_pattern,
           BARCODE_PATTERN_LENGTH);
    info->narrow_max = narrow_max;
    info->wide_min = wide_min;

    if ((uint64_t)wide_min * 100U <
        (uint64_t)narrow_max * barcode_scanner_wide_ratio_percent(scanner))
    {
        return BARCODE_REJECT_SEPARATION;
    }

    index = find_pattern(info->pattern);
    alt_index = find_pattern(reverse ? info->raw_pattern : reversed);
    if (alt_index >= 0)
    {
        info->alt_symbol = symbol_for_index(alt_index);
    }
    if (index >= 0)
    {
        info->symbol = symbol_for_index(index);
        return BARCODE_REJECT_NONE;
    }
    return (alt_index >= 0) ? BARCODE_REJECT_WRONG_ORIENTATION :
                              BARCODE_REJECT_NO_MATCH;
}

/* Returns false if the payload buffer overflowed (the frame is dropped). */
static bool accept_character(barcode_scanner_t *scanner, char character)
{
    if (character == '*')
    {
        if (!scanner->in_frame)
        {
            /* Do not start a new frame while a result is waiting to be taken. */
            if (!scanner->result_ready)
            {
                scanner->in_frame = true;
                scanner->payload_length = 0U;
            }
        }
        else if (scanner->payload_length > 0U)
        {
            if (scanner->scan_direction < 0)
            {
                uint8_t i;
                uint8_t half_length = scanner->payload_length / 2U;

                for (i = 0U; i < half_length; i++)
                {
                    uint8_t opposite = (uint8_t)(
                        scanner->payload_length - 1U - i);
                    uint8_t value = scanner->payload[i];
                    scanner->payload[i] = scanner->payload[opposite];
                    scanner->payload[opposite] = value;
                }
            }
            scanner->result_ready = true;
            scanner->in_frame = false;
            scanner->stats.frames_ok++;
        }
        return true;
    }

    if (scanner->in_frame)
    {
        if (scanner->payload_length < BARCODE_MAX_PAYLOAD)
        {
            scanner->payload[scanner->payload_length++] = (uint8_t)character;
        }
        else
        {
            (void)abort_frame(scanner);
            return false;
        }
    }
    return true;
}

static void update_ok_stats(
    barcode_scanner_t *scanner,
    const barcode_char_info_t *info,
    bool was_in_frame)
{
    uint8_t i;

    for (i = 0U; i < BARCODE_ELEMENTS; i++)
    {
        add_sample((info->raw_pattern[i] == 'w') ? &scanner->stats.wide :
                                                   &scanner->stats.narrow,
                   info->run_counts[i]);
    }
    /* The gap before the start '*' is the quiet zone, not a character gap. */
    if (was_in_frame && (info->gap_counts > scanner->stats.gap_ok_max))
    {
        scanner->stats.gap_ok_max = info->gap_counts;
    }
}

static void finish_character(barcode_scanner_t *scanner)
{
    barcode_char_info_t info;
    barcode_reject_t reject;
    bool was_in_frame = scanner->in_frame;

    init_info(scanner, &info);
    reject = classify_character(scanner, scanner->scan_direction < 0, &info);

    if (reject == BARCODE_REJECT_NONE)
    {
        update_ok_stats(scanner, &info, was_in_frame);
        if (!accept_character(scanner, info.symbol))
        {
            reject = BARCODE_REJECT_OVERFLOW;
            info.aborted_frame = true;
        }
    }
    else
    {
        info.aborted_frame = abort_frame(scanner);
    }

    info.reject = reject;
    publish_event(scanner, &info);
    reset_character(scanner);
}

/* White-to-black edge: a character starts here. Inside a frame, gap_counts
 * is the white gap since the previous character. */
static void start_character(
    barcode_scanner_t *scanner,
    int32_t count_delta,
    uint32_t gap_counts)
{
    int8_t direction = (count_delta < 0) ? -1 : 1;

    scanner->gap_counts = gap_counts;

    if (scanner->in_frame)
    {
        barcode_reject_t reject = BARCODE_REJECT_NONE;

        add_sample(&scanner->stats.gap, gap_counts);
        if (direction != scanner->scan_direction)
        {
            reject = BARCODE_REJECT_DIRECTION;
        }
        else if (gap_counts > barcode_scanner_max_gap_counts(scanner))
        {
            reject = BARCODE_REJECT_GAP;
        }

        if (reject != BARCODE_REJECT_NONE)
        {
            barcode_char_info_t info;

            init_info(scanner, &info);
            info.reject = reject;
            info.direction = direction;
            info.offending_counts = gap_counts;
            info.aborted_frame = abort_frame(scanner);
            publish_event(scanner, &info);
        }
    }

    if (!scanner->in_frame)
    {
        scanner->scan_direction = direction;
    }
    scanner->collecting = true;
    reset_character(scanner);
}

static void reject_run(barcode_scanner_t *scanner, barcode_reject_t reject,
                       uint32_t width_counts)
{
    barcode_char_info_t info;

    init_info(scanner, &info);
    info.reject = reject;
    if (reject == BARCODE_REJECT_DIRECTION)
    {
        info.direction = (int8_t)-scanner->scan_direction;
    }
    info.offending_counts = width_counts;
    info.aborted_frame = abort_frame(scanner);
    publish_event(scanner, &info);

    reset_character(scanner);
    scanner->collecting = false;
}

/* ------------------------------------------------------------ public API */

bool barcode_scanner_init(
    barcode_scanner_t *scanner,
    const barcode_scanner_config_t *config,
    bool initial_black,
    int32_t initial_encoder_count)
{
    if ((scanner == NULL) || !config_valid(config))
    {
        return false;
    }

    memset(scanner, 0, sizeof(*scanner));
    scanner->config = *config;
    scanner->initialized = true;
    scanner->last_black = initial_black;
    scanner->last_encoder_count = initial_encoder_count;
    /*
     * If we start on black, we are inside a bar of unknown length, so wait
     * for the next white-to-black edge instead of collecting a partial run.
     */
    scanner->collecting = false;
    scanner->scan_direction = 0;
    return true;
}

bool barcode_scanner_update(
    barcode_scanner_t *scanner,
    bool black,
    int32_t encoder_count)
{
    int32_t count_delta;
    uint32_t width_counts;

    if ((scanner == NULL) || !scanner->initialized)
    {
        return false;
    }
    if (black == scanner->last_black)
    {
        return true;
    }

    count_delta = signed_count_delta(scanner->last_encoder_count,
                                     encoder_count);
    width_counts = absolute_value(count_delta);

    scanner->edge.sequence++;
    scanner->edge.to_black = black;
    scanner->edge.delta = count_delta;
    scanner->edge.width = width_counts;

    if (!scanner->collecting)
    {
        if (black)
        {
            start_character(scanner, count_delta, width_counts);
        }
    }
    else if (width_counts < barcode_scanner_min_run_counts(scanner))
    {
        reject_run(scanner, BARCODE_REJECT_GLITCH, width_counts);
    }
    else if (((count_delta < 0) ? -1 : 1) != scanner->scan_direction)
    {
        reject_run(scanner, BARCODE_REJECT_DIRECTION, width_counts);
    }
    else
    {
        scanner->run_counts[scanner->run_count++] = width_counts;
        if (scanner->run_count == BARCODE_ELEMENTS)
        {
            /* The 9th element is a bar, so this is a black-to-white edge. */
            finish_character(scanner);
            scanner->collecting = false;
        }
    }

    scanner->last_black = black;
    scanner->last_encoder_count = encoder_count;
    return true;
}

bool barcode_scanner_take_result(
    barcode_scanner_t *scanner,
    uint8_t *payload,
    uint8_t payload_capacity,
    uint8_t *length)
{
    if ((scanner == NULL) || (payload == NULL) || (length == NULL) ||
        !scanner->result_ready ||
        (payload_capacity < scanner->payload_length))
    {
        return false;
    }

    memcpy(payload, scanner->payload, scanner->payload_length);
    *length = scanner->payload_length;
    scanner->result_ready = false;
    return true;
}

void barcode_scanner_reset(barcode_scanner_t *scanner)
{
    if (scanner == NULL)
    {
        return;
    }

    reset_character(scanner);
    scanner->collecting = false;
    scanner->in_frame = false;
    scanner->scan_direction = 0;
    scanner->payload_length = 0U;
    scanner->result_ready = false;
    scanner->gap_counts = 0U;
}

bool barcode_scanner_get_edge(
    const barcode_scanner_t *scanner,
    barcode_edge_t *edge)
{
    if ((scanner == NULL) || (edge == NULL) || (scanner->edge.sequence == 0U))
    {
        return false;
    }
    *edge = scanner->edge;
    return true;
}

bool barcode_scanner_take_event(
    barcode_scanner_t *scanner,
    barcode_char_info_t *info)
{
    if ((scanner == NULL) || (info == NULL) || !scanner->event_pending)
    {
        return false;
    }
    *info = scanner->event;
    scanner->event_pending = false;
    return true;
}

const barcode_stats_t *barcode_scanner_get_stats(
    const barcode_scanner_t *scanner)
{
    return (scanner != NULL) ? &scanner->stats : NULL;
}

void barcode_scanner_reset_stats(barcode_scanner_t *scanner)
{
    if (scanner != NULL)
    {
        memset(&scanner->stats, 0, sizeof(scanner->stats));
    }
}

bool barcode_scanner_suggest_config(
    const barcode_scanner_t *scanner,
    barcode_scanner_config_t *suggested)
{
    const barcode_stats_t *stats;
    uint32_t module;

    if ((scanner == NULL) || (suggested == NULL) || !scanner->initialized ||
        (scanner->stats.narrow.count == 0U))
    {
        return false;
    }

    stats = &scanner->stats;
    *suggested = scanner->config;

    module = (uint32_t)(stats->narrow.sum / stats->narrow.count);
    suggested->counts_per_module = (module < 1U) ? 1U : module;

    suggested->min_run_counts = stats->narrow.min / 2U;
    if (suggested->min_run_counts < 1U)
    {
        suggested->min_run_counts = 1U;
    }

    if (stats->gap_ok_max != 0U)
    {
        suggested->max_gap_counts = stats->gap_ok_max + (stats->gap_ok_max / 2U);
    }
    return true;
}

#ifndef BARCODE_SCANNER_H
#define BARCODE_SCANNER_H

#include <stdbool.h>
#include <stdint.h>

#define BARCODE_MAX_PAYLOAD     (32U)
#define BARCODE_ELEMENTS        (9U)
#define BARCODE_PATTERN_LENGTH  (BARCODE_ELEMENTS + 1U)

typedef enum
{
    BARCODE_REJECT_NONE = 0,
    BARCODE_REJECT_GLITCH,
    BARCODE_REJECT_GAP,
    BARCODE_REJECT_DIRECTION,
    BARCODE_REJECT_SEPARATION,
    BARCODE_REJECT_NO_MATCH,
    BARCODE_REJECT_WRONG_ORIENTATION,
    BARCODE_REJECT_OVERFLOW,
    BARCODE_REJECT_COUNT
} barcode_reject_t;

/* counts_per_module must be positive; tolerance must be 0..50. The last
 * three fields use derived defaults when zero; a nonzero ratio must be >=100. */
typedef struct
{
    uint32_t counts_per_module;
    uint8_t module_tolerance_percent;
    uint32_t min_run_counts;
    uint32_t max_gap_counts;
    uint8_t wide_ratio_percent;
} barcode_scanner_config_t;

typedef struct
{
    uint32_t sequence;
    bool to_black;
    int32_t delta;
    uint32_t width;
} barcode_edge_t;

typedef struct
{
    barcode_reject_t reject;
    char symbol;
    char alt_symbol;
    int8_t direction;
    bool in_frame;
    bool aborted_frame;
    uint8_t run_count;
    uint32_t run_counts[BARCODE_ELEMENTS];
    char raw_pattern[BARCODE_PATTERN_LENGTH];
    char pattern[BARCODE_PATTERN_LENGTH];
    uint32_t gap_counts;
    uint32_t offending_counts;
    uint32_t narrow_max;
    uint32_t wide_min;
} barcode_char_info_t;

typedef struct
{
    uint32_t count;
    uint32_t min;
    uint32_t max;
    uint64_t sum;
} barcode_sample_stats_t;

typedef struct
{
    uint32_t rejects[BARCODE_REJECT_COUNT];
    uint32_t frames_ok;
    uint32_t events_dropped;
    barcode_sample_stats_t narrow;
    barcode_sample_stats_t wide;
    barcode_sample_stats_t gap;
    uint32_t gap_ok_max;
} barcode_stats_t;

/* Owned by the line/barcode task; do not share this mutable state. */
typedef struct
{
    barcode_scanner_config_t config;
    bool initialized;
    bool last_black;
    bool collecting;
    bool in_frame;
    bool result_ready;
    int32_t last_encoder_count;
    int8_t scan_direction;
    uint32_t run_counts[BARCODE_ELEMENTS];
    uint8_t run_count;
    uint8_t payload[BARCODE_MAX_PAYLOAD];
    uint8_t payload_length;
    uint32_t gap_counts;
    barcode_edge_t edge;
    barcode_char_info_t event;
    bool event_pending;
    barcode_stats_t stats;
} barcode_scanner_t;

/* Feed colour samples with a signed, free-running 32-bit encoder count.
 * Direction changes are detectable only when reflected between colour edges. */
bool barcode_scanner_init(barcode_scanner_t *scanner,
                          const barcode_scanner_config_t *config,
                          bool initial_black, int32_t initial_encoder_count);
bool barcode_scanner_update(barcode_scanner_t *scanner, bool black,
                            int32_t encoder_count);
/* An undersized destination leaves the completed result available. */
bool barcode_scanner_take_result(barcode_scanner_t *scanner, uint8_t *payload,
                                 uint8_t payload_capacity, uint8_t *length);
/* Reset decoding state without clearing config or accumulated statistics. */
void barcode_scanner_reset(barcode_scanner_t *scanner);
bool barcode_scanner_set_config(barcode_scanner_t *scanner,
                                const barcode_scanner_config_t *config);
uint32_t barcode_scanner_min_run_counts(const barcode_scanner_t *scanner);
uint32_t barcode_scanner_max_gap_counts(const barcode_scanner_t *scanner);
uint8_t barcode_scanner_wide_ratio_percent(const barcode_scanner_t *scanner);
bool barcode_scanner_get_edge(const barcode_scanner_t *scanner,
                              barcode_edge_t *edge);
bool barcode_scanner_take_event(barcode_scanner_t *scanner,
                                barcode_char_info_t *info);
const barcode_stats_t *barcode_scanner_get_stats(const barcode_scanner_t *scanner);
void barcode_scanner_reset_stats(barcode_scanner_t *scanner);
bool barcode_scanner_suggest_config(const barcode_scanner_t *scanner,
                                    barcode_scanner_config_t *suggested);
const char *barcode_reject_name(barcode_reject_t reject);

=== C:\Users\Yong Jun\school\INF2004-Embedded_Systems\project\INF2004_Project\app_program\subsystem3\line_barcode\barcode_scanner_test.c ===
#include "../../app.h"
#include "barcode_scanner_test.h"

#if APP_TEST
#include "../../test_report.h"
#include "barcode_scanner.h"

static barcode_scanner_t scanner;
static const barcode_scanner_config_t config = {10U, 0U, 0U, 0U, 0U};

static bool feed_character(const char *pattern, int32_t *count, int direction)
{
	unsigned i;
	bool black = true;

	*count += direction * 10;
	if (!barcode_scanner_update(&scanner, black, *count)) return false;
	for (i = 0U; i < BARCODE_ELEMENTS; i++) {
		unsigned index = direction > 0 ? i : BARCODE_ELEMENTS - 1U - i;
		*count += direction * (pattern[index] == 'w' ? 20 : 10);
		black = !black;
		if (!barcode_scanner_update(&scanner, black, *count)) return false;
	}
	return true;
}

static bool frame_test(int direction)
{
	const char *star = "nwnnwnwnn";
	const char *a = "wnnnnwnnw";
	const char *b = "nnwnnwnnw";
	int32_t count = 0;
	uint8_t result[2];
	uint8_t length = 0;

	if (!barcode_scanner_init(&scanner, &config, false, count) ||
	    !feed_character(star, &count, direction) ||
	    !feed_character(direction > 0 ? a : b, &count, direction) ||
	    !feed_character(direction > 0 ? b : a, &count, direction) ||
	    !feed_character(star, &count, direction)) return false;
	if (barcode_scanner_take_result(&scanner, result, 1U, &length)) return false;
	if (!barcode_scanner_take_result(&scanner, result, sizeof(result), &length)) return false;
	return length == 2U && result[0] == 'A' && result[1] == 'B' &&
	       !barcode_scanner_take_result(&scanner, result, sizeof(result), &length);
}

static bool rejection_test(void)
{
	barcode_char_info_t event;
	barcode_scanner_config_t invalid = {0};
	int32_t count = 0;
	const char *star = "nwnnwnwnn";

	if (barcode_scanner_init(&scanner, &invalid, false, count)) return false;
	if (!barcode_scanner_init(&scanner, &config, false, count) ||
	    !feed_character(star, &count, 1) ||
	    !barcode_scanner_update(&scanner, true, count += 10) ||
	    !barcode_scanner_update(&scanner, false, count += 1) ||
	    !barcode_scanner_take_event(&scanner, &event) ||
	    event.reject != BARCODE_REJECT_GLITCH || !event.aborted_frame) return false;

	count = 0;
	if (!barcode_scanner_init(&scanner, &config, false, count) ||
	    !feed_character(star, &count, 1) ||
	    !barcode_scanner_update(&scanner, true, count += 41) ||
	    !barcode_scanner_take_event(&scanner, &event) ||
	    event.reject != BARCODE_REJECT_GAP || !event.aborted_frame) return false;

	count = 0;
	if (!barcode_scanner_init(&scanner, &config, false, count) ||
	    !feed_character(star, &count, 1) ||
	    !barcode_scanner_update(&scanner, true, count -= 10) ||
	    !barcode_scanner_take_event(&scanner, &event) ||
	    event.reject != BARCODE_REJECT_DIRECTION || !event.aborted_frame) return false;

	count = 0;
	if (!barcode_scanner_init(&scanner, &config, false, count) ||
	    !feed_character(star, &count, 1) ||
	    !barcode_scanner_update(&scanner, true, count += 10) ||
	    !barcode_scanner_update(&scanner, false, count += 10) ||
	    !barcode_scanner_update(&scanner, true, count -= 10) ||
	    !barcode_scanner_take_event(&scanner, &event) ||
	    event.reject != BARCODE_REJECT_DIRECTION || !event.aborted_frame) return false;

	count = 0;
	return barcode_scanner_init(&scanner, &config, false, count) &&
	       feed_character("nnnnnnnnn", &count, 1) &&
	       barcode_scanner_take_event(&scanner, &event) &&
	       event.reject == BARCODE_REJECT_SEPARATION;
}

BOOL barcode_scanner_test(void)
{
	BOOL forward_ok;
	BOOL reverse_ok;
	BOOL rejection_ok;
	TEST_INIT();

	forward_ok = frame_test(1);
	reverse_ok = frame_test(-1);
	rejection_ok = rejection_test();
	TEST_CHECK("BAR-U01", forward_ok, "forward frame and result capacity");
	TEST_CHECK("BAR-U02", reverse_ok, "reverse frame and payload order");
	TEST_CHECK("BAR-U03", rejection_ok, "invalid config and bad scan rejection");
	TEST_SUMMARY("barcode");
	return forward_ok && reverse_ok && rejection_ok;
}

#include "../../app.h"
#include "line_adc_reader.h"

#include <sys/sysdef.h>

#define LINE_ADC_OVERSAMPLES 8U
#define LINE_ADC_MAX_VALUE 4095U
#define LINE_ADC_FIRST_PIN 26U
#define LINE_ADC_BLACK_THRESHOLD 1000U

static line_adc_reader_t s_task_reader;
static line_adc_config_t s_task_reader_config;
static bool s_task_start_attempted;

static ER initialize_task_reader(void *context)
{
	return line_adc_reader_init(context, &s_task_reader_config);
}

static bool valid_channel(const line_adc_channel_t *channel)
{
	return channel->channel <= 2U && channel->threshold <= LINE_ADC_MAX_VALUE;
}

static bool valid_config(const line_adc_config_t *config)
{
	return config != NULL && valid_channel(&config->barcode) &&
	       valid_channel(&config->center) && valid_channel(&config->right) &&
	       config->barcode.channel != config->center.channel &&
	       config->barcode.channel != config->right.channel &&
	       config->center.channel != config->right.channel &&
	       config->max_sample_span_ms > 0U &&
	       config->max_sample_span_ms <= INT32_MAX;
}

static bool is_black(const line_adc_channel_t *config, uint16_t raw)
{
	return config->black_above ? raw > config->threshold : raw < config->threshold;
}

static uint64_t clock_ms(const SYSTIM *time)
{
	return ((uint64_t)(uint32_t)time->hi << 32) | time->lo;
}

ER line_adc_reader_default_config(line_adc_config_t *config,
				  uint32_t max_sample_span_ms)
{
	line_adc_config_t imported = {
		{2U, LINE_ADC_BLACK_THRESHOLD, true},
		{1U, LINE_ADC_BLACK_THRESHOLD, true},
		{0U, LINE_ADC_BLACK_THRESHOLD, true},
		max_sample_span_ms
	};

	if (config == NULL || !valid_config(&imported)) return E_PAR;
	*config = imported;
	return E_OK;
}

ER line_adc_reader_init(line_adc_reader_t *reader, const line_adc_config_t *config)
{
	const line_adc_channel_t *channels[3];
	ID device;
	unsigned i;

	if (reader == NULL || !valid_config(config)) return E_PAR;
	if (reader->initialized) return E_OBJ;
	device = tk_opn_dev((const UB *)"adca", TD_READ | TD_EXCL);
	if (device < E_OK) return device;
	if (device == E_OK) return E_SYS;

	channels[0] = &config->barcode;
	channels[1] = &config->center;
	channels[2] = &config->right;
	for (i = 0U; i < 3U; i++) {
		UINT pin = LINE_ADC_FIRST_PIN + channels[i]->channel;
		out_w(GPIO_CTRL(pin), GPIO_CTRL_FUNCSEL_NULL);
		clr_w(GPIO(pin), GPIO_IE | GPIO_PUE | GPIO_PDE);
	}
	reader->config = *config;
	reader->device = device;
	reader->faulted = false;
	reader->initialized = true;
	return E_OK;
}

ER line_adc_reader_start_task(const line_adc_config_t *reader_config,
			      const line_barcode_task_config_t *task_config)
{
	line_barcode_task_config_t task;
	ER er;

	if (!valid_config(reader_config) || task_config == NULL) return E_PAR;
	if (s_task_start_attempted) return E_OBJ;
	s_task_reader_config = *reader_config;
	task = *task_config;
	task.initialize = initialize_task_reader;
	task.read = line_adc_reader_read;
	task.read_context = &s_task_reader;
	er = line_barcode_task_start(&task);
	if (er == E_OK) s_task_start_attempted = true;
	return er;
}

ER line_adc_reader_start_default_task(uint32_t max_sample_span_ms,
				      const line_barcode_task_config_t *task_config)
{
	line_adc_config_t reader_config;
	ER er = line_adc_reader_default_config(&reader_config, max_sample_span_ms);

	if (er != E_OK) return er;
	return line_adc_reader_start_task(&reader_config, task_config);
}

static ER read_channel(line_adc_reader_t *reader, const line_adc_channel_t *channel,
		       ir_reading_t *reading)
{
	UW sum = 0U;
	unsigned i;

	for (i = 0U; i < LINE_ADC_OVERSAMPLES; i++) {
		SZ actual = 0;
		ER er;
		reader->pending_value = 0U;
		er = tk_srea_dev(reader->device, channel->channel,
				 &reader->pending_value, 1, &actual);
		if (er != E_OK) {
			tm_printf((UB*)"line ADC read failed: channel=%d error=%d\n",
				  (int)channel->channel, (int)er);
			return er < E_OK ? er : E_IO;
		}
		if (actual != 1 || reader->pending_value > LINE_ADC_MAX_VALUE) return E_IO;
		sum += reader->pending_value;
	}
	reading->raw = (uint16_t)(sum / LINE_ADC_OVERSAMPLES);
	reading->black = is_black(channel, reading->raw);
	return E_OK;
}

ER line_adc_reader_read(void *context, ir_readings_t *readings,
			uint32_t *timestamp_ms)
{
	line_adc_reader_t *reader = context;
	ir_readings_t result;
	SYSTIM start, barcode_start, end;
	uint64_t start_ms, barcode_ms, end_ms;
	ER er;

	if (reader == NULL || readings == NULL || timestamp_ms == NULL) return E_PAR;
	if (!reader->initialized || reader->faulted) return E_OBJ;
	er = tk_get_otm(&start);
	if (er != E_OK) goto fault;
	er = read_channel(reader, &reader->config.center, &result.center);
	if (er != E_OK) goto fault;
	er = read_channel(reader, &reader->config.right, &result.right);
	if (er != E_OK) goto fault;
	er = tk_get_otm(&barcode_start);
	if (er != E_OK) goto fault;
	er = read_channel(reader, &reader->config.barcode, &result.barcode);
	if (er != E_OK) goto fault;
	er = tk_get_otm(&end);
	if (er != E_OK) goto fault;
	start_ms = clock_ms(&start);
	barcode_ms = clock_ms(&barcode_start);
	end_ms = clock_ms(&end);
	if (barcode_ms < start_ms || end_ms < barcode_ms) {
		tm_printf((UB*)"line ADC sampling clock moved backward\n");
		er = E_TMOUT;
		goto fault;
	}
	if (end_ms - start_ms > reader->config.max_sample_span_ms) {
		tm_printf((UB*)"line ADC sample span exceeded budget: limit=%u ms\n",
			  (unsigned)reader->config.max_sample_span_ms);
		er = E_TMOUT;
		goto fault;
	}
	*readings = result;
	*timestamp_ms = barcode_start.lo;
	return E_OK;

fault:
	reader->faulted = true;
	return er < E_OK ? er : E_IO;
}

ER line_adc_reader_close(line_adc_reader_t *reader)
{
	ER er;

	if (reader == NULL) return E_PAR;
	if (!reader->initialized || reader->faulted) return E_OBJ;
	er = tk_cls_dev(reader->device, 0U);
	if (er != E_OK) return er < E_OK ? er : E_IO;
	reader->initialized = false;
	reader->device = 0;
	return E_OK;
}

#if APP_TEST
#include "../../test_report.h"

BOOL line_adc_reader_test(void)
{
	line_adc_config_t config = {{2U, 1000U, true}, {1U, 2000U, false},
				    {0U, 3000U, true}, 50U};
	line_adc_config_t imported = {0};
	BOOL config_ok;
	BOOL threshold_ok;
	BOOL imported_ok;
	TEST_INIT();

	config_ok = valid_config(&config);
	config.right.channel = 1U;
	config_ok = config_ok && !valid_config(&config);
	config.right.channel = 3U;
	config_ok = config_ok && !valid_config(&config);
	config.right.channel = 0U;
	config.right.threshold = 4096U;
	config_ok = config_ok && !valid_config(&config);
	config.right.threshold = 3000U;

		       !is_black(&config.barcode, 1000U) &&

#ifndef LINE_ADC_READER_H
#define LINE_ADC_READER_H

#include <tk/tkernel.h>
#include "line_barcode_task.h"
#include "line_observation.h"

typedef struct {
	uint8_t channel;
	uint16_t threshold;
	bool black_above;
} line_adc_channel_t;

typedef struct {
	line_adc_channel_t barcode;
	line_adc_channel_t center;
	line_adc_channel_t right;
	uint32_t max_sample_span_ms;
} line_adc_config_t;

typedef struct {
	line_adc_config_t config;
	ID device;
	UW pending_value;
	bool initialized;
	bool faulted;
} line_adc_reader_t;

/* Import the original line/barcode ADC mapping and thresholds. The acquisition
 * span remains caller-selected because it depends on the chosen task period. */
ER line_adc_reader_default_config(line_adc_config_t *config,
				  uint32_t max_sample_span_ms);

/* Zero-initialize the reader and keep it alive until close or reboot. A
 * single owner must reserve the ADC and pins before calling init. Channels
 * 0..2 map to GP26..28; GP29 is prohibited. */
ER line_adc_reader_init(line_adc_reader_t *reader, const line_adc_config_t *config);

/* Starts the line/barcode task with a static-lifetime ADC reader. ADC
 * ownership is acquired inside that task; this function is not called by
 * default. */
ER line_adc_reader_start_task(const line_adc_config_t *reader_config,
			      const line_barcode_task_config_t *task_config);

/* Uses the imported sensor mapping/thresholds with a caller-selected
 * acquisition span, then starts the line/barcode task. */
ER line_adc_reader_start_default_task(uint32_t max_sample_span_ms,
				      const line_barcode_task_config_t *task_config);

/* Compatible with line_barcode_read_t. On error, outputs are unchanged and
 * the reader is latched faulted; its ADC ownership is retained until reboot. */
ER line_adc_reader_read(void *context, ir_readings_t *readings,
			uint32_t *timestamp_ms);

/* Close only an unfaulted reader that no task is using. Pins remain analog. */
ER line_adc_reader_close(line_adc_reader_t *reader);

#if APP_TEST
BOOL line_adc_reader_test(void);
#endif
