=== C:\Users\Yong Jun\school\INF2004-Embedded_Systems\project\INF2004_Project\app_program\subsystem3\line_barcode\line_barcode_task.c ===
#include "../../app.h"
#include "../../vehicle_buffers.h"
#include "line_barcode_task.h"

#define LINE_BARCODE_PRIORITY 10
#define LINE_FAULT_RETRY_MS 1000U

typedef struct {
	barcode_scanner_t scanner;
	barcode_scanner_config_t barcode_config;
	wheel_odometry_message_t pending;
	uint32_t start_ms;
	uint32_t max_age_ms;
	uint32_t last_odom_ms;
	uint32_t last_odom_sequence;
	uint32_t last_left;
	uint32_t last_right;
	uint32_t mean_count;
	int32_t half_count;
	uint32_t line_sequence;
	uint32_t barcode_sequence;
	uint32_t last_sample_ms;
	ER delivery_error;
	bool have_pending;
	bool have_odom;
	bool scanner_ready;
	bool have_sample;
} line_state_t;

static line_barcode_task_config_t s_config;
static line_state_t s_state;
static bool s_started;

static int32_t signed_bits(uint32_t bits)
{
	return bits <= INT32_MAX ? (int32_t)bits :
	       INT32_MIN + (int32_t)(bits - 0x80000000U);
}

static bool add_odometry(line_state_t *state, const wheel_odometry_message_t *message)
{
	uint32_t left = (uint32_t)message->left_count;
	uint32_t right = (uint32_t)message->right_count;

	if (message->header.type != VEHICLE_MESSAGE_WHEEL_ODOMETRY ||
	    message->header.version != VEHICLE_MESSAGE_VERSION || message->valid != 1U)
		return false;
	if (state->have_odom) {
		uint32_t ld = left - state->last_left;
		uint32_t rd = right - state->last_right;
		uint32_t elapsed = message->header.timestamp_ms - state->last_odom_ms;
		int64_t sum;
		int64_t whole;
		int32_t remainder;
		if (message->header.sequence - state->last_odom_sequence != 1U ||
		    elapsed == 0U || elapsed > INT32_MAX ||
		    ld == 0x80000000U || rd == 0x80000000U)
			return false;
		sum = (int64_t)signed_bits(ld) + signed_bits(rd) + state->half_count;
		whole = sum / 2;
		remainder = (int32_t)(sum % 2);
		if (remainder < 0) {
			whole--;
			remainder += 2;
		}
		state->mean_count += (uint32_t)whole;
		state->half_count = remainder;
	}
	state->last_left = left;
	state->last_right = right;
	state->last_odom_ms = message->header.timestamp_ms;
	state->last_odom_sequence = message->header.sequence;
	state->have_odom = true;
	return true;
}

/* A future-dated message is retained until a later sensor sample. */
static uint16_t receive_odometry(line_state_t *state, uint32_t timestamp_ms,
				 bool *updated)
{
	wheel_odometry_message_t message;
	INT received;

	*updated = false;
	for (;;) {
		uint32_t ahead;
		if (state->have_pending) {
			message = state->pending;
		} else {
			received = tk_rcv_mbf(g_mbf_line_odometry, &message, TMO_POL);
			if (received == E_TMOUT) break;
			if (received != (INT)sizeof(message))
				return VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_LINE_ODOMETRY;
		}
		if (message.header.type != VEHICLE_MESSAGE_WHEEL_ODOMETRY ||
		    message.header.version != VEHICLE_MESSAGE_VERSION || message.valid != 1U)
			return VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_LINE_ODOMETRY;
		ahead = message.header.timestamp_ms - timestamp_ms;
		if (ahead != 0U && ahead <= INT32_MAX) {
			state->pending = message;
			state->have_pending = true;
			break;
		}
		state->have_pending = false;
		if (timestamp_ms - message.header.timestamp_ms > state->max_age_ms ||
		    !add_odometry(state, &message))
			return VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_LINE_ODOMETRY;
		*updated = true;
	}
	if (state->have_odom) {
		if (timestamp_ms - state->last_odom_ms > state->max_age_ms)
			return VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_LINE_ODOMETRY;
	} else if (timestamp_ms - state->start_ms > state->max_age_ms) {
		return VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_LINE_ODOMETRY;
	}
	return 0U;
}

static uint16_t process_readings(line_state_t *state, const ir_readings_t *readings,
				 uint32_t timestamp_ms)
{
	line_observation_message_t line = {0};
	barcode_event_message_t barcode = {0};
	uint8_t position;
	bool odom_updated;
	uint16_t fault;
	ER er;

	if (state->have_sample) {
		uint32_t elapsed = timestamp_ms - state->last_sample_ms;
		if (elapsed == 0U || elapsed > INT32_MAX)
			return VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_LINE_CLOCK;
	}
	fault = receive_odometry(state, timestamp_ms, &odom_updated);
	if (fault != 0U) return fault;
	if (state->have_odom) {
		int32_t count = signed_bits(state->mean_count);
		if (!state->scanner_ready) {
			if (!barcode_scanner_init(&state->scanner, &state->barcode_config,
						  readings->barcode.black, count))
				return VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_LINE_BARCODE;
			state->scanner_ready = true;
		} else {
			if (readings->barcode.black != state->scanner.last_black) {
				uint32_t since_sample = state->last_odom_ms - state->last_sample_ms;
				if (!odom_updated || since_sample == 0U || since_sample > INT32_MAX)
					return VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_LINE_ODOMETRY;
			}
			if (!barcode_scanner_update(&state->scanner, readings->barcode.black, count))
				return VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_LINE_BARCODE;
		}
	}
	position = line_following_position(readings);
	line.header.type = VEHICLE_MESSAGE_LINE_OBSERVATION;
	line.header.version = VEHICLE_MESSAGE_VERSION;
	line.header.sequence = ++state->line_sequence;
	line.header.timestamp_ms = timestamp_ms;
	line.lateral_error = position == POS_LEFT ? -1 : position == POS_RIGHT ? 1 : 0;
	line.confidence = position == POS_LOST ? 0U : 1000U;
	line.line_detected = position != POS_LOST;
	line.intersection_detected = position == POS_JUNCTION;
	er = tk_snd_mbf(g_mbf_line_observations, &line, (INT)sizeof(line), TMO_POL);
	if (er != E_OK) {
		state->delivery_error = er;
		return VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_LINE_DELIVERY;
	}
	if (state->scanner_ready && state->scanner.result_ready) {
		uint8_t payload[BARCODE_MAX_PAYLOAD];
		uint8_t length;
		unsigned i;
		if (!barcode_scanner_take_result(&state->scanner, payload, sizeof(payload), &length) ||
		    length > sizeof(barcode.payload))
			return VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_LINE_BARCODE;
		barcode.header.type = VEHICLE_MESSAGE_BARCODE_EVENT;
		barcode.header.version = VEHICLE_MESSAGE_VERSION;
		barcode.header.sequence = ++state->barcode_sequence;
		barcode.header.timestamp_ms = timestamp_ms;
		for (i = 0U; i < length; i++) barcode.payload[i] = (char)payload[i];
		barcode.length = length;
		barcode.valid = 1U;
		er = tk_snd_mbf(g_mbf_barcode_events, &barcode, (INT)sizeof(barcode), TMO_POL);
		if (er != E_OK) {
			state->delivery_error = er;
			return VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_LINE_DELIVERY;
		}
	}
	state->last_sample_ms = timestamp_ms;
	state->have_sample = true;
	return 0U;
}

static void report_line_fault(uint16_t code, uint32_t timestamp_ms)
{
	fault_message_t fault = {0};
	bool sent = false;

	fault.header.type = VEHICLE_MESSAGE_FAULT;
	fault.header.version = VEHICLE_MESSAGE_VERSION;
	fault.header.sequence = 1U;
	fault.header.timestamp_ms = timestamp_ms;
	fault.source = code == VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_LINE_DELIVERY ||
		       code == VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_LINE_ODOMETRY ?
		       VEHICLE_FAULT_COMMUNICATION :
		       (code == VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_LINE_CLOCK ||
			code == VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_LINE_SCHEDULE ?
			VEHICLE_FAULT_SYSTEM : VEHICLE_FAULT_SENSOR);
	fault.code = code;
	fault.recoverable = 0U;
	tm_printf((UB*)"line/barcode stopped; fault code %u\n", (unsigned)code);
	for (;;) {
		ER er;
		if (!sent) {
			er = tk_snd_mbf(g_mbf_faults, &fault, (INT)sizeof(fault), TMO_POL);
			if (er == E_OK) sent = true;
			else tm_printf((UB*)"line/barcode fault delivery failed: %d\n", (int)er);
		}
		er = tk_dly_tsk(LINE_FAULT_RETRY_MS);
		if (er < E_OK) {
			tm_printf((UB*)"line fault retry delay failed: %d\n", (int)er);
			er = tk_slp_tsk(TMO_FEVR);
			tm_printf((UB*)"line fault sleep returned: %d\n", (int)er);
		}
	}
}

static void line_barcode_task(INT stacd, void *exinf)
{
	SYSTIM now;
	uint32_t timestamp_ms = 0U;
	ER er;

	(void)stacd;
	(void)exinf;
	er = tk_get_otm(&now);
	if (er < E_OK) report_line_fault(VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_LINE_CLOCK, timestamp_ms);
	timestamp_ms = now.lo;
	s_state.start_ms = timestamp_ms;
	if (s_config.initialize != NULL) {
		er = s_config.initialize(s_config.read_context);
		if (er != E_OK) {
			tm_printf((UB*)"line sensor initialization failed: %d\n", (int)er);
			report_line_fault(VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_LINE_SENSOR, timestamp_ms);
		}
	}
	for (;;) {
		ir_readings_t readings;
		uint32_t sample_ms;
		uint16_t fault;
		er = s_config.read(s_config.read_context, &readings, &sample_ms);
		if (er != E_OK) {
			tm_printf((UB*)"line sensor read failed: %d\n", (int)er);
			report_line_fault(VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_LINE_SENSOR, timestamp_ms);
		}
		er = tk_get_otm(&now);
		if (er < E_OK) report_line_fault(VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_LINE_CLOCK, timestamp_ms);
		timestamp_ms = now.lo;
		if (timestamp_ms - sample_ms > s_config.odometry_max_age_ms)
			report_line_fault(VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_LINE_SENSOR, timestamp_ms);
		fault = process_readings(&s_state, &readings, sample_ms);
		if (fault != 0U) {
			if (fault == VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_LINE_DELIVERY)
				tm_printf((UB*)"line/barcode delivery failed: %d\n",
					  (int)s_state.delivery_error);
			report_line_fault(fault, timestamp_ms);
		}
		er = tk_dly_tsk(s_config.period_ms);
		if (er < E_OK) report_line_fault(VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_LINE_SCHEDULE, timestamp_ms);
	}
}

ER line_barcode_task_start(const line_barcode_task_config_t *config)
{
	T_CTSK task = {0};
	ID id;
	ER er;

	if (s_started) return E_OBJ;
	if (config == NULL || config->read == NULL || config->period_ms == 0U ||
	    config->period_ms > INT32_MAX || config->odometry_max_age_ms == 0U ||
	    config->odometry_max_age_ms > INT32_MAX ||
	    !barcode_scanner_init(&s_state.scanner, &config->barcode, false, 0))
		return E_PAR;
	if (g_mbf_line_odometry <= 0 || g_mbf_line_observations <= 0 ||
	    g_mbf_barcode_events <= 0 || g_mbf_faults <= 0)
		return E_OBJ;
	s_config = *config;
	s_state.barcode_config = config->barcode;
	s_state.max_age_ms = config->odometry_max_age_ms;
	task.task = (FP)line_barcode_task;
	task.tskatr = TA_HLNG | TA_RNG3;
	task.itskpri = LINE_BARCODE_PRIORITY;
	task.stksz = TASK_STACK_SIZE;
	id = tk_cre_tsk(&task);
	if (id < E_OK) return id;
	s_started = true;
	er = tk_sta_tsk(id, 0);
	if (er < E_OK) {
		ER cleanup = tk_del_tsk(id);
		if (cleanup < E_OK) tm_printf((UB*)"line task cleanup failed: %d\n", (int)cleanup);
		else s_started = false;
		return er;
	}
	return E_OK;
}

#if APP_TEST
#include "../../test_report.h"

static line_state_t s_test_state;

static BOOL test_drain(ID buffer)
{
	union {
		wheel_odometry_message_t odometry;
		line_observation_message_t line;
		barcode_event_message_t barcode;
	} message;
	INT result;

	do {
		result = tk_rcv_mbf(buffer, &message, TMO_POL);
	} while (result > 0);
	return result == E_TMOUT;
}

static BOOL test_sample(line_state_t *state, ir_readings_t *readings,
			uint32_t count, uint32_t sequence, uint32_t timestamp)
{
	wheel_odometry_message_t odometry = {0};
	line_observation_message_t line;

	odometry.header.type = VEHICLE_MESSAGE_WHEEL_ODOMETRY;
	odometry.header.version = VEHICLE_MESSAGE_VERSION;
	odometry.header.sequence = sequence;
	odometry.header.timestamp_ms = timestamp;
	odometry.left_count = signed_bits(count);
	odometry.right_count = signed_bits(count);
	odometry.valid = 1U;
	if (tk_snd_mbf(g_mbf_line_odometry, &odometry, (INT)sizeof(odometry), TMO_POL) != E_OK ||
	    process_readings(state, readings, timestamp) != 0U ||
	    tk_rcv_mbf(g_mbf_line_observations, &line, TMO_POL) != (INT)sizeof(line))
		return FALSE;
	return line.header.type == VEHICLE_MESSAGE_LINE_OBSERVATION &&
	       line.line_detected == 1U && line.lateral_error == -1;
}

static BOOL test_character(line_state_t *state, ir_readings_t *readings,
			   const char *pattern, uint32_t *count,
			   uint32_t *sequence, uint32_t *timestamp, int direction)
{
	unsigned i;
	*count += (uint32_t)(direction * 10);
	*timestamp += 10U;
	readings->barcode.black = true;
	if (!test_sample(state, readings, *count, ++*sequence, *timestamp)) return FALSE;
	for (i = 0U; i < BARCODE_ELEMENTS; i++) {
		unsigned index = direction > 0 ? i : BARCODE_ELEMENTS - 1U - i;
		int width = pattern[index] == 'w' ? 20 : 10;
		*count += (uint32_t)(direction * width);
		*timestamp += 10U;
		readings->barcode.black = !readings->barcode.black;
		if (!test_sample(state, readings, *count, ++*sequence, *timestamp)) return FALSE;
	}
	return TRUE;
}

static BOOL task_frame_test(int direction)
{
	ir_readings_t readings = {0};
	barcode_event_message_t result;
	uint32_t count = direction > 0 ? (uint32_t)INT32_MAX - 40U :
			 (uint32_t)INT32_MIN + 40U;
	uint32_t sequence = UINT32_MAX - 10U;
	uint32_t timestamp = 100U;

	s_test_state = (line_state_t){0};
	s_test_state.barcode_config = (barcode_scanner_config_t){10U, 0U, 0U, 0U, 0U};
	s_test_state.max_age_ms = 50U;
	readings.center.black = true;
	if (!test_sample(&s_test_state, &readings, count, sequence, timestamp) ||
	    !test_character(&s_test_state, &readings, "nwnnwnwnn", &count, &sequence, &timestamp, direction) ||
	    !test_character(&s_test_state, &readings, "wnnnnwnnw", &count, &sequence, &timestamp, direction) ||
	    !test_character(&s_test_state, &readings, "nwnnwnwnn", &count, &sequence, &timestamp, direction))
		return FALSE;
	return tk_rcv_mbf(g_mbf_barcode_events, &result, TMO_POL) == (INT)sizeof(result) &&
	       result.header.type == VEHICLE_MESSAGE_BARCODE_EVENT && result.valid == 1U &&
	       result.length == 1U && result.payload[0] == 'A';
}

static BOOL task_mean_test(void)
{
	wheel_odometry_message_t message = {0};

	s_test_state = (line_state_t){0};
	message.header.type = VEHICLE_MESSAGE_WHEEL_ODOMETRY;
	message.header.version = VEHICLE_MESSAGE_VERSION;
	message.header.sequence = UINT32_MAX;
	message.header.timestamp_ms = UINT32_MAX - 5U;
	message.left_count = INT32_MAX - 1;
	message.right_count = INT32_MIN + 1;
	message.valid = 1U;
	if (!add_odometry(&s_test_state, &message)) return FALSE;
	message.header.sequence = 0U;
	message.header.timestamp_ms = 4U;
	message.left_count = INT32_MIN;
	message.right_count = INT32_MIN + 4;
	if (!add_odometry(&s_test_state, &message) ||
	    s_test_state.mean_count != 2U || s_test_state.half_count != 1)
		return FALSE;
	message.header.sequence = 1U;
	message.header.timestamp_ms = 5U;
	message.left_count = INT32_MIN + 1;
	if (!add_odometry(&s_test_state, &message) ||
	    s_test_state.mean_count != 3U || s_test_state.half_count != 0)
		return FALSE;
	message.header.sequence = 3U;
	message.header.timestamp_ms = 6U;
	if (add_odometry(&s_test_state, &message) || s_test_state.mean_count != 3U)
		return FALSE;
	message.header.sequence = 2U;
	message.left_count = INT32_MIN;
	if (!add_odometry(&s_test_state, &message) ||
	    s_test_state.mean_count != 2U || s_test_state.half_count != 1)
		return FALSE;
	message.header.sequence = 3U;
	message.header.timestamp_ms = 7U;
	message.left_count = INT32_MIN + 1;
	return add_odometry(&s_test_state, &message) &&
	       s_test_state.mean_count == 3U && s_test_state.half_count == 0;
}

static BOOL task_fault_test(void)
{
	ir_readings_t readings = {0};
	wheel_odometry_message_t odometry = {0};
	line_observation_message_t line;
	BOOL ok;
	unsigned i;

	s_test_state = (line_state_t){0};
	s_test_state.barcode_config = (barcode_scanner_config_t){10U, 0U, 0U, 0U, 0U};
	s_test_state.max_age_ms = 50U;
	s_test_state.start_ms = 100U;
	readings.center.black = true;
	ok = process_readings(&s_test_state, &readings, 100U) == 0U;
	ok = tk_rcv_mbf(g_mbf_line_observations, &line, TMO_POL) == (INT)sizeof(line) && ok;
	odometry.header.type = VEHICLE_MESSAGE_WHEEL_ODOMETRY;
	odometry.header.version = VEHICLE_MESSAGE_VERSION;
	odometry.header.sequence = 1U;
	odometry.header.timestamp_ms = 110U;
	odometry.valid = 1U;
	ok = tk_snd_mbf(g_mbf_line_odometry, &odometry, (INT)sizeof(odometry), TMO_POL) == E_OK && ok;
	ok = process_readings(&s_test_state, &readings, 105U) == 0U &&
	     s_test_state.have_pending && !s_test_state.have_odom && ok;
	ok = tk_rcv_mbf(g_mbf_line_observations, &line, TMO_POL) == (INT)sizeof(line) && ok;
	ok = process_readings(&s_test_state, &readings, 110U) == 0U &&
	     s_test_state.scanner_ready && !s_test_state.have_pending && ok;
	ok = tk_rcv_mbf(g_mbf_line_observations, &line, TMO_POL) == (INT)sizeof(line) && ok;
	readings.barcode.black = true;
	ok = process_readings(&s_test_state, &readings, 111U) ==
	     VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_LINE_ODOMETRY && ok;

	s_test_state = (line_state_t){0};
	s_test_state.barcode_config = (barcode_scanner_config_t){10U, 0U, 0U, 0U, 0U};
	s_test_state.max_age_ms = 50U;
	readings.barcode.black = false;
	if (!test_sample(&s_test_state, &readings, 0U, 1U, 100U)) ok = FALSE;
	for (i = 0U; i < VEHICLE_MB_LINE_CAPACITY; i++) {
		if (tk_snd_mbf(g_mbf_line_observations, &line, (INT)sizeof(line), TMO_POL) != E_OK)
			ok = FALSE;
	}
	if (process_readings(&s_test_state, &readings, 110U) != VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_LINE_DELIVERY ||
	    s_test_state.delivery_error != E_TMOUT)
		ok = FALSE;
	ok = test_drain(g_mbf_line_odometry) && ok;
	ok = test_drain(g_mbf_line_observations) && ok;
	ok = test_drain(g_mbf_barcode_events) && ok;

	s_test_state = (line_state_t){0};
	s_test_state.barcode_config = (barcode_scanner_config_t){10U, 0U, 0U, 0U, 0U};
	s_test_state.max_age_ms = 50U;
	if (!test_sample(&s_test_state, &readings, 0U, 1U, 100U)) ok = FALSE;
	odometry.header.sequence = 2U;
	odometry.header.timestamp_ms = 101U;
	if (tk_snd_mbf(g_mbf_line_odometry, &odometry, (INT)sizeof(odometry), TMO_POL) != E_OK)
		ok = FALSE;
	if (process_readings(&s_test_state, &readings, 200U) != VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_LINE_ODOMETRY)
		ok = FALSE;
	ok = test_drain(g_mbf_line_odometry) && ok;
	ok = test_drain(g_mbf_line_observations) && ok;
	return ok;
}

BOOL line_barcode_task_test(void)
{
	BOOL frame_ok;
	BOOL mean_ok;
	BOOL fault_ok;
	TEST_INIT();

	frame_ok = task_frame_test(1);
	frame_ok = task_frame_test(-1) && frame_ok;
	mean_ok = task_mean_test();
	fault_ok = task_fault_test();
	frame_ok = test_drain(g_mbf_line_odometry) && frame_ok;
	frame_ok = test_drain(g_mbf_line_observations) && frame_ok;
	frame_ok = test_drain(g_mbf_barcode_events) && frame_ok;
	TEST_CHECK("LB-U01", frame_ok, "simulated line and barcode messages");
	TEST_CHECK("LB-U02", mean_ok, "wheel-count and sequence wrap");
	TEST_CHECK("LB-U03", fault_ok, "odometry synchronization and full queues");
	TEST_SUMMARY("line/barcode task");
	return frame_ok && mean_ok && fault_ok;
}

#ifndef LINE_BARCODE_TASK_H
#define LINE_BARCODE_TASK_H

#include <tk/tkernel.h>
#include "barcode_scanner.h"
#include "line_observation.h"

/* Initialization runs in the task context before its first sample. The
 * callbacks and context belong exclusively to this task and must remain
 * valid for its lifetime. E_OK means all readings were freshly acquired;
 * timestamp_ms is a time no later than barcode acquisition, measured on the
 * tk_get_otm clock; acquisition must follow within the configured age limit.
 * The other readings must be acquired close enough to that time for the
 * configured age limit. The callback must not use network APIs. */
typedef ER (*line_barcode_init_t)(void *context);
typedef ER (*line_barcode_read_t)(void *context, ir_readings_t *readings,
				  uint32_t *timestamp_ms);

typedef struct {
	RELTIM period_ms;
	/* Maximum age for both the sensor sample and the odometry used with it. */
	uint32_t odometry_max_age_ms;
	barcode_scanner_config_t barcode;
	line_barcode_init_t initialize;
	line_barcode_read_t read;
	void *read_context;
} line_barcode_task_config_t;

/* The single startup owner calls this after buffer creation. No hardware
 * reader or calibration is supplied by default, and startup is not automatic. */
ER line_barcode_task_start(const line_barcode_task_config_t *config);

#if APP_TEST
BOOL line_barcode_task_test(void);
#endif

#include "line_observation.h"

#include <stddef.h>

uint8_t line_following_position(const ir_readings_t *readings)
{
	if (readings == NULL) {
		return POS_LOST;
	}
	if (readings->center.black && readings->right.black) {
		return POS_JUNCTION;
	}
	if (readings->center.black) {
		return POS_LEFT;
	}
	if (readings->right.black) {
		return POS_RIGHT;
	}
	return POS_LOST;
}

BOOL line_observation_test(void)
{
	ir_readings_t readings = {0};
	BOOL positions_ok;
	BOOL independence_ok;
	TEST_INIT();

	positions_ok = line_following_position(&readings) == POS_LOST;
	readings.center.black = true;
	positions_ok = positions_ok &&
		       line_following_position(&readings) == POS_LEFT;
	readings.center.black = false;
	readings.right.black = true;
	positions_ok = positions_ok &&
		       line_following_position(&readings) == POS_RIGHT;
	readings.center.black = true;
	positions_ok = positions_ok &&
		       line_following_position(&readings) == POS_JUNCTION;

	readings.barcode.black = true;
	independence_ok = line_following_position(&readings) == POS_JUNCTION;
	readings.center.black = false;
	independence_ok = independence_ok &&
			  line_following_position(&readings) == POS_RIGHT;
	readings.center.black = true;
	readings.right.black = false;
	independence_ok = independence_ok &&
			  line_following_position(&readings) == POS_LEFT;
	readings.center.black = false;
	independence_ok = independence_ok &&
			  line_following_position(&readings) == POS_LOST &&
			  line_following_position(NULL) == POS_LOST;

	TEST_CHECK("LINE-U01", positions_ok, "line sensor combinations");
	TEST_CHECK("LINE-U02", independence_ok, "barcode input and missing readings");
	TEST_SUMMARY("line observation");
	return positions_ok && independence_ok;
}

#include "imu_processor.h"
#include "imu_processor_config.h"
#include <math.h>
#include <stddef.h>

#ifndef M_PI
#define M_PI 3.14159265358979323846f
#endif

static float get_pitch_deg(const accel_data_t *a) {
    return atan2f(-a->ax, sqrtf(a->ay * a->ay + a->az * a->az)) * (180.0f / (float)M_PI);
}

static float get_roll_deg(const accel_data_t *a) {
    return atan2f(a->ay, a->az) * (180.0f / (float)M_PI);
}

/* Detects when the robot has been lifted by hand or overturned */
static bool check_lifted_or_overturned(const accel_data_t *a, float pitch_deg, float roll_deg) {
    if (a->az < OVERTURN_MIN_AZ) {
        return true; /* Overturned on its side or upside-down */
    }
    if (fabsf(roll_deg) > LIFT_ROLL_DEG) {
        return true; /* Lifted by hand or tipped sideways */
    }
    if (fabsf(pitch_deg) > LIFT_PITCH_DEG) {
        return true; /* Lifted steeper than any ramp */
    }
    return false;
}

static uint8_t quantize_height_qcm(float height_m) {
    float height_cm = height_m * 100.0f;
    if (height_cm < 0.0f) height_cm = 0.0f;
    if (height_cm >= 15.5f) return 62U;
    uint32_t qcm = (uint32_t)(height_cm * 4.0f + 0.5f); /* 0.25 cm steps */
    if (qcm > 62U) qcm = 62U;
    return (uint8_t)qcm;
}

static uint8_t quantize_pitch_bucket(float pitch_deg) {
    int32_t idx = (int32_t)((pitch_deg + 35.0f) / 5.0f);
    if (idx < 0)  idx = 0;
    if (idx > 15) idx = 15;
    return (uint8_t)idx;
}

bool imu_processor_init(imu_state_t *state) {
    if (state == NULL) return false;
    state->fsm_state             = IMU_STATE_MONITORING;
    state->motion_event          = IMU_EVENT_STATIONARY;
    state->hump_peak_m           = 0.0f;
    state->current_hump_m        = 0.0f;
    state->hump_active           = false;
    state->shock_detected        = false; /* Collision detection removed per Prof */
    state->tilt_detected         = false; /* Latches true when lifted or overturned */
    state->last_distance_m       = 0.0f;
    state->hump_start_distance_m = 0.0f;
    state->current_pitch_deg     = 0.0f;
    state->current_roll_deg      = 0.0f;
    state->hump_start_ms         = 0U;
    state->packed_telemetry      = 0U;
    return true;
}

bool imu_processor_update(imu_state_t *state, const accel_data_t *accel,
                          float current_distance_m, uint32_t now_ms) {
    if (state == NULL || accel == NULL ||
        !isfinite(accel->ax) || !isfinite(accel->ay) ||
        !isfinite(accel->az) || !isfinite(current_distance_m)) return false;
    state->current_pitch_deg = get_pitch_deg(accel);
    state->current_roll_deg  = get_roll_deg(accel);

    /* 1. Global Safety Watchdog: Lifted or Overturned (immediately disarms motors) */
    if (check_lifted_or_overturned(accel, state->current_pitch_deg, state->current_roll_deg)) {
        state->tilt_detected = true;
        state->hump_active   = false;
        state->fsm_state     = IMU_STATE_TILT;
        goto pack_telemetry;
    }

    /* 2. Terrain FSM */
    switch (state->fsm_state) {
    case IMU_STATE_MONITORING:
        state->motion_event = (fabsf(accel->ax) > 2.0f) ? IMU_EVENT_ACCEL : IMU_EVENT_STATIONARY;

        if (state->current_pitch_deg > PITCH_START_DEG) {
            state->hump_start_distance_m = current_distance_m;
            state->last_distance_m       = current_distance_m;
            state->current_hump_m        = 0.0f;
            state->hump_peak_m           = 0.0f; /* Reset so each hump records its own peak */
            state->hump_active           = true;
            state->hump_start_ms         = now_ms;
            state->motion_event          = IMU_EVENT_CLIMBING;
            state->fsm_state             = IMU_STATE_HUMP_RISING;
        }
        break;

    case IMU_STATE_HUMP_RISING:
        state->motion_event = IMU_EVENT_CLIMBING;

        if ((uint32_t)(now_ms - state->hump_start_ms) > 5000U) {
            state->fsm_state   = IMU_STATE_MONITORING;
            state->hump_active = false;
            break;
        }

        float delta_dist = current_distance_m - state->last_distance_m;
        if (delta_dist > 0.0f) {
            float pitch_rad = state->current_pitch_deg * ((float)M_PI / 180.0f);
            state->current_hump_m += delta_dist * sinf(pitch_rad);
        }
        state->last_distance_m = current_distance_m;

        if (state->current_pitch_deg <= PITCH_END_DEG) {
            if (state->current_hump_m > MIN_HUMP_HEIGHT_M) {
                if (state->current_hump_m > state->hump_peak_m) {
                    state->hump_peak_m = state->current_hump_m;
                }
            }
            state->hump_start_ms = now_ms;
            state->fsm_state = IMU_STATE_HUMP_FALLING;
        }
        break;

    case IMU_STATE_HUMP_FALLING:
        state->motion_event = IMU_EVENT_DESCENDING;

        if (state->current_pitch_deg > PITCH_START_DEG) {
            state->last_distance_m = current_distance_m;
            state->hump_start_ms   = now_ms;
            state->fsm_state       = IMU_STATE_HUMP_RISING;
            break;
        }

        if ((fabsf(state->current_pitch_deg) < PITCH_END_DEG) ||
            ((uint32_t)(now_ms - state->hump_start_ms) > 4000U)) {
            state->fsm_state   = IMU_STATE_MONITORING;
            state->hump_active = false;
        }
        break;

    case IMU_STATE_TILT:
        if (!check_lifted_or_overturned(accel, state->current_pitch_deg, state->current_roll_deg)) {
            state->fsm_state = IMU_STATE_MONITORING;
        }
        break;

    default:
        state->fsm_state = IMU_STATE_MONITORING;
        break;
    }

    /* 3. Pack 16-Bit Word */
pack_telemetry:
    {
        uint8_t h_qcm = quantize_height_qcm(state->hump_peak_m);
        uint8_t p_idx = quantize_pitch_bucket(state->current_pitch_deg);
        state->packed_telemetry = imu_pack_telemetry((uint8_t)state->fsm_state,
                                                     (uint8_t)state->motion_event,
                                                     h_qcm,
                                                     p_idx);
    }
    return true;
}

static BOOL terrain_test(void)
{
	imu_state_t state;
	const accel_data_t flat = {.az = GRAVITY_MS2};
	const accel_data_t ramp = {.ax = -1.4f, .az = 9.70f};

	return imu_processor_init(&state) &&
	       imu_processor_update(&state, &flat, 0.0f, 10U) &&
	       state.fsm_state == IMU_STATE_MONITORING &&
	       imu_processor_update(&state, &ramp, 0.0f, 20U) &&
	       state.fsm_state == IMU_STATE_HUMP_RISING &&
	       imu_processor_update(&state, &ramp, 0.30f, 50U) &&
	       imu_processor_update(&state, &flat, 0.30f, 80U) &&
	       state.fsm_state == IMU_STATE_HUMP_FALLING &&
	       state.hump_peak_m > 0.015f &&
	       imu_processor_update(&state, &flat, 0.30f, 100U) &&
	       state.fsm_state == IMU_STATE_MONITORING && !state.hump_active;
}

static BOOL safety_test(void)
{
	imu_state_t state;
	const accel_data_t flat = {.az = GRAVITY_MS2};
	const accel_data_t ramp = {.ax = -1.4f, .az = 9.70f};
	const accel_data_t lifted_roll = {.ay = 5.0f, .az = 8.4f};
	const accel_data_t overturned = {.az = -5.0f};
	const accel_data_t invalid = {.ax = __builtin_huge_valf(), .az = GRAVITY_MS2};

	return imu_processor_init(&state) &&
	       imu_processor_update(&state, &ramp, 0.0f, 5U) &&
	       state.hump_active &&
	       imu_processor_update(&state, &lifted_roll, 0.0f, 10U) &&
	       state.tilt_detected && !state.hump_active &&
	       state.fsm_state == IMU_STATE_TILT &&
	       (state.packed_telemetry & 7U) == IMU_STATE_TILT &&
	       imu_processor_update(&state, &flat, 0.0f, 20U) &&
	       state.fsm_state == IMU_STATE_MONITORING &&
	       imu_processor_update(&state, &overturned, 0.0f, 30U) &&
	       state.tilt_detected && state.fsm_state == IMU_STATE_TILT &&
	       (state.packed_telemetry & 7U) == IMU_STATE_TILT &&
	       !imu_processor_update(&state, &invalid, 0.0f, 40U) &&
	       state.fsm_state == IMU_STATE_TILT &&
	       !imu_processor_update(&state, NULL, 0.0f, 40U) &&
	       !imu_processor_init(NULL);
}

static BOOL clock_test(void)
{
	imu_state_t first;
	imu_state_t second;
	const accel_data_t ramp = {.ax = -1.4f, .az = 9.70f};

	return imu_processor_init(&first) && imu_processor_init(&second) &&

	       imu_processor_update(&second, &ramp, 0.0f, 4100U) &&
	       imu_processor_update(&first, &ramp, 0.0f, 5101U) &&
	       first.fsm_state == IMU_STATE_MONITORING &&
	       imu_processor_update(&second, &ramp, 0.0f, 5101U) &&
	       second.fsm_state == IMU_STATE_HUMP_RISING &&
	       imu_processor_init(&first) &&
	       imu_processor_update(&first, &ramp, 0.0f, UINT32_MAX - 1000U) &&
	       imu_processor_update(&first, &ramp, 0.0f, 4000U) &&
	       first.fsm_state == IMU_STATE_MONITORING;
}

static BOOL accel_decode_test(void)
{
	const uint8_t bytes[6] = {0x00U, 0x80U, 0xF0U, 0x7FU, 0x00U, 0x10U};
	uint8_t invalid[6] = {0};
	lsm303dlhc_axis_map_t mapping = {{2U, 0U, 1U}, {-1, 1, -1}};
	accel_data_t sample = {0};
	BOOL ok;

	ok = lsm303dlhc_accel_decode(bytes, &mapping, &sample) &&
	     sample.raw_x == -256 && sample.raw_y == -2048 &&
	     sample.raw_z == -2047 && !sample.i2c_ok &&
	     sample.ax < -2.63f && sample.ax > -2.65f &&
	     sample.ay < -21.08f && sample.ay > -21.10f;
	mapping.axis[1] = 2U;
	ok = !lsm303dlhc_accel_decode(bytes, &mapping, &sample) &&
	     sample.raw_x == -256 && ok;
	mapping.axis[1] = 0U;
	mapping.sign[2] = 0;
	ok = !lsm303dlhc_accel_decode(bytes, &mapping, &sample) &&
	     sample.raw_y == -2048 && ok;
	mapping.sign[2] = -1;
	invalid[0] = 1U;
	ok = !lsm303dlhc_accel_decode(invalid, &mapping, &sample) &&
	     !lsm303dlhc_accel_decode(NULL, &mapping, &sample) &&
	     sample.raw_z == -2047 && ok;
	mapping.axis[0] = 0U;
	mapping.axis[1] = 1U;
	mapping.axis[2] = 2U;
	mapping.sign[0] = -1;
	mapping.sign[1] = 1;
	mapping.sign[2] = 1;
	ok = lsm303dlhc_accel_decode(bytes, &mapping, &sample) &&
	     sample.raw_x == 2048 && sample.raw_y == 2047 && sample.raw_z == 256 && ok;
	return ok;
}

typedef struct {
	lsm303dlhc_transfer_t transfer;
	ER result;
	unsigned calls;
} test_accel_transport_t;

static ER test_read_fresh(void *context, lsm303dlhc_transfer_t *transfer)
{
	test_accel_transport_t *transport = context;
	transport->calls++;
	*transfer = transport->transfer;
	return transport->result;
}

static BOOL accel_reader_test(void)
{
	const lsm303dlhc_axis_map_t mapping = {{0U, 1U, 2U}, {1, -1, 1}};
	lsm303dlhc_axis_map_t invalid = {{0U, 0U, 2U}, {1, 1, 1}};
	lsm303dlhc_reader_t reader = {0};
	test_accel_transport_t transport = {0};
	accel_data_t sample = {0};
	uint32_t timestamp = 0U;
	BOOL ok;
	unsigned i;

	ok = lsm303dlhc_reader_init(&reader, &invalid, test_read_fresh, &transport) == E_PAR &&
	     lsm303dlhc_reader_init(&reader, &mapping, NULL, &transport) == E_PAR &&
	     lsm303dlhc_reader_init(&reader, &mapping, test_read_fresh, &transport) == E_OK &&
	     lsm303dlhc_reader_init(&reader, &mapping, test_read_fresh, &transport) == E_OBJ;
	transport.transfer.bytes[1] = 0x10U;
	transport.transfer.bytes[3] = 0x20U;
	transport.transfer.length = 6U;
	transport.transfer.fresh = true;
	transport.transfer.timestamp_valid = true;
	transport.transfer.timestamp_ms = UINT32_MAX;
	ok = lsm303dlhc_reader_read(&reader, &sample, &timestamp) == E_OK &&
	     sample.raw_x == 256 && sample.raw_y == -512 && sample.i2c_ok &&
	     timestamp == UINT32_MAX && transport.calls == 1U && ok;
	for (i = 0U; i < 6U; i++) {
		reader = (lsm303dlhc_reader_t){0};
		transport.transfer.length = 6U;
		transport.transfer.fresh = true;
		transport.transfer.timestamp_valid = true;
		transport.transfer.bytes[0] = 0U;
		transport.result = E_OK;
		if (i == 0U) transport.transfer.length = 5U;
		if (i == 1U) transport.transfer.fresh = false;
		if (i == 2U) transport.transfer.timestamp_valid = false;
		if (i == 3U) transport.transfer.bytes[0] = 1U;
		if (i == 4U) transport.result = E_TMOUT;
		if (i == 5U) transport.result = 1;
		ok = lsm303dlhc_reader_init(&reader, &mapping, test_read_fresh, &transport) == E_OK &&
		     ok;
		sample.raw_x = 77;
		timestamp = 42U;
		ok = lsm303dlhc_reader_read(&reader, &sample, &timestamp) ==
		     (i == 4U ? E_TMOUT : E_IO) &&
		     reader.faulted && sample.raw_x == 77 && timestamp == 42U && ok;
		{
			unsigned calls = transport.calls;
			ok = lsm303dlhc_reader_read(&reader, &sample, &timestamp) == E_OBJ &&
			     transport.calls == calls && ok;
		}
	}
	return ok;
}

static BOOL producer_test(void)
{
	imu_producer_t producer;
	imu_estimate_message_t message = {0};
	wheel_odometry_message_t odometry = {0};
	accel_data_t accel = {.az = GRAVITY_MS2, .i2c_ok = true};
	BOOL ok;

	ok = !imu_producer_init(&producer, 0U) &&
	     !imu_producer_init(&producer, UINT32_MAX) &&
	     imu_producer_init(&producer, 10U);
	message.header.sequence = 99U;
	ok = !imu_producer_update(&producer, &accel, NULL, 100U, &message) &&
	     message.header.sequence == 99U && !producer.have_sample && ok;
	odometry.header.type = VEHICLE_MESSAGE_WHEEL_ODOMETRY;
	odometry.header.version = VEHICLE_MESSAGE_VERSION;
	odometry.header.sequence = 0U;
	odometry.header.timestamp_ms = 102U;
	ok = !imu_producer_update(&producer, &accel, &odometry, 103U, &message) &&
	     !producer.have_odometry && message.header.sequence == 99U && ok;
	odometry.valid = 1U;
	ok = !imu_producer_update(&producer, &accel, &odometry, 100U, &message) &&
	     !producer.have_odometry && message.header.sequence == 99U && ok;
	odometry.header.timestamp_ms = 100U;
	ok = imu_producer_update(&producer, &accel, &odometry, 100U, &message) &&
	     !message.shock_detected && !message.tilt_detected &&
	     message.header.type == VEHICLE_MESSAGE_IMU_ESTIMATE &&
	     message.header.sequence == 1U && message.header.timestamp_ms == 100U &&
	     message.validity == VEHICLE_IMU_VALID_ROLL_PITCH &&
	     message.roll_mdeg == 0 && message.pitch_mdeg == 0 &&
	     message.yaw_mdeg == 0 && message.gyro_x == 0 && ok;
	accel.az = -5.0f;
	ok = imu_producer_update(&producer, &accel, NULL, 101U, &message) &&
	     message.tilt_detected && !message.shock_detected &&
	     (message.terrain_telemetry & 7U) == IMU_STATE_TILT &&
	     message.roll_mdeg >= 179000 && message.roll_mdeg <= 180001 && ok;
	odometry.header.sequence = 1U;
	odometry.header.timestamp_ms = 102U;
	accel.az = 50.0f;
	ok = imu_producer_update(&producer, &accel, &odometry, 103U, &message) &&
	     !message.shock_detected && message.tilt_detected &&
	     (message.terrain_telemetry & 7U) == IMU_STATE_MONITORING && ok;
	odometry.header.sequence = 3U;
	odometry.header.timestamp_ms = 104U;
	ok = !imu_producer_update(&producer, &accel, &odometry, 104U, &message) &&
	     !imu_producer_update(&producer, &accel, NULL, 113U, &message) &&
	     !imu_producer_update(&producer, &accel, NULL, 103U, &message) &&
	     producer.sequence == 3U && message.header.sequence == 3U && ok;
	accel.i2c_ok = false;
	ok = !imu_producer_update(&producer, &accel, NULL, 104U, &message) &&
	     producer.sequence == 3U && ok;
	accel.i2c_ok = true;
	accel.ax = __builtin_huge_valf();
	ok = !imu_producer_update(&producer, &accel, NULL, 104U, &message) &&
	     producer.sequence == 3U && ok;

	accel.ax = 0.0f;
	accel.az = GRAVITY_MS2;
	odometry.header.sequence = UINT32_MAX;
	odometry.header.timestamp_ms = UINT32_MAX - 5U;
	ok = imu_producer_init(&producer, 10U) &&
	     imu_producer_update(&producer, &accel, &odometry, UINT32_MAX - 1U, &message) &&
	     ok;
	odometry.header.sequence = 0U;
	odometry.header.timestamp_ms = 1U;
	ok = imu_producer_update(&producer, &accel, &odometry, 2U, &message) &&
	     message.header.timestamp_ms == 2U && ok;
	return ok;
}

BOOL imu_processor_test(void)
{
	BOOL terrain_ok;
	BOOL safety_ok;
	BOOL clock_ok;
	BOOL producer_ok;
	BOOL accel_ok;
	BOOL reader_ok;
	TEST_INIT();

	terrain_ok = terrain_test();
	safety_ok = safety_test();
	clock_ok = clock_test();
	producer_ok = producer_test();
	accel_ok = accel_decode_test();
	reader_ok = accel_reader_test();
	TEST_CHECK("IMU-U01", terrain_ok, "flat, ramp and hump transitions");
	TEST_CHECK("IMU-U02", safety_ok, "tilt and invalid samples");
	TEST_CHECK("IMU-U03", clock_ok, "independent timeouts and clock wrap");
	TEST_CHECK("IMU-U04", producer_ok, "typed estimates and odometry validation");
	TEST_CHECK("IMU-U07", accel_ok, "LSM303DLHC acceleration decoding");
	TEST_CHECK("IMU-U08", reader_ok, "fresh accelerometer transfer and fault latch");
	TEST_SUMMARY("imu");
	return terrain_ok && safety_ok && clock_ok && producer_ok && accel_ok && reader_ok;
}

#include "imu_producer.h"

#include <stddef.h>

bool imu_producer_init(imu_producer_t *producer, uint32_t max_odometry_age_ms)
{
	imu_producer_t initial = {0};

	if (producer == NULL || max_odometry_age_ms == 0U ||
	    max_odometry_age_ms > INT32_MAX ||
	    !imu_processor_init(&initial.processor))
		return false;
	initial.max_odometry_age_ms = max_odometry_age_ms;
	initial.initialized = true;
	*producer = initial;
	return true;
}

bool imu_producer_add_odometry(imu_producer_t *producer,
			       const wheel_odometry_message_t *odometry)
{
	uint32_t elapsed;

	if (producer == NULL || !producer->initialized || odometry == NULL ||
	    odometry->header.type != VEHICLE_MESSAGE_WHEEL_ODOMETRY ||
	    odometry->header.version != VEHICLE_MESSAGE_VERSION ||
	    odometry->valid != 1U)
		return false;
	if (producer->have_odometry) {
		elapsed = odometry->header.timestamp_ms -
			  producer->odometry.header.timestamp_ms;
		if (odometry->header.sequence - producer->odometry.header.sequence != 1U ||
		    elapsed == 0U || elapsed > INT32_MAX)
			return false;
	}
	producer->odometry = *odometry;
	producer->have_odometry = true;
	return true;
}

bool imu_producer_update(imu_producer_t *producer, const accel_data_t *accel,
			 const wheel_odometry_message_t *odometry,
			 uint32_t sample_ms, imu_estimate_message_t *message)
{
	imu_producer_t next;
	imu_estimate_message_t estimate = {0};
	uint32_t elapsed;

	if (producer == NULL || accel == NULL || message == NULL ||
	    !producer->initialized || !accel->i2c_ok)
		return false;
	next = *producer;
	if (next.have_sample) {
		elapsed = sample_ms - next.last_sample_ms;
		if (elapsed == 0U || elapsed > INT32_MAX) return false;
	}
	if (odometry != NULL) {
		if (!imu_producer_add_odometry(&next, odometry)) return false;
	}
	if (!next.have_odometry ||
	    sample_ms - next.odometry.header.timestamp_ms > next.max_odometry_age_ms)
		return false;
	if (!imu_processor_update(&next.processor, accel,
				  (float)next.odometry.distance_mm / 1000.0f,
				  sample_ms))
		return false;
	estimate.header.type = VEHICLE_MESSAGE_IMU_ESTIMATE;
	estimate.header.version = VEHICLE_MESSAGE_VERSION;
	estimate.header.sequence = ++next.sequence;
	estimate.header.timestamp_ms = sample_ms;
	estimate.roll_mdeg = (int32_t)(next.processor.current_roll_deg * 1000.0f);
	estimate.pitch_mdeg = (int32_t)(next.processor.current_pitch_deg * 1000.0f);
	estimate.validity = VEHICLE_IMU_VALID_ROLL_PITCH;
	estimate.terrain_telemetry = next.processor.packed_telemetry;
	estimate.shock_detected = next.processor.shock_detected;
	estimate.tilt_detected = next.processor.tilt_detected;
	next.last_sample_ms = sample_ms;
	next.have_sample = true;
	*producer = next;
	*message = estimate;
	return true;
}
=== C:\Users\Yong Jun\school\INF2004-Embedded_Systems\project\INF2004_Project\app_program\subsystem4\imu\imu_producer.h ===
#ifndef IMU_PRODUCER_H
#define IMU_PRODUCER_H

#include "imu_processor.h"
#include "../../vehicle_messages.h"

typedef struct {
	imu_state_t processor;
	wheel_odometry_message_t odometry;
	uint32_t max_odometry_age_ms;
	uint32_t last_sample_ms;
	uint32_t sequence;
	bool initialized;
	bool have_odometry;
	bool have_sample;
} imu_producer_t;

/* The producer is owned by one task; max_odometry_age_ms must be 1..INT32_MAX. */
bool imu_producer_init(imu_producer_t *producer, uint32_t max_odometry_age_ms);

/* Accept one ordered odometry message without producing an estimate.
 * The owner checks its age and retains future messages until their timestamp.
 * On failure the producer is unchanged. */
bool imu_producer_add_odometry(imu_producer_t *producer,
			       const wheel_odometry_message_t *odometry);

/* Pass a new odometry message only when one is available, otherwise NULL.
 * The owner retains future-dated messages until their timestamp. The first
 * sample requires odometry; subsequent samples may reuse it while fresh.
 * On failure neither the producer nor output changes. The owner must report
 * failures and independently validate the sensor sample's age. */
bool imu_producer_update(imu_producer_t *producer, const accel_data_t *accel,
			 const wheel_odometry_message_t *odometry,
			 uint32_t sample_ms, imu_estimate_message_t *message);

#endif
=== C:\Users\Yong Jun\school\INF2004-Embedded_Systems\project\INF2004_Project\app_program\subsystem4\imu\imu_task.c ===
#include "../../app.h"
#include "../../vehicle_buffers.h"
#include "imu_producer.h"
#include "imu_task.h"

#define IMU_TASK_PRIORITY 10
#define IMU_FAULT_RETRY_MS 1000U

typedef struct {
	imu_producer_t producer;
	wheel_odometry_message_t pending;
	uint32_t start_ms;
	uint32_t last_sample_ms;
	ER delivery_error;
	bool have_pending;
	bool have_sample;
} imu_task_state_t;

static imu_task_config_t s_config;
static imu_task_state_t s_state;
static bool s_started;

static uint16_t receive_odometry(imu_task_state_t *state, uint32_t sample_ms)
{
	unsigned count;

	/* One retained message plus at most one buffer capacity per cycle. */
	for (count = 0U; count < VEHICLE_MB_ODOMETRY_CAPACITY + 1U; count++) {
		wheel_odometry_message_t message;
		uint32_t ahead;
		INT received;

		if (state->have_pending) {
			message = state->pending;
		} else {
			received = tk_rcv_mbf(g_mbf_imu_odometry, &message, TMO_POL);
			if (received == E_TMOUT) break;
			if (received != (INT)sizeof(message))
				return VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_IMU_ODOMETRY;
		}
		if (message.header.type != VEHICLE_MESSAGE_WHEEL_ODOMETRY ||
		    message.header.version != VEHICLE_MESSAGE_VERSION || message.valid != 1U)
			return VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_IMU_ODOMETRY;
		ahead = message.header.timestamp_ms - sample_ms;
		if (ahead != 0U && ahead <= INT32_MAX) {
			state->pending = message;
			state->have_pending = true;
			break;
		}
		state->have_pending = false;
		if (sample_ms - message.header.timestamp_ms >
		    state->producer.max_odometry_age_ms ||
		    !imu_producer_add_odometry(&state->producer, &message))
			return VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_IMU_ODOMETRY;
	}
	if (state->producer.have_odometry) {
		if (sample_ms - state->producer.odometry.header.timestamp_ms >
		    state->producer.max_odometry_age_ms)
			return VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_IMU_ODOMETRY;
	} else if (sample_ms - state->start_ms > state->producer.max_odometry_age_ms) {
		return VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_IMU_ODOMETRY;
	}
	return 0U;
}

static uint16_t process_sample(imu_task_state_t *state, const accel_data_t *sample,
			       uint32_t sample_ms)
{
	imu_estimate_message_t estimate;
	uint16_t fault;
	ER er;
	uint32_t elapsed;

	if (!sample->i2c_ok || !__builtin_isfinite(sample->ax) ||
	    !__builtin_isfinite(sample->ay) || !__builtin_isfinite(sample->az))
		return VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_IMU_SENSOR;
	elapsed = sample_ms - (state->have_sample ? state->last_sample_ms : state->start_ms);
	if (elapsed > INT32_MAX || (state->have_sample && elapsed == 0U))
		return VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_IMU_CLOCK;
	fault = receive_odometry(state, sample_ms);
	if (fault != 0U) return fault;
	if (state->producer.have_odometry) {
		if (!imu_producer_update(&state->producer, sample, NULL, sample_ms, &estimate))
			return VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_IMU_PROCESSING;
		er = tk_snd_mbf(g_mbf_imu_estimates, &estimate, (INT)sizeof(estimate), TMO_POL);
		if (er != E_OK) {
			state->delivery_error = er;
			return VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_IMU_DELIVERY;
		}
	}
	state->last_sample_ms = sample_ms;
	state->have_sample = true;
	return 0U;
}

static void report_imu_fault(uint16_t code, uint32_t timestamp_ms)
{
	fault_message_t fault = {0};
	bool sent = false;

	fault.header.type = VEHICLE_MESSAGE_FAULT;
	fault.header.version = VEHICLE_MESSAGE_VERSION;
	fault.header.sequence = 1U;
	fault.header.timestamp_ms = timestamp_ms;
	fault.source = code == VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_IMU_DELIVERY ||
		       code == VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_IMU_ODOMETRY ?
		       VEHICLE_FAULT_COMMUNICATION :
		       (code == VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_IMU_CLOCK ||
			code == VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_IMU_SCHEDULE ?
			VEHICLE_FAULT_SYSTEM : VEHICLE_FAULT_SENSOR);
	fault.code = code;
	fault.recoverable = 0U;
	tm_printf((UB*)"IMU stopped; fault code %u\n", (unsigned)code);
	for (;;) {
		ER er;
		if (!sent) {
			er = tk_snd_mbf(g_mbf_faults, &fault, (INT)sizeof(fault), TMO_POL);
			if (er == E_OK) sent = true;
			else tm_printf((UB*)"IMU fault delivery failed: %d\n", (int)er);
		}
		er = tk_dly_tsk(IMU_FAULT_RETRY_MS);
		if (er < E_OK) {
			tm_printf((UB*)"IMU fault retry delay failed: %d\n", (int)er);
			er = tk_slp_tsk(TMO_FEVR);
			tm_printf((UB*)"IMU fault sleep returned: %d\n", (int)er);
		}
	}
}

static void imu_task(INT stacd, void *exinf)
{
	SYSTIM now;
	uint32_t timestamp_ms = 0U;
	ER er;

	(void)stacd;
	(void)exinf;
	er = tk_get_otm(&now);
	if (er != E_OK) report_imu_fault(VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_IMU_CLOCK, timestamp_ms);
	timestamp_ms = now.lo;
	s_state.start_ms = timestamp_ms;
	if (s_config.initialize != NULL) {
		er = s_config.initialize(s_config.read_context);
		if (er != E_OK) {
			tm_printf((UB*)"IMU sensor initialization failed: %d\n", (int)er);
			report_imu_fault(VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_IMU_SENSOR, timestamp_ms);
		}
	}
	for (;;) {
		accel_data_t sample;
		uint32_t sample_ms;
		uint16_t fault;

		er = s_config.read(s_config.read_context, &sample, &sample_ms);
		if (er != E_OK) {
			tm_printf((UB*)"IMU sensor read failed: %d\n", (int)er);
			report_imu_fault(VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_IMU_SENSOR, timestamp_ms);
		}
		er = tk_get_otm(&now);
		if (er != E_OK) report_imu_fault(VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_IMU_CLOCK, timestamp_ms);
		timestamp_ms = now.lo;
		if (timestamp_ms - sample_ms > s_config.sample_max_age_ms)
			report_imu_fault(VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_IMU_SENSOR, timestamp_ms);
		fault = process_sample(&s_state, &sample, sample_ms);
		if (fault != 0U) {
			if (fault == VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_IMU_DELIVERY)
				tm_printf((UB*)"IMU delivery failed: %d\n", (int)s_state.delivery_error);
			report_imu_fault(fault, timestamp_ms);
		}
		er = tk_dly_tsk(s_config.period_ms);
		if (er != E_OK) report_imu_fault(VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_IMU_SCHEDULE, timestamp_ms);
	}
}

ER imu_task_start(const imu_task_config_t *config)
{
	T_CTSK task = {0};
	imu_task_state_t initial = {0};
	ID id;
	ER er;

	if (s_started) return E_OBJ;
	if (config == NULL || config->read == NULL || config->period_ms == 0U ||
	    config->period_ms > INT32_MAX || config->sample_max_age_ms == 0U ||
	    config->sample_max_age_ms > INT32_MAX ||
	    !imu_producer_init(&initial.producer, config->odometry_max_age_ms))
		return E_PAR;
	if (g_mbf_imu_odometry <= 0 || g_mbf_imu_estimates <= 0 || g_mbf_faults <= 0)
		return E_OBJ;
	s_config = *config;
	s_state = initial;
	task.task = (FP)imu_task;
	task.tskatr = TA_HLNG | TA_RNG3;
	task.itskpri = IMU_TASK_PRIORITY;
	task.stksz = TASK_STACK_SIZE;
	id = tk_cre_tsk(&task);
	if (id < E_OK) return id;
	s_started = true;
	er = tk_sta_tsk(id, 0);
	if (er != E_OK) {
		ER cleanup = tk_del_tsk(id);
		if (cleanup != E_OK) tm_printf((UB*)"IMU task cleanup failed: %d\n", (int)cleanup);
		else s_started = false;
		return er < E_OK ? er : E_SYS;
	}
	return E_OK;
}

#if APP_TEST
#include "../../test_report.h"

static BOOL drain_test_buffer(ID buffer)
{
	union {
		wheel_odometry_message_t odometry;
		imu_estimate_message_t estimate;
	} message;
	INT result;

	do {
		result = tk_rcv_mbf(buffer, &message, TMO_POL);
	} while (result > 0);
	return result == E_TMOUT;
}

static wheel_odometry_message_t test_odometry(uint32_t sequence, uint32_t timestamp)
{
	wheel_odometry_message_t message = {0};
	message.header.type = VEHICLE_MESSAGE_WHEEL_ODOMETRY;
	message.header.version = VEHICLE_MESSAGE_VERSION;
	message.header.sequence = sequence;
	message.header.timestamp_ms = timestamp;
	message.valid = 1U;
	return message;
}

static BOOL task_flow_test(void)
{
	imu_task_state_t state = {0};
	accel_data_t sample = {.az = 9.80665f, .i2c_ok = true};
	wheel_odometry_message_t odometry;
	imu_estimate_message_t estimate;
	BOOL ok;

	ok = imu_producer_init(&state.producer, 10U);
	state.start_ms = 100U;
	ok = process_sample(&state, &sample, 100U) == 0U &&
	     tk_rcv_mbf(g_mbf_imu_estimates, &estimate, TMO_POL) == E_TMOUT && ok;
	odometry = test_odometry(UINT32_MAX, 102U);
	ok = tk_snd_mbf(g_mbf_imu_odometry, &odometry, (INT)sizeof(odometry), TMO_POL) == E_OK && ok;
	odometry = test_odometry(0U, 103U);
	ok = tk_snd_mbf(g_mbf_imu_odometry, &odometry, (INT)sizeof(odometry), TMO_POL) == E_OK && ok;
	ok = process_sample(&state, &sample, 101U) == 0U && state.have_pending &&
	     !state.producer.have_odometry && ok;
	ok = process_sample(&state, &sample, 103U) == 0U && !state.have_pending &&
	     state.producer.odometry.header.sequence == 0U && ok;
	ok = tk_rcv_mbf(g_mbf_imu_estimates, &estimate, TMO_POL) == (INT)sizeof(estimate) &&
	     estimate.header.type == VEHICLE_MESSAGE_IMU_ESTIMATE &&
	     estimate.header.sequence == 1U && estimate.header.timestamp_ms == 103U &&
	     estimate.validity == VEHICLE_IMU_VALID_ROLL_PITCH && ok;
	ok = process_sample(&state, &sample, 104U) == 0U &&
	     tk_rcv_mbf(g_mbf_imu_estimates, &estimate, TMO_POL) == (INT)sizeof(estimate) &&
	     estimate.header.sequence == 2U && ok;
	odometry = test_odometry(2U, 105U);
	ok = tk_snd_mbf(g_mbf_imu_odometry, &odometry, (INT)sizeof(odometry), TMO_POL) == E_OK && ok;
	ok = process_sample(&state, &sample, 105U) == VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_IMU_ODOMETRY && ok;
	ok = drain_test_buffer(g_mbf_imu_odometry) && ok;
	ok = drain_test_buffer(g_mbf_imu_estimates) && ok;
	state = (imu_task_state_t){0};
	ok = imu_producer_init(&state.producer, 10U) && ok;
	state.start_ms = UINT32_MAX - 2U;
	odometry = test_odometry(UINT32_MAX, UINT32_MAX - 1U);
	ok = tk_snd_mbf(g_mbf_imu_odometry, &odometry, (INT)sizeof(odometry), TMO_POL) == E_OK && ok;
	ok = process_sample(&state, &sample, UINT32_MAX) == 0U &&
	     tk_rcv_mbf(g_mbf_imu_estimates, &estimate, TMO_POL) == (INT)sizeof(estimate) && ok;
	odometry = test_odometry(0U, 1U);
	ok = tk_snd_mbf(g_mbf_imu_odometry, &odometry, (INT)sizeof(odometry), TMO_POL) == E_OK && ok;
	ok = process_sample(&state, &sample, 2U) == 0U &&
	     tk_rcv_mbf(g_mbf_imu_estimates, &estimate, TMO_POL) == (INT)sizeof(estimate) &&
	     estimate.header.timestamp_ms == 2U && ok;
	ok = drain_test_buffer(g_mbf_imu_odometry) && ok;
	ok = drain_test_buffer(g_mbf_imu_estimates) && ok;
	return ok;
}

static BOOL task_fault_test(void)
{
	imu_task_state_t state = {0};
	accel_data_t sample = {.az = 9.80665f, .i2c_ok = true};
	wheel_odometry_message_t odometry = test_odometry(1U, 100U);
	BOOL ok;

	ok = imu_producer_init(&state.producer, 10U);
	state.start_ms = 100U;
	ok = process_sample(&state, &sample, 111U) == VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_IMU_ODOMETRY && ok;
	sample.i2c_ok = false;
	ok = process_sample(&state, &sample, 100U) == VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_IMU_SENSOR && ok;
	sample.i2c_ok = true;
	ok = tk_snd_mbf(g_mbf_imu_odometry, &odometry, (INT)sizeof(odometry), TMO_POL) == E_OK && ok;
	ok = process_sample(&state, &sample, 100U) == 0U && ok;
	ok = process_sample(&state, &sample, 100U) == VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_IMU_CLOCK && ok;
	ok = process_sample(&state, &sample, 101U) == 0U && ok;
	ok = process_sample(&state, &sample, 102U) == VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_IMU_DELIVERY &&
	     state.delivery_error == E_TMOUT && ok;
	ok = process_sample(&state, &sample, 111U) == VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_IMU_ODOMETRY && ok;
	ok = drain_test_buffer(g_mbf_imu_odometry) && ok;
	ok = drain_test_buffer(g_mbf_imu_estimates) && ok;
	state = (imu_task_state_t){0};
	ok = imu_producer_init(&state.producer, 10U) && ok;
	state.start_ms = 100U;
	odometry.valid = 0U;
	ok = tk_snd_mbf(g_mbf_imu_odometry, &odometry, (INT)sizeof(odometry), TMO_POL) == E_OK && ok;
	ok = process_sample(&state, &sample, 100U) == VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_IMU_ODOMETRY && ok;
	sample.ax = __builtin_huge_valf();
	ok = process_sample(&state, &sample, 100U) == VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_IMU_SENSOR && ok;
	ok = drain_test_buffer(g_mbf_imu_odometry) && ok;
	ok = drain_test_buffer(g_mbf_imu_estimates) && ok;
	return ok;
}

BOOL imu_task_test(void)
{
	BOOL flow_ok;
	BOOL fault_ok;
	TEST_INIT();

	flow_ok = task_flow_test();
	fault_ok = task_fault_test();
	TEST_CHECK("IMU-U05", flow_ok, "task odometry and estimate delivery");
	TEST_CHECK("IMU-U06", fault_ok, "task sensor, timing and delivery faults");
	TEST_SUMMARY("IMU task");
	return flow_ok && fault_ok;
}

#ifndef IMU_TASK_H
#define IMU_TASK_H

#include <tk/tkernel.h>
#include "imu_types.h"

/* Initialization runs in the task context before the first read. The
 * callbacks and context are exclusively owned by this task and must stay
 * valid for its lifetime. E_OK from read requires a fresh sample and its
 * acquisition timestamp on the tk_get_otm clock. No network APIs. */
typedef ER (*imu_init_t)(void *context);
typedef ER (*imu_read_t)(void *context, accel_data_t *sample,
			 uint32_t *timestamp_ms);

typedef struct {
	RELTIM period_ms;
	uint32_t sample_max_age_ms;
	uint32_t odometry_max_age_ms;
	imu_init_t initialize;
	imu_read_t read;
	void *read_context;
} imu_task_config_t;

/* Called by the single startup owner after buffer creation. No reader is
 * supplied and the task is not started automatically. */

#ifndef IMU_TYPES_H
#define IMU_TYPES_H

#include <stdint.h>
#include <stdbool.h>

typedef struct {
    int16_t raw_x;
    int16_t raw_y;
    int16_t raw_z;
    float   ax;
    float   ay;
    float   az;
    bool    i2c_ok;
} accel_data_t;

/* 3-bit IMU FSM States (0..5 fit in 3 bits) */
typedef enum {
    IMU_STATE_MONITORING   = 0,
    IMU_STATE_HUMP_RISING  = 1,
    IMU_STATE_HUMP_FALLING = 2,
    IMU_STATE_SHOCK        = 3,
    IMU_STATE_TILT         = 4,
    IMU_STATE_FAULT        = 5
} imu_fsm_state_t;

/* 3-bit Motion Events (0..4 fit in 3 bits) */
typedef enum {
    IMU_EVENT_STATIONARY = 0,
    IMU_EVENT_ACCEL      = 1,
    IMU_EVENT_CLIMBING   = 2,
    IMU_EVENT_DESCENDING = 3,
    IMU_EVENT_SHOCK      = 4
} imu_event_type_t;

typedef struct {
    float           hump_peak_m;
    float           current_hump_m;
    bool            hump_active;
    bool            shock_detected; /* collision detection is disabled */
    bool            tilt_detected;  /* latched until imu_processor_init() */
    imu_fsm_state_t fsm_state;
    imu_event_type_t motion_event;

    float           last_distance_m;
    float           hump_start_distance_m;
    float           current_pitch_deg;
    float           current_roll_deg;
    uint32_t        hump_start_ms;
    uint16_t        packed_telemetry; /* 16-bit Minimum-Bit packed word */
} imu_state_t;

/* Minimum-Bit Data Representation (16 bits / 2 bytes total):
 * Bits [2:0]   = fsm_state       (3 bits: 0..5)
 * Bits [5:3]   = motion_event    (3 bits: 0..4)
 * Bits [11:6]  = hump_height_qcm (6 bits: 0..62 -> 0.00..15.50 cm in 0.25 cm steps)
 * Bits [15:12] = pitch_bucket    (4 bits: 0..15 -> -35..+40 deg in 5 deg steps)
 */
static inline uint16_t imu_pack_telemetry(uint8_t state,
                                          uint8_t event,
                                          uint8_t height_qcm,
                                          uint8_t pitch_idx) {
    return (uint16_t)(((uint16_t)(state      & 0x07U))        |
                      ((uint16_t)(event      & 0x07U) << 3U)  |
                      ((uint16_t)(height_qcm & 0x3FU) << 6U)  |
                      ((uint16_t)(pitch_idx  & 0x0FU) << 12U));
}

#endif // IMU_TYPES_H
=== C:\Users\Yong Jun\school\INF2004-Embedded_Systems\project\INF2004_Project\app_program\subsystem4\imu\lsm303dlhc_accel.c ===
#include "lsm303dlhc_accel.h"
#include "imu_processor_config.h"

#include <stddef.h>

bool lsm303dlhc_accel_decode(const uint8_t bytes[6],
			     const lsm303dlhc_axis_map_t *mapping,
			     accel_data_t *sample)
{
	int16_t sensor[3];
	int16_t vehicle[3];
	accel_data_t result = {0};
	unsigned i;

	if (bytes == NULL || mapping == NULL || sample == NULL ||
	    mapping->axis[0] > 2U || mapping->axis[1] > 2U || mapping->axis[2] > 2U ||
	    mapping->axis[0] == mapping->axis[1] ||
	    mapping->axis[0] == mapping->axis[2] ||
	    mapping->axis[1] == mapping->axis[2])
		return false;
	for (i = 0U; i < 3U; i++) {
		uint16_t bits = (uint16_t)bytes[2U * i] |
				((uint16_t)bytes[2U * i + 1U] << 8U);
		int32_t value;
		if ((bits & 0x000FU) != 0U ||
		    (mapping->sign[i] != 1 && mapping->sign[i] != -1))
			return false;
		value = (int32_t)(bits >> 4U);
		if (value >= 2048) value -= 4096;
		sensor[i] = (int16_t)value;
	}
	for (i = 0U; i < 3U; i++)
		vehicle[i] = (int16_t)(sensor[mapping->axis[i]] * mapping->sign[i]);
	result.raw_x = vehicle[0];
	result.raw_y = vehicle[1];
	result.raw_z = vehicle[2];
	result.ax = (float)vehicle[0] *
		    (IMU_ACCEL_SENSITIVITY_2G_G_PER_LSB * GRAVITY_MS2);
	result.ay = (float)vehicle[1] *
		    (IMU_ACCEL_SENSITIVITY_2G_G_PER_LSB * GRAVITY_MS2);
	result.az = (float)vehicle[2] *
		    (IMU_ACCEL_SENSITIVITY_2G_G_PER_LSB * GRAVITY_MS2);
	*sample = result;
	return true;
}
=== C:\Users\Yong Jun\school\INF2004-Embedded_Systems\project\INF2004_Project\app_program\subsystem4\imu\lsm303dlhc_accel.h ===
#ifndef LSM303DLHC_ACCEL_H
#define LSM303DLHC_ACCEL_H

#include "imu_types.h"

typedef struct {
	/* For each vehicle axis, select sensor X=0, Y=1, or Z=2 and sign +1/-1. */
	uint8_t axis[3];
	int8_t sign[3];
} lsm303dlhc_axis_map_t;

/* Decode six bytes from OUT_X_L_A through OUT_Z_H_A. The caller must first
 * verify a successful, fresh six-byte transfer and configure the device for
 * little-endian, normal-mode, +/-2g operation. The sensitivity and default
 * orientation match the original vehicle driver. No I2C access occurs here.
 * The explicit mapping describes sensor orientation relative to the vehicle.
 * The decoder leaves i2c_ok false; only the transport owner may set it after
 * verifying the transfer. On failure, sample is unchanged. */
bool lsm303dlhc_accel_decode(const uint8_t bytes[6],
			     const lsm303dlhc_axis_map_t *mapping,
			     accel_data_t *sample);

#endif
=== C:\Users\Yong Jun\school\INF2004-Embedded_Systems\project\INF2004_Project\app_program\subsystem4\imu\lsm303dlhc_i2c.c ===
#include "lsm303dlhc_i2c.h"

#include "../../../device/include/dev_i2c.h"
#include <tm/tmonitor.h>

#define LSM303DLHC_CTRL_REG1_A    0x20U
#define LSM303DLHC_CTRL_REG4_A    0x23U
#define LSM303DLHC_STATUS_REG_A   0x27U
#define LSM303DLHC_OUT_X_L_A      0x28U
#define LSM303DLHC_AUTO_INCREMENT 0x80U
#define LSM303DLHC_CTRL1_100HZ_XYZ 0x57U
#define LSM303DLHC_CTRL4_NORMAL_2G 0x00U
#define IMU_32_CTRL1_100HZ_XYZ    0x2FU
#define IMU_32_CTRL4_NORMAL_2G_BDU 0x80U
#define LSM303DLHC_ZYXDA          0x08U
#define LSM303DLHC_MAX_READY_WAIT_MS 100U

static lsm303dlhc_i2c_t s_task_sensor;
static lsm303dlhc_i2c_config_t s_task_sensor_config;
static bool s_task_start_attempted;

static ER initialize_task_sensor(void *context)
{
	return lsm303dlhc_i2c_init(context, &s_task_sensor_config);
}

static ER read_fresh(void *context, lsm303dlhc_transfer_t *transfer);

static ER read_registers(lsm303dlhc_i2c_t *sensor, uint8_t reg,
			 uint8_t *data, uint8_t length)
{
	uint8_t address = reg;
	T_I2C_EXEC exec;
	SZ actual = 0;
	ER er;

	exec.sadr = sensor->address;
	exec.snd_size = 1;
	exec.snd_data = &address;
	exec.rcv_size = length;
	exec.rcv_data = data;
	er = tk_swri_dev(sensor->device, TDN_I2C_EXEC, &exec, sizeof(exec), &actual);
	if (er != E_OK) {
		tm_printf((UB*)"IMU I2C read failed: addr=0x%02x reg=0x%02x error=%d\n",
			  (unsigned)sensor->address, (unsigned)reg, (int)er);
		return er;
	}
	if (actual != (SZ)sizeof(exec))
		tm_printf((UB*)"IMU I2C read incomplete: reg=0x%02x size=%d\n",
			  (unsigned)reg, (int)actual);
	return actual == (SZ)sizeof(exec) ? E_OK : E_IO;
}

static ER write_register(lsm303dlhc_i2c_t *sensor, uint8_t reg, uint8_t value)
{
	uint8_t data[2] = {reg, value};
	SZ actual = 0;
	ER er = tk_swri_dev(sensor->device, (W)sensor->address, data, sizeof(data), &actual);

	if (er != E_OK) {
		tm_printf((UB*)"IMU I2C write failed: addr=0x%02x reg=0x%02x error=%d\n",
			  (unsigned)sensor->address, (unsigned)reg, (int)er);
		return er;
	}
	if (actual != (SZ)sizeof(data))
		tm_printf((UB*)"IMU I2C write incomplete: reg=0x%02x size=%d\n",
			  (unsigned)reg, (int)actual);
	return actual == (SZ)sizeof(data) ? E_OK : E_IO;
}

ER lsm303dlhc_i2c_default_config(lsm303dlhc_i2c_config_t *config,
				 uint32_t data_ready_timeout_ms)
{
	lsm303dlhc_i2c_config_t imported = {
		.address = 0x19U,
		.data_ready_timeout_ms = data_ready_timeout_ms,
		.mapping = {{0U, 1U, 2U}, {-1, 1, -1}},
		.profile = LSM303DLHC_I2C_PROFILE_DLHC
	};

	const uint8_t zeroes[6] = {0};
	accel_data_t decoded;

	if (config == NULL || data_ready_timeout_ms == 0U ||
	    data_ready_timeout_ms > LSM303DLHC_MAX_READY_WAIT_MS ||
	    !lsm303dlhc_accel_decode(zeroes, &imported.mapping, &decoded))
		return E_PAR;
	*config = imported;
	return E_OK;
}

static ER fail_init(lsm303dlhc_i2c_t *sensor, ER cause)
{
	ER stop_result = E_OK;
	ER close_result;

	if (sensor->measurement_may_be_active) {
		stop_result = write_register(sensor, LSM303DLHC_CTRL_REG1_A, 0U);
		if (stop_result == E_OK) sensor->measurement_may_be_active = false;
	}
	close_result = tk_cls_dev(sensor->device, 0U);
	if (close_result == E_OK) {
		sensor->device = 0;
		sensor->opened = false;
	}
	sensor->faulted = true;
	if (stop_result != E_OK) return stop_result;
	return close_result != E_OK ? close_result : cause;
}

ER lsm303dlhc_i2c_init(lsm303dlhc_i2c_t *sensor,
		       const lsm303dlhc_i2c_config_t *config)
{
	uint8_t control1, control4, expected_control1, expected_control4;
	const uint8_t zeroes[6] = {0};
	accel_data_t decoded;
	SYSTIM now;
	ER er;

	if (sensor == NULL || config == NULL ||
	    (config->address != 0x18U && config->address != 0x19U) ||
	    (config->profile != LSM303DLHC_I2C_PROFILE_DLHC &&
	     config->profile != LSM303DLHC_I2C_PROFILE_32_FAMILY) ||
	    config->data_ready_timeout_ms == 0U ||
	    config->data_ready_timeout_ms > LSM303DLHC_MAX_READY_WAIT_MS ||
	    !lsm303dlhc_accel_decode(zeroes, &config->mapping, &decoded))
		return E_PAR;
	if (sensor->opened || sensor->initialized || sensor->faulted ||
	    sensor->reader.initialized)
		return E_OBJ;
	expected_control1 = config->profile == LSM303DLHC_I2C_PROFILE_32_FAMILY ?
		IMU_32_CTRL1_100HZ_XYZ : LSM303DLHC_CTRL1_100HZ_XYZ;
	expected_control4 = config->profile == LSM303DLHC_I2C_PROFILE_32_FAMILY ?
		IMU_32_CTRL4_NORMAL_2G_BDU : LSM303DLHC_CTRL4_NORMAL_2G;
	sensor->device = tk_opn_dev((const UB *)"iicb", TD_UPDATE | TD_EXCL);
	if (sensor->device < E_OK) return sensor->device;
	sensor->opened = true;
	sensor->address = config->address;
	sensor->data_ready_timeout_ms = config->data_ready_timeout_ms;
	er = write_register(sensor, LSM303DLHC_CTRL_REG1_A, 0U);
	if (er != E_OK) return fail_init(sensor, er);
	er = write_register(sensor, LSM303DLHC_CTRL_REG4_A, expected_control4);
	if (er != E_OK) return fail_init(sensor, er);
	er = tk_get_otm(&now);
	if (er != E_OK) return fail_init(sensor, er);
	sensor->next_sample_not_before_ms = now.lo;
	sensor->measurement_may_be_active = true;
	er = write_register(sensor, LSM303DLHC_CTRL_REG1_A, expected_control1);
	if (er != E_OK) return fail_init(sensor, er);
	er = read_registers(sensor, LSM303DLHC_CTRL_REG1_A, &control1, 1U);
	if (er != E_OK) return fail_init(sensor, er);
	er = read_registers(sensor, LSM303DLHC_CTRL_REG4_A, &control4, 1U);
	if (er != E_OK) return fail_init(sensor, er);
	if (control1 != expected_control1 || control4 != expected_control4) {
		tm_printf((UB*)"IMU control readback mismatch: CTRL1=0x%02x CTRL4=0x%02x\n",
			  (unsigned)control1, (unsigned)control4);
		return fail_init(sensor, E_IO);
	}
	er = lsm303dlhc_reader_init(&sensor->reader, &config->mapping,
				    read_fresh, sensor);
	if (er != E_OK) return fail_init(sensor, er);
	sensor->initialized = true;
	sensor->faulted = false;
	return E_OK;
}

ER lsm303dlhc_i2c_start_imu_task(const lsm303dlhc_i2c_config_t *sensor_config,
				const imu_task_config_t *task_config)
{
	imu_task_config_t task;
	ER er;

	if (sensor_config == NULL || task_config == NULL) return E_PAR;
	if (s_task_start_attempted) return E_OBJ;
	s_task_sensor_config = *sensor_config;
	task = *task_config;
	task.initialize = initialize_task_sensor;
	task.read = lsm303dlhc_i2c_read;
	task.read_context = &s_task_sensor;
	er = imu_task_start(&task);
	if (er == E_OK) s_task_start_attempted = true;
	return er;
}

static ER read_fresh(void *context, lsm303dlhc_transfer_t *transfer)
{
	lsm303dlhc_i2c_t *sensor = context;
	uint32_t wait_start, next_timestamp;
	SYSTIM now;
	uint8_t status;
	ER er;

	er = tk_get_otm(&now);
	if (er != E_OK) return er;
	wait_start = now.lo;
	for (;;) {
		er = read_registers(sensor, LSM303DLHC_STATUS_REG_A, &status, 1U);
		if (er != E_OK) return er;
		if (status & LSM303DLHC_ZYXDA) break;
		er = tk_get_otm(&now);
		if (er != E_OK) return er;
		if (now.lo - wait_start >= sensor->data_ready_timeout_ms) {
			tm_printf((UB*)"IMU data-ready timeout: status=0x%02x elapsed=%u ms limit=%u ms\n",
				  (unsigned)status, (unsigned)(now.lo - wait_start),
				  (unsigned)sensor->data_ready_timeout_ms);
			return E_TMOUT;
		}
		er = tk_dly_tsk(1U);
		if (er != E_OK) return er;
	}
	er = tk_get_otm(&now);
	if (er != E_OK) return er;
	next_timestamp = now.lo;
	er = read_registers(sensor, LSM303DLHC_OUT_X_L_A | LSM303DLHC_AUTO_INCREMENT,
			    transfer->bytes, sizeof(transfer->bytes));
	if (er != E_OK) return er;
	transfer->length = sizeof(transfer->bytes);
	transfer->fresh = true;
	transfer->timestamp_valid = true;
	transfer->timestamp_ms = sensor->next_sample_not_before_ms;
	sensor->next_sample_not_before_ms = next_timestamp;
	return E_OK;
}

ER lsm303dlhc_i2c_read(void *context, accel_data_t *sample, uint32_t *timestamp_ms)
{
	lsm303dlhc_i2c_t *sensor = context;
	ER er;

	if (sensor == NULL || sample == NULL || timestamp_ms == NULL) return E_PAR;
	if (!sensor->initialized || sensor->faulted) return E_OBJ;
	er = lsm303dlhc_reader_read(&sensor->reader, sample, timestamp_ms);
	if (er != E_OK) sensor->faulted = true;
	return er;
}

ER lsm303dlhc_i2c_close(lsm303dlhc_i2c_t *sensor)
{
	ER er, power_down_result = E_OK;

	if (sensor == NULL) return E_PAR;
	if (!sensor->opened) return E_OBJ;
	if (sensor->measurement_may_be_active) {
		power_down_result = write_register(sensor, LSM303DLHC_CTRL_REG1_A, 0U);
		if (power_down_result == E_OK) {
			sensor->measurement_may_be_active = false;
		} else {
			sensor->faulted = true;
		}
	}
	er = tk_cls_dev(sensor->device, 0U);
	if (er != E_OK) return er;
	sensor->opened = false;
	sensor->initialized = false;
	sensor->device = 0;
	return power_down_result;
}

#ifndef LSM303DLHC_I2C_H

typedef enum {
	LSM303DLHC_I2C_PROFILE_DLHC = 0,
	LSM303DLHC_I2C_PROFILE_32_FAMILY
} lsm303dlhc_i2c_profile_t;

typedef struct {
	uint8_t address;
	uint32_t data_ready_timeout_ms;
	lsm303dlhc_axis_map_t mapping;
	lsm303dlhc_i2c_profile_t profile;
} lsm303dlhc_i2c_config_t;

typedef struct {
	lsm303dlhc_reader_t reader;
	ID device;
	uint8_t address;
	uint32_t data_ready_timeout_ms;
	uint32_t next_sample_not_before_ms;
	bool opened;
	bool initialized;
	bool measurement_may_be_active;
	bool faulted;
} lsm303dlhc_i2c_t;

/* Import the original DLHC profile, address, orientation and Â±2g behavior.
 * The readiness timeout is caller-selected and must be 1..100 ms. */
ER lsm303dlhc_i2c_default_config(lsm303dlhc_i2c_config_t *config,
				 uint32_t data_ready_timeout_ms);

/* Pass a zero-initialized context. Opens the kernel I2C1 device "iicb"
 * exclusively and configures the selected accelerometer profile for 100 Hz,
 * normal-mode +/-2g operation. The 0x32-family profile is experimental and
 * requires hardware validation; it does not verify identity. The caller owns
 * this context until close or reboot. I2C must be enabled in config_device.h
 * and the bus/device must be
 * exclusively owned. */
ER lsm303dlhc_i2c_init(lsm303dlhc_i2c_t *sensor,
		       const lsm303dlhc_i2c_config_t *config);

/* Convenience startup path. Sensor initialization and reads run through the
 * IMU task using a static-lifetime context. This function is not called by
 * default. */
ER lsm303dlhc_i2c_start_imu_task(const lsm303dlhc_i2c_config_t *sensor_config,
				const imu_task_config_t *task_config);

/* Compatible with imu_task_config_t.read. Waits for fresh XYZ data with a
 * bounded data-ready wait; kernel I2C transaction timeout is separate.
 * Any transport/configuration error latches this reader until restart. */
ER lsm303dlhc_i2c_read(void *context, accel_data_t *sample, uint32_t *timestamp_ms);

/* Call only after the IMU task has stopped using the context. */
ER lsm303dlhc_i2c_close(lsm303dlhc_i2c_t *sensor);

=== C:\Users\Yong Jun\school\INF2004-Embedded_Systems\project\INF2004_Project\app_program\subsystem4\imu\lsm303dlhc_reader.c ===
#include "lsm303dlhc_reader.h"

ER lsm303dlhc_reader_init(lsm303dlhc_reader_t *reader,
			  const lsm303dlhc_axis_map_t *mapping,
			  lsm303dlhc_read_fresh_t read_fresh, void *transport_context)
{
	const uint8_t zero[6] = {0};
	accel_data_t decoded;
	lsm303dlhc_reader_t initial = {0};

	if (reader == NULL || mapping == NULL || read_fresh == NULL ||
	    !lsm303dlhc_accel_decode(zero, mapping, &decoded))
		return E_PAR;
	if (reader->initialized) return E_OBJ;
	initial.mapping = *mapping;
	initial.read_fresh = read_fresh;
	initial.transport_context = transport_context;
	initial.initialized = true;
	*reader = initial;
	return E_OK;
}

ER lsm303dlhc_reader_read(void *context, accel_data_t *sample, uint32_t *timestamp_ms)
{
	lsm303dlhc_reader_t *reader = context;
	lsm303dlhc_transfer_t transfer = {0};
	accel_data_t decoded;
	ER result;

	if (reader == NULL || sample == NULL || timestamp_ms == NULL) return E_PAR;
	if (!reader->initialized || reader->faulted) return E_OBJ;
	result = reader->read_fresh(reader->transport_context, &transfer);
	if (result != E_OK || transfer.length != sizeof(transfer.bytes) ||
	    !transfer.fresh || !transfer.timestamp_valid ||
	    !lsm303dlhc_accel_decode(transfer.bytes, &reader->mapping, &decoded)) {
		reader->faulted = true;
		return result < E_OK ? result : E_IO;
	}
	decoded.i2c_ok = true;
	*sample = decoded;
	*timestamp_ms = transfer.timestamp_ms;
	return E_OK;
}
=== C:\Users\Yong Jun\school\INF2004-Embedded_Systems\project\INF2004_Project\app_program\subsystem4\imu\lsm303dlhc_reader.h ===
#ifndef LSM303DLHC_READER_H
#define LSM303DLHC_READER_H

#include <tk/tkernel.h>
#include "lsm303dlhc_accel.h"

typedef struct {
	uint8_t bytes[6];
	uint8_t length;
	bool fresh;
	bool timestamp_valid;
	uint32_t timestamp_ms;
} lsm303dlhc_transfer_t;

/* The transport exclusively owns the bus and must verify the sensor is
 * configured for the decoder's mode. E_OK with fresh=true means it verified
 * a new sample and a complete transfer, not merely a successful bus call.
 * The timestamp must be on the tk_get_otm clock and no later than acquisition.
 * It may block for its documented sensor-ready and kernel I2C timeouts; it
 * must not use network APIs. */
typedef ER (*lsm303dlhc_read_fresh_t)(void *context, lsm303dlhc_transfer_t *transfer);

typedef struct {
	lsm303dlhc_axis_map_t mapping;
	lsm303dlhc_read_fresh_t read_fresh;
	void *transport_context;
	bool initialized;
	bool faulted;
} lsm303dlhc_reader_t;

/* Single owner: initialize a zeroed reader once, after reserving the bus.
 * This adapter neither configures nor accesses the sensor or bus itself. */
ER lsm303dlhc_reader_init(lsm303dlhc_reader_t *reader,
			  const lsm303dlhc_axis_map_t *mapping,
			  lsm303dlhc_read_fresh_t read_fresh, void *transport_context);

/* Compatible with imu_read_t. Errors leave outputs unchanged and latch the
 * reader faulted until restart. The IMU task independently checks timestamps. */
ER lsm303dlhc_reader_read(void *context, accel_data_t *sample, uint32_t *timestamp_ms);

#endif

#include "hcsr04.h"

#include "obs_profile_config.h"

#define HCSR04_MIN_SOUND_SPEED_MM_S 300000U
#define HCSR04_MAX_SOUND_SPEED_MM_S 400000U
#define HCSR04_ROUND_TRIP_DIVISOR 2000000ULL

bool hcsr04_distance_mm(uint32_t echo_high_us, uint32_t sound_speed_mm_s,
			uint16_t *distance_mm)
{
	uint64_t distance;

	if (distance_mm == 0 || echo_high_us == 0U ||
	    sound_speed_mm_s < HCSR04_MIN_SOUND_SPEED_MM_S ||
	    sound_speed_mm_s > HCSR04_MAX_SOUND_SPEED_MM_S)
		return false;
	distance = ((uint64_t)echo_high_us * sound_speed_mm_s +
		    HCSR04_ROUND_TRIP_DIVISOR / 2U) /
		   HCSR04_ROUND_TRIP_DIVISOR;
	if (distance == 0U || distance >= OBS_DIST_NONE ||
	    distance > UINT16_MAX)
		return false;
	*distance_mm = (uint16_t)distance;
	return true;
}

/* Convert the measured Echo high-pulse width to round-trip-corrected
 * millimetres. Caller supplies sound speed in mm/s (for example 343000 at
 * approximately 20 C). The output is unchanged for invalid/unrepresentable
 * input and OBS_DIST_NONE is never returned as a distance. */
bool hcsr04_distance_mm(uint32_t echo_high_us, uint32_t sound_speed_mm_s,
			uint16_t *distance_mm);
