=== C:\Users\Yong Jun\school\INF2004-Embedded_Systems\project\INF2004_Project\app_program\subsystem6\odometry\odometry_producer.c ===
#include "odometry_producer.h"

#include <limits.h>
#include <math.h>
#include <stddef.h>

static uint32_t signed_bits(int32_t count, int8_t sign)
{
	uint32_t bits = (uint32_t)count;
	return (sign > 0) ? bits : 0U - bits;
}

static int32_t bits_to_signed(uint32_t bits)
{
	return (bits <= INT32_MAX) ? (int32_t)bits :
	       INT32_MIN + (int32_t)(bits - 0x80000000U);
}

static bool add_delta(int64_t total, int32_t delta, int64_t *result)
{
	if ((delta > 0 && total > INT64_MAX - delta) ||
	    (delta < 0 && total < INT64_MIN - delta)) return false;
	*result = total + delta;
	return true;
}

bool odometry_producer_init(odometry_producer_t *producer,
                            const odometry_producer_config_t *config,
                            int32_t left_count, int32_t right_count,
                            uint32_t timestamp_ms)
{
	double distance;
	odometry_producer_t initial = {0};

	if (producer == NULL || config == NULL ||
	    (config->left_sign != 1 && config->left_sign != -1) ||
	    (config->right_sign != 1 && config->right_sign != -1) ||
	    !wheel_odometry_distance_precise(&config->left, 0, &distance) ||
	    !wheel_odometry_distance_precise(&config->right, 0, &distance)) return false;
	initial.config = *config;
	initial.last_left = signed_bits(left_count, config->left_sign);
	initial.last_right = signed_bits(right_count, config->right_sign);
	initial.last_timestamp_ms = timestamp_ms;
	initial.initialized = true;
	*producer = initial;
	return true;
}

bool odometry_producer_update(odometry_producer_t *producer,
                              int32_t left_count, int32_t right_count,
                              uint32_t timestamp_ms,
                              wheel_odometry_message_t *message)
{
	uint32_t elapsed;
	uint32_t left_bits, right_bits;
	int32_t left_delta, right_delta;
	int64_t left_total, right_total;
	double left_cm, right_cm;
	double distance_mm;
	wheel_odometry_message_t next = {0};

	if (producer == NULL || message == NULL || !producer->initialized)
		return false;
	elapsed = timestamp_ms - producer->last_timestamp_ms;
	if (elapsed == 0U || elapsed > INT32_MAX) return false;
	left_bits = signed_bits(left_count, producer->config.left_sign);
	right_bits = signed_bits(right_count, producer->config.right_sign);
	if (!wheel_odometry_count_delta(left_bits, producer->last_left, &left_delta) ||
	    !wheel_odometry_count_delta(right_bits, producer->last_right, &right_delta) ||
	    !add_delta(producer->left_total, left_delta, &left_total) ||
	    !add_delta(producer->right_total, right_delta, &right_total) ||
	    !wheel_odometry_distance_precise(&producer->config.left, left_total, &left_cm) ||
	    !wheel_odometry_distance_precise(&producer->config.right, right_total, &right_cm))
		return false;

	distance_mm = (left_cm + right_cm) * 5.0;
	if (!isfinite(distance_mm) || distance_mm > INT32_MAX ||
	    distance_mm < INT32_MIN) return false;

	next.header.type = VEHICLE_MESSAGE_WHEEL_ODOMETRY;
	next.header.version = VEHICLE_MESSAGE_VERSION;
	next.header.sequence = producer->sequence + 1U;
	next.header.timestamp_ms = timestamp_ms;
	next.left_count = bits_to_signed(left_bits);
	next.right_count = bits_to_signed(right_bits);
	next.distance_mm = (int32_t)distance_mm;
	next.valid = 1U;

	producer->last_left = left_bits;
	producer->last_right = right_bits;
	producer->left_total = left_total;
	producer->right_total = right_total;
	producer->last_timestamp_ms = timestamp_ms;
	producer->sequence = next.header.sequence;
	*message = next;
	return true;
}

#ifndef ODOMETRY_PRODUCER_H
#define ODOMETRY_PRODUCER_H

#include "wheel_odometry.h"
#include "../../vehicle_messages.h"

typedef struct {
	wheel_odometry_config_t left;
	wheel_odometry_config_t right;
	int8_t left_sign;
	int8_t right_sign;
} odometry_producer_config_t;

typedef struct {
	odometry_producer_config_t config;
	uint32_t last_left;
	uint32_t last_right;
	int64_t left_total;
	int64_t right_total;
	uint32_t last_timestamp_ms;
	uint32_t sequence;
	bool initialized;
} odometry_producer_t;

/* Calibration must be supplied by the owning task; signs are +1 or -1.
 * The initial snapshot defines zero distance. Input counts are wrapping
 * encoder values; output counts are polarity-normalized absolute counts.
 * Call only from the single owning task. */
bool odometry_producer_init(odometry_producer_t *producer,
                            const odometry_producer_config_t *config,
                            int32_t left_count, int32_t right_count,
                            uint32_t timestamp_ms);

/* On failure neither state nor message changes. Samples must be ordered:
 * consecutive timestamps and per-wheel count changes must be less than
 * half their respective 32-bit wrap periods. The owner must report failures
 * and consumers must check timestamp freshness and sequence gaps. */
bool odometry_producer_update(odometry_producer_t *producer,
                              int32_t left_count, int32_t right_count,
                              uint32_t timestamp_ms,
                              wheel_odometry_message_t *message);

#endif

#include "../../app.h"
#include "../../vehicle_buffers.h"

static BOOL calculation_test(void)
{
	odometry_producer_t state;
	const odometry_producer_config_t config = {{2500U, 20.0f}, {2500U, 20.0f}, 1, -1};
	wheel_odometry_message_t message;

	return odometry_producer_init(&state, &config, 0, 0, 10U) &&
	       odometry_producer_update(&state, 2500, -2500, 110U, &message) &&
	       message.header.type == VEHICLE_MESSAGE_WHEEL_ODOMETRY &&
	       message.header.version == VEHICLE_MESSAGE_VERSION &&
	       message.header.sequence == 1U && message.header.timestamp_ms == 110U &&
	       message.left_count == 2500 && message.right_count == 2500 &&
	       message.distance_mm == 200 && message.valid == 1U &&
	       odometry_producer_update(&state, 1250, -1250, 210U, &message) &&
	       message.header.sequence == 2U && message.distance_mm == 100;
}

static BOOL boundary_test(void)
{
	odometry_producer_t state;
	odometry_producer_config_t config = {{2500U, 20.0f}, {2500U, 20.0f}, 1, -1};
	wheel_odometry_message_t message = {0};

	if (!odometry_producer_init(&state, &config, INT32_MAX - 1, INT32_MIN + 2,
	                            UINT32_MAX - 50U) ||
	    !odometry_producer_update(&state, INT32_MIN + 1, INT32_MAX, 49U, &message) ||
	    state.left_total != 3 || state.right_total != 3 ||
	    message.left_count != INT32_MIN + 1 || message.right_count != INT32_MIN + 1)
		return false;
	if (odometry_producer_update(&state, INT32_MIN + 1, INT32_MAX, 49U, &message) ||
	    odometry_producer_update(&state, INT32_MIN + 1, INT32_MAX, 48U, &message) ||
	    state.sequence != 1U || message.header.sequence != 1U)
		return false;
	if (odometry_producer_update(&state, 1, INT32_MAX, 50U, &message) ||
	    state.sequence != 1U || message.header.sequence != 1U)
		return false;
	config.right_sign = 0;
	if (odometry_producer_init(&state, &config, 0, 0, 0U)) return false;
	config.right_sign = -1;
	config.left.counts_per_revolution = 0U;
	if (odometry_producer_init(&state, &config, 0, 0, 0U)) return false;
	config.left.counts_per_revolution = 2500U;
	if (!odometry_producer_init(&state, &config, 0, 0, 0U)) return false;
	state.left_total = INT64_MAX;
	if (odometry_producer_update(&state, 1, 0, 1U, &message) ||
	    state.left_total != INT64_MAX || state.sequence != 0U)
		return false;
	state.left_total = INT64_MAX / 2;
	state.right_total = INT64_MAX / 2;
	if (odometry_producer_update(&state, 1, -1, 1U, &message) ||
	    state.left_total != INT64_MAX / 2 || state.sequence != 0U)
		return false;
	config.left.counts_per_revolution = 1U;
	config.right.counts_per_revolution = 1U;
	config.left.wheel_circumference_cm = 1.0f;
	config.right.wheel_circumference_cm = 1.0f;
	return odometry_producer_init(&state, &config, 0, 0, 0U) &&
	       odometry_producer_update(&state, 16777217, -16777217, 1U, &message) &&
	       message.distance_mm == 167772170;
}

static BOOL drain_buffer(ID buffer)
{
	wheel_odometry_message_t message;
	INT result;
	do {
		result = tk_rcv_mbf(buffer, &message, TMO_POL);
	} while (result > 0);
	return result == E_TMOUT;
}

static BOOL publish_test(void)
{
	wheel_odometry_message_t sent = {0}, line = {0}, imu = {0};
	odometry_producer_t producer;
	const odometry_producer_config_t config = {{2500U, 20.0f}, {2500U, 20.0f}, 1, -1};
	odometry_publish_result_t result;
	BOOL ok;
	unsigned i;

	if (!odometry_producer_init(&producer, &config, 0, 0, 0U) ||
	    !odometry_producer_update(&producer, 2500, -2500, 100U, &sent))
		return FALSE;
	result = odometry_publish(&sent);
	sent.left_count = 0;
	sent.distance_mm = 0;
	ok = result.line == E_OK && result.imu == E_OK;
	ok = tk_rcv_mbf(g_mbf_line_odometry, &line, TMO_POL) == (INT)sizeof(line) && ok;
	ok = tk_rcv_mbf(g_mbf_imu_odometry, &imu, TMO_POL) == (INT)sizeof(imu) && ok;
	ok = ok && line.header.sequence == 1U && imu.header.sequence == 1U &&
	     line.left_count == 2500 && imu.left_count == 2500 &&
	     line.distance_mm == 200 && imu.distance_mm == 200;

	for (i = 0U; i < VEHICLE_MB_ODOMETRY_CAPACITY; i++) {
		if (tk_snd_mbf(g_mbf_line_odometry, &sent, (INT)sizeof(sent), TMO_POL) != E_OK)
			ok = FALSE;
	}
	result = odometry_publish(&sent);
	ok = result.line == E_TMOUT && result.imu == E_OK && ok;
	ok = tk_rcv_mbf(g_mbf_imu_odometry, &imu, TMO_POL) == (INT)sizeof(imu) && ok;
	ok = drain_buffer(g_mbf_line_odometry) && ok;
	for (i = 0U; i < VEHICLE_MB_ODOMETRY_CAPACITY; i++) {
		if (tk_snd_mbf(g_mbf_imu_odometry, &sent, (INT)sizeof(sent), TMO_POL) != E_OK)
			ok = FALSE;
	}
	result = odometry_publish(&sent);
	ok = result.line == E_OK && result.imu == E_TMOUT && ok;
	ok = tk_rcv_mbf(g_mbf_line_odometry, &line, TMO_POL) == (INT)sizeof(line) && ok;
	sent.valid = 0U;
	result = odometry_publish(&sent);
	ok = result.line == E_PAR && result.imu == E_PAR && ok;
	result = odometry_publish(NULL);
	ok = result.line == E_PAR && result.imu == E_PAR && ok;
	ok = drain_buffer(g_mbf_line_odometry) && ok;
	ok = drain_buffer(g_mbf_imu_odometry) && ok;
	return ok;
}

static BOOL motion_publish_test(void)
{
	wheel_odometry_message_t sent = {0}, line, imu, motion;
	odometry_publish_result_t result;
	BOOL ok;
	unsigned i;

	sent.header.type = VEHICLE_MESSAGE_WHEEL_ODOMETRY;
	sent.header.version = VEHICLE_MESSAGE_VERSION;
	sent.header.sequence = 7U;
	sent.left_count = 123;
	sent.valid = 1U;
	result = odometry_publish(&sent);
	ok = result.line == E_OK && result.imu == E_OK && !result.motion_attempted;
	ok = tk_rcv_mbf(g_mbf_line_odometry, &line, TMO_POL) == (INT)sizeof(line) && ok;
	ok = tk_rcv_mbf(g_mbf_imu_odometry, &imu, TMO_POL) == (INT)sizeof(imu) && ok;
	ok = tk_rcv_mbf(g_mbf_motion_odometry, &motion, TMO_POL) == E_TMOUT && ok;
	result = odometry_publish_with_motion(&sent);
	sent.left_count = 456;
	ok = result.line == E_OK && result.imu == E_OK && result.motion_attempted &&
	     result.motion == E_OK && ok;
	ok = tk_rcv_mbf(g_mbf_line_odometry, &line, TMO_POL) == (INT)sizeof(line) && ok;
	ok = tk_rcv_mbf(g_mbf_imu_odometry, &imu, TMO_POL) == (INT)sizeof(imu) && ok;
	ok = tk_rcv_mbf(g_mbf_motion_odometry, &motion, TMO_POL) == (INT)sizeof(motion) &&
	     line.left_count == 123 && imu.left_count == 123 && motion.left_count == 123 && ok;
	for (i = 0U; i < VEHICLE_MB_ODOMETRY_CAPACITY; i++) {
		if (tk_snd_mbf(g_mbf_motion_odometry, &sent, (INT)sizeof(sent), TMO_POL) != E_OK)
			ok = FALSE;
	}
	result = odometry_publish_with_motion(&sent);
	ok = result.line == E_OK && result.imu == E_OK && result.motion_attempted &&
	     result.motion == E_TMOUT && ok;
	ok = tk_rcv_mbf(g_mbf_line_odometry, &line, TMO_POL) == (INT)sizeof(line) && ok;
	ok = tk_rcv_mbf(g_mbf_imu_odometry, &imu, TMO_POL) == (INT)sizeof(imu) && ok;
	ok = drain_buffer(g_mbf_motion_odometry) && ok;
	for (i = 0U; i < VEHICLE_MB_ODOMETRY_CAPACITY; i++) {
		if (tk_snd_mbf(g_mbf_line_odometry, &sent, (INT)sizeof(sent), TMO_POL) != E_OK)
			ok = FALSE;
	}
	result = odometry_publish_with_motion(&sent);
	ok = result.line == E_TMOUT && result.imu == E_OK &&
	     result.motion_attempted && result.motion == E_OK && ok;
	ok = tk_rcv_mbf(g_mbf_imu_odometry, &imu, TMO_POL) == (INT)sizeof(imu) && ok;
	ok = tk_rcv_mbf(g_mbf_motion_odometry, &motion, TMO_POL) == (INT)sizeof(motion) && ok;
	sent.valid = 0U;
	result = odometry_publish_with_motion(&sent);
	ok = result.line == E_PAR && result.imu == E_PAR &&
	     !result.motion_attempted && ok;
	ok = drain_buffer(g_mbf_line_odometry) && ok;
	ok = drain_buffer(g_mbf_imu_odometry) && ok;
	ok = drain_buffer(g_mbf_motion_odometry) && ok;
	return ok;
}

BOOL odometry_producer_test(void)
{
	BOOL calculation_ok;
	BOOL boundary_ok;
	BOOL publish_ok;
	BOOL motion_ok;
	TEST_INIT();

	calculation_ok = calculation_test();
	boundary_ok = boundary_test();
	publish_ok = publish_test();
	motion_ok = motion_publish_test();
	TEST_CHECK("ODO-U04", calculation_ok, "signed wheel counts and distance");
	TEST_CHECK("ODO-U05", boundary_ok, "wrap and invalid sample rejection");
	TEST_CHECK("INT-04", publish_ok, "independent odometry buffer delivery");
	TEST_CHECK("INT-05", motion_ok, "optional motion odometry delivery");
	TEST_SUMMARY("odometry producer");
	return calculation_ok && boundary_ok && publish_ok && motion_ok;
}

=== C:\Users\Yong Jun\school\INF2004-Embedded_Systems\project\INF2004_Project\app_program\subsystem6\odometry\odometry_publish.c ===
#include "../../app.h"
#include "../../vehicle_buffers.h"
#include "odometry_publish.h"

static odometry_publish_result_t publish(const wheel_odometry_message_t *message,
					 bool include_motion)
{
	odometry_publish_result_t result = {E_PAR, E_PAR, E_PAR, false};

	if (message == NULL || message->header.type != VEHICLE_MESSAGE_WHEEL_ODOMETRY ||
	    message->header.version != VEHICLE_MESSAGE_VERSION || message->valid != 1U)
		return result;
	result.line = tk_snd_mbf(g_mbf_line_odometry, message,
	                         (INT)sizeof(*message), TMO_POL);
	result.imu = tk_snd_mbf(g_mbf_imu_odometry, message,
	                        (INT)sizeof(*message), TMO_POL);
	if (include_motion) {
		result.motion_attempted = true;
		result.motion = tk_snd_mbf(g_mbf_motion_odometry, message,
					   (INT)sizeof(*message), TMO_POL);
	}
	return result;
}

odometry_publish_result_t odometry_publish(const wheel_odometry_message_t *message)
{
	return publish(message, false);
}

odometry_publish_result_t odometry_publish_with_motion(const wheel_odometry_message_t *message)
{
	return publish(message, true);
}
=== C:\Users\Yong Jun\school\INF2004-Embedded_Systems\project\INF2004_Project\app_program\subsystem6\odometry\odometry_publish.h ===
#ifndef ODOMETRY_PUBLISH_H
#define ODOMETRY_PUBLISH_H

#include <tk/tkernel.h>
#include "../../vehicle_messages.h"

typedef struct {
	ER line;
	ER imu;
	ER motion;
	bool motion_attempted;
} odometry_publish_result_t;

/* Line and IMU sends are attempted independently. Motion is not sent by this
 * function; motion_attempted is false and motion is not a delivery result.
 * The caller must handle
 * each result, including a full buffer; this function never discards messages. */
odometry_publish_result_t odometry_publish(const wheel_odometry_message_t *message);

/* Also attempts the dedicated motion buffer for a valid message. All sends
 * are non-blocking and independent, so partial delivery is possible. */
odometry_publish_result_t odometry_publish_with_motion(const wheel_odometry_message_t *message);

#endif
=== C:\Users\Yong Jun\school\INF2004-Embedded_Systems\project\INF2004_Project\app_program\subsystem6\odometry\odometry_task.c ===
#include "../../app.h"
#include "../../vehicle_buffers.h"
#include "encoder_source.h"
#include "odometry_publish.h"
#include "odometry_task.h"

#define ODOMETRY_TASK_PRIORITY 10
#define ODOMETRY_FAULT_RETRY_MS 1000U

static odometry_producer_config_t s_config;
static RELTIM s_period_ms;
static bool s_started;
static bool s_publish_motion;

static uint16_t process_snapshot(odometry_producer_t *producer,
				 const encoder_snapshot_t *snapshot,
				 uint32_t timestamp_ms,
				 bool publish_motion,
				 odometry_publish_result_t *delivery)
{
	wheel_odometry_message_t message;

	if (!snapshot->valid) return VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_ODOMETRY_SOURCE;
	if (!odometry_producer_update(producer, snapshot->left_count,
				      snapshot->right_count, timestamp_ms, &message))
		return VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_ODOMETRY_CALCULATION;
	*delivery = publish_motion ? odometry_publish_with_motion(&message) :
				    odometry_publish(&message);
	if (delivery->line != E_OK || delivery->imu != E_OK ||
	    (publish_motion && (!delivery->motion_attempted || delivery->motion != E_OK)))
		return VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_ODOMETRY_DELIVERY;
	return 0U;
}

static void report_fault(uint16_t code, uint32_t timestamp_ms)
{
	fault_message_t fault = {0};
	bool sent = false;

	fault.header.type = VEHICLE_MESSAGE_FAULT;
	fault.header.version = VEHICLE_MESSAGE_VERSION;
	fault.header.sequence = 1U;
	fault.header.timestamp_ms = timestamp_ms;
	fault.source = code == VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_ODOMETRY_DELIVERY ?
		       VEHICLE_FAULT_COMMUNICATION :
		       (code == VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_ODOMETRY_CLOCK ||
			code == VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_ODOMETRY_SCHEDULE ?
			VEHICLE_FAULT_SYSTEM : VEHICLE_FAULT_SENSOR);
	fault.code = code;
	fault.recoverable = 0U;
	tm_printf((UB*)"odometry stopped; fault code %u\n", (unsigned)code);

	for (;;) {
		ER er;
		if (!sent) {
			er = tk_snd_mbf(g_mbf_faults, &fault, (INT)sizeof(fault), TMO_POL);
			if (er == E_OK) {
				sent = true;
			} else {
				tm_printf((UB*)"odometry fault delivery failed: %d\n", (int)er);
			}
		}
		er = tk_dly_tsk(ODOMETRY_FAULT_RETRY_MS);
		if (er < E_OK) {
			tm_printf((UB*)"odometry fault retry delay failed: %d\n", (int)er);
			er = tk_slp_tsk(TMO_FEVR);
			tm_printf((UB*)"odometry fault sleep returned: %d\n", (int)er);
		}
	}
}

static void odometry_task(INT stacd, void *exinf)
{
	odometry_producer_t producer = {0};
	encoder_snapshot_t snapshot;
	odometry_publish_result_t delivery = {0};
	SYSTIM now;
	uint32_t last_timestamp = 0U;
	uint16_t fault;
	ER er;

	(void)stacd;
	(void)exinf;
	er = encoder_source_snapshot(&snapshot);
	if (er < E_OK || !snapshot.valid) {
		tm_printf((UB*)"odometry initial snapshot failed: %d\n", (int)er);
		report_fault(VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_ODOMETRY_SOURCE, last_timestamp);
	}
	er = tk_get_otm(&now);
	if (er < E_OK) {
		tm_printf((UB*)"odometry clock failed: %d\n", (int)er);
		report_fault(VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_ODOMETRY_CLOCK, last_timestamp);
	}
	last_timestamp = now.lo;
	if (!odometry_producer_init(&producer, &s_config, snapshot.left_count,
				    snapshot.right_count, last_timestamp))
		report_fault(VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_ODOMETRY_CALCULATION, last_timestamp);

	for (;;) {
		er = tk_dly_tsk(s_period_ms);
		if (er < E_OK) {
			tm_printf((UB*)"odometry delay failed: %d\n", (int)er);
			report_fault(VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_ODOMETRY_SCHEDULE, last_timestamp);
		}
		er = encoder_source_snapshot(&snapshot);
		if (er < E_OK) {
			tm_printf((UB*)"odometry snapshot failed: %d\n", (int)er);
			report_fault(VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_ODOMETRY_SOURCE, last_timestamp);
		}
		er = tk_get_otm(&now);
		if (er < E_OK) {
			tm_printf((UB*)"odometry clock failed: %d\n", (int)er);
			report_fault(VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_ODOMETRY_CLOCK, last_timestamp);
		}
		last_timestamp = now.lo;
		fault = process_snapshot(&producer, &snapshot, last_timestamp,
					 s_publish_motion, &delivery);
		if (fault != 0U) {
			if (fault == VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_ODOMETRY_DELIVERY)
				tm_printf((UB*)"odometry delivery failed: line=%d imu=%d motion_attempted=%u motion=%d\n",
					  (int)delivery.line, (int)delivery.imu,
					  (unsigned)delivery.motion_attempted, (int)delivery.motion);
			report_fault(fault, last_timestamp);
		}
	}
}

static ER start_odometry(const odometry_producer_config_t *config, RELTIM period_ms,
			 bool publish_motion)
{
	odometry_producer_t validation;
	encoder_snapshot_t snapshot;
	T_CTSK task = {0};
	ID id;
	ER er;

#if TK_SUPPORT_SMP
	if (tk_get_prc() != TP_PRC1) return E_CTX;
#endif
	if (s_started) return E_OBJ;
	if (period_ms == 0U || period_ms > INT32_MAX ||
	    !odometry_producer_init(&validation, config, 0, 0, 0U))
		return E_PAR;
	if (g_mbf_line_odometry <= 0 || g_mbf_imu_odometry <= 0 || g_mbf_faults <= 0 ||
	    (publish_motion && g_mbf_motion_odometry <= 0))
		return E_OBJ;
	er = encoder_source_snapshot(&snapshot);
	if (er < E_OK) return er;
	if (!snapshot.valid) return E_IO;

	s_config = *config;
	s_period_ms = period_ms;
	s_publish_motion = publish_motion;
	task.task = (FP)odometry_task;
	task.tskatr = TA_HLNG | TA_RNG3;
	task.itskpri = ODOMETRY_TASK_PRIORITY;
	task.stksz = TASK_STACK_SIZE;
#if TK_SUPPORT_SMP
	task.tskatr |= TA_ASSPRC;
	task.assprc = TP_PRC1;
#endif
	id = tk_cre_tsk(&task);
	if (id < E_OK) return id;
	s_started = true;
	er = tk_sta_tsk(id, 0);
	if (er < E_OK) {
		ER cleanup = tk_del_tsk(id);
		if (cleanup < E_OK) {
			tm_printf((UB*)"odometry task cleanup failed: %d\n", (int)cleanup);
		} else {
			s_started = false;
		}
		return er;
	}
	return E_OK;
}

ER odometry_task_start(const odometry_producer_config_t *config, RELTIM period_ms)
{
	return start_odometry(config, period_ms, false);
}

ER odometry_task_start_with_motion(const odometry_producer_config_t *config,
				   RELTIM period_ms)
{
	return start_odometry(config, period_ms, true);
}

#if APP_TEST
#include "../../test_report.h"

static BOOL drain_odometry_buffer(ID buffer)
{
	wheel_odometry_message_t message;
	INT result;

	do {
		result = tk_rcv_mbf(buffer, &message, TMO_POL);
	} while (result > 0);
	return result == E_TMOUT;
}

BOOL odometry_task_test(void)
{
	const odometry_producer_config_t config = {{2500U, 20.0f}, {2500U, 20.0f}, 1, -1};
	odometry_producer_t producer = {0};
	encoder_snapshot_t snapshot = {2500, -2500, true};
	odometry_publish_result_t delivery = {0};
	wheel_odometry_message_t line = {0}, imu = {0};
	BOOL step_ok;
	BOOL fault_ok;
	BOOL motion_ok;
	unsigned i;
	TEST_INIT();

	step_ok = odometry_producer_init(&producer, &config, 0, 0, 0U);
	if (step_ok) {
		step_ok = process_snapshot(&producer, &snapshot, 100U, false, &delivery) == 0U;
		step_ok = tk_rcv_mbf(g_mbf_line_odometry, &line, TMO_POL) ==
			  (INT)sizeof(line) && step_ok;
		step_ok = tk_rcv_mbf(g_mbf_imu_odometry, &imu, TMO_POL) ==
			  (INT)sizeof(imu) && step_ok;
		step_ok = step_ok && line.distance_mm == 200 && imu.distance_mm == 200 &&
			  line.header.sequence == 1U && imu.header.sequence == 1U;
	}

	snapshot.valid = false;
	fault_ok = process_snapshot(&producer, &snapshot, 200U, false, &delivery) ==
		   VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_ODOMETRY_SOURCE && producer.sequence == 1U;
	fault_ok = tk_rcv_mbf(g_mbf_line_odometry, &line, TMO_POL) == E_TMOUT && fault_ok;
	fault_ok = tk_rcv_mbf(g_mbf_imu_odometry, &imu, TMO_POL) == E_TMOUT && fault_ok;
	snapshot.valid = true;
	fault_ok = process_snapshot(&producer, &snapshot, 100U, false, &delivery) ==
		   VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_ODOMETRY_CALCULATION && fault_ok;

	line.header.type = VEHICLE_MESSAGE_WHEEL_ODOMETRY;
	line.header.version = VEHICLE_MESSAGE_VERSION;
	line.valid = 1U;
	for (i = 0U; i < VEHICLE_MB_ODOMETRY_CAPACITY; i++) {
		if (tk_snd_mbf(g_mbf_line_odometry, &line, (INT)sizeof(line), TMO_POL) != E_OK)
			fault_ok = FALSE;
	}
	fault_ok = process_snapshot(&producer, &snapshot, 200U, false, &delivery) ==
		   VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_ODOMETRY_DELIVERY && fault_ok;
	fault_ok = delivery.line == E_TMOUT && delivery.imu == E_OK && fault_ok;
	fault_ok = tk_rcv_mbf(g_mbf_imu_odometry, &imu, TMO_POL) ==
		   (INT)sizeof(imu) && fault_ok;
	fault_ok = drain_odometry_buffer(g_mbf_line_odometry) && fault_ok;
	fault_ok = drain_odometry_buffer(g_mbf_imu_odometry) && fault_ok;

	motion_ok = odometry_producer_init(&producer, &config, 0, 0, 0U);
	for (i = 0U; i < VEHICLE_MB_ODOMETRY_CAPACITY; i++) {
		if (tk_snd_mbf(g_mbf_motion_odometry, &line, (INT)sizeof(line), TMO_POL) != E_OK)
			motion_ok = FALSE;
	}
	motion_ok = process_snapshot(&producer, &snapshot, 100U, true, &delivery) ==
		    VEHICLE_FAULT_CODE_ODOMETRY_DELIVERY &&
		    delivery.line == E_OK && delivery.imu == E_OK &&
		    delivery.motion_attempted && delivery.motion == E_TMOUT && motion_ok;
	motion_ok = drain_odometry_buffer(g_mbf_line_odometry) && motion_ok;
	motion_ok = drain_odometry_buffer(g_mbf_imu_odometry) && motion_ok;
	motion_ok = drain_odometry_buffer(g_mbf_motion_odometry) && motion_ok;

	TEST_CHECK("ODO-U08", step_ok, "task step delivers simulated snapshot");
	TEST_CHECK("ODO-U09", fault_ok, "task step rejects invalid snapshots and delivery failures");
	TEST_CHECK("ODO-U10", motion_ok, "full motion buffer stops odometry");
	TEST_SUMMARY("odometry task");
	return step_ok && fault_ok && motion_ok;
}
#endif

static BOOL encoder_pair_test(void)
{
	encoder_pair_t pair;
	encoder_snapshot_t snapshot;
	uint32_t pins = 0U;

	encoder_pair_init(&pair, pins);
	pins = (1U << ENCODER_LEFT_B_PIN) | (1U << ENCODER_RIGHT_A_PIN);
	encoder_pair_update(&pair, 0x00000880U, pins);
	pins |= (1U << ENCODER_LEFT_A_PIN) | (1U << ENCODER_RIGHT_B_PIN);
	encoder_pair_update(&pair, 0x00008008U, pins);
	snapshot = encoder_pair_snapshot(&pair);
	return snapshot.valid && snapshot.left_count == 2 && snapshot.right_count == -2;
}

static BOOL encoder_pair_invalid_test(void)
{
	encoder_pair_t pair;
	encoder_snapshot_t snapshot;
	uint32_t pins;

	encoder_pair_init(&pair, 0U);
	pins = (1U << ENCODER_LEFT_A_PIN) | (1U << ENCODER_LEFT_B_PIN);
	encoder_pair_update(&pair, 0x00000088U, pins);
	snapshot = encoder_pair_snapshot(&pair);
	if (snapshot.valid) return FALSE;
	pins &= ~(1U << ENCODER_LEFT_A_PIN);
	encoder_pair_update(&pair, 0x00000004U, pins);
	if (encoder_pair_snapshot(&pair).valid) return FALSE;

	encoder_pair_init(&pair, 0U);
	encoder_pair_update(&pair, 0x00000004U, 1U << ENCODER_LEFT_A_PIN);
	if (encoder_pair_snapshot(&pair).valid) return FALSE;

	encoder_pair_init(&pair, 0U);
	encoder_pair_update(&pair, 0x00000008U,
			    (1U << ENCODER_LEFT_A_PIN) | (1U << ENCODER_LEFT_B_PIN));
	return !encoder_pair_snapshot(&pair).valid;
}

BOOL odometry_test(void)
{
	encoder_t encoder;
	wheel_odometry_config_t config = {2500U, 20.0f};
	wheel_odometry_result_t result;
	float distance = 0.0f;
	BOOL encoder_ok;
	BOOL distance_ok;
	BOOL invalid_ok;
	BOOL pair_ok;
	BOOL pair_invalid_ok;
	TEST_INIT();

	encoder_init(&encoder, 0U, 0U);
	encoder_update(&encoder, 0U, 1U);
	encoder_update(&encoder, 1U, 1U);
	encoder_update(&encoder, 1U, 0U);
	encoder_update(&encoder, 0U, 0U);
	encoder_ok = encoder_get_count(&encoder) == 4;
	encoder_update(&encoder, 1U, 0U);
	encoder_update(&encoder, 1U, 1U);
	encoder_update(&encoder, 0U, 1U);
	encoder_update(&encoder, 0U, 0U);
	encoder_ok = encoder_ok && encoder_get_count(&encoder) == 0;
	encoder_update(&encoder, 1U, 1U);
	encoder_ok = encoder_ok && encoder_get_count(&encoder) == 0;
	encoder_init(&encoder, 0U, 0U);
	encoder.count = INT32_MAX;
	encoder_update(&encoder, 0U, 1U);
	encoder_ok = encoder_ok && encoder_get_count(&encoder) == INT32_MIN;
	encoder_update(&encoder, 0U, 0U);
	encoder_ok = encoder_ok && encoder_get_count(&encoder) == INT32_MAX;

	distance_ok = wheel_odometry_calculate(&config, 2500, 1.0f, &result) &&
		      result.distance_cm > 19.999f && result.distance_cm < 20.001f &&
		      result.rpm > 59.999f && result.rpm < 60.001f &&
		      result.speed_cm_per_second > 19.999f &&
		      result.speed_cm_per_second < 20.001f;
	distance_ok = distance_ok &&
		      wheel_odometry_distance(&config, -2500, &distance) &&
		      distance > -20.001f && distance < -19.999f;

	config.counts_per_revolution = 0U;
	invalid_ok = !wheel_odometry_distance(&config, 2500, &distance);
	config.counts_per_revolution = 2500U;
	invalid_ok = invalid_ok &&
		     !wheel_odometry_calculate(&config, 2500, 0.0f, &result);
	pair_ok = encoder_pair_test();
	pair_invalid_ok = encoder_pair_invalid_test();

	TEST_CHECK("ODO-U01", encoder_ok, "quadrature forward, reverse and invalid transitions");
	TEST_CHECK("ODO-U02", distance_ok, "calibrated wheel distance and speed");
	TEST_CHECK("ODO-U03", invalid_ok, "invalid calibration and elapsed time rejected");
	TEST_CHECK("ODO-U06", pair_ok, "coherent independent encoder counts");
	TEST_CHECK("ODO-U07", pair_invalid_ok, "ambiguous encoder events latch invalid");
	TEST_SUMMARY("odometry");
	return encoder_ok && distance_ok && invalid_ok && pair_ok && pair_invalid_ok;
}

#include "wheel_odometry.h"

#include <float.h>
#include <math.h>
#include <stddef.h>

bool wheel_odometry_count_delta(uint32_t current, uint32_t previous, int32_t *delta)
{
	uint32_t difference = current - previous;
	if (delta == NULL || difference == 0x80000000U) return false;
	*delta = difference <= INT32_MAX ? (int32_t)difference :
		 INT32_MIN + (int32_t)(difference - 0x80000000U);
	return true;
}

static bool valid_config(const wheel_odometry_config_t *config)
{
	return config != NULL &&
	       config->counts_per_revolution > 0U &&
	       isfinite(config->wheel_circumference_cm) &&
	       config->wheel_circumference_cm > 0.0f;
}

bool wheel_odometry_distance_precise(const wheel_odometry_config_t *config,
				     int64_t counts, double *distance_cm)
{
	double distance;

	if (!valid_config(config) || distance_cm == NULL) {
		return false;
	}
	distance = ((double)counts * (double)config->wheel_circumference_cm) /
		   (double)config->counts_per_revolution;
	if (!isfinite(distance)) {
		return false;
	}
	*distance_cm = distance;
	return true;
}

bool wheel_odometry_distance(const wheel_odometry_config_t *config,
			     int64_t counts, float *distance_cm)
{
	double distance;

	if (distance_cm == NULL ||
	    !wheel_odometry_distance_precise(config, counts, &distance) ||
	    distance > (double)FLT_MAX || distance < -(double)FLT_MAX) {
		return false;
	}
	*distance_cm = (float)distance;
	return true;
}

bool wheel_odometry_calculate(const wheel_odometry_config_t *config,
			      int64_t delta_counts, float dt_seconds,
			      wheel_odometry_result_t *result)
{
	float distance;
	double revolutions;
	double rpm;
	double speed;

	if (result == NULL || !isfinite(dt_seconds) || dt_seconds <= 0.0f ||
	    !wheel_odometry_distance(config, delta_counts, &distance)) {
		return false;
	}
	revolutions = (double)delta_counts / (double)config->counts_per_revolution;
	rpm = revolutions * 60.0 / (double)dt_seconds;
	speed = (double)distance / (double)dt_seconds;
	if (!isfinite(rpm) || !isfinite(speed) ||
	    rpm > (double)FLT_MAX || rpm < -(double)FLT_MAX ||
	    speed > (double)FLT_MAX || speed < -(double)FLT_MAX) {
		return false;
	}
	result->distance_cm = distance;
	result->speed_cm_per_second = (float)speed;
	result->rpm = (float)rpm;
	return true;
}

#ifndef WHEEL_ODOMETRY_H
#define WHEEL_ODOMETRY_H

#include <stdbool.h>
#include <stdint.h>

/* Counts include all quadrature transitions and the gearbox ratio. */
typedef struct {
	uint32_t counts_per_revolution;
	float wheel_circumference_cm;
} wheel_odometry_config_t;

typedef struct {
	float distance_cm;
	float speed_cm_per_second;
	float rpm;
} wheel_odometry_result_t;

/* Reject the ambiguous half-range change between wrapping 32-bit counts. */
bool wheel_odometry_count_delta(uint32_t current, uint32_t previous, int32_t *delta);

bool wheel_odometry_distance(const wheel_odometry_config_t *config,
			     int64_t counts, float *distance_cm);
bool wheel_odometry_distance_precise(const wheel_odometry_config_t *config,
				     int64_t counts, double *distance_cm);
bool wheel_odometry_calculate(const wheel_odometry_config_t *config,
			      int64_t delta_counts, float dt_seconds,
			      wheel_odometry_result_t *result);

/*
 *	app.h
 *	Definitions shared by the whole team.  Changed only by team agreement.
 *
 *	Anything that belongs to ONE subsystem goes in that subsystem's own
 *	module's own header instead.
 */

#ifndef APP_H
#define APP_H

#include <tk/tkernel.h>		/* micro T-Kernel API (tk_xxx_yyy) */
#include <tm/tmonitor.h>	/* T-Monitor API: tm_printf() console output */
#include <bsp/libbsp.h>		/* Board helpers: gpio_set_pin/val, pwm_... */
#include "vehicle_messages.h"

/* ---------------------------------------------------------------------
 *  Pins: free for projects are GP0..GP22 and GP26..GP28.  GP0/GP1 are
 *  taken by UART0 when built with "make CRASH_REPORT=1" (see README).
 *  Never use GP23/24/25/29: they are wired to the Pico W radio.
 *  Claim every pin in TEAM.md section 2 before using it.
 * --------------------------------------------------------------------- */

/* ---------------------------------------------------------------------
 *  Task priorities: 1 is the highest; the kernel's USB console (3) and
 *  WiFi (4) tasks sit above every application task.  Each subsystem
 *  has its own band (TEAM.md section 4), defined in its own header.
 * --------------------------------------------------------------------- */
#define TASK_STACK_SIZE		2048	/* bytes; tm_printf needs ~1 KB */

/* Create and start a task.  prc = TP_PRC1 (core 0), TP_PRC2 (core 1), or
   0 for "any core".  Defined in app_main.c. */
IMPORT ID app_start_task(FP entry, PRI pri, UW prc, const char *name);

/* Print a readable message if a kernel call failed (negative error code) */
#define CHECK(er, what)							\
	do {								\
		INT check_er_ = (INT)(er);	/* evaluate once */	\
		if (check_er_ < E_OK) {					\
			tm_printf((UB*)"ERROR %s: %d\n", (what), check_er_); \
		}							\
	} while (0)

#endif /* APP_H */

/*
 *	app_main.c
 *	START HERE.  usermain() below is your program's entry point.
 *
 *	How micro T-Kernel starts your code
 *	-----------------------------------
 *	  reset -> kernel initialises itself -> creates the "initial task"
 *	        -> initial task calls usermain()
 *
 *	usermain() runs inside that initial task at priority 1 (the highest).
 *	On a single core nothing you create runs until usermain() blocks, but
 *	on the dual-core build the OTHER core may start a new task at once.
 *	So usermain() creates vehicle buffers first and starts the selected
 *	vehicle or diagnostic tasks before sleeping.
 *	If usermain() RETURNS, the kernel shuts the whole system down.
 *
 *	Output appears on the USB serial port (115200 8N1 in any terminal).
 */

typedef struct {
	bool go;
	bool candidate_pressed;
	bool stable_pressed;
	bool ready;
	unsigned stable_samples;
} go_button_t;

static bool go_button_sample(go_button_t *button, bool pressed)
{
	if (pressed != button->candidate_pressed) {
		button->candidate_pressed = pressed;
		button->stable_samples = 1U;
	} else if (button->stable_samples < 2U) {
		button->stable_samples++;
	}
	if (button->stable_samples < 2U) return button->go;
	if (!button->ready) {
		if (!pressed) button->ready = true;
		return false;
	}
	if (pressed != button->stable_pressed) {
		button->stable_pressed = pressed;
		if (pressed) button->go = !button->go;
	}
	return button->go;
}
#endif

#if APP_TEST
#include "test_report.h"

static BOOL go_button_test(void)
{
	go_button_t button = {0};
	BOOL startup_ok, toggle_ok, bounce_ok;
	TEST_INIT();

	startup_ok = !go_button_sample(&button, true);
	startup_ok = !go_button_sample(&button, true) && startup_ok;
	startup_ok = !go_button_sample(&button, false) && startup_ok;
	startup_ok = !go_button_sample(&button, false) && startup_ok;
	TEST_CHECK("GO-U01", startup_ok, "held button at startup cannot enable GO");

	bounce_ok = !go_button_sample(&button, true);
	bounce_ok = !go_button_sample(&button, false) && bounce_ok;
	bounce_ok = !go_button_sample(&button, false) && bounce_ok;
	TEST_CHECK("GO-U02", bounce_ok, "a single noisy sample cannot toggle GO");

	toggle_ok = !go_button_sample(&button, true);
	toggle_ok = go_button_sample(&button, true) && toggle_ok;
	toggle_ok = go_button_sample(&button, true) && toggle_ok;
	toggle_ok = go_button_sample(&button, false) && toggle_ok;
	toggle_ok = go_button_sample(&button, false) && toggle_ok;
	toggle_ok = go_button_sample(&button, true) && toggle_ok;
	toggle_ok = !go_button_sample(&button, true) && toggle_ok;
	TEST_CHECK("GO-U03", toggle_ok, "distinct stable presses toggle GO once");

	TEST_SUMMARY("GO button");
	return startup_ok && bounce_ok && toggle_ok;
}
#endif

#if APP_VEHICLE
/*
 * === Final-integration startup (VEHICLE=1) ==============================
 *
 * Hardware facts, kept in ONE place.  Set each verification flag to 1 ONLY
 * after measuring it on the robot (TEAM.md section 2): startup refuses to
 * run the vehicle without them, because the safety layer faults unless the
 * obstacle stream is alive, and the obstacle task needs verified electrical
 * and servo settings.
 */
#define VEHICLE_ECHO_VERIFIED_3V3_SAFE	1	/* GP4 divider measured <= 3.3 V */
#define VEHICLE_SERVO_LIMITS_VERIFIED	1	/* 1500/1700/1900 us; measured +/-20 deg */
#define VEHICLE_GO_PIN			20U

/* Odometry calibration, verified with ODO-H01 (encoder bring-up). */
#define VEHICLE_COUNTS_PER_REV		2500U
#define VEHICLE_WHEEL_CIRC_CM		20.4f
#define VEHICLE_LEFT_SIGN		1
#define VEHICLE_RIGHT_SIGN		1

static go_button_t s_go_button;

static bool vehicle_read_inputs(void *context, vehicle_controller_inputs_t *inputs)
{
	go_button_t *button = context;

	inputs->go = go_button_sample(button, gpio_get_val(VEHICLE_GO_PIN) == 0U);
	inputs->emergency_stopped = false;
	return true;
}

static void vehicle_start(void)
{
#if !VEHICLE_ECHO_VERIFIED_3V3_SAFE || !VEHICLE_SERVO_LIMITS_VERIFIED
	/* Repeat forever so a late serial monitor still sees why. */
	for (;;) {
		tm_printf((UB*)"vehicle startup blocked: hardware not verified\n");
#if !VEHICLE_ECHO_VERIFIED_3V3_SAFE
		tm_printf((UB*)"  - measure the HC-SR04 echo divider on GP4 (<= 3.3V),\n");
		tm_printf((UB*)"    then set VEHICLE_ECHO_VERIFIED_3V3_SAFE in app_main.c\n");
#endif
#if !VEHICLE_SERVO_LIMITS_VERIFIED
		tm_printf((UB*)"  - verify servo clearance and scan angles on the robot,\n");
		tm_printf((UB*)"    then set VEHICLE_SERVO_LIMITS_VERIFIED in app_main.c\n");
#endif
		tm_printf((UB*)"no vehicle tasks started\n\n");
		tk_dly_tsk(5000);
	}
#else
	/* Calibration shared by odometry and motion feedback. */
	static const wheel_odometry_config_t wheel_calibration = {
		VEHICLE_COUNTS_PER_REV, VEHICLE_WHEEL_CIRC_CM
	};
	static const odometry_producer_config_t odometry_calibration = {
		{VEHICLE_COUNTS_PER_REV, VEHICLE_WHEEL_CIRC_CM},
		{VEHICLE_COUNTS_PER_REV, VEHICLE_WHEEL_CIRC_CM},
		VEHICLE_LEFT_SIGN, VEHICLE_RIGHT_SIGN
	};
	/* PID gains and base/max PWM are S5 placeholders: tune on blocks. */
	static motion_task_config_t motion_config = {
		{{{0}, {0}, 150U, 250U},
		 {2.0f, 0.0f, 0.0f, 10.0f, 20U, 60U}},
		10U,	/* period_ms */
		100U,	/* command_max_age_ms */
		500U,	/* max_duration_ms */
		20U	/* max_linear_speed_cm_s */
	};
	/* Sensor deadlines; producers below publish well inside them. */
	static const vehicle_controller_task_config_t controller_config = {
		{200U, 200U, 500U, 5000U, 500U, 300U},
		50U,	/* period_ms, <= half the motion status period */
		500U,	/* motion_status_max_age_ms */
		vehicle_read_inputs, &s_go_button
	};
	static const line_barcode_task_config_t line_config = {
		20U,	/* period_ms */
		200U,	/* odometry_max_age_ms */
		/* counts_per_module must be calibrated to the printed code. */
		{250U, 25U, 0U, 0U, 0U},
		NULL, NULL, NULL	/* reader installed by the ADC helper */
	};
	static const imu_task_config_t imu_config = {
		50U,	/* period_ms */
		200U,	/* sample_max_age_ms */
		200U,	/* odometry_max_age_ms */
		NULL, NULL, NULL	/* reader installed by the I2C helper */
	};
	static const obs_task_config_t obstacle_config = {
		50U,	/* period_ms */
		500U,	/* ahead_max_age_ms */
		1000U,	/* scan_max_age_ms */
		5000U,	/* scan_timeout_ms */
		500U,	/* command_max_age_ms */
		300U,	/* emergency_distance_mm */
		NULL, NULL, NULL, NULL	/* callbacks installed by the port */
	};
	/* Static: start functions may keep pointers past this task's exit. */
	static lsm303dlhc_i2c_config_t imu_sensor_config;
	static obs_pico_port_config_t obstacle_hardware;
	ER er;

	motion_config.control.feedback.left = wheel_calibration;
	motion_config.control.feedback.right = wheel_calibration;

	er = gpio_set_pin(VEHICLE_GO_PIN, GPIO_MODE_IN);
	if (er != E_OK) {
		tm_printf((UB*)"GO button initialization failed: %d; tasks not started\n",
			  (int)er);
		return;
	}
	clr_w(GPIO(VEHICLE_GO_PIN), GPIO_PDE);
	set_w(GPIO(VEHICLE_GO_PIN), GPIO_PUE);

	/* GPIO interrupt clients register before the dispatcher starts. */
	CHECK(encoder_source_register(), "encoder_source_register");
	CHECK(obs_pico_port_register(), "obs_pico_port_register");
	CHECK(gpio_dispatcher_start(), "gpio_dispatcher_start");

	/* Consumers before producers: motion drains commands and odometry,
	   the controller drains every sensor stream. */
	CHECK(motion_task_start_hardware(&motion_config), "motion_task_start_hardware");
	CHECK(vehicle_controller_task_start(&controller_config),
	      "vehicle_controller_task_start");

	CHECK(odometry_task_start_with_motion(&odometry_calibration, 50U),
	      "odometry_task_start");
	CHECK(line_adc_reader_start_default_task(10U, &line_config),
	      "line_adc_reader_start_default_task");
	CHECK(lsm303dlhc_i2c_default_config(&imu_sensor_config, 20U),
	      "lsm303dlhc_i2c_default_config");
	imu_sensor_config.profile = LSM303DLHC_I2C_PROFILE_32_FAMILY;
	CHECK(lsm303dlhc_i2c_start_imu_task(&imu_sensor_config, &imu_config),
	      "lsm303dlhc_i2c_start_imu_task");

	obstacle_hardware = obs_pico_port_default_config();
	obstacle_hardware.echo_voltage_verified_3v3_safe = true;
	obstacle_hardware.servo_limits_verified = true;
	CHECK(obs_pico_port_task_start(&obstacle_hardware, &obstacle_config),
	      "obs_pico_port_task_start");

#if defined(TM_MQTT) && TM_MQTT
	CHECK(wifi_service_start_mqtt(100U, 1000U, 1000U, 1000U),
	      "wifi_service_start_mqtt");
#endif

	tm_printf((UB*)"vehicle tasks started; controller WAITING until GO\n\n");
#endif
}

/*
 * GPIO/encoder/obstacle registration demands core 0 (tk_get_prc() ==
 * TP_PRC1), but usermain may be scheduled on either core, so the whole
 * startup sequence runs in this one-shot task pinned to TP_PRC1.
 */
LOCAL void vehicle_startup_task(INT stacd, void *exinf)
{
	(void)stacd;
	(void)exinf;
	vehicle_start();	/* never returns when startup is blocked */
	tk_ext_tsk();
}
#endif /* APP_VEHICLE */

#if (APP_TEST || APP_VEHICLE || APP_SENSOR_TEST) && \
	defined(TM_CONSOLE_USB_CDC) && TM_CONSOLE_USB_CDC
/* usb_console.c: 0 not started, 1 started, 2 enumerated, 3 host asserted DTR */
IMPORT UW tm_usb_state(void);

/*
 * Hold the test run or vehicle startup until a serial monitor opens the
 * port, so no boot message is printed before anyone can read it.  DTR
 * (state 3) is how a terminal announces itself, but some terminal programs
 * never assert it, so once the USB link is enumerated (state 2) a timeout
 * lets the run proceed anyway.
 */
LOCAL void wait_for_serial_monitor(void)
{
	UW	enumerated_ms = 0;

	tm_printf((UB*)"waiting for serial monitor...\n");
	while (tm_usb_state() < 3U) {
		if (tm_usb_state() == 2U) {
			enumerated_ms += 100U;
			if (enumerated_ms >= 60000U) break;	/* DTR-less terminal */
		}
		tk_dly_tsk(100);
	}
	tk_dly_tsk(500);	/* let the terminal finish opening before output bursts */
}
#endif

/*
 * Create and start one task (declared in app.h for every subsystem).
 *   prc = TP_PRC1 (core 0), TP_PRC2 (core 1), or 0 for "any core".
 */
EXPORT ID app_start_task(FP entry, PRI pri, UW prc, const char *name)
{
	T_CTSK	ctsk = {0};
	ID	tid;
	ER	er;

	ctsk.task    = entry;		/* function the task runs */
	ctsk.itskpri = pri;		/* initial priority (1 = highest) */
	ctsk.stksz   = TASK_STACK_SIZE;	/* kernel allocates the stack */
	ctsk.tskatr  = TA_HLNG		/* written in a high-level language (C) */
		     | TA_RNG3;		/* runs at user protection level */
#if TK_SUPPORT_SMP
	if (prc != 0) {
		ctsk.tskatr |= TA_ASSPRC;	/* honour the affinity below */
		ctsk.assprc  = prc;		/* which core(s) it may run on */
	}
#else
	(void)prc;			/* single-core build: no affinity */
#endif

	/* tk_cre_tsk: create the task.  It starts in the DORMANT state. */
	tid = tk_cre_tsk(&ctsk);
	if (tid < E_OK) {
		CHECK(tid, name);
		return tid;
	}

	/* tk_sta_tsk: DORMANT -> READY.  The int is passed as stacd. */
	er = tk_sta_tsk(tid, 0);
	CHECK(er, name);
	return tid;
}

EXPORT INT usermain(void)
{
	T_RVER	ver;
	ER	er;

	/* tk_ref_ver: ask the kernel who it is */
	tk_ref_ver(&ver);
	tm_printf((UB*)"\n=== micro T-Kernel on Raspberry Pi Pico W"
		  " (spec 0x%04x, product 0x%04x) ===\n", ver.spver, ver.prver);
#if TK_SUPPORT_SMP
	tm_printf((UB*)"dual-core (SMP) build, usermain on core %d\n",
		  tk_get_prc() - 1);	/* tk_get_prc() counts from 1 */
#else
	tm_printf((UB*)"single-core build\n");
#endif

	/* Phase 1: every subsystem creates its kernel objects */
#if APP_SENSOR_TEST != 5
	er = vehicle_buffers_init();
	if (er < E_OK) {
		tm_printf((UB*)"vehicle buffer initialization failed; tasks not started\n");
		for (;;) {
			tk_dly_tsk(1000);
		}
	}
#endif
#if APP_SENSOR_TEST
#if defined(TM_CONSOLE_USB_CDC) && TM_CONSOLE_USB_CDC
	wait_for_serial_monitor();
#endif
#if APP_SENSOR_TEST == 5
	tm_printf((UB*)"starting encoder-only diagnostic\n");
	er = encoder_bringup_start();
#else
	er = hardware_sensor_test_start();
#endif
	if (er < E_OK) {
		for (;;) {
#if APP_SENSOR_TEST == 5
			tm_printf((UB*)"encoder task failed to start: %d\n", (int)er);
#else
			tm_printf((UB*)"hardware sensor test task failed to start: %d\n",
				  (int)er);
#endif
			tk_dly_tsk(5000);
		}
	}
	tk_slp_tsk(TMO_FEVR);
	return 0;
#endif
#if APP_TEST
#if defined(TM_CONSOLE_USB_CDC) && TM_CONSOLE_USB_CDC
	wait_for_serial_monitor();
#endif
	if (!vehicle_buffers_test()) {
		for (;;) {
			tm_printf((UB*)"[TEST] INT-03 FAIL  vehicle buffers; tasks not started\n");
			tk_dly_tsk(1000);
		}
	}
	if (!odometry_test()) {
		for (;;) {
			tm_printf((UB*)"[TEST] ODO FAIL  odometry calculations; tasks not started\n");
			tk_dly_tsk(1000);
		}
	}
	if (!odometry_producer_test()) {
		for (;;) {
			tm_printf((UB*)"[TEST] ODO-PRODUCER FAIL  producer tests; tasks not started\n");
			tk_dly_tsk(1000);
		}
	}
	if (!odometry_task_test()) {
		for (;;) {
			tm_printf((UB*)"[TEST] ODO-TASK FAIL  task tests; tasks not started\n");
			tk_dly_tsk(1000);
		}
	}
	if (!line_observation_test()) {
		for (;;) {
			tm_printf((UB*)"[TEST] LINE FAIL  line classification; tasks not started\n");
			tk_dly_tsk(1000);
		}
	}
	if (!barcode_scanner_test()) {
		for (;;) {
			tm_printf((UB*)"[TEST] BAR FAIL  barcode decoder; tasks not started\n");
			tk_dly_tsk(1000);
		}
	}
	if (!line_barcode_task_test()) {
		for (;;) {
			tm_printf((UB*)"[TEST] LB-TASK FAIL  task tests; tasks not started\n");
			tk_dly_tsk(1000);
		}
	}
	if (!line_adc_reader_test()) {
		for (;;) {
			tm_printf((UB*)"[TEST] LINE-ADC FAIL  reader tests; tasks not started\n");
			tk_dly_tsk(1000);
		}
	}
	if (!imu_processor_test()) {
		for (;;) {
			tm_printf((UB*)"[TEST] IMU FAIL  processing; tasks not started\n");
			tk_dly_tsk(1000);
		}
	}
	if (!imu_task_test()) {
		for (;;) {
			tm_printf((UB*)"[TEST] IMU-TASK FAIL  task tests; tasks not started\n");
			tk_dly_tsk(1000);
		}
	}
	if (!obs_profile_test()) {
		for (;;) {
			tm_printf((UB*)"[TEST] OBS FAIL  profiling; tasks not started\n");
			tk_dly_tsk(1000);
		}
	}
	if (!obs_task_test()) {
		for (;;) {
			tm_printf((UB*)"[TEST] OBS-TASK FAIL  task tests; tasks not started\n");
			tk_dly_tsk(1000);
		}
	}
	if (!wheel_speed_test()) {
		for (;;) {
			tm_printf((UB*)"[TEST] MOT FAIL  wheel speed control; tasks not started\n");
			tk_dly_tsk(1000);
		}
	}
	if (!motion_task_test()) {
		for (;;) {
			tm_printf((UB*)"[TEST] MOT-TASK FAIL  simulation tests; tasks not started\n");
			tk_dly_tsk(1000);
		}
	}
	if (!car_gate_test()) {
		for (;;) {
			tm_printf((UB*)"[TEST] VC FAIL  controller gate; tasks not started\n");
			tk_dly_tsk(1000);
		}
	}
	if (!line_steering_test()) {
		for (;;) {
			tm_printf((UB*)"[TEST] VC FAIL  line steering; tasks not started\n");
			tk_dly_tsk(1000);
		}
		if (!go_button_test()) {
			for (;;) {
				tm_printf((UB*)"[TEST] GO FAIL  button tests; tasks not started\n");
				tk_dly_tsk(1000);
			}
		}
	}
	if (!controller_monitor_test()) {
		for (;;) {
			tm_printf((UB*)"[TEST] VC-MONITOR FAIL  monitor tests; tasks not started\n");
			tk_dly_tsk(1000);
		}
	}
	if (!vehicle_controller_task_test()) {
		for (;;) {
			tm_printf((UB*)"[TEST] VC-TASK FAIL  vehicle controller tests; tasks not started\n");
			tk_dly_tsk(1000);
		}
	}
	if (!wifi_service_test()) {
		for (;;) {
			tm_printf((UB*)"[TEST] WIFI FAIL  service tests; tasks not started\n");
			tk_dly_tsk(1000);
		}
	}
	if (!wifi_mqtt_bridge_test()) {
		for (;;) {
			tm_printf((UB*)"[TEST] WIFI-BRIDGE FAIL  bridge tests; tasks not started\n");
			tk_dly_tsk(1000);
		}
	}
	if (!gpio_irq_test()) {
		for (;;) {
			tm_printf((UB*)"[TEST] GPIO FAIL  interrupt routing; tasks not started\n");
			tk_dly_tsk(1000);
		}
	}
#endif

	/* Phase 2: start the selected vehicle or diagnostic tasks. */
#if APP_ODOMETRY_TEST
	er = encoder_bringup_start();
	if (er < E_OK) {
		for (;;) {
			tm_printf((UB*)"encoder task start failed: %d\n", (int)er);
			tk_dly_tsk(1000);
		}
	}
	tm_printf((UB*)"encoder bring-up task started\n\n");
#elif APP_VEHICLE
#if defined(TM_CONSOLE_USB_CDC) && TM_CONSOLE_USB_CDC
	wait_for_serial_monitor();
#endif
	/* Pinned to core 0: the GPIO IRQ registrations reject other cores. */
	if (app_start_task((FP)vehicle_startup_task, 11, TP_PRC1,
			   "vehicle_startup") < E_OK) {
		for (;;) {
			tm_printf((UB*)"vehicle startup task failed to start\n");
			tk_dly_tsk(5000);
		}
	}
#else
	tm_printf((UB*)"no vehicle tasks started\n\n");
#endif

#if APP_TEST
	tm_printf((UB*)"[TEST] === test run start ===\n");
	tm_printf((UB*)"[TEST] INT-03 PASS  vehicle buffer startup result (repeated)\n");
	tm_printf((UB*)"[TEST] ODO PASS  odometry startup tests (repeated)\n");
	tm_printf((UB*)"[TEST] ODO-PRODUCER PASS  producer tests (repeated)\n");
	tm_printf((UB*)"[TEST] ODO-TASK PASS  task tests (repeated)\n");
	tm_printf((UB*)"[TEST] LINE PASS  line classification tests (repeated)\n");
	tm_printf((UB*)"[TEST] BAR PASS  barcode decoder tests (repeated)\n");
	tm_printf((UB*)"[TEST] LB-TASK PASS  task tests (repeated)\n");
	tm_printf((UB*)"[TEST] LINE-ADC PASS  reader tests (repeated)\n");
	tm_printf((UB*)"[TEST] IMU PASS  IMU processing tests (repeated)\n");
	tm_printf((UB*)"[TEST] OBS PASS  obstacle profile tests (repeated)\n");
	tm_printf((UB*)"[TEST] MOT PASS  wheel speed tests (repeated)\n");
	tm_printf((UB*)"[TEST] VC PASS  controller gate tests (repeated)\n");
	tm_printf((UB*)"[TEST] GPIO PASS  interrupt routing tests (repeated)\n");
	tm_printf((UB*)"[TEST] === test run end ===\n");
#endif

#if APP_TEST
	/* Keep the startup test result visible after USB serial connects. */
	for (;;) {
		tk_dly_tsk(5000);
		tm_printf((UB*)"[TEST] INT-03 PASS  vehicle buffer startup result (repeated)\n");
		tm_printf((UB*)"[TEST] ODO PASS  odometry startup tests (repeated)\n");
		tm_printf((UB*)"[TEST] ODO-PRODUCER PASS  producer tests (repeated)\n");
		tm_printf((UB*)"[TEST] ODO-TASK PASS  task tests (repeated)\n");
		tm_printf((UB*)"[TEST] LINE PASS  line classification tests (repeated)\n");
		tm_printf((UB*)"[TEST] BAR PASS  barcode decoder tests (repeated)\n");
		tm_printf((UB*)"[TEST] LB-TASK PASS  task tests (repeated)\n");
		tm_printf((UB*)"[TEST] LINE-ADC PASS  reader tests (repeated)\n");
		tm_printf((UB*)"[TEST] IMU PASS  IMU processing tests (repeated)\n");
		tm_printf((UB*)"[TEST] OBS PASS  obstacle profile tests (repeated)\n");
		tm_printf((UB*)"[TEST] MOT PASS  wheel speed tests (repeated)\n");
		tm_printf((UB*)"[TEST] VC PASS  controller gate tests (repeated)\n");
		tm_printf((UB*)"[TEST] GPIO PASS  interrupt routing tests (repeated)\n");
	}
#else
	/* Sleep forever.  Returning from usermain() would shut down. */
	tk_slp_tsk(TMO_FEVR);
	return 0;
#endif
}

_Static_assert(SERVO_TEST_US == 0 ||
	       (SERVO_TEST_US >= 1000 && SERVO_TEST_US <= 2000),
	       "SERVO_TEST_US must be 0 or a pulse width from 1000 to 2000 us");

#define SENSOR_TEST_PRIORITY 10
#define SENSOR_TEST_INTERVAL_MS 500U
#define SENSOR_TEST_ERROR_DELAY_MS 5000U

static void report_error_forever(const char *sensor, const char *operation, ER error)
{
	for (;;) {
		tm_printf((UB*)"[HWTEST][%s] %s failed: %d\n",
			  sensor, operation, (int)error);
		tk_dly_tsk(SENSOR_TEST_ERROR_DELAY_MS);
	}
}

#if APP_SENSOR_TEST == 1
static void sensor_test_task(INT stacd, void *exinf)
{
	line_adc_reader_t reader = {0};
	line_adc_config_t config;
	ER error;

	(void)stacd;
	(void)exinf;
	error = line_adc_reader_default_config(&config, 100U);
	if (error != E_OK) report_error_forever("IR", "default config", error);
	error = line_adc_reader_init(&reader, &config);
	if (error != E_OK) report_error_forever("IR", "reader init", error);

	tm_printf((UB*)"[HWTEST][IR] live ADC readings; GP26-28\n");
	for (;;) {
		ir_readings_t readings;
		uint32_t timestamp_ms;

		error = line_adc_reader_read(&reader, &readings, &timestamp_ms);
		if (error != E_OK) report_error_forever("IR", "read", error);
		tm_printf((UB*)"[HWTEST][IR] t=%u ms barcode=%u black=%u "
			  "center=%u black=%u right=%u black=%u\n",
			  (unsigned)timestamp_ms,
			  (unsigned)readings.barcode.raw, readings.barcode.black ? 1U : 0U,
			  (unsigned)readings.center.raw, readings.center.black ? 1U : 0U,
			  (unsigned)readings.right.raw, readings.right.black ? 1U : 0U);
		error = tk_dly_tsk(SENSOR_TEST_INTERVAL_MS);
		if (error != E_OK) report_error_forever("IR", "delay", error);
	}
}
#elif APP_SENSOR_TEST == 2
#define IMU_PROBE_INTERVAL_MS 20U
#define IMU_PROBE_READY_WAIT_MS 100U
#define IMU_PROBE_ZYXDA 0x08U
#define IMU_PROBE_CTRL1 0x2FU
#define IMU_PROBE_CTRL4 0x80U

static ER imu_check_trace_clock(void)
{
	SYSTIM before, after;
	uint32_t ticks_before, ticks_after, elapsed_ms, elapsed_ticks;
	ER error;

	error = tk_get_otm(&before);
	if (error != E_OK) return error;
	ticks_before = in_w(TIMER_TIMERAWL);
	error = tk_dly_tsk(250U);
	if (error != E_OK) return error;
	ticks_after = in_w(TIMER_TIMERAWL);
	error = tk_get_otm(&after);
	if (error != E_OK) return error;
	elapsed_ms = after.lo - before.lo;
	elapsed_ticks = ticks_after - ticks_before;
	tm_printf((UB*)"[HWTEST][IMU] trace clock comparison: kernel_ms=%u timer_ticks=%u "
		  "(expected about 1000 ticks/ms)\n",
		  (unsigned)elapsed_ms, (unsigned)elapsed_ticks);
	return E_OK;
}

static void imu_report_identity(const lsm303dlhc_i2c_t *sensor)
{
	uint8_t reg = 0x0FU, identity = 0U;
	T_I2C_EXEC exec = {sensor->address, 1, &reg, 1, &identity};
	SZ actual = 0;
	ER error = tk_swri_dev(sensor->device, TDN_I2C_EXEC, &exec,
			       sizeof(exec), &actual);

	if (error != E_OK || actual != (SZ)sizeof(exec))
		tm_printf((UB*)"[HWTEST][IMU] WHO_AM_I read failed: %d size=%d\n",
			  (int)error, (int)actual);
	else
		tm_printf((UB*)"[HWTEST][IMU] WHO_AM_I: 0x%02x\n", (unsigned)identity);
}

static ER imu_write_probe_register(const lsm303dlhc_i2c_t *sensor,
				   uint8_t reg, uint8_t value)
{
	uint8_t data[2] = {reg, value};
	uint8_t readback = 0U;
	T_I2C_EXEC exec = {sensor->address, 1, &reg, 1, &readback};
	SZ actual = 0;
	ER error = tk_swri_dev(sensor->device, (W)sensor->address, data,
			       sizeof(data), &actual);

	if (error != E_OK || actual != (SZ)sizeof(data)) {
		tm_printf((UB*)"[HWTEST][IMU] probe register 0x%02x write failed: %d size=%d\n",
			  (unsigned)reg, (int)error, (int)actual);
		return error != E_OK ? error : E_IO;
	}
	actual = 0;
	error = tk_swri_dev(sensor->device, TDN_I2C_EXEC, &exec, sizeof(exec), &actual);
	if (error != E_OK || actual != (SZ)sizeof(exec)) {
		tm_printf((UB*)"[HWTEST][IMU] probe register 0x%02x readback failed: %d size=%d\n",
			  (unsigned)reg, (int)error, (int)actual);
		return error != E_OK ? error : E_IO;
	}
	if (readback != value) {
		tm_printf((UB*)"[HWTEST][IMU] probe register 0x%02x readback mismatch: "
			  "expected=0x%02x actual=0x%02x\n",
			  (unsigned)reg, (unsigned)value, (unsigned)readback);
		return E_IO;
	}
	return E_OK;
}

static ER imu_set_probe_mode(const lsm303dlhc_i2c_t *sensor)
{
	ER error = imu_write_probe_register(sensor, 0x23U, IMU_PROBE_CTRL4);

	if (error != E_OK) return error;
	return imu_write_probe_register(sensor, 0x20U, IMU_PROBE_CTRL1);
}

static ER imu_read_probe_register(const lsm303dlhc_i2c_t *sensor, uint8_t reg,
				  uint8_t *value)
{
	T_I2C_EXEC exec = {sensor->address, 1, &reg, 1, value};
	SZ actual = 0;
	ER error = tk_swri_dev(sensor->device, TDN_I2C_EXEC, &exec,
			       sizeof(exec), &actual);

	if (error != E_OK || actual != (SZ)sizeof(exec)) {
		tm_printf((UB*)"[HWTEST][IMU] register 0x%02x read failed: %d size=%d\n",
			  (unsigned)reg, (int)error, (int)actual);
		return error != E_OK ? error : E_IO;
	}
	return E_OK;
}

static ER imu_read_status(const lsm303dlhc_i2c_t *sensor, uint8_t *status)
{
	return imu_read_probe_register(sensor, 0x27U, status);
}

static ER imu_probe_bdu(const lsm303dlhc_i2c_t *sensor)
{
	const uint8_t low_regs[3] = {0x28U, 0x2AU, 0x2CU};
	uint8_t first[3], held[3], released[3], high;
	bool changed_while_held = false, changed_after_release = false;
	unsigned i;
	ER error;

	tm_printf((UB*)"[HWTEST][IMU] BDU probe: move the board continuously for 5 seconds\n");
	error = tk_dly_tsk(2000U);
	if (error != E_OK) return error;
	for (i = 0; i < 3U; i++) {
		error = imu_read_probe_register(sensor, low_regs[i], &first[i]);
		if (error != E_OK) return error;
	}
	error = tk_dly_tsk(1000U);
	if (error != E_OK) return error;
	for (i = 0; i < 3U; i++) {
		error = imu_read_probe_register(sensor, low_regs[i], &held[i]);
		if (error != E_OK) return error;
		if (held[i] != first[i]) changed_while_held = true;
	}
	for (i = 0; i < 3U; i++) {
		error = imu_read_probe_register(sensor, low_regs[i] + 1U, &high);
		if (error != E_OK) return error;
	}
	error = tk_dly_tsk(200U);
	if (error != E_OK) return error;
	for (i = 0; i < 3U; i++) {
		error = imu_read_probe_register(sensor, low_regs[i], &released[i]);
		if (error != E_OK) return error;
		if (released[i] != held[i]) changed_after_release = true;
		error = imu_read_probe_register(sensor, low_regs[i] + 1U, &high);
		if (error != E_OK) return error;
	}
	tm_printf((UB*)"[HWTEST][IMU] BDU low bytes: first=%02x,%02x,%02x held=%02x,%02x,%02x "
		  "released=%02x,%02x,%02x result=%s\n",
		  (unsigned)first[0], (unsigned)first[1], (unsigned)first[2],
		  (unsigned)held[0], (unsigned)held[1], (unsigned)held[2],
		  (unsigned)released[0], (unsigned)released[1], (unsigned)released[2],
		  changed_while_held ? "changed-while-held" :
		  changed_after_release ? "consistent-not-proof" : "inconclusive");
	return E_OK;
}

static ER imu_read_unverified(const lsm303dlhc_i2c_t *sensor,
			      const lsm303dlhc_axis_map_t *mapping,
			      accel_data_t *sample, uint8_t *status, uint8_t *after,
			      uint32_t *burst_us, uint32_t *status_us,
			      T_I2C_PROBE_TRACE *trace)
{
	uint8_t reg = 0xA7U;
	uint8_t bytes[7];
	T_I2C_EXEC exec = {sensor->address, 1, &reg, sizeof(bytes), bytes};
	SZ actual = 0;
	uint32_t start = in_w(TIMER_TIMERAWL);
	uint32_t burst_end, status_start, status_end;
	ER error = tk_swri_dev(sensor->device, TDN_I2C_EXEC, &exec, sizeof(exec), &actual);

	burst_end = in_w(TIMER_TIMERAWL);
	if (error != E_OK || actual != (SZ)sizeof(exec)) {
		tm_printf((UB*)"[HWTEST][IMU] direct read failed: %d size=%d\n",
			  (int)error, (int)actual);
		return error != E_OK ? error : E_IO;
	}
	error = dev_i2c_probe_trace(1U, trace);
	if (error != E_OK) {
		tm_printf((UB*)"[HWTEST][IMU] I2C trace read failed: %d\n", (int)error);
		return error;
	}
	if (trace->irq_count == 0U || trace->receive_irq_count == 0U) {
		tm_printf((UB*)"[HWTEST][IMU] I2C trace missing receive interrupt\n");
		return E_IO;
	}
	status_start = in_w(TIMER_TIMERAWL);
	error = imu_read_status(sensor, after);
	status_end = in_w(TIMER_TIMERAWL);
	if (error != E_OK) return error;
	if (!lsm303dlhc_accel_decode(&bytes[1], mapping, sample)) {
		tm_printf((UB*)"[HWTEST][IMU] direct read returned invalid accelerometer data\n");
		return E_IO;
	}
	*status = bytes[0];
	*burst_us = burst_end - start;
	*status_us = status_end - status_start;
	return E_OK;
}

static ER imu_check_freshness(const lsm303dlhc_i2c_t *sensor, uint8_t before,
			      uint8_t after)
{
	uint8_t ready;
	SYSTIM start, now;
	ER error;

	if (!(before & IMU_PROBE_ZYXDA) || (after & IMU_PROBE_ZYXDA)) {
		tm_printf((UB*)"[HWTEST][IMU] freshness inconclusive: before=0x%02x after=0x%02x\n",
			  (unsigned)before, (unsigned)after);
		return E_OK;
	}
	error = tk_get_otm(&start);
	if (error != E_OK) return error;
	for (;;) {
		error = tk_dly_tsk(1U);
		if (error != E_OK) return error;
		error = imu_read_status(sensor, &ready);
		if (error != E_OK) return error;
		error = tk_get_otm(&now);
		if (error != E_OK) return error;
		if (now.lo - start.lo > IMU_PROBE_READY_WAIT_MS ||
		    (!(ready & IMU_PROBE_ZYXDA) &&
		     now.lo - start.lo >= IMU_PROBE_READY_WAIT_MS)) {
			tm_printf((UB*)"[HWTEST][IMU] freshness timeout: before=0x%02x after=0x%02x "
				  "status=0x%02x elapsed=%u ms limit=%u ms\n",
				  (unsigned)before, (unsigned)after, (unsigned)ready,
				  (unsigned)(now.lo - start.lo), IMU_PROBE_READY_WAIT_MS);
			return E_TMOUT;
		}
		if (ready & IMU_PROBE_ZYXDA) break;
	}
	tm_printf((UB*)"[HWTEST][IMU] data-ready transition observed: before=0x%02x "
		  "after=0x%02x ready=0x%02x wait=%u ms\n",
		  (unsigned)before, (unsigned)after, (unsigned)ready,
		  (unsigned)(now.lo - start.lo));
	return E_OK;
}

static void sensor_test_task(INT stacd, void *exinf)
{
	lsm303dlhc_i2c_t sensor = {0};
	lsm303dlhc_i2c_config_t config;
	unsigned probe_count;
	ER error;

	(void)stacd;
	(void)exinf;
	error = lsm303dlhc_i2c_default_config(&config, 20U);
	if (error != E_OK) report_error_forever("IMU", "default config", error);
	config.profile = LSM303DLHC_I2C_PROFILE_32_FAMILY;
	error = lsm303dlhc_i2c_init(&sensor, &config);
	if (error != E_OK) report_error_forever("IMU", "sensor init", error);
	error = imu_check_trace_clock();
	if (error != E_OK) report_error_forever("IMU", "trace clock comparison", error);
	imu_report_identity(&sensor);
	error = imu_set_probe_mode(&sensor);
	if (error != E_OK) report_error_forever("IMU", "probe mode", error);

	tm_printf((UB*)"[HWTEST][IMU] fast BDU probe CTRL1=0x2f CTRL4=0x80; "
		  "direct readings, freshness unverified\n");
	tm_printf((UB*)"[HWTEST][IMU] shared I2C wake-up fix enabled\n");
	error = imu_probe_bdu(&sensor);
	if (error != E_OK) report_error_forever("IMU", "BDU probe", error);
	for (probe_count = 0U; probe_count < 5U; probe_count++) {
		accel_data_t sample;
		SYSTIM now;
		uint32_t burst_us, status_us;
		uint8_t status, after;
		T_I2C_PROBE_TRACE trace;

		error = imu_read_unverified(&sensor, &config.mapping, &sample, &status, &after,
					    &burst_us, &status_us, &trace);
		if (error != E_OK) report_error_forever("IMU", "read", error);
		error = tk_get_otm(&now);
		if (error != E_OK) report_error_forever("IMU", "clock", error);
		error = imu_check_freshness(&sensor, status, after);
		if (error != E_OK) report_error_forever("IMU", "freshness check", error);
		tm_printf((UB*)"[HWTEST][IMU] unverified t_read=%u ms burst_us=%u status_us=%u "
			  "status=0x%02x raw_x=%d raw_y=%d raw_z=%d "
			  "ax_mg=%ld ay_mg=%ld az_mg=%ld\n",
			  (unsigned)now.lo, (unsigned)burst_us, (unsigned)status_us,
			  (unsigned)status, (int)sample.raw_x, (int)sample.raw_y,
			  (int)sample.raw_z,
			  (long)(sample.ax * 1000.0f / 9.80665f),
			  (long)(sample.ay * 1000.0f / 9.80665f),
			  (long)(sample.az * 1000.0f / 9.80665f));
		tm_printf((UB*)"[HWTEST][IMU] I2C trace driver_us=%u first_irq_us=%u "
			  "last_irq_us=%u irq_count=%u\n",
			  (unsigned)(trace.end_us - trace.start_us),
			  (unsigned)(trace.first_irq_us - trace.start_us),
			  (unsigned)(trace.last_irq_us - trace.start_us),
			  (unsigned)trace.irq_count);
		tm_printf((UB*)"[HWTEST][IMU] I2C phases recv_command_us=%u "
			  "first_rx_us=%u last_rx_us=%u rx_resume_us=%u "
			  "stop_arm_us=%u stop_pending=%u\n",
			  (unsigned)(trace.receive_command_us - trace.start_us),
			  (unsigned)(trace.first_receive_irq_us - trace.start_us),
			  (unsigned)(trace.last_receive_irq_us - trace.start_us),
			  (unsigned)(trace.receive_resume_us - trace.start_us),
			  (unsigned)(trace.stop_arm_us - trace.start_us),
			  (unsigned)trace.stop_pending);
		error = tk_dly_tsk(IMU_PROBE_INTERVAL_MS);
		if (error != E_OK) report_error_forever("IMU", "delay", error);
	}
	error = lsm303dlhc_i2c_close(&sensor);
	if (error != E_OK) report_error_forever("IMU", "probe close", error);
	sensor = (lsm303dlhc_i2c_t){0};
	error = lsm303dlhc_i2c_init(&sensor, &config);
	if (error != E_OK) report_error_forever("IMU", "vehicle reader init", error);
	tm_printf((UB*)"[HWTEST][IMU] vehicle reader: CTRL1=0x2f CTRL4=0x80; "
		  "data-ready gating enabled; sample time is a lower bound\n");
	for (;;) {
		accel_data_t sample;
		SYSTIM now;
		uint32_t timestamp;

		error = lsm303dlhc_i2c_read(&sensor, &sample, &timestamp);
		if (error != E_OK) report_error_forever("IMU", "vehicle reader", error);
		error = tk_get_otm(&now);
		if (error != E_OK) report_error_forever("IMU", "clock", error);
		tm_printf((UB*)"[HWTEST][IMU] vehicle t_lower=%u ms age_bound=%u ms "
			  "raw_x=%d raw_y=%d raw_z=%d ax_mg=%ld ay_mg=%ld az_mg=%ld\n",
			  (unsigned)timestamp, (unsigned)(now.lo - timestamp),
			  (int)sample.raw_x, (int)sample.raw_y, (int)sample.raw_z,
			  (long)(sample.ax * 1000.0f / 9.80665f),
			  (long)(sample.ay * 1000.0f / 9.80665f),
			  (long)(sample.az * 1000.0f / 9.80665f));
		error = tk_dly_tsk(50U);
		if (error != E_OK) report_error_forever("IMU", "delay", error);
	}
}
#elif APP_SENSOR_TEST == 3
static void sensor_test_task(INT stacd, void *exinf)
{
	static const obs_task_config_t task_config = {
		50U, 500U, 1000U, 5000U, 500U, 300U,
		NULL, NULL, NULL, NULL
	};
	obs_pico_port_config_t hardware;
	ER error;

	(void)stacd;
	(void)exinf;
	hardware = obs_pico_port_default_config();
	hardware.echo_voltage_verified_3v3_safe = true;
	hardware.servo_limits_verified = true;

	error = obs_pico_port_register();
	if (error != E_OK) report_error_forever("ULTRASONIC", "GPIO registration", error);
	error = gpio_dispatcher_start();
	if (error != E_OK) report_error_forever("ULTRASONIC", "GPIO dispatcher", error);
	error = obs_pico_port_task_start(&hardware, &task_config);
	if (error != E_OK) report_error_forever("ULTRASONIC", "sensor task start", error);

	tm_printf((UB*)"[HWTEST][ULTRASONIC] live distance readings; GP5 trigger, "
		  "GP4 echo; servo moves to the ahead angle\n");
	for (;;) {
		obstacle_event_message_t event;
		INT received = tk_rcv_mbf(g_mbf_obstacle_events, &event, TMO_FEVR);

		if (received != (INT)sizeof(event))
			report_error_forever("ULTRASONIC", "event receive",
					     received < E_OK ? (ER)received : E_IO);
		if (event.header.type != VEHICLE_MESSAGE_OBSTACLE_EVENT ||
		    event.header.version != VEHICLE_MESSAGE_VERSION)
			report_error_forever("ULTRASONIC", "invalid event", E_SYS);
		if (event.distance_mm == OBS_DIST_NONE) {
			tm_printf((UB*)"[HWTEST][ULTRASONIC] t=%u ms distance=NONE "
				  "(clear or beyond range)\n",
				  (unsigned)event.header.timestamp_ms);
		} else {
			tm_printf((UB*)"[HWTEST][ULTRASONIC] t=%u ms distance=%u mm\n",
				  (unsigned)event.header.timestamp_ms,
				  (unsigned)event.distance_mm);
		}
	}
}
#elif APP_SENSOR_TEST == 4
#define SERVO_TEST_PIN 15U
#define SERVO_TEST_SLICE 7U
#define SERVO_TEST_DIV_REG (PWM_BASE + PWM_CHx_DIV + (SERVO_TEST_SLICE * 0x14U))
#define SERVO_TEST_DIVIDER 125U
#define SERVO_TEST_WRAP 19999U
#define SERVO_TEST_DELAY_MS 5000U
#define SERVO_TEST_SLICE_BASE (PWM_BASE + SERVO_TEST_SLICE * 0x14U)

static void sensor_test_task(INT stacd, void *exinf)
{
	ER error;

	(void)stacd;
	(void)exinf;
	error = pwm_set_enabled(SERVO_TEST_PIN, FALSE);
	if (error != E_OK) report_error_forever("SERVO", "disable PWM", error);
	error = pwm_set_cc(SERVO_TEST_PIN, 0U);
	if (error != E_OK) report_error_forever("SERVO", "clear PWM", error);
#if SERVO_TEST_US == 0
	tm_printf((UB*)"[HWTEST][SERVO] PWM off. Build with SERVO_TEST_US to select one pulse.\n");
#else
	error = pwm_set_wrap(SERVO_TEST_PIN, SERVO_TEST_WRAP);
	if (error != E_OK) report_error_forever("SERVO", "PWM period", error);
	out_w(SERVO_TEST_DIV_REG, SERVO_TEST_DIVIDER << 4);
	error = pwm_set_pin(SERVO_TEST_PIN);
	if (error != E_OK) report_error_forever("SERVO", "PWM pin", error);

	tm_printf((UB*)"[HWTEST][SERVO] GP15 will output %u us in 5 seconds; "
		  "disconnect servo power now if unsafe.\n", (unsigned)SERVO_TEST_US);
	error = tk_dly_tsk(SERVO_TEST_DELAY_MS);
	if (error != E_OK) report_error_forever("SERVO", "delay", error);
	error = pwm_set_cc(SERVO_TEST_PIN, SERVO_TEST_US);
	if (error != E_OK) report_error_forever("SERVO", "pulse width", error);
	error = pwm_set_enabled(SERVO_TEST_PIN, TRUE);
	if (error != E_OK) report_error_forever("SERVO", "enable PWM", error);
	tm_printf((UB*)"[HWTEST][SERVO] GP15 configured for %u us; no automatic sweep.\n",
		  (unsigned)SERVO_TEST_US);
	tm_printf((UB*)"[HWTEST][SERVO] reset_done=%u gpio_ctrl=0x%x pad=0x%x "
		  "csr=0x%x div=0x%x top=%u cc=0x%x\n",
		  (unsigned)((in_w(RESETS_RESET_DONE) & RESETS_RESET_PWM) != 0U),
		  (unsigned)in_w(GPIO_CTRL(SERVO_TEST_PIN)),
		  (unsigned)in_w(GPIO(SERVO_TEST_PIN)),
		  (unsigned)in_w(SERVO_TEST_SLICE_BASE + PWM_CHx_CSR),
		  (unsigned)in_w(SERVO_TEST_DIV_REG),
		  (unsigned)in_w(SERVO_TEST_SLICE_BASE + PWM_CHx_TOP),
		  (unsigned)in_w(SERVO_TEST_SLICE_BASE + PWM_CHx_CC));
#endif
	tk_slp_tsk(TMO_FEVR);
}
#else
#error APP_SENSOR_TEST must be 0, 1 (IR), 2 (IMU), 3 (ultrasonic), or 4 (servo)
#endif

ER hardware_sensor_test_start(void)
{
	ID task = app_start_task((FP)sensor_test_task, SENSOR_TEST_PRIORITY,
				 TP_PRC1, "hardware_sensor_test");

	return task < E_OK ? (ER)task : E_OK;
}

=== C:\Users\Yong Jun\school\INF2004-Embedded_Systems\project\INF2004_Project\app_program\test_report.h ===
/*
 *	test_report.h
 *	Minimal on-target test helpers.
 *
 *	Every check prints ONE line in a fixed format, so a saved console log
 *	can be searched for results ("grep '\[TEST\]' log.txt"):
 *
 *	    [TEST] S1-U01 PASS  ADC reads in range
 *	    [TEST] S1-U02 FAIL  queue rejects overflow   (task_x.c:57)
 *	    [TEST] subsystem1 SUMMARY 1 passed, 1 failed
 *
 *	Usage, inside a test function:
 *	    TEST_INIT();
 *	    TEST_CHECK("S1-U01", value >= 0 && value <= 4095, "ADC reads in range");
 *	    TEST_SUMMARY("subsystem1");
 *
 *	Test IDs must match the ones in testing/<subsystem>/TEST_LOG.md.
 */

#ifndef TEST_REPORT_H
#define TEST_REPORT_H

#include <tm/tmonitor.h>

#define TEST_INIT()	INT test_pass_ = 0, test_fail_ = 0

#define TEST_CHECK(id, cond, desc)					\
	do {								\
		if (cond) {						\
			test_pass_++;					\
			tm_printf((UB*)"[TEST] %s PASS  %s\n", (id), (desc)); \
		} else {						\
			test_fail_++;					\
			tm_printf((UB*)"[TEST] %s FAIL  %s   (%s:%d)\n",	\
				  (id), (desc), __FILE__, __LINE__);	\
		}							\
	} while (0)

#define TEST_SUMMARY(name)						\
	tm_printf((UB*)"[TEST] %s SUMMARY %d passed, %d failed\n",	\
		  (name), test_pass_, test_fail_)

#endif /* TEST_REPORT_H */

#include "app.h"
#include "vehicle_buffers.h"

#if APP_TEST
#include "test_report.h"
#endif

/*
 * The RP2040 kernel uses an INT-sized header and rounds each message up to
 * sizeof(INT) bytes in its ring buffer (kernel/tkernel/messagebuf.h).
 */
#define BUFFER_WORDS(type, capacity) \
	((capacity) * (1U + ((sizeof(type) + sizeof(INT) - 1U) / sizeof(INT))))
#define DEFINE_BUFFER(name, type, capacity) \
	ID g_mbf_##name; \
	static INT s_##name##_storage[BUFFER_WORDS(type, capacity)]

DEFINE_BUFFER(line_observations, line_observation_message_t, VEHICLE_MB_LINE_CAPACITY);
DEFINE_BUFFER(barcode_events, barcode_event_message_t, VEHICLE_MB_BARCODE_CAPACITY);
DEFINE_BUFFER(imu_estimates, imu_estimate_message_t, VEHICLE_MB_IMU_CAPACITY);
DEFINE_BUFFER(obstacle_events, obstacle_event_message_t, VEHICLE_MB_OBSTACLE_CAPACITY);
DEFINE_BUFFER(obstacle_commands, obstacle_command_message_t, VEHICLE_MB_OBSTACLE_COMMAND_CAPACITY);
DEFINE_BUFFER(line_odometry, wheel_odometry_message_t, VEHICLE_MB_ODOMETRY_CAPACITY);
DEFINE_BUFFER(imu_odometry, wheel_odometry_message_t, VEHICLE_MB_ODOMETRY_CAPACITY);
DEFINE_BUFFER(motion_odometry, wheel_odometry_message_t, VEHICLE_MB_ODOMETRY_CAPACITY);
DEFINE_BUFFER(motion_commands, motion_command_message_t, VEHICLE_MB_MOTION_CAPACITY);
DEFINE_BUFFER(motion_status, motion_status_message_t, VEHICLE_MB_STATUS_CAPACITY);
DEFINE_BUFFER(remote_commands, remote_command_message_t, VEHICLE_MB_REMOTE_CAPACITY);
DEFINE_BUFFER(network_status, network_status_message_t, VEHICLE_MB_NETWORK_CAPACITY);
DEFINE_BUFFER(telemetry, telemetry_message_t, VEHICLE_MB_TELEMETRY_CAPACITY);
DEFINE_BUFFER(faults, fault_message_t, VEHICLE_MB_FAULT_CAPACITY);

typedef struct {
	ID *id;
	INT *storage;
	SZ storage_size;
	INT message_size;
	UINT capacity;
	const char *name;
} vehicle_buffer_definition_t;

#define BUFFER_ENTRY(name, type, capacity) \
	{ &g_mbf_##name, s_##name##_storage, (SZ)sizeof(s_##name##_storage), \
	  (INT)sizeof(type), (capacity), #name }

static const vehicle_buffer_definition_t s_buffers[] = {
	BUFFER_ENTRY(line_observations, line_observation_message_t, VEHICLE_MB_LINE_CAPACITY),
	BUFFER_ENTRY(barcode_events, barcode_event_message_t, VEHICLE_MB_BARCODE_CAPACITY),
	BUFFER_ENTRY(imu_estimates, imu_estimate_message_t, VEHICLE_MB_IMU_CAPACITY),
	BUFFER_ENTRY(obstacle_events, obstacle_event_message_t, VEHICLE_MB_OBSTACLE_CAPACITY),
	BUFFER_ENTRY(obstacle_commands, obstacle_command_message_t, VEHICLE_MB_OBSTACLE_COMMAND_CAPACITY),
	BUFFER_ENTRY(line_odometry, wheel_odometry_message_t, VEHICLE_MB_ODOMETRY_CAPACITY),
	BUFFER_ENTRY(imu_odometry, wheel_odometry_message_t, VEHICLE_MB_ODOMETRY_CAPACITY),
	BUFFER_ENTRY(motion_odometry, wheel_odometry_message_t, VEHICLE_MB_ODOMETRY_CAPACITY),
	BUFFER_ENTRY(motion_commands, motion_command_message_t, VEHICLE_MB_MOTION_CAPACITY),
	BUFFER_ENTRY(motion_status, motion_status_message_t, VEHICLE_MB_STATUS_CAPACITY),
	BUFFER_ENTRY(remote_commands, remote_command_message_t, VEHICLE_MB_REMOTE_CAPACITY),
	BUFFER_ENTRY(network_status, network_status_message_t, VEHICLE_MB_NETWORK_CAPACITY),
	BUFFER_ENTRY(telemetry, telemetry_message_t, VEHICLE_MB_TELEMETRY_CAPACITY),
	BUFFER_ENTRY(faults, fault_message_t, VEHICLE_MB_FAULT_CAPACITY)
};

ER vehicle_buffers_init(void)
{
	UINT i;

	for (i = 0; i < sizeof(s_buffers) / sizeof(s_buffers[0]); ++i) {
		T_CMBF config = {0};
		ID id;

		config.mbfatr = TA_TFIFO | TA_USERBUF;
		config.bufsz = s_buffers[i].storage_size;
		config.maxmsz = s_buffers[i].message_size;
		config.bufptr = s_buffers[i].storage;
		id = tk_cre_mbf(&config);
		if (id < E_OK) {
			ER error = id;
			tm_printf((UB*)"ERROR creating vehicle buffer %s: %d\n",
				  s_buffers[i].name, (int)error);
			while (i > 0) {
				ER cleanup;
				--i;
				cleanup = tk_del_mbf(*s_buffers[i].id);
				if (cleanup < E_OK) {
					tm_printf((UB*)"ERROR deleting vehicle buffer %s: %d\n",
						  s_buffers[i].name, (int)cleanup);
				} else {
					*s_buffers[i].id = 0;
				}
			}
			return error;
		}
		*s_buffers[i].id = id;
	}
	return E_OK;
}

#if APP_TEST
BOOL vehicle_buffers_test(void)
{
	union {
		line_observation_message_t line;
		barcode_event_message_t barcode;
		imu_estimate_message_t imu;
		obstacle_event_message_t obstacle;
		obstacle_command_message_t obstacle_command;
		wheel_odometry_message_t odometry;
		motion_command_message_t motion_command;
		motion_status_message_t motion_status;
		remote_command_message_t remote;
		network_status_message_t network;
		telemetry_message_t telemetry;
		fault_message_t fault;
	} sent, received;
	UINT i;
	BOOL ok = TRUE;
	TEST_INIT();

	for (i = 0; i < sizeof(s_buffers) / sizeof(s_buffers[0]); ++i) {
		UINT j;
		const vehicle_buffer_definition_t *buffer = &s_buffers[i];
		BOOL buffer_ok = TRUE;

		for (j = 0; j < buffer->capacity; ++j) {
			UINT k;
			for (k = 0; k < (UINT)buffer->message_size; ++k) {
				((UB *)&sent)[k] = (UB)(j + 1U);
			}
			if (tk_snd_mbf(*buffer->id, &sent, buffer->message_size,
				       TMO_POL) != E_OK) {
				buffer_ok = FALSE;
				break;
			}
		}
		if (j == buffer->capacity &&
		    tk_snd_mbf(*buffer->id, &sent, buffer->message_size,
			       TMO_POL) != E_TMOUT) {
			buffer_ok = FALSE;
		}
		for (j = 0; j < buffer->capacity; ++j) {
			UINT k;
			if (tk_rcv_mbf(*buffer->id, &received, TMO_POL) !=
			    buffer->message_size) {
				buffer_ok = FALSE;
				break;
			}
			for (k = 0; k < (UINT)buffer->message_size; ++k) {
				if (((UB *)&received)[k] != (UB)(j + 1U)) {
					buffer_ok = FALSE;
					break;
				}
			}
		}
		if (!buffer_ok) {
			tm_printf((UB*)"vehicle buffer test failed: %s\n", buffer->name);
			ok = FALSE;
		}
	}
	TEST_CHECK("INT-03", ok, "vehicle buffers copy messages and hold configured capacity");
	TEST_SUMMARY("vehicle buffers");
	return ok;
}
#endif

#ifndef VEHICLE_MESSAGES_H
#define VEHICLE_MESSAGES_H

#include <stdbool.h>
#include <stdint.h>

/*
 * Inter-task messages are copied into ÂµT-Kernel message buffers. Messages
 * therefore contain values only; they must not contain pointers to task-owned
 * or stack storage.
 */

#define VEHICLE_MESSAGE_VERSION            1U
#define VEHICLE_BARCODE_PAYLOAD_SIZE       32U
#define VEHICLE_TELEMETRY_PAYLOAD_SIZE     128U
#define VEHICLE_REMOTE_ARGUMENT_SIZE       32U
#define VEHICLE_MOTION_STATUS_PERIOD_MS  100U
/* A clear validity bit means the corresponding fields are not measurements. */
#define VEHICLE_IMU_VALID_ROLL_PITCH       0x0001U
#define VEHICLE_IMU_VALID_YAW              0x0002U
#define VEHICLE_IMU_VALID_GYRO             0x0004U

typedef enum {
    VEHICLE_MESSAGE_LINE_OBSERVATION = 1,
    VEHICLE_MESSAGE_BARCODE_EVENT,
    VEHICLE_MESSAGE_IMU_ESTIMATE,
    VEHICLE_MESSAGE_OBSTACLE_EVENT,
    VEHICLE_MESSAGE_OBSTACLE_COMMAND,
    VEHICLE_MESSAGE_WHEEL_ODOMETRY,
    VEHICLE_MESSAGE_MOTION_COMMAND,
    VEHICLE_MESSAGE_MOTION_STATUS,
    VEHICLE_MESSAGE_REMOTE_COMMAND,
    VEHICLE_MESSAGE_NETWORK_STATUS,
    VEHICLE_MESSAGE_TELEMETRY,
    VEHICLE_MESSAGE_FAULT
} vehicle_message_type_t;

typedef enum {
    VEHICLE_BUFFER_LINE_OBSERVATIONS = 1,
    VEHICLE_BUFFER_BARCODE_EVENTS,
    VEHICLE_BUFFER_IMU_ESTIMATES,
    VEHICLE_BUFFER_OBSTACLE_EVENTS,
    VEHICLE_BUFFER_OBSTACLE_COMMANDS,
    VEHICLE_BUFFER_LINE_ODOMETRY,
    VEHICLE_BUFFER_IMU_ODOMETRY,
    VEHICLE_BUFFER_MOTION_COMMANDS,
    VEHICLE_BUFFER_MOTION_STATUS,
    VEHICLE_BUFFER_REMOTE_COMMANDS,
    VEHICLE_BUFFER_NETWORK_STATUS,
    VEHICLE_BUFFER_TELEMETRY,
    VEHICLE_BUFFER_FAULTS,
    VEHICLE_BUFFER_MOTION_ODOMETRY
} vehicle_buffer_id_t;

typedef enum {
    VEHICLE_BUFFER_KEEP_LATEST = 1,
    VEHICLE_BUFFER_KEEP_ORDERED,
    VEHICLE_BUFFER_PRIORITY
} vehicle_buffer_policy_t;

typedef enum {
    VEHICLE_MOTION_STOP = 0,
    VEHICLE_MOTION_ARM,
    VEHICLE_MOTION_VELOCITY,
    VEHICLE_MOTION_TURN,
    VEHICLE_MOTION_EMERGENCY_STOP
} vehicle_motion_mode_t;

typedef enum {
    VEHICLE_FAULT_NONE = 0,
    VEHICLE_FAULT_SENSOR,
    VEHICLE_FAULT_MOTION,
    VEHICLE_FAULT_COMMUNICATION,
    VEHICLE_FAULT_SYSTEM
} vehicle_fault_source_t;

typedef enum {

    VEHICLE_OBSTACLE_BYPASS_COMPLETE,
    VEHICLE_OBSTACLE_ABORT,
    VEHICLE_OBSTACLE_RESET

typedef enum {

typedef struct {
    uint16_t type;
    uint16_t version;
    uint32_t sequence;
    uint32_t timestamp_ms;
} vehicle_message_header_t;

typedef struct {
    vehicle_message_header_t header;
    int16_t lateral_error;
    uint16_t confidence;
    uint8_t line_detected;
    uint8_t intersection_detected;
} line_observation_message_t;

typedef struct {
    vehicle_message_header_t header;
    char payload[VEHICLE_BARCODE_PAYLOAD_SIZE];
    uint8_t length;
    uint8_t valid;
} barcode_event_message_t;

typedef struct {
    vehicle_message_header_t header;
    int32_t roll_mdeg;
    int32_t pitch_mdeg;
    int32_t yaw_mdeg;
    int16_t gyro_x;
    int16_t gyro_y;
    int16_t gyro_z;
    uint16_t validity;
    uint16_t terrain_telemetry;
    uint8_t shock_detected;
    uint8_t tilt_detected;
} imu_estimate_message_t;

typedef struct {
    vehicle_message_header_t header;
    /* Minimum reported range, or 0x0fff when all measurements report none. */
    uint16_t distance_mm;
    uint8_t obstacle_class;
    uint8_t emergency;
    uint16_t target_distance_mm;
    uint16_t width_mm;
    uint16_t left_mm;
    uint16_t right_mm;
    uint16_t lateral_mm;
    uint16_t advance_mm;
    uint8_t advisory_action;
} obstacle_event_message_t;

typedef struct {
    vehicle_message_header_t header;
    uint8_t command;
} obstacle_command_message_t;

typedef struct {
    vehicle_message_header_t header;
    int32_t left_count;
    int32_t right_count;
    int32_t distance_mm;
    uint8_t valid;
} wheel_odometry_message_t;

typedef struct {
    vehicle_message_header_t header;
    int16_t linear_velocity;
    int16_t angular_velocity;
    uint16_t duration_ms;
    uint8_t mode;
    uint8_t stop_reason;
} motion_command_message_t;

typedef struct {
    vehicle_message_header_t header;
    /* Signed commanded motor PWM, not measured motion or proof of a stop. */
    int16_t motor_1_pwm;
    int16_t motor_2_pwm;
    /* active is accepted command state; ARM acceptance is not reported. */
    uint8_t active;
    uint8_t faulted;
    uint16_t fault_code;
} motion_status_message_t;

typedef struct {
    vehicle_message_header_t header;
    uint8_t command;
    int32_t value;
    char argument[VEHICLE_REMOTE_ARGUMENT_SIZE];
} remote_command_message_t;

typedef struct {
    vehicle_message_header_t header;
    uint8_t connected;
    uint8_t mqtt_ready;
    uint8_t retry_count;
    uint8_t error_code;
} network_status_message_t;

typedef struct {
    vehicle_message_header_t header;
    uint8_t event_type;
    uint8_t priority;
    uint16_t payload_length;
    char payload[VEHICLE_TELEMETRY_PAYLOAD_SIZE];
} telemetry_message_t;

typedef struct {
    vehicle_message_header_t header;
    uint8_t source;
    uint8_t recoverable;
    uint16_t code;
} fault_message_t;

/*
 * These limits describe the initial queue contract. They are not ÂµT-Kernel
 * object IDs; the task startup module will create the actual message buffers.
 */
#define VEHICLE_MB_LINE_CAPACITY       4U
#define VEHICLE_MB_BARCODE_CAPACITY    8U
#define VEHICLE_MB_IMU_CAPACITY        2U
#define VEHICLE_MB_OBSTACLE_CAPACITY   4U
#define VEHICLE_MB_OBSTACLE_COMMAND_CAPACITY 4U
#define VEHICLE_MB_ODOMETRY_CAPACITY   2U
#define VEHICLE_MB_MOTION_CAPACITY     2U
#define VEHICLE_MB_STATUS_CAPACITY     2U
#define VEHICLE_MB_REMOTE_CAPACITY     4U
#define VEHICLE_MB_NETWORK_CAPACITY    2U
#define VEHICLE_MB_TELEMETRY_CAPACITY  8U
#define VEHICLE_MB_FAULT_CAPACITY      4U
